- CR: 701.9 (discard: the player chooses unless the effect says otherwise), 702.21a (Ward: counter that spell or ability unless its controller pays the cost), 118.3 (a cost that can't be paid isn't), 602.2b (an activation is paid like a spell's cost), 733 (a taken-back activation is rewound).
- Gap class: P (an existing cost kind made runnable in two more places)
- Code: `rules/pay-unless.ts` (`discardOf`, `canPayUnless`, `acceptUnless`, `discardUnless`, stage `'discard'` of the instruction), `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionCards`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`breakDown`, `costRefusal`), `rules/activate.ts` (`payDiscard`, `answerDiscardCost`), `rules/discard-cost.ts`, `api.ts`, `ast/supported.ts` (`activationCostIssues`), `ast/validate.ts` (`checkActivationCosts`); AI: `casual-costs.ts` (`wardDiscardAnswer`, `chooseCostDiscard`)
- AST node: `Cost` `{ kind: 'discard', count }` in `Keyword` `ward` (and in `counterUnlessPays`), and in the cost of an `activated` ability: one discard cost, not from the hand, not beside a crew cost. Cycling and Equip still take only mana and life.
- Decision and action: Ward asks the payer (`confirmOptional`, `purpose: 'ward'`, `cost` with the discard); accepting pays life, then the cards: all of the hand when it holds exactly N, otherwise a `chooseCards` decision (`purpose: 'discard'`, no `forCost`, no `cancel`) from the payer's hand; then the mana. A payer with fewer than N cards cannot pay: the spell or ability is countered and nobody is asked. An activation asks a `chooseCards` with `forCost` after its mana and sacrifice, and `cancel` rewinds it; an ability whose controller has too few cards is not offered.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-discard-costs.test.ts` (the Ward question and the choice, declining, one card without a question, an empty hand, only the payer's hand and one card, an ability aimed at the guard; the activation: choice, one card, an empty hand not offered, cancel, repeated), `ast-costs-validate.test.ts`; AI: `cost-games.test.ts`, `casual-costs.test.ts`
- Example cards: "Ward—Discard a card." (Ward costs of this kind), "Discard a card: ~ gets +1/+1 until end of turn." SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Tithe Warded Guard`, `SYNTHETIC Scavenger Bear`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; Ward and activated costs need the parser.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 13, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Ward—Discard a card." lowers to `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'ward', cost: [{ kind: 'discard', count: 1 }] } }`; "Ward—Discard two cards" has `count: 2`; "Discard a card: ~ gets +1/+1 until end of turn." to an `activated` ability with `cost: [{ kind: 'discard', count: 1 }]` and the `modifyPt` effect; "{1}, Discard a card: ..." has the mana cost before the discard. "Discard a creature card" (a filter) and "discard your hand" are not built. The `FeatureRef` is `cost:discard`.
- Notes: A discard cost never includes the source: an activated ability does not function from the hand with it (`validateCard` refuses it).

## Cost reductions: "~ costs {1} less to cast for each creature on the battlefield" and "Artifact spells you cast cost {1} less to cast" (engine slice; CR 118.7, 601.2f, 113.6d, 604.2)

- CR: 118.7 and 118.7a (a reduction of generic mana affects only the generic part of the cost), 601.2f (the total cost: mana cost or alternative cost, plus additional costs and increases, minus reductions; reduced to nothing it is {0}, never less; then it is locked in), 601.2h (the total is locked in before payment, so a sacrifice paid afterwards does not change it), 113.6d (an object's ability that modifies what it costs to cast functions on the stack), 604.2 (a static ability works while its permanent is on the battlefield), 903.8 (commander tax is an additional cost), 702.33a (kicker is too).
- Gap class: P (a new static ability kind read by the casting code)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `costReduction`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`spellCost`, `reduceGeneric`, `castCostOf`), `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`costReductionSources`: the battlefield index of reducing permanents), `rules/legality.ts` (`canCast`), `rules/cast.ts` (`beginCast` for the kicker question, `toPayment`), `view.ts` (`ObjectView.castCost`), `ast/validate-cost.ts` (`checkCostReduction`); AI: `casual-costs.ts` (`effectiveCost`, `discountsHand`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'costReduction', spells: { kind: 'this' } | ObjectFilter, amount } }` with `amount` a positive number or a `countOf`. `this` is on a card's face; a filter describes the spell being cast (types, `anyOf`, `controller: 'you'` for "you cast") and sits on a permanent. Several reductions add up. A permanent that lost all its abilities reduces nothing (CR 613.1f).
- Decision and action: none new. The `payMana` decision, `totalCost` of the casting context, the legal actions and `ObjectView.castCost` all show the reduced cost; the commander tax and the kicker's cost are in it before the reduction; a cost reduced to nothing asks for no payment.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-cost-reduction.test.ts` (the self reduction by creatures of both players, exactly enough lands and one short, never below zero with the colored part left alone, the spell not counting itself, the cost locked in at CR 601.2f, cancel; the Bench for artifact spells and artifact creatures, not for others' or the opponent's, and not once the permanent lost its abilities; the Chant for instants and sorceries with {R}, {2}{U} and {1}{B}{B}; two permanents stacking, a count and a number together; commander tax reduced, a kicked spell reduced on its kicker cost, the locked-in cost before a sacrifice; the view and the offer agree for every land count), `scenario-xx-cost-legal-actions.test.ts`, `ast-costs-validate.test.ts`; AI: `cost-games.test.ts` (an independent restatement of the reductions checked at every lock-in), `casual-costs.test.ts`. Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: Blasphemous Act ("This spell costs {1} less to cast for each creature on the battlefield."), "Artifact spells you cast cost {1} less to cast.", "Instant and sorcery spells you cast cost {1} less to cast." (16 cards). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Wrath of Many`, `Tinker Bench`, `Spell Chant`, `Giant Discount`, `Market Haze`, `Sacrifice Rite`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its text (stage P11).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 13, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "This spell costs {1} less to cast for each creature on the battlefield." (and "~ costs {1} less ..." with the name) lowers to an ability of the face `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'costReduction', spells: { kind: 'this' }, amount: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['creature'] } } } }`; "This spell costs {2} less to cast." has `amount: 2`. "Artifact spells you cast cost {1} less to cast." to `spells: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you' }, amount: 1`; "Instant and sorcery spells you cast cost {1} less to cast." to `spells: { anyOf: [{ types: ['instant'] }, { types: ['sorcery'] }], controller: 'you' }`; "Creature spells you cast cost {2} less to cast." to `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }`; "Noncreature spells you cast ..." to `{ notTypes: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }`; "Spells you cast cost {1} less ..." to `{ controller: 'you' }`. "... cost {1} less to cast for each <noun>" on a permanent has a `countOf` amount (counted with the permanent as the source). "Cost {W} less" (colored), "cost {1} more" (an increase), "Affinity" and "Convoke" are not built. `FeatureRef`s: `static:costReduction`, `amount:countOf`.
- Notes: (1) The reduction applies to the cost as a whole, so it also lowers commander tax and a kicker's generic mana. (2) A hybrid, Phyrexian or snow symbol is never reduced (CR 118.7e to 118.7g are not built). (3) A reduction from the spell itself needs no permanent: it is read from the card while it is being cast.

## "If you control a commander, you may cast this spell without paying its mana cost" (engine slice; CR 118.9, 903.3d, 903.8)

- CR: 118.9 (an alternative cost: "You may cast [this object] without paying its mana cost"), 118.9a (one alternative cost at a time, announced as the spell is cast, CR 601.2b), 118.9c (the mana cost is unchanged), 118.9d (additional costs, increases and reductions apply to an alternative cost), 903.3d (controlling a commander is a permanent on the battlefield that is a commander), 903.3 (a token or a copy is not a commander), 903.8 (commander tax).
- Gap class: P and a condition (an alternative cost with a condition)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`AlternativeCost`, `Condition` `controlCommander`), `cards/definition.ts` (`CardFace.alternativeCosts`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/legality.ts` (`freeCostAllowed`, `freeCastObjects`, `canCast`), `rules/priority.ts` (the option), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`spellCost`: no mana cost, tax and additional costs kept), `rules/cast.ts` (`beginCast`, `finishCast`), `state/types.ts` (`StackData.free`), `state/decisions.ts` and `state/actions.ts` (`alternative: 'free'`), `invariants.ts`, `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`; AI: `casual-priority.ts`
- AST node: `CardFace.alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'free', condition?: Condition }]` (one) and `Condition` `{ kind: 'controlCommander' }`, usable in every place a condition is (a `conditional` effect, an entering replacement, an intervening "if" except on a look-back trigger).
- Decision and action: a card whose condition holds is offered as a second `castSpell` option with `alternative: 'free'` beside the ordinary one (which needs the mana); the action carries the same `alternative`. The cast is the usual transaction: targets, then the total cost (nothing, plus commander tax, kicker and additional mana, minus reductions), then the sacrifice and discard of the additional costs. The spell on the stack, its `spellCast` event and its view say it was free.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-free-cast.test.ts` (a commander on the battlefield, none, in the command zone, in the graveyard or hand, an opponent's, a stolen one, read for whoever casts it; a card without the alternative; no condition; casting it: no mana, the event and the view, beside the ordinary option, with no lands at all, cancel, a free counterspell in response, a free creature; a free cast pays the sacrifice, is not offered with nothing to sacrifice, pays commander tax and reductions on the tax; every offered free cast is accepted and every other refused), `scenario-xx-cost-legal-actions.test.ts`, `ast-costs-validate.test.ts`; AI: `cost-games.test.ts` (a free cast only while a commander is controlled, with the casual agent casting free), `casual-costs.test.ts`. Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "If you control a commander, you may cast this spell without paying its mana cost." (5 cards, popular: free counterspells and removal). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Free Counter`, `Free Exile`, `Free Offering`, `Always Free`, `Free Colossus`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its text (stage P11).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 13, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the sentence "If you control a commander, you may cast this spell without paying its mana cost." (also with `~`) is removed from the spell text and lowers to `CardFace.alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'free', condition: { kind: 'controlCommander' } }]`; "You may cast this spell without paying its mana cost." has no condition. The rest of the text lowers as the spell. "You may cast ~ ... rather than pay its mana cost" with another cost, "Without paying its mana cost" for another card (cascade, a spell cast from an effect) and "cast from your hand" restrictions are not built. The `FeatureRef` is `condition:controlCommander`; the alternative cost itself has no `FeatureRef` (SUPPORTED_FEATURES `castAlternatives` lists `free`).
- Notes: (1) The condition is read when the options are listed and again when the spell is cast. (2) The commander on the battlefield may be the only creature a sacrifice cost can use; the engine then chooses it. (3) "A commander you control" in the command zone does not count.

## More mana production (engine slice; D-099, D-102; CR 106.7, 107.4e, 602.5, 605.3, 119.4, 613.1f)

Six primitives for the mana lines the corpus replay left unread. The engine side only. All tests use SYNTHETIC cards (`packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-mana-plus.ts`); run them with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-could-produce packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-mana-combination packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-hybrid-mana-costs packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-activate-only-if packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-mana-pay-life packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-grant-mana-ability packages/ai/test/mana-plus-games`. Code shared by all six: `rules/mana-abilities.ts` (the abilities a permanent has, what using one takes, `activationChoices`), `rules/mana-outcomes.ts` (what an ability can make), `rules/mana-sources.ts` (payment sources, `planFor`, `payableSources`), `rules/mana-engine.ts` (activating by hand, applying a plan), `mana/payment.ts` and `mana/costed.ts` (the planner). The `FeatureRef`s are `manaProduction:couldProduce`, `manaProduction:combination`, `manaCost:payLife`, `ability:activateOnlyIf` and `static:grantAbility`; the hybrid symbols need no new one (`manaCost:mana`).

### "{T}: Add one mana of any color that a land an opponent controls could produce." (`couldProduce`)

- CR: 106.7 (the type of mana a permanent could produce; costs ignored; no type means no mana; the Exotic Orchard example), 106.1a and 106.1b ("any color" is the five colors, "any type" includes colorless), 605.3b (the type is chosen as the ability resolves), 613.1f (what a land has now is what it could produce), 602.5b (an activation restriction is not looked at)
- Gap class: P (a production that reads the board), with a fixed point (D-102)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ManaProduction` `couldProduce`), `rules/mana-outcomes.ts` (`couldProduceTypes`, `couldProduceOutcomes`, `productionOutcomes`), `ast/validate-mana-ability.ts`, `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'couldProduce', lands: ObjectFilter, anyType?: boolean }` as the `production` of a `mana` ability. `lands` describes permanents on the battlefield and `controller` is relative to the ability's controller; `validateCard` refuses another zone. With no `anyType` colorless is left out.
- Decision and action: the ability is offered once per type it could make, with that color, in the order W, U, B, R, G, C (a single type is made without a question); none, and the ability is not offered, not a payment source and not in `PayManaDecision.manaSources`. `PayManaDecision.manaSources[].colors` lists the types; a manual tap must name one; a refusal names them. It is read when the ability is activated, so it changes as lands enter, leave, lose their abilities or are given one.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-could-produce.test.ts` (an opponent with only Islands and Swamps gives blue or black and nothing else, a refused color names the list, one type without a question, no opponent land: no mana and not offered, my own lands do not count, colorless is not a color, several-colored lands, a filter land's colors, the order of the types; tapped lands, a land with a life cost and one with an unmet "Activate only if" count; a land that enters adds its color, one that loses all abilities or leaves takes it away, a Lantern's granted ability counts; two Orchards alone make nothing, an Orchard facing a Forest and the other Orchard makes {G} for both, a chain through an Orchard, a Rock is not a land; "any type" includes colorless and sees my own lands; the planner pays for red with the Orchard when the opponent has a Mountain, the decision lists the colors, a manual tap names one; every offered activation is accepted and every other refused; determinism; validation), `ast-features.test.ts`; AI: `mana-plus-games.test.ts` (an independent fixed point over the board checks the exact colors offered at every priority decision of 100 games). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: Exotic Orchard, Fellwar Stone and two more (D-099: the replay's weight 0.17); Reflecting Pool for the "any type" form. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Orchard`, `Fellwar Rock`, `Reflecting Pond`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P2).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 14, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "{T}: Add one mana of any color that a land an opponent controls could produce." lowers to `{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'couldProduce', lands: { types: ['land'], controller: 'opponent' } } }`; "{T}: Add one mana of any type that a land you control could produce." to the same with `controller: 'you'` and `anyType: true`. Fellwar Stone's artifact has the same ability and is not a land, so an Orchard does not look at it. A rider ("When that mana is spent to cast a creature spell ..., ...") and a spend restriction are not built; the whole line stays unread.
- Notes: (1) The CR example is built exactly: two Orchards alone make nothing, but an Orchard that faces a Forest and the other Orchard makes {G}. D-099 said the opposite and was corrected in D-102. (2) A land that has the ability "{T}: Add one mana of any color" from a Lantern counts as one that could make every color. (3) The set is computed once per state and cached, so it costs nothing on a board with no such ability.

### "{R/W}, {T}: Add {R}{R}, {R}{W}, or {W}{W}." (`combination`, and a hybrid mana cost)

- CR: 605.3b (the combination is chosen as the ability resolves: no stack), 605.3a (the cost is paid with mana the player has), 107.4e (a hybrid symbol is paid with either half; `{2/W}` with one {W} or two mana of any type), 601.2g (mana abilities and the planner), 106.4 (the pool does not order mana)
- Gap class: P (a production with a choice among combinations) and P (hybrid symbols in the planner's activation costs)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ManaProduction` `combination`), `rules/mana-engine.ts` (`activateManaAbility`, `pickOutcome`), `rules/mana-abilities.ts` (`activationChoices`), `rules/mana-sources.ts` (`payableSources`), `api.ts` (`isOffered`), `rules/pay-manual.ts`, `mana/costed.ts` (`activationDemands`), `mana/payment.ts` (`planPayment`, `assignUnits`), `ast/validate-mana.ts` (`checkCombinations`), `ast/supported.ts` (`PLAIN_COST_SYMBOL`), `state/actions.ts`, `state/decisions.ts`, `testing/` (`tapForMana(card, { combination })`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'combination', options: ManaColor[][] }` (at least two combinations that differ as multisets, each at least one valid mana) and, in the cost of the same ability, `{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['R/W'] }` (also `C/W` and `2/W`; X, snow and Phyrexian stay refused for a mana ability).
- Decision and action: `Action` `activateAbility` and the priority option gain `combination?: ManaColor[]` (with `color: null`), a `payManual` tap gains `combination?`, `PayManaDecision.manaSources[]` gains `combinations?` (in place of `colors`), all in the order W, U, B, R, G, C ({R}{W} is `['W', 'R']`; a combination is accepted in any order). By hand the hybrid symbol comes out of the pool, so the filter is on offer only while the pool holds a half; the planner pays it with a source of either half (it may be another land that taps for nothing else) and picks the combination the cost needs, the first that does.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mana-combination.test.ts` (the three combinations on offer with {R} in the pool; the cost taken from the pool, the combination added, no stack; the other half pays; green does not, and the filter is not on offer without a half; any order; a combination outside the list refused with the list; no choice or a color refused; a combination for an ability that has none refused; the planner picks {R}{R} for {1}{R}{R} and {B}{B} for {1}{B}{B}, and sends a spare mana to the pool; a cost it cannot help with is not offered; the filter is not used when a land pays alone; `manaSources` lists the combinations; manual payment with and without a wrong combination; `payAuto` gives the same state as the matching manual payment; every combination offered is accepted and every other pair refused; validation), `scenario-xx-hybrid-mana-costs.test.ts` (either half; white only; neither half; a plain source before the filter; `{C/W}`; `{2/G}` from one green or two of any type; two filters; determinism; a chain is not planned but works by hand; a hybrid cost the pool cannot pay refuses the activation; validation of hybrid, X, snow and Phyrexian; six filter lands plan in well under a second; a seeded property check against an independent brute force of the one-level model), `mana-payment*.test.ts`; AI: `mana-plus-games.test.ts`. Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: the filter lands of Shadowmoor and Eventide ("{T}: Add {C}. {R/W}, {T}: Add {R}{R}, {R}{W}, or {W}{W}.", 10 cards). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Filter Land`, `Bare Filter Land`, `Twist Filter Land`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (hybrid costs on a mana ability and the "or" list of pairs).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 14, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "{R/W}, {T}: Add {R}{R}, {R}{W}, or {W}{W}." lowers to `{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['R/W'] }, { kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'combination', options: [['R', 'R'], ['R', 'W'], ['W', 'W']] } }`; the line before it on the same land ("{T}: Add {C}.") is an ordinary `fixed` mana ability, so the card has two. "{2/G}, {T}: Add {G}{W}." has a `fixed` production and the symbol `2/G`.
- Notes: (1) The planner still pays a costed ability one level deep: a filter land paid by another filter land's mana is not planned (by hand it works). (2) Six costed sources at most are considered at once (`MAX_COSTED`).

### "{T}: Add {C}{C}. Activate only if you control five or more lands." (`activateOnlyIf`)

- CR: 602.5 (a player can't begin to activate an ability that's prohibited), 602.5b (a restriction on the use of an activated ability), 602.5c (a restriction of a granted ability applies only to that ability), 605.3 (a mana ability follows the rules for activating one), 106.7 (a restriction is not looked at by another ability that asks what this one could produce)
- Gap class: P (a condition on activating)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Ability` `activated` and `mana`, `ManaAbility.activateOnlyIf`), `rules/mana-abilities.ts` (`activationAllowed`, `physicalRefusal`), `rules/activate-legality.ts` (`activationRefusal`), `rules/mana-sources.ts`, `rules/mana-engine.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/validate-mana-ability.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`abilityFeatures`)
- AST node: `activateOnlyIf?: Condition` on an `activated` and on a `mana` ability, any existing condition (`controlsAtLeast`, `controlCommander`, `and`, `or`, `not`, ...), read for the controller of the permanent with the permanent as the source, when the ability is activated.
- Decision and action: none new. The priority options, the payment planner, `PayManaDecision.manaSources`, a manual tap and `activationRefusal` all skip an ability whose condition is false; a refusal says "can be activated only if its condition holds". Playing the fifth land turns it on, losing one turns it off.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-activate-only-if.test.ts` (four lands against five, the opponent's lands do not count, a land played or sacrificed changes it; the planner and `manaSources` skip it and a manual tap is refused; "a Swamp": the colorless ability stays, the Swamp must be mine, the Tainted Land is not a Swamp; the planner uses the green only with a Swamp; a non-mana activated ability is listed and refused the same way; an Orchard sees a land whose condition is false; every offered activation is accepted and the gated one refused when absent; validation and the declared feature), `scenario-xx-grant-mana-ability.test.ts` (a granted ability keeps its own restriction), `ast-supported.test.ts`; AI: `mana-plus-games.test.ts` (the Temple, the Tainted Land and a gated Fountain are on offer exactly when the rule says, at every decision). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: Temple of the False God ("{T}: Add {C}{C}. Activate only if you control five or more lands."), Tainted Wood and the other "Tainted" lands ("{T}: Add {B} or {G}. Activate only if you control a Swamp."). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Temple`, `Tainted Land`, `Gated Fountain`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read the restriction sentence.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 14, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the sentence "Activate only if you control five or more lands." is removed from the ability's text and sets `activateOnlyIf: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['land'] }, count: 5 }` on the ability before it ("{T}: Add {C}{C}." is a `fixed` production of two {C}); "Activate only if you control a Swamp." to `{ kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['land'], subtypes: ['Swamp'] }, count: 1 }` (the second ability of the card; the first, "{T}: Add {C}.", has none). "Activate only if you control a commander" is `{ kind: 'controlCommander' }`. The `FeatureRef`s are `ability:activateOnlyIf` and the condition's own (`condition:controlsAtLeast`). "Activate only once each turn", "only during your turn" and "only as a sorcery" are not built (the last has `timing`).
- Notes: (1) The land counts itself (it is a land you control). (2) A restriction is not a cost, so it is ignored when another ability asks what this one could produce (D-102).

### "{T}, Pay 1 life: Add one mana of any color." (a `payLife` cost on a mana ability)

- CR: 119.4 (life can be paid only if the life total is at least the amount; paying is losing it), 605.3 and 605.3b (costs are paid as the ability is activated; the mana is added at once), 704.5a (a player at 0 life loses), 107.4f (Phyrexian mana is the other payment that costs life)
- Gap class: P (a cost kind made runnable on a mana ability)
- Code: `rules/mana-abilities.ts` (`lifeCostOf`, `payManaAbilityCosts`, `physicalRefusal`), `rules/mana-sources.ts` (`paymentSources`, `planFor`), `mana/payment-shared.ts` (`PaymentSource.lifeLoss`), `mana/payment.ts` (`planPayment`), `ast/features.ts` (`manaAbilityCostKinds`), `ast/validate-mana-ability.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'payLife', amount }` (at least 1) among the `cost` of a `mana` ability, beside `tap`, `sacrifice` and `mana`.
- Decision and action: none new. The ability is offered only when the life total is at least the amount; tapping it by hand adds the mana, pays the life (a `lifeChanged` event after `tapped` and before `manaAdded`) and may take the player to 0, who then loses. The planner's budget is `lifeAvailable` (the life total minus 1 for `planFor`): the life a source costs and the damage it deals to its controller, with the life paid for Phyrexian mana, stay inside it, so auto-pay never takes its own player to 0, uses a source that costs nothing before one that costs life, and leaves a costly source out when it would not fit.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mana-pay-life.test.ts` (tap, life, mana, no stack, event order; all five colors; a life total equal to the payment is enough and the player loses; below it the source is not offered and a refusal says why; two mana for 2 life; the planner uses a free source first, pays the life when it must, never goes to 0, counts two payments together, does not touch a Rock it does not need; manual payment may go to 0 and loses; a manual tap of a source that costs more than the life is refused; legal actions agree for 1, 2 and 3 life; the planner itself: a source's life loss is inside the budget, a free source is preferred, Phyrexian life and a source's life are added; validation), `scenario-xx-mana-also.test.ts` (pain lands, now inside the same budget); AI: `mana-plus-games.test.ts` (a life source is on offer exactly when the life total allows it; the life paid by `payAuto` is exactly what the tapped sources cost and never takes a player with life to 0). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: Mana Confluence ("{T}, Pay 1 life: Add one mana of any color.") and five more (6 cards in the replay, D-099; `manaCost:payLife` blocked them). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Confluence`, `Blood Rock`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; the parser already reads the cost (stage P2), so each card becomes runnable as soon as it reads the rest.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 14, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "{T}, Pay 1 life: Add one mana of any color." lowers to `{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }, { kind: 'payLife', amount: 1 }], production: { kind: 'anyColor' } }`; "{T}, Pay 2 life: Add {B}{B}." to the same cost with `amount: 2` and a `fixed` production. The `FeatureRef` is `manaCost:payLife`, now supported.
- Notes: (1) Paying 0 life is no cost, so `validateCard` refuses `amount` below 1. (2) The cost is paid whether the ability is used by hand or in a plan; a Lantern that gives the same land a free mana ability lets the planner use that instead.

### "Lands you control have "{T}: Add one mana of any color."" (`grantAbility`)

- CR: 613.1f (layer 6: ability-adding and -removing effects), 613.7 (timestamp order within a layer; an ability granted after "loses all abilities" still applies), 611.3a (a static ability applies to whatever its text describes at any moment), 602.5c (a granted ability's restriction applies only to it), 605.1a (a mana ability), 106.7 (what a land could produce includes what it was given)
- Gap class: P (a static ability kind in a layer the engine has)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `grantAbility`, `ManaAbility`), `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`staticEffectsOn`, `Modifier`), `rules/derive-layers.ts` (`StaticGrant`, `applyAbilityLayer`, `hasAbility`), `ast/validate.ts` (`checkStatic`), `ast/validate-attach.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantAbility', subject: StaticSubject, ability: ManaAbility } }` with a `this`, `attached` or `filter` subject. Only a mana ability can be granted; `validateCard` checks it like one of the card's own.
- Decision and action: none new. A land has the granted ability after its printed ones (so existing ability indexes do not move), once per distinct ability, while the permanent that grants it is on the battlefield; "loses all abilities" removes it if that effect came later (the permanent's timestamp against the effect's) and not if the permanent came later; a land that enters or a Lantern that enters later changes it at once.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-grant-mana-ability.test.ts` (a Forest gets a second ability for any color; only my lands, not an artifact or the opponent's; two Lanterns give it once; a land or a Lantern entering later; the Lantern leaving; "loses all abilities" removes the printed and the granted one; a Lantern cast after the effect gives it back (CR 613.7); a granted ability keeps its own "Activate only if"; the planner pays for red with a Plains through it; the decision lists it and a manual tap names it; an Orchard sees it; determinism and legal actions; validation: only a mana ability, checked like any), `scenario-17-layers.test.ts`; AI: `mana-plus-games.test.ts` (a Prism Lantern gives every untapped land any color at every decision; a "loses all abilities" effect and a Lantern in either order). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: Chromatic Lantern ("Lands you control have "{T}: Add one mana of any color."") and similar. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Prism Lantern`, `Murk Lantern`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read the quoted ability.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 14, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: `Lands you control have "{T}: Add one mana of any color."` lowers to `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantAbility', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['land'], controller: 'you' } }, ability: { kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'anyColor' } } } }`; the permanent's own "{T}: Add one mana of any color." (Chromatic Lantern has one too) is a separate ability. `FeatureRef`s: `static:grantAbility`, `staticSubject:filter`. A grant of an ability that is not a mana ability ("Creatures you control have "{T}: ..."", "have "Sacrifice this: ..."") is not built.
- Notes: (1) The granted ability is the one of the land, activated by its controller with the land as the source. (2) The subject `this` makes a permanent have a second mana ability of its own text. (3) Mana spend restrictions ("Spend this mana only to cast ...") stay out.

## Choices made as a permanent enters (engine slice; D-100, D-106; CR 614.12, 614.12a, 607.2d, 205.3m, 105.1, 400.7)

Three entries for the lines the corpus replay left unread because the engine had no choice as a permanent enters other than the shock land's payment. The engine side only. All tests use SYNTHETIC cards (`packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-chosen.ts`, and cards defined in the test file); run them with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-enters-choose packages/engine/test/ast-chosen-validate tools/creature-types packages/ai/test/casual-options packages/ai/test/chosen-games`. Code shared by all three: `rules/chosen.ts` (the options, `sameCreatureType`, `chosenFromOption`, `chosenOf`), `rules/enters.ts` (`entersChoice`), `data/creature-types.ts` (generated), `ast/validate-chosen.ts`, `state/types.ts` (`GameObject.chosen`, `ChosenValues`).

### "As ~ enters, choose a creature type." / "As ~ enters, choose a color." (`asEntersChoose`, the `chooseOption` decision)

- CR: 614.12 (a replacement effect that modifies how a permanent enters; its example is a token that is a copy of Voice of All, which chooses a color of its own), 614.12a (a choice a replacement effect needs is made before the permanent enters), 614.1c ("As [this] enters" is a replacement effect), 205.3m (the creature types, "Time Lord" two words), 105.1 (the five colors), 707.2 (what a copy copies: not a choice), 400.7 (a new object remembers nothing), 305.1 (a land is played, not cast)
- Gap class: P (a replacement effect that asks, and a new decision kind)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Replacement` `asEntersChoose`), `state/decisions.ts` (`ChooseOptionDecision`), `state/actions.ts` (`chooseOption`), `state/events.ts` (`optionChosen`), `rules/zones.ts` (`moveObject` asks and stores), `api.ts` (`playLand`, `answerChooseOption`), `rules/stack.ts` (`finish`, `answerResolutionEntersChosen`), `rules/effects.ts` (the `move` instruction, `createToken`), `rules/enter-token.ts` (`enterToken` instruction), `rules/tokens.ts`, `legal-actions.ts`, `view.ts` (`ObjectView.chosen`), `invariants.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/validate-chosen.ts`, `ast/features.ts`; `tools/creature-types/generate.ts`; AI: `random-agent.ts`, `casual-options.ts`; testing DSL: `Actor.chooseOption`, `PermanentOptions.chosen`, `expect.chosen`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'asEntersChoose', what: 'creatureType' | 'color' } }`, at most one question per face (not with `entersTappedUnlessPays`), on a permanent face only (and on a custom token's definition).
- Decision and action: before the permanent enters, its controller is asked `chooseOption { purpose, options, source, sourceName, sourceOracleText, face? }`; the answer is `chooseOption { option }` (a creature type spelled as the decision lists it, or `W`, `U`, `B`, `R`, `G`); one legal action per option plus concede, no cancel. It is asked whatever puts the permanent there: a permanent spell resolving (the spell stays on the stack while it waits), a land played from the hand (it stays in the hand, no land drop is used), a card an effect puts onto the battlefield (a graveyard return, a flicker, a search), and a token, including a token that is a copy (it asks for its own choice; nothing exists yet, so `source` is the spell or ability making it). The answer is stored on the new object (`GameObject.chosen`), shown to both players in the view, and gone when it leaves (a flickered creature asks again).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-enters-choose.test.ts` (the spell on the stack while it asks and the decision's contents; every CR 205.3m type once as options; the answer stored, the events in order; the five colors; a countered spell never asks; wrong answers refused (case, other purpose, other seat, pass, cancel), nothing done by them; legal actions are every option plus concede and each is accepted; concede; the opponent's view shows the choice and only the chooser got the question; a land asks in the hand and is played by the answer, on either seat's turn; a card returned from a graveyard asks, and the spell finishes after; two returns keep their own answers; a flicker asks again and the old choice is gone; a token copy of a creature that chose a type or a color asks for its own, the original keeps its own; a copy of a creature that asks nothing is made at once; determinism and replay), `ast-chosen-validate.test.ts`, `tools/creature-types/creature-types.test.ts` (the table is what CR 205.3m lists, byte for byte; the parser refuses wording it does not know); AI: `casual-options.test.ts`, `chosen-games.test.ts` (100 games: every question has the right options and asker, the answer lands on the permanent that enters, a flicker and a raise ask again). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "As ~ enters, choose a creature type." (56 cards, weight 0.066) and "As ~ enters, choose a color." (43), counts from D-100 (the corpus replay); Voice of All is the card CR 614.12 itself uses as its example. No real card was looked up for this slice (no Scryfall access), so no other name is given. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Rally Banner`, `Hue Banner`, `Banner Captain`, `Shifting Captain`, `Hue Captain`, `Tribal Land`, `Tribal Discount`, `Tribal Scout`, `Raise Dead`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities (a card of this family has the question and something that reads the answer, both in the table below).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 15, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the sentence "As ~ enters, choose a creature type." (also "As this creature enters", "As this land enters", "As this artifact enters", "As this enchantment enters", and "As ~ enters, choose a creature type" before a period or a comma that starts another sentence) is one ability: `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'asEntersChoose', what: 'creatureType' } }`; "As ~ enters, choose a color." to the same with `what: 'color'`. The other sentences of the card are separate abilities of the card and read the choice as the next two entries say. "Choose a color" with more words ("choose a color other than ...", "choose two colors"), "choose a basic land type", "choose a card name", "choose a number", "choose an opponent", "choose a creature type" inside an activated or triggered ability's effect ("Choose a creature type. ..." as a spell's first instruction), and "As ~ enters, you may choose ..." are not built and the whole card stays unread. `FeatureRef`: `replacement:asEntersChoose`.
- Notes: (1) A face asks one question when it enters: two choices on one card, or a choice and a shock land's payment, are refused by `validateCard`. (2) The choice is made as the permanent enters and is the permanent's: a copy effect does not copy it, a token copy makes its own. (3) The answer is spelled as the CR spells the type (Time Lord, C’tan, Shi’ar); a card that writes the plain apostrophe matches it. (4) A card put onto the battlefield from a library by a search asks too (the move goes through the same pipeline).

### "Creatures you control of the chosen type get +1/+1." (`chosenType`, `chosenColor` filters)

- CR: 607.2d (the ability that refers to "the chosen [value]" is linked to the one that chose it), 611.3a (a static ability applies to whatever its text describes at any moment, so a creature that enters later is included), 613.4c (layer 7c), 205.3m (creature types), 113.7a and 608.2h (last-known information), 400.7
- Gap class: P (a filter feature relative to the source's choice)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.chosenType`, `chosenColor`), `rules/filter-match.ts` (`FilterContext.sourceChosen`, `matchesFilterWith`), `rules/select.ts` (`matchesFilter` reads `chosenOf(state, ctx.source)`), `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`affects` passes the lord's choice), `rules/chosen.ts` (`chosenOf`, `sameCreatureType`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkFilter`), `ast/validate-chosen.ts` (`chosenIssues`: the card must ask for the choice it reads), `ast/features.ts` (`selectorKinds`)
- AST node: `ObjectFilter.chosenType?: true` and `chosenColor?: true`, anywhere a filter is: the `filter` of a static subject (`{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', chosenType: true } }, power: 1, toughness: 1 } }`), a trigger's `what`, a target slot, a cost reduction's `spells`, a condition's or a count's filter, an effect's `each` selector.
- Decision and action: none new. The filter matches an object that has the type (after layer 4) or the color (after layer 5) its source chose when the source entered; "other" leaves out the source itself; a source with no such choice, or one that has left the battlefield and whose last-known information has none, matches nothing. A source that has left reads what it chose (a "dies" trigger). Two permanents that chose differently each read their own.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-enters-choose.test.ts` (only my creatures of the type are pumped, not the opponent's or other types; one that enters later is; another type chosen pumps those; a lord of "other creatures" does not pump itself; the lord leaving ends the pump and the choice; "loses all abilities" on the lord ends the pump; a given choice from the setup; two permanents with different choices; no choice pumps nothing; a type spelled with the plain apostrophe; a color pumps its creatures; creature spells of the chosen type cost {1} less, generic mana only, via the view's `castCost`; "whenever another creature of the chosen type enters under your control" triggers for those only; a "dies" trigger reads the dead creature's choice; an activated ability's target slot lists only creatures of the chosen type), `ast-chosen-validate.test.ts` (a reference without the choice, with the other choice, in a trigger, a spell text, a cost reduction, an `anyOf` member and a token definition); AI: `chosen-games.test.ts` (power and toughness, the Shifting Captain's types, the cost of creature cards in hand and the Scout's triggers recomputed from the cards' texts at every priority decision). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "Creatures you control of the chosen type get +1/+1." (9 cards, D-100), "Other creatures you control of the chosen type get +1/+1." and the many cost reducers and triggers of the same shape; no real card was looked up for this slice. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Rally Banner`, `Hue Banner`, `Banner Captain`, `Hue Captain`, `Tribal Discount`, `Tribal Scout`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P13).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 15, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Creatures you control of the chosen type get +1/+1." lowers to `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', chosenType: true } }, power: 1, toughness: 1 } }`; "Other creatures you control of the chosen type get +1/+1." to the same with `other: true` on the filter; "Creatures you control of the chosen color get +1/+1." with `chosenColor: true`. "Creature spells of the chosen type you cast cost {1} less to cast." to `{ kind: 'costReduction', spells: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', chosenType: true }, amount: 1 }`. "Whenever another creature of the chosen type enters under your control, ..." to an `entersBattlefield` trigger whose `what` is `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', other: true, chosenType: true }`. "of the chosen type" is the creature type only: "a creature spell of the chosen type", "creatures of the chosen type" without "you control" (any controller: leave `controller` out), and "nontoken", "other" combine as for any filter. The card must also have the `asEntersChoose` ability, or the card fails `validateCard`. `FeatureRef`s `selector:chosenType`, `selector:chosenColor`. "Of the chosen type" read from a spell or an ability that does not belong to the permanent that chose, "the creature type you chose", "the chosen player" and "protection from the chosen color" are not built.
- Notes: (1) Changeling (every creature type, CR 702.73) is not built; when it is, the derived subtypes must carry every creature type for a Changeling to match (`docs/ROADMAP.md`). (2) The filter reads the object's current types, so a creature that became a Frog is not of the chosen type any more.

### "~ is the chosen type in addition to its other types." (static `setTypes`)

- CR: 205.1b (an effect that adds a type in addition to its other types leaves the rest), 613.1d (layer 4), 613.7a (a static ability's timestamp is its permanent's), 613.6 (an effect that has begun to apply goes on applying when its ability is removed afterwards), 613.1f (layer 6 comes after layer 4), 607.2d
- Gap class: P (a static ability in layer 4)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `setTypes`), `rules/derive.ts` (`withStaticTypes`, read in `finish` before layer 4), `rules/derive-layers.ts` (`applyTypeLayer`), `ast/validate.ts` (`checkStatic`), `ast/features.ts` (`staticKinds`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'setTypes', subject: { kind: 'this' }, chosenType: true, additive: true } }`; the subject is `this` only and the type is the chosen creature type only.
- Decision and action: none new. The permanent has the chosen creature type besides its own while it is on the battlefield and has made the choice, in layer 4 in timestamp order with the continuous effects of resolved spells: a later "becomes a Frog" replaces its creature types, the added one included; an earlier one does not touch it. It is itself of the chosen type for the lords that read it (a lord of that type pumps it), and "loses all abilities" later ends what its other static abilities do (a pump of others) but not the type, which layer 4 had added before layer 6.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-enters-choose.test.ts` (the creature has the chosen type and its own; none before it has chosen; it is pumped by a Banner of that type; it pumps the others and not itself; "loses all abilities" ends the pump and keeps the type; a later "becomes a Frog" replaces the types), `ast-chosen-validate.test.ts` (subject `this` only, additive only, the creature type choice on the same card); AI: `chosen-games.test.ts` (the Shifting Captain's types at every priority decision). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "~ is the chosen type in addition to its other types." (5 cards, D-100); no real card was looked up for this slice. SYNTHETIC fixture: `SYNTHETIC Shifting Captain`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 15, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "~ is the chosen type in addition to its other types." (also "This creature is the chosen type in addition to its other types.") lowers to `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'setTypes', subject: { kind: 'this' }, chosenType: true, additive: true } }` and the card also has `asEntersChoose` of `creatureType`. "Each creature you control is the chosen type in addition to its other types." (a subject that is not the permanent itself) and "Target creature becomes the chosen type" are not built. `FeatureRef`: `static:setTypes`.
- Notes: (1) Only the static form is built; the effect-level `setTypes` has no "chosen type". (2) The type is read from the permanent's printed abilities, so a copy effect that gave it other abilities (layer 1) changes it, as it should.

## X in costs (engine slice; D-103, D-107; CR 107.3, 107.3a, 107.3b, 107.3g, 107.3m, 601.2b, 601.2f, 602.2b, 119.4, 202.3e)

Entries for the lines the corpus replay left unread because the engine refused every spell with an {X} (104 cards of the top 1,000 templates, D-103). The engine side only. All tests use SYNTHETIC cards (`packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-x.ts`, `SYNTHETIC X Draw` in `synthetic.ts`, and cards defined in the test files); run them with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-x-costs packages/engine/test/ast-x-validate packages/ai/test/casual-x packages/ai/test/x-games`. Code shared by all entries: `rules/x-value.ts` (`xSymbolCount`, `withX`, `spellXCost`, `abilityXCost`, `largestPayableX`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`spellCost` takes `x`, `largestSpellX`), `rules/cast.ts` (`toX`, `answerX`), `rules/activate.ts` (`beginActivate`, `toActivationTargets`, `setX`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`breakDown` and `costRefusal` take `x`), `rules/effects.ts` and `rules/effect-support.ts` (`SelectContext.x`, `amountOf`), `rules/zones.ts` and `rules/enters.ts` (`entersWithCounters`), `state/decisions.ts` (`ChooseXDecision`), `state/actions.ts` (`chooseX`), `state/types.ts` (`CastingContext.stage` `'x'`, `ActivationContext.x`, `StackData.x`), `state/events.ts` (`spellCast.x`, `abilityActivated.x`), `api.ts`, `legal-actions.ts`, `view.ts` (`ObjectView.x`, the {X} in `castCost`), `invariants.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`, `ast/features.ts`; AI: `casual-x.ts`, `random-agent.ts`; testing DSL: `Actor.chooseX`, `CastHandle.x`, `CastOptions.x`, `ActivateOptions.x`.

How X is announced, for every entry below. When a spell with an {X} in its mana cost (or "pay X life" among its additional costs) is cast, the kicker question comes first (when the card has kicker), then a `chooseX { purpose: 'spell', min: 0, max, perX: { mana, life }, source, sourceName, sourceOracleText }` decision for the caster, then the targets, then the payment. `max` is the largest X the caster could pay for the whole cost (every mana source, the pool, commander tax, the kicker if it was paid, additional costs and the reductions); every value from 0 to `max` is legal and the legal actions are one `chooseX { value }` for each, `cancel` (rewinds the cast) and `concede`. With `max` 0 nothing is asked and X is 0. The total cost has X generic mana for each {X} (`{X}{X}{U}` with X = 2 is {4}{U}) and reductions apply to it. A spell cast without paying its mana cost has X = 0 and no question (CR 107.3b). An activated ability with an {X} in its cost asks the same decision (`purpose: 'ability'`) right after it goes on the stack, before its targets. The value is `StackData.x` of the spell or ability on the stack (`ObjectView.x`, the `spellCast` and `abilityActivated` events), and `Amount` `{ kind: 'x' }` reads it as the effect runs. The parser sets `spell.x: true` on a spell whose effect reads X; `validateCard` refuses an `x` amount anywhere else (a triggered or static ability, a spell or ability with no X in its cost).

### "~ deals X damage to any target." (an {X} in the mana cost and an `x` amount in the effect)

- CR: 107.3a (X is announced as the spell is cast; every X in its costs equals it), 601.2b (the announcement comes before the targets), 601.2f (the total cost has X in it, reductions apply), 107.3b (a free cast has X = 0), 608.2 (the effect reads X on resolution), 202.3e (the mana value of a spell with X on the stack counts it)
- Gap class: S (a new step of the casting transaction and of the activation; designed in D-103 and D-107, so no separate proposal)
- Code: see the list above
- AST node: `CardFace.manaCost` with `'X'` symbols (`['X', 'R']`), `spell: { x: true, targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'anyTarget' } }], effect: { kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'x' }, to: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } } }`.
- Decision and action: `chooseX` and `chooseX { value }` as described above. After the answer the targets decision, the `payMana` decision with the total cost locked in (`['3', 'R']` for X = 3), then the spell is cast. A kicker adds its mana to the total after X is chosen, and the offer of X accounts for it.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-x-costs.test.ts` (X asked first, then targets, then payment; X = 0; the largest X; no question when only X = 0 can be paid; castable whenever X = 0 can be paid; the legal actions are 0 to max, cancel, concede; every legal action is accepted and nothing else is, the other seat and a pass are refused; cancel rewinds the cast and asks again; the offered maximum with lands, with the pool of any color, with a Signet, with commander tax (CR 903.8), with a cost reduction (and one that takes more than the generic mana), with a kicker asked before X; two {X} cost 2X; a free cast has no question and no payment; the mana value of a spell with X on the stack counts it (CR 202.3e); determinism), `ast-x-validate.test.ts`; AI: `casual-x.test.ts`, `x-games.test.ts` (100 games: every question's contents and offered values recomputed from the cards' texts, the maximum accepted and one more refused, the total cost asked at the payment, the damage dealt). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "~ deals X damage to any target." and its many variants; no real card was looked up for this slice (no Scryfall access). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC X Burn`, `Kicked X Burn`, `Free X Burn`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 16, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: a spell with {X} among the symbols of its mana cost keeps them as `'X'` in `manaCost`; "~ deals X damage to any target." (also "This spell deals X damage to any target", "deals X damage to target creature", "deals X damage to target player or planeswalker" with their target kinds) lowers to a `dealDamage` effect with `amount: { kind: 'x' }`, and `spell.x` is `true`. "X" in any other amount position of an effect that takes an `Amount` lowers the same way (`gainLife`, `loseLife`, `scry`, `surveil`, `createToken` counts, `discard`). `FeatureRef`s: `amount:x`, `ability:xCost`. "X can't be 0." (a minimum for X), "where X is ..." (X defined by the text, CR 107.3c), "Y", and any X in a triggered or static ability's own text (CR 107.3j) are not built: the whole card stays unread.
- Notes: (1) Two {X} are 2X mana, not X; the parser keeps both symbols. (2) A spell cast from the command zone pays commander tax in addition (X is part of what the planner must afford). (3) A spell with an {X} in its cost cast by paying its flashback cost has X = 0 (the flashback cost has no {X}).

### "Draw X cards." and "Put X +1/+1 counters on target creature." (an `x` amount in `draw` and `addCounters`)

- CR: 107.3a, 608.2 (the amount is read as the effect runs), 122.1 (counters)
- Gap class: P (an existing amount shape, with X read from the spell on the stack)
- Code: `rules/effect-support.ts` (`amountOf`), `rules/effects.ts` (`runEffect` fills `SelectContext.x` from the resolving object)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: { kind: 'x' } }`; `{ kind: 'addCounters', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, counter: '+1/+1', count: { kind: 'x' } }` with `targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'] } } }]`; `spell.x: true` for both.
- Decision and action: as in the first entry. Two spells with X on the stack read their own values.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-x-costs.test.ts` (draw X; X counters on a creature; two casts of one card with different X; "two {X}" draws X for 2X mana), `casual-x.test.ts` (draws all but the last library card; puts the counters on its own creature); `x-games.test.ts` (the cards drawn and the counters added equal X at every resolution). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "Draw X cards." and "Put X +1/+1 counters on target creature." (counts from D-103); SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC X Draw`, `SYNTHETIC Double X Draw`, `SYNTHETIC X Pump`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 16, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Draw X cards." to the `draw` effect above (`count: { kind: 'x' }`), "You draw X cards" and "target player draws X cards" with the matching player selector; "Put X +1/+1 counters on target creature." to the `addCounters` effect above, "on each creature you control" with an `each` selector. `spell.x` is `true`. The wording "X +1/+1 counters" is the one counter kind `'+1/+1'`. `FeatureRef`s `effect:draw`, `effect:addCounters`, `amount:x`.
- Notes: (1) The AI reads this text to pick X, so a card whose text says something else (a different verb) gets "all of it".

### "~ enters with X +1/+1 counters on it." (`entersWithCounters` with an `x` count)

- CR: 107.3m (an enters-the-battlefield replacement effect that refers to X uses the X of the spell that became the permanent; the permanent's own X is 0), 107.3g (a card in any zone but the stack has X = 0), 614.1c (a replacement effect of the entering object), 122.6 (counters as it enters)
- Gap class: P (an existing replacement with a count that can be X)
- Code: `rules/zones.ts` (`moveObject` reads the X of a spell leaving the stack), `rules/enters.ts` (`entersWithCounters`), `ast/types.ts`, `ast/validate.ts` (the card needs an {X} in its mana cost)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'entersWithCounters', counter: '+1/+1', count: { kind: 'x' } } }`, on a card whose mana cost has an {X}; `spell.x` is not needed.
- Decision and action: as in the first entry (the announcement is the same). The permanent enters with that many counters; it has no X of its own afterwards (`stack` null, no `ObjectView.x`). A permanent put onto the battlefield without being cast (a flicker, a reanimation, a token) enters with none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-x-costs.test.ts` (X = 3 enters with 3 counters and is 4/4 with a 1/1 body; X = 0 has none; flickered after being cast with X 3 comes back with none; cancel at the X question), `ast-x-validate.test.ts` (needs an {X}; a plain number still has to be positive); AI: `x-games.test.ts` (at every entry of the Hydra Cub: counters equal the X of the spell, none when it did not come from the stack). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "~ enters with X +1/+1 counters on it." (55 cards, D-103); SYNTHETIC fixture: `SYNTHETIC Hydra Cub`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 16, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "~ enters with X +1/+1 counters on it." (also "This creature enters with X +1/+1 counters on it") lowers to the replacement above. "Enters with a +1/+1 counter on it" with a number is the older `count: 1`, unchanged. "When ~ enters, ... X ..." (an enters trigger that reads the spell's X, CR 107.3m) and "enters with X counters of another kind" with a counter name other than `'+1/+1'` follow the counter name as for any `entersWithCounters`; a trigger that reads X is not built and the card stays unread. `FeatureRef`s `replacement:entersWithCounters`, `amount:x`.
- Notes: (1) A copy of the permanent that enters as a copy has X = 0 (CR 107.3g); copies of spells with X (CR 707.10) are not built because spells cannot be copied yet.

### "As an additional cost to cast this spell, pay X life." (`payXLife`)

- CR: 118.8 (additional costs), 107.3a (X in an additional cost is announced), 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 119.4 (life is paid only if the life total is at least the amount), 118.3 (a cost that can't be paid fully isn't paid)
- Gap class: S (one more cost; designed in D-103 and D-107)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Cost` `payXLife`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`spellCost` adds `perX.life * x` to the life paid), `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`costKinds`)
- AST node: `CardFace.additionalCosts: [{ kind: 'payXLife' }]` (at most one), with `spell.x: true` when the effect reads X.
- Decision and action: X is announced as above; the offered values are 0 to the life the caster could pay while keeping at least 1 life (so a player at 1 life is not asked and pays nothing). The life is paid with the total cost after the targets (CR 601.2f-h) and given back if the cast is cancelled. A spell with {X} and "pay X life" pays X in both; cast without paying its mana cost it has X = 0 (CR 107.3b).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-x-costs.test.ts` (asked, life paid after the targets, damage X; cancel after the life was paid gives it back; at 1 life X is 0 and not asked; the life limit and the mana limit apply together; a spell with an {X} and "pay X life" asks X for both, and nothing when cast free), `ast-x-validate.test.ts` (the card needs an {X} or "pay X life" to say `x`; at most one "pay X life"; an {X} in an additional mana cost is refused); AI: `casual-x.test.ts` (no more than leaves it above 10 life), `x-games.test.ts` (the offered maximum is life minus 1). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "As an additional cost to cast this spell, pay X life." (wording from D-101's list of what it refused); no real card was looked up for this slice. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Blood Burn`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 16, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "As an additional cost to cast this spell, pay X life." (and the `~` form) is removed from the spell text and lowers to `additionalCosts: [{ kind: 'payXLife' }]`; the rest of the text lowers as the spell, with `spell.x: true` when it reads X. "pay X life" together with other additional costs lists them in the text's order. "Pay half your life, rounded up" and "pay X life" as a cost of a keyword are not built. `FeatureRef`s `cost:payXLife`, `ability:xCost`.
- Notes: (1) CR 119.4 would let the caster pay all of their life; a cast keeps 1 (D-101, D-107), an activated ability may pay the whole total.

### "{X}, {T}: ..." and "Pay X life: ..." (X in the cost of an activated ability)

- CR: 602.2b (an activation follows 601.2b-i: X is announced, then targets and costs), 107.3a, 107.3k (the X of an activated ability is independent of every other X of the object), 119.4, 118.3
- Gap class: S (the same step in the activation transaction)
- Code: `rules/activate.ts` (`beginActivate`, `toActivationTargets`, `setX`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`breakDown` pays X generic mana and X life, `costRefusal` takes `x`), `rules/x-value.ts` (`abilityXCost`), `ast/supported.ts` (`activationCostIssues` allows X in an ability's own cost only), `ast/validate.ts` (`hasXCost`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['X'] }, { kind: 'tap' }], targets: [...], effect: { kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'x' }, ... } }`; `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'payXLife' }], ... }`.
- Decision and action: after the ability goes on the stack, a `chooseX { purpose: 'ability' }` decision (the ability's stack object is `source`), then the targets, then the costs: {T}, life (for "pay X life") and counters first, then the mana. `max` for "pay X life" is the life total (CR 119.4: an ability may pay all of it). An ability that can pay no X is activated with X = 0 and not asked. Cancel at the question rewinds the activation. Another X of the same object (another ability) is independent (CR 107.3k).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-x-costs.test.ts` (X asked after the ability is on the stack and before the targets; the ability's X on its stack object and in `abilityActivated`; two activations of two abilities with their own X; two {X} in an ability cost are 2X; X = 0 with no mana; cancel rewinds; the legal actions agree with the engine; "pay X life" up to the life total, paid after the targets; cancel at the question costs nothing), `ast-x-validate.test.ts` (an ability with no X in its cost cannot read X; a triggered ability cannot; X in a keyword's cost or on a mana ability is refused); AI: `x-games.test.ts` (random agents activate the abilities; damage and cost recomputed). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "{X}, {T}: ..." and "Pay X life: ..." wordings; no real card was looked up for this slice. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC X Cannon`, `SYNTHETIC Blood Lance`, `SYNTHETIC Double Well`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 16, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "{X}, {T}: ~ deals X damage to target creature." lowers to an `activated` ability whose `cost` has `{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['X'] }` (and `{ kind: 'tap' }`), with an effect that reads `amount: { kind: 'x' }`; "{X}{X}, {T}: Draw X cards." keeps both symbols; "Pay X life: ..." to `cost: [{ kind: 'payXLife' }]`. The ability may be any activated ability the engine supports apart from a mana ability (a mana ability with X is refused). `FeatureRef`s `ability:xCost`, `amount:x`, `cost:payXLife`, `cost:mana`.
- Notes: (1) X in a keyword's cost (Cycling, Equip, Ward, Kicker, Flashback) is refused. (2) A permanent that was given the ability by another effect has no X for it (CR 107.3j): not built.

## Keyword batch 1 (D-104, D-110)

### "Changeling"

- CR: 702.73a (Changeling), 604.3, 613.3, 205.3d, 205.3m, 613.6, 707.2
- Gap class: K (a characteristic-defining ability read by `derive`)
- Code: `rules/derive.ts` (`finish`), `rules/derive-layers.ts` (`applyTypeLayer`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`hasPrintedKeyword`), `data/creature-types.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'changeling' } }` in `CardFace.abilities`.
- Decision and action: No decision. The object has every creature type of CR 205.3m in its derived `subtypes` in every zone (hand, library, graveyard, exile, stack, battlefield), first in layer 4 so later effects replace them; a chosen-type lord (D-100) pumps it whatever type was chosen. A card that is neither a creature nor kindred gets none. A copy and a token copy have it; "loses all abilities" does not take away what layer 4 gave.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-changeling-devoid.test.ts` (every type, every zone, on the stack and in the view, a lord of any chosen type, "becomes a Frog", a copy, loses all abilities, a noncreature), `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts`; AI: `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (every view of a changeling in the games has every type). Mutation-checked: the keyword read off fails 6 tests.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixture: `SYNTHETIC Chameleon Drifter`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Changeling" (a keyword line, alone or with others: "Flying, changeling"; the reminder text in parentheses is dropped) lowers to the keyword above. "Changeling" granted by another card is not supported (see the notes).
- Notes: (1) It cannot be granted: a keyword gained in layer 6 would not add types in layer 4 (`GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS` leaves it out). (2) `ObjectView.subtypes` of a changeling holds about 300 names; the web should show "every creature type".

### "Devoid"

- CR: 702.114a (Devoid), 604.3, 613.3, 202.2, 105.3, 613.1e
- Gap class: K (a characteristic-defining ability read by `derive`)
- Code: `rules/derive.ts` (`finish`), `rules/derive-layers.ts` (`applyColorLayer`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'devoid' } }`; the card's `colors` is `[]` (as Scryfall lists it) and its `manaCost` keeps the colored symbols.
- Decision and action: No decision. The object is colorless in every zone, first in layer 5, so a later "becomes blue" colors it; its mana cost and mana value are unchanged. The engine makes it colorless itself, whatever colors the card data lists.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-changeling-devoid.test.ts` (every zone, the cost on the stack, a color effect, a card written blue, a color filter); AI: `keyword-batch-games.test.ts`. Mutation-checked: the keyword read off fails the written-blue test.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixture: `SYNTHETIC Void Wisp`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Devoid" lowers to the keyword above and the face's `colors` to `[]`; the mana cost is kept as printed.
- Notes: (1) It cannot be granted. (2) Color identity (CR 903.4) still comes from the cost symbols; the card data's `colorIdentity` is separate.

### "Exalted"

- CR: 702.83a (Exalted), 702.83b, 506.5, 603.2c, 603.3b, 611.2a
- Gap class: K (a triggered ability expanded by `derive`) plus P (the `alone` flag of the attacks trigger, `modifyPt` on the triggering permanent)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`EXALTED_ABILITY`), `rules/triggers.ts` (`occurrences`), `rules/trigger-stack.ts` (`groupIdentical`), `ast/types.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` and `ast/validate-continuous.ts` (`triggerSubject` for `modifyPt`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'exalted' } }`, which expands to `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attacks', what: { controller: 'you', types: ['creature'] }, alone: true }, effect: { kind: 'modifyPt', objects: { kind: 'triggerSubject' }, power: 1, toughness: 1, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. When exactly one creature is declared as an attacker, each instance of exalted on a permanent its controller controls (any permanent, not only a creature) triggers and the attacker gets +1/+1 until end of turn. With two or more attackers, nothing triggers (no pending trigger, no ordering question). The trigger needs the attacker on the battlefield when it resolves. Exalted triggers of different permanents are one group when the controller orders triggers.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-exalted-annihilator.test.ts` (one attacker, two attackers, a creature and a non-creature permanent with exalted, the opponent's exalted, two instances, the attacker gone in response), `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts` (the expansion, `alone` and `triggerSubject` validate); AI: `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (a trigger per exalted permanent for an attack alone, none otherwise). Mutation-checked: the `alone` test removed fails the two-attackers test (it first passed because the ordering question left the stack empty; the test now checks the waiting triggers).
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Exalted Squire`, `SYNTHETIC Exalted Banner`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Exalted" (a keyword line) lowers to the keyword. The spelled-out ability "Whenever a creature you control attacks alone, that creature gets +1/+1 until end of turn." lowers to the triggered ability above: `trigger.attacks` with the subject "a creature you control" and the new `alone` feature (`trigger:alone`), and "that creature" as `{ kind: 'triggerSubject' }` in a `modifyPt` effect.
- Notes: (1) "Attacks alone" on `~` ("Whenever ~ attacks alone") is `what: { kind: 'this' }, alone: true`.

### "Annihilator 2"

- CR: 702.86a (Annihilator), 702.86b, 508.5, 701.21a, 608.2d
- Gap class: K (expanded) plus P (a selector for the defending player, a sacrifice by another player)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts`, `rules/defending.ts` (`defendingPlayerOf`), `rules/select.ts` (`resolvePlayers`), `rules/effects.ts` (`sacrifice`), `rules/sacrifice-choice.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'annihilator', count: 2 } }`, which expands to `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attacks', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: {}, count: 2 }, player: { kind: 'defendingPlayer' } } }`.
- Decision and action: When the creature attacks, the trigger resolves and the defending player (CR 508.5: the player attacked, the controller of the planeswalker attacked, the protector of the battle attacked; still the one it attacked if the creature has died) sacrifices N permanents of their choice: with no more permanents than N the engine chooses (`permanentsChosen` with `automatic: true`), with none nothing happens, otherwise a `choosePermanent` decision with `purpose: 'sacrifice'`, `min` and `max` N, `legal` all of the defending player's permanents, goes to the defending player and is answered by `choosePermanents`. Several instances trigger separately; two different counts are two abilities the controller orders.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-exalted-annihilator.test.ts` (0, 1, 2 and more permanents, the decision and who may answer it, wrong answers refused, a planeswalker target, the creature dying first, two instances, legal actions and `applyAction` agree), `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts`; AI: `casual-keyword-batch.test.ts` (the defending agent answers and keeps its best permanent), `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (the question goes to the defender with the right count and the defender's permanents only). Mutation-checked: the defending player read as the controller fails 9 tests; the sacrificing seat ignored fails 8.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Annihilator Titan` (2), `SYNTHETIC Eater Imp` (1).
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Annihilator N" (N a number; a keyword line) lowers to the keyword. "Defending player" in other text lowers to `{ kind: 'defendingPlayer' }` (`selector:defendingPlayer`); "sacrifices N permanents" with another player lowers to `sacrifice` with `player` and a `choose` selector.
- Notes: (1) The engine has two seats, so there is one defending player; several defending players (CR 508.5a) are not built.

### "Persist"

- CR: 702.79a (Persist), 603.10a, 603.4, 400.7, 122.6, 111.8, 110.2, 700.4
- Gap class: K (expanded) plus P (`thisCard`, `sourceHasCounter`, `withCounters`)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`returnWithCounter`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/select.ts` (`thisCard`), `rules/zones.ts` (`LastKnownInfo.successor`, `withCounters`), `rules/effects.ts` (`moveToZone`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'persist' } }`, which expands to `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'dies', what: { kind: 'this' } }, interveningIf: { kind: 'not', term: { kind: 'sourceHasCounter', counter: '-1/-1' } }, effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'thisCard' }, to: 'battlefield', controller: 'owner', withCounters: { counter: '-1/-1', count: 1 } } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. When the creature dies with no -1/-1 counter on it (read as it last existed, when it dies and again on resolution) the trigger returns the card from the graveyard to the battlefield under its owner's control with a -1/-1 counter on it, as a new object (it has the counter as it enters, so the `counterAdded` event follows the `zoneChanged`). With a -1/-1 counter, or exiled, or a card that left the graveyard before the trigger resolved, or a token, nothing returns. "When this dies" triggers of its own trigger on each death.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-persist-undying.test.ts` (returns once as a new object, the counter on entering, a counter stops it, the second death stays, two triggers ordered, +1/+1 counters do not stop it, a sacrifice, a flicker, the card exiled in response, a creature controlled by another player, a token), `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts`; AI: `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (every death triggers or not as the counter says, every return is a new object with the counter). Mutation-checked: `sourceHasCounter` always false fails 4 tests, the successor pointer removed fails 9.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Persist Bear`, `SYNTHETIC Persist Wisp` (also gains 1 life when it dies).
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Persist" (a keyword line) lowers to the keyword. "When ~ dies, if it had no -1/-1 counters on it, return it to the battlefield under its owner's control with a -1/-1 counter on it." lowers to the triggered ability above (`condition:sourceHasCounter` under `not`, `selector:thisCard`, a `moveToZone` with `withCounters`); the same shape with another counter or "return it to your hand" works for other cards.
- Notes: (1) It cannot be granted. (2) A Persist permanent that is not a creature does not trigger (only a creature "dies").

### "Undying"

- Decision and action: As Persist: returns with a +1/+1 counter if it had none (a 2/2 comes back as a 3/3), and a -1/-1 counter does not stop it.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-persist-undying.test.ts` (returns 3/3, the second death stays, -1/-1 counters do not stop it), `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts`; AI: `keyword-batch-games.test.ts`.

- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22

- Notes: None beyond Persist's.

### "Infect" and "Wither"

- CR: 702.90 (Infect), 702.80 (Wither), 120.3a, 120.3b, 120.3d, 120.3e, 120.3c, 120.3f, 704.5h, 702.2, 615, 613.1f
- Gap class: K (read where damage is dealt)
- Code: `rules/damage.ts` (`dealDamage`, `sourceInfo`), `rules/sba.ts` (deathtouch), `state/events.ts` (`poisonChanged`), `ast/features.ts` (`GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'infect' } }` and `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'wither' } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. Damage from a source with infect gives a player that many poison counters and no life loss (`poisonChanged`); to a creature it puts that many -1/-1 counters on it (`counterAdded`), not marked damage; wither does the creature half only (a player loses life as ever). A planeswalker loses loyalty, lifelink gains life, commander damage counts, `damageDealt` is emitted as ever, and a prevention shield takes its part first (prevented damage gives no poison and no counters). Deathtouch with infect or wither destroys the creature (any damage dealt is lethal). The source's last-known information is used if it has left (CR 702.90d). Both can be granted.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-infect-toxic.test.ts` (poison and no life loss, counters not damage and staying after the turn, -1/-1 cancelling +1/+1, a pinger's noncombat damage, a planeswalker, lifelink, deathtouch, ten poison counters, nine, granted infect, Fog and a partial shield, wither), `casual-keyword-batch.test.ts`; AI soak: `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (poison and counters re-derived from the damage events of every step). Mutation-checked: infect as life loss fails 8 tests; deathtouch needing marked damage fails the viper test.
