- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Infect Bear`, `SYNTHETIC Infect Lifelinker`, `SYNTHETIC Infect Viper`, `SYNTHETIC Infect Pinger`, `SYNTHETIC Wither Imp`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Infect" and "Wither" (keyword lines, also "Flying, infect") lower to the keywords. "Gains infect until end of turn" and "Creatures you control have infect" lower to `grantKeyword` with `{ kind: 'infect' }`.
- Notes: (1) The web needs a log line for `poisonChanged` and the poison counters on the player strip.

### "Toxic 1"

- CR: 702.164 (Toxic), 120.3g, 702.164b, 704.5c, 122.1
- Gap class: K (read where damage is dealt)
- Code: `rules/damage.ts` (`toxicValue`), `rules/sba.ts` (ten poison counters, CR 704.5c)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'toxic', count: 1 } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. Combat damage to a player from a creature with toxic gives that player poison counters equal to the creature's total toxic value (the sum of its toxic N, CR 702.164b) in addition to the damage's other results (life loss, or poison from infect). Damage to a creature and noncombat damage give none. Ten poison counters lose the game at the next state-based action check (`playerLost` with reason `poison`).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-infect-toxic.test.ts` (life and poison together, no poison to a creature, two instances add, infect and toxic add, poison can end the game); AI: `casual-keyword-batch.test.ts`, `keyword-batch-games.test.ts`. Mutation-checked: summing replaced by a maximum fails the two-instances test.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Toxic Rat` (1), `SYNTHETIC Toxic Brute` (2).
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Toxic N" (N a number; a keyword line) lowers to the keyword with `count: N`.
- Notes: (1) It cannot be granted (parametric).

### "Split second"

- CR: 702.61 (Split Second), 702.61a, 702.61b, 117.1, 602.1
- Gap class: K (a restriction read where legality is decided)
- Code: `rules/split-second.ts`, `rules/legality.ts` (`canCast`), `rules/activate-legality.ts` (`activationRefusal`), `rules/priority.ts` (the options are built from these)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'splitSecond' } }` on an instant or sorcery.
- Decision and action: No decision. While a spell with the keyword is on the stack, no `castSpell` (a flashback or free cast included) and no `activate` option is offered to anyone, the caster included, and `applyAction` refuses them with an error. Mana abilities (`activateAbility`) and a pass stay; triggered abilities trigger and go on the stack above the spell as normal; once the spell has resolved everything is offered again.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-split-second.test.ts` (no response, no activation, no cycling, no flashback; the caster too; `applyAction` refuses; mana abilities stay; a trigger goes on the stack; a split second spell cast in response; the legal actions never offer what `applyAction` refuses); AI: `casual-keyword-batch.test.ts` (the agent holding a counterspell passes), `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (no cast or activation option in any game decision under split second, and the engine refuses a cast sent anyway). Mutation-checked: the check removed from `canCast` fails 4 tests.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Hush Bolt`, `SYNTHETIC Hush Gain`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Split second" (a keyword line; the reminder text in parentheses is dropped) lowers to the keyword.
- Notes: (1) It cannot be granted.

### "Basic landcycling {1}" and "Islandcycling {2}"

- CR: 702.29e (Typecycling), 702.29f, 701.23, 701.20, 701.24, 602.2
- Gap class: K (expanded like Cycling)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`typecyclingAbility`), `rules/search.ts`, `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'typecycling', filter: { types: ['land'], supertypes: ['basic'] }, cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }] } }` for basic landcycling; `filter: { subtypes: ['Island'] }` for Islandcycling (Plainscycling, Swampcycling, Mountaincycling and Forestcycling the same with their type). It expands to an `activated` ability with `functionsIn: 'hand'`, `cost` the keyword's cost plus `discardThis`, and `effect: { kind: 'search', player: { kind: 'you' }, filter, max: 1, destinations: [{ zone: 'hand', count: 1 }], reveal: true, shuffle: true }`.
- Decision and action: Activated from the hand at instant speed like Cycling; the cost is paid and the card discarded, then the ability on the stack resolves with a `chooseCards` decision (`purpose: 'search'`, `min` 0: finding nothing is allowed, `reveal: true`) offering the matching cards of the library (an Island-type search finds a nonbasic land with the Island type); with none to find nothing is asked, `librarySearched` with 0 found. The found card is revealed (`cardsRevealed`), goes to the hand, and the library is shuffled either way. The opponent's view shows no card of the search.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-typecycling.test.ts` (basic landcycling: the offer, the reveal and shuffle events, finding nothing, an empty library of basics, hidden information, only from the hand and only with the mana, instant speed; Islandcycling: the Island type including a dual land; Plains, Swamp, Mountain and Forest), `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts` (the filter, the cost, the expansion); AI: `casual-keyword-batch.test.ts` (typecycles when it has no land and nothing to cast, not with a land in hand), `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (every search offers exactly the right cards of the library). Mutation-checked: no shuffle fails 3 tests.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Landcycler Beast`, `SYNTHETIC Islandcycler Drake`, `SYNTHETIC Tide Pool`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Basic landcycling {1}" to the filter above; "[Landtype]cycling {N}" ("Islandcycling {2}", "Plainscycling {1}", "Swampcycling", "Mountaincycling", "Forestcycling") to `filter: { subtypes: ['<Type>'] }`; a creature or other subtype ("Wizardcycling {2}") the same with that subtype. The cost is `[{ kind: 'mana', symbols: [...] }]` (or `payLife`), without the discard.
- Notes: (1) Only mana and life costs. (2) "When you cycle this card" triggers (CR 702.29c) are not built, for Cycling either.

### "Multikicker {2}"

- CR: 702.33c (Multikicker), 702.33d, 601.2b, 601.2f, 119.4, 733, 113.7a
- Gap class: S (one more question in the casting transaction, asked with the existing `chooseX`)
- Code: `rules/cast.ts` (`beginCast`, `answerKicks`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`CostRequest.kicks`), `rules/alt-costs.ts`, `rules/kicks.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts` (`timesKicked`), `rules/zones.ts`, `state/decisions.ts` (`ChooseXDecision`), `state/types.ts` (`StackData.kicks`, `GameObject.kicks`), `api.ts`, `view.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'multikicker', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }] } }`; "for each time it was kicked" is `{ kind: 'timesKicked' }` as an `Amount` (`addCounters`, `dealDamage`, `draw` and the rest); "if it was kicked" is the `kicked` condition, true for one or more.
- Decision and action: As the spell is cast, first (before X and the targets), a `chooseX` decision with `purpose: 'multikicker'`, `cost` the cost of one time, `min` 0, `max` the most times the whole cost can be paid (from every mana source, life leaving at least 1) and `perX` zero, answered by `chooseX { value }`; `cancel` rewinds the cast. It is not asked when not even one kick can be paid. The total cost has the multikicker cost once for each kick. The count is kept on the spell (`StackData.kicks`, the `spellCast.kicks` event, `ObjectView.kicks`) and on the permanent that enters from it, where its "when this enters" trigger reads it (also from last-known information once it has left). 0 times is an unkicked cast with no `kicks`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-multikicker.test.ts` (asked first, the legal actions, the most accepted and one more refused, not asked with no kick payable, cancel, 0, 1 and 3 kicks and the cost, damage per kick, a cost of life and its limit, plain Kicker counts once), `ast-kicker-validate.test.ts`, `ast-keywords-validate.test.ts`; AI: `casual-keyword-batch.test.ts` (pays all the kicks it can, life only above the line, does not cast a Barrage it cannot kick), `keyword-batch-games.test.ts` (every question's legal actions and bound, the count on the stack object, the counters of the Cub, the damage of the Barrage). Mutation-checked: one payment for many kicks fails 4 tests; the count kept as 1 fails 4.
- Example cards: wordings from D-104; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Strider Cub`, `SYNTHETIC Volley Barrage`, `SYNTHETIC Blood Tithe`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P15) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 17, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Multikicker {2}" (also "Multikicker {1}{G}" and "Multikicker—Pay 2 life") to the keyword; "for each time it was kicked" and "for each time this spell was kicked" to `{ kind: 'timesKicked' }`; "put a +1/+1 counter on it for each time it was kicked" to `addCounters` with that count in an enters trigger. The scenario DSL answers the question with `cast(card, { kicks })` or `chooseX(count)`.
- Notes: (1) A card has one kicker or multikicker ability (two kicker costs, CR 702.33b, are not built). (2) X in a kicker cost is refused. (3) A target that exists only if the spell was kicked is refused, as for Kicker.

## Trigger slice (D-108, D-111)

Engine slice, `ENGINE_VERSION` 18. The kinds are found in the event stream like the older ones (D-059); what each wording must lower to is under its entry. Mutation checks were run on scratch copies of the files (never in the shared tree); each is listed in the entry of the behaviour it pins.

### "At the beginning of combat on your turn, ..." and the other steps

- CR: 500.2, 503, 504.1, 504.2, 505, 507, 511, 512, 603.2, 603.3, 603.2b, 508.8
- Gap class: P (a trigger kind that generalises `upkeep` and `endStep`)
- Code: `rules/triggers.ts` (`STEP_OF`, `occurrences`, `matches`, `isRelevant`), `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `step`, `TriggerStep`, `TRIGGER_STEPS`), `ast/validate.ts` (`checkTrigger`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'step', step: 'beginCombat', whose: 'you' }, effect }`. `step` is one of `upkeep`, `draw`, `main1`, `beginCombat`, `combatEnd`, `main2`, `endStep`; `whose` is `you` (the turn of the controller of the ability), `each` or `opponent` (a turn of any other player). `upkeep` and `endStep` stay as kinds of their own and behave the same as the step form.
- Decision and action: No decision. The trigger is noticed as the step begins (`stepBegan`) and goes on the stack at the first priority of the step. The draw step draws first (CR 504.1, the card is in the hand when the trigger goes on the stack, CR 504.2), and a commander drawn from a library asks its owner before the step goes on. The beginning of combat and the end of combat step happen even when nothing attacks (CR 507, 508.8: only declare blockers and combat damage are skipped).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-step-triggers.test.ts` (every step times every `whose` on both players' turns, the beginning of combat with and without an attacker, the draw step's order against the draw, APNAP order, ordering one's own triggers, determinism, the legal actions), `ast-trigger-slice-validate.test.ts`; AI: `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (every step watcher triggers exactly once, on the turns its text names, in 100 games). Mutation-checked: `opponent` treated as `each` fails 7 tests.
- Example cards: wordings from the P15 replay and D-108; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Step Watcher <step> <whose>` (21 cards, `synthetic-triggers-more.ts`).
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after P17) to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "At the beginning of combat on your turn, ..." and "At the beginning of each combat, ..." (`beginCombat`, `you` or `each`); "At the beginning of your draw step, ..." (`draw`, `you`); "At the beginning of each opponent's upkeep, ..." and "At the beginning of each opponent's draw step, ..." (`opponent`); "At the beginning of your precombat main phase, ..." (`main1`), "your postcombat main phase" (`main2`), "At the beginning of the end of combat step" or "of each combat's end" (`combatEnd`, `each`); "At the beginning of each player's upkeep" (`each`). "Your upkeep" and "your end step" may keep the old kinds.
- Notes: (1) The first player's skipped first draw (CR 103.8a) still has a draw step and its trigger. (2) Extra and skipped steps do not exist in the engine yet.

### "Whenever you draw a card, ...", "Whenever an opponent draws a card, ...", "Whenever you draw your second card each turn, ..."

- CR: 121.1, 121.2, 121.4, 504.1, 603.2c, 903.9b
- Gap class: P (a trigger kind and a per-turn counter)
- Code: `rules/triggers.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts` (`drawCard`), `rules/flow.ts` (`startTurn`), `state/types.ts` (`SeatState.cardsDrawnThisTurn`), `state/events.ts` (`cardDrawn.nth`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'drawn', who: 'you' | 'opponent' | 'any', nth?: number }`; "your second card each turn" is `nth: 2` ("an opponent draws their second card each turn" is `who: 'opponent', nth: 2`).
- Decision and action: No decision. One trigger for each card drawn, when it is drawn (a spell that draws three triggers three times, `nth` 1, 2 and 3); `nth` is the count of cards that player has drawn this turn, the turn's draw step included. A commander that goes to the command zone instead of the hand was not drawn and does not count; drawing from an empty library draws nothing and triggers nothing.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-drawn-triggers.test.ts` (you, an opponent, any player, three draws, the empty library, the draw step, the commander replaced and drawn, nth in every combination, reset each turn, determinism, the legal actions); AI: `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (the engine's `nth` against an independent count; each draw watcher exactly once per card). Mutation-checked: ignoring `nth` fails 7 tests; not resetting the count each turn fails 1.
- Example cards: wordings from D-108; none looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Draw Watcher`, `SYNTHETIC Opponent Draw Watcher`, `SYNTHETIC Any Draw Watcher`, `SYNTHETIC Second Draw Watcher`, `SYNTHETIC Opponent Second Draw Watcher`, `SYNTHETIC Cantrip`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Whenever you draw a card" to `who: 'you'`; "Whenever an opponent draws a card" to `who: 'opponent'`; "Whenever a player draws a card" to `any`; "Whenever you draw your second card each turn" to `who: 'you', nth: 2` (also "third", "first"); "Whenever an opponent draws their second card each turn" to `who: 'opponent', nth: 2`.
- Notes: (1) "Draw a card for the first time each turn" wordings are `nth: 1`. (2) The card drawn is not read by the ability (no `triggerSubject` use).

### "Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player, ..." and "Whenever ~ deals combat damage to a player, ..."

- CR: 120.2a, 510.2, 510.4, 603.2, 603.2c, 603.2g, 120.3b
- Gap class: P (a trigger kind read from the damage events)
- Code: `rules/triggers.ts`, `rules/damage.ts` (the events it already makes), `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `dealsCombatDamage`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'dealsCombatDamage', what: THIS | filter | { kind: 'attached' }, to: 'player' | 'opponent' | 'creature' | 'any', filter? }`. `what` is the creature that deals the damage (the trigger's `triggerSubject`); `to` is what it hit: a player, a player the controller of the ability does not control (`opponent`), a creature (narrowed by `filter`, only with `to: 'creature'`), or anything (a planeswalker or battle too).
- Decision and action: No decision. One trigger for each source and each player or creature it damages (blocked by two creatures and dividing the damage: two triggers for "to a creature"; a trampler hitting a blocker and a player: one for "to a creature" and one for "to a player", two for "any"), in the step the damage is dealt (a double striker triggers in both steps). Damage that was entirely prevented triggers nothing (CR 603.2g); partly prevented damage triggers for what was dealt. Infect damage to a player is damage. Noncombat damage is not read.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-combat-damage-triggers.test.ts` (unblocked, two attackers, the creature itself, the other player's watcher, blocked, double block, the recipient filter, a planeswalker, noncombat damage, all damage prevented, part prevented, double strike in two steps, infect, determinism, the legal actions); AI: `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (every hit watcher, each source and recipient, in 100 games). Mutation-checked: `to: 'player'` matching creatures fails 3 tests. Equivalent mutant: reading noncombat damage too changes nothing reachable, because the engine's damage source for a spell or ability is the stack object, not a permanent.
- Example cards: wordings from D-108 (59 cards in 12 templates in the P15 replay, UNVERIFIED here); none looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Player Hit Watcher`, `Opponent Hit Watcher`, `Creature Hit Watcher`, `Big Creature Hit Watcher`, `Any Damage Watcher`, `Every Hit Watcher`, `Biting Bear`, `Scrapping Bear`, `Striking Bear`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player" (`what: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }`, `to: 'player'`); "Whenever ~ deals combat damage to a player" (`what: { kind: 'this' }`); "... to an opponent" (`to: 'opponent'`); "... deals combat damage to a creature" (`to: 'creature'`); "... deals combat damage" (`to: 'any'`); "Whenever equipped creature deals combat damage to a player" (`what: { kind: 'attached' }`); "Whenever one or more creatures you control deal combat damage to a player" is NOT this (it triggers once for the group, not built).
- Notes: (1) "One or more" group triggers and triggers for noncombat damage are not built. (2) A planeswalker has no `filter`.

### "Whenever ~ leaves the battlefield, ..."

- CR: 603.6c, 603.10a, 113.7a, 700.4, 701.21, 111.7
- Gap class: P (a look-back kind)
- Code: `rules/triggers.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`LOOK_BACK_TRIGGER_KINDS`), `ast/supported.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'leavesBattlefield', what: THIS | filter | { kind: 'attached' }, to?: Zone }`. `to` is `graveyard`, `exile`, `hand`, `library` or `command`; left out it is any zone. A look-back kind: it reads the permanent as it last was (types, controller, power, toughness, counters, effects, attachments), and permanents that leave together see each other leave; an intervening "if" that counts permanents is refused on it.
- Decision and action: No decision. Dying, exile, bounce, sacrifice and being put into a library all trigger it; a token that goes to another zone triggers it; a card put into a zone other than the one `to` names does not.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-leaves-triggers.test.ts` (five ways of leaving against five `to` values, a stolen creature's last controller, simultaneous departures and a leaver seeing itself, a token, entering and the hand not triggering, exile only, determinism, the legal actions); AI: `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (own leave triggers and the watchers of the last controller, in 100 games). Mutation-checked: dropping it from `LOOK_BACK_TRIGGER_KINDS` fails 11 tests.
- Example cards: wordings from D-108 (7 templates, 32 cards in the replay, UNVERIFIED here); none looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Leaver any`, `Leaver graveyard`, `Leaver exile`, `Leaver hand`, `Leaver library`, `Departure Watcher`, `Rival Departure Watcher`, `Exile Watcher`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Whenever ~ leaves the battlefield" and "When ~ leaves the battlefield" (`what: { kind: 'this' }`); "... leaves the battlefield and is put into a graveyard / exile / its owner's hand / library" (`to`); "Whenever a creature you control leaves the battlefield" (a filter); "Whenever equipped creature leaves the battlefield" (`attached`).
- Notes: (1) Separate effect steps of one resolution that move permanents back to back are one run only as `docs/ROADMAP.md` says for `dies`; the last-known power of a permanent that left earlier in the same resolution lacks what its lord gave it only when the lord left before it.

### "Whenever a creature with power 4 or greater enters, ..." (power, toughness and mana value in a filter)

- CR: 202.3, 202.3e, 208.1, 603.6a, 603.10a, 113.7a, 115.1, 603.4, 613.4c, 613.8a, 613.8b
- Gap class: P (a filter feature)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`NumberComparison`, `ObjectFilter` `power`, `toughness`, `manaValue`), `rules/filter-match.ts` (`compares`), `rules/select.ts` (`describeObject`), `rules/mana-value.ts`, `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`StaticBase`, `dependsOnPt`), `rules/derive.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkFilter`), `ast/supported.ts` (`filterComparesPt`)
- AST node: `{ types: ['creature'], power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } }` ("power 4 or greater"; `atMost` is "or less", `equal` is exactly). The same for `toughness` and `manaValue`. Valid in every filter: a trigger's subject, a target slot (`types: ['creature'], power: ...` in a `TargetSpec`), `controlsAtLeast` conditions, a `countOf` amount, a static ability's subject, a search.
- Decision and action: No decision. A permanent entering is read as it enters, counters and effects included (a 2/2 that enters with two +1/+1 counters has power 4); an object that left is read from its last-known information; a spell on the stack reads its mana value with the announced X ({X}{U} with X = 3 is 4); an object with no power or toughness matches no power or toughness comparison. In static abilities a "+N/+N" effect that compares power or toughness applies after the ones that do not (CR 613.8a), so a 3/3 with a plain anthem is a 4/4 for "power 4 or greater"; a keyword or ability grant that compares power or toughness is refused by `validateCard` (`docs/ROADMAP.md`), mana value works everywhere.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-object-filter-compare.test.ts` (enters by printed power, by counters, by a static effect, another player's creature, last-known power with counters, -1/-1 counters, a static bonus, a lord that leaves together with its creature, toughness, mana value of a spell and of an {X} spell, target slots, the intervening "if" and its second check, static modifiers: one, two, counters, toughness, mana value, leaving the lord), `ast-trigger-slice-validate.test.ts` (shapes, refusals); AI: `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (power as it enters or as it left, mana value as cast). Mutation-checked: reading a stale `derive` instead of last-known information fails 1 test (the lord that leaves with its creature); applying the comparing statics in the ordinary pass fails 1.
- Example cards: wordings from D-108 (14 templates in the replay, UNVERIFIED here); none looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Big Arrival Watcher`, `Frail Death Watcher`, `Tough Death Watcher`, `Cheap Spell Watcher`, `Exact Spell Watcher`, `Giant Slayer`, `Wall Breaker`, `Grower`, `Titan Roost`, `Titan Anthem`, `Frail Anthem`, `Cheap Lord`, `Big Stompers`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "with power 4 or greater" to `power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 }`, "with power 3 or less" to `atMost`, "with power 2" (an exact number) to `equal`; the same for "toughness" and "mana value" ("with mana value 3 or less", "with converted mana cost" is old wording of the same); "creature with power 4 or greater" is `types: ['creature']` plus the comparison. "Each creature with power greater than ..." (comparing with another object's power) is not this.
- Notes: (1) A comparison with another object's value ("with power less than ~'s power"), "greater than" and "less than" as strict comparisons (use the number plus or minus one), "even" and "odd" values are not built. (2) A card that is not a creature in a zone where it shows a power (a Vehicle) has none until it is a creature.

### "Whenever equipped creature dies, ..." (the attached permanent as a trigger subject)

- CR: 301.5a, 303.4b, 603.10a, 113.7a, 704.5m, 704.5n, 701.3
- Gap class: P (a subject for six trigger kinds)
- Code: `rules/triggers.ts` (`Candidate.attachedTo`, `matchesWhat`), `ast/types.ts` (`TriggerSubject`), `ast/validate.ts` (`checkTriggerSubject`)
- AST node: `what: { kind: 'attached' }` in `dies`, `attacks`, `becomesTapped`, `becomesTarget`, `dealsCombatDamage` and `leavesBattlefield` (not `entersBattlefield`). It matches the permanent the Aura or Equipment is attached to; when that host dies or leaves, the host is still what the permanent was attached to (state-based actions undo it later), and the permanent itself may be last-known information if it left too.
- Decision and action: No decision. The trigger follows the Equipment when it moves (CR 701.3) and does not exist while it is unattached.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-attached-triggers.test.ts` (dies, another creature dies, an opponent's creature carrying your Equipment, moving the Equipment, an Aura that goes to the graveyard after its host died, becomes tapped, attacks, combat damage, leaves by bounce, the legal actions, determinism); AI: `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (the Equipment and Aura triggers of 100 games). Mutation-checked: the subject never matching fails 9 tests.
- Example cards: wordings from D-108 (6 templates in the replay, UNVERIFIED here); none looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Last Gift Blade`, `Drumbeat Blade`, `Doom Aura`, `Tap Aura`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Whenever equipped creature dies", "Whenever enchanted creature dies", "Whenever equipped creature attacks", "Whenever equipped creature deals combat damage to a player", "Whenever enchanted creature becomes tapped", "Whenever equipped creature leaves the battlefield" (`what: { kind: 'attached' }`). "Equipped" and "enchanted" are the same node.
- Notes: (1) "Fortified land" and "enchanted land" use the same node; the host type is not checked by the trigger.

### "Whenever an opponent casts a spell, you may draw a card unless that player pays {1}."

- CR: 118.12, 118.12a, 118.3, 603.5, 601.2i, 608.2h, 119.4, 701.9
- Gap class: P (an effect that reuses Ward's question and payment) and a caster for `castSpell`
- Code: `rules/pay-unless.ts` (`UnlessInstruction`, `notPaid`), `rules/effects.ts` (`unlessPays`, `mayEffect`), `rules/stack.ts`, `rules/triggers.ts` (`by: 'opponent'`), `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `unlessPays`, `Instruction` `unlessPays` and `mayEffect`), `state/decisions.ts` (`purpose: 'unlessPays'`), `api.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkPlayerSelector`), `packages/ai/src/casual-permanents.ts`, `casual-costs.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'castSpell', filter: { stackKind: 'spell' }, by: 'opponent' }, effect: { kind: 'unlessPays', player: { kind: 'controllerOf', of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' } }, cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }], effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 }, optional: true } }`. `player` is any player selector (`eachOpponent` for "that player loses 2 life unless they pay {2}"); `cost` is `mana`, `payLife` and `discard` costs without X; `optional: true` for "you may".
- Decision and action: As the ability resolves, the payer is asked first (`confirmOptional`, `purpose: 'unlessPays'`, `cost`, `source` the ability on the stack, no `subject`); accepting asks for the mana (`payMana`, the same purpose, `cancel` is "do not pay"), for the cards to discard when there are more than needed, and takes the life. A payer who cannot pay the whole cost is not asked (CR 118.3). When the payer does not pay, the effect happens (the controller's, with "you" the controller); with `optional` the controller is then asked (`confirmOptional`, no purpose) and declining drops only that effect, not the rest of the ability. If the spell was countered meanwhile, its caster is still the payer (last-known information).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-unless-pays.test.ts` (the opponent's spell triggers it and one's own does not, the question asked of the payer first with its purpose and cost, only the payer can answer, paying and cancelling the payment, decline then "you may", declining the draw, cannot pay, no "you may", life, too little life, discard, no card to discard, the spell countered in response, an upkeep payer who is not the controller, "you may" inside a longer effect, determinism, the legal actions), `ast-trigger-slice-validate.test.ts`; AI: `casual-unless-pays.test.ts` (pays {1} and with the planner, life only above the line, a discard only with a card it is glad to lose, the free draw accepted, random agent legal), `trigger-slice-games.test.ts` (who is asked, what follows a decline and a payment, in 100 games). Mutation-checked: a payer who cannot pay still asked fails 5 tests; a decline doing nothing fails 12; the "you may" ending the whole ability fails 1; the AI not answering like Ward fails 1; the AI discard answer always accepting fails 1.
- Example cards: wordings from D-108 ("an opponent casts" with "unless that player pays", several popular cards; UNVERIFIED here); none looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Tax Collector`, `Strict Tax Collector`, `Blood Tax Collector`, `Tithe Collector`, `Toll Keeper`, `Rival Spell Watcher`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read its other abilities.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 18, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Whenever an opponent casts a spell, you may draw a card unless that player pays {1}." to the node above; without "you may" no `optional`; "unless that player pays {N}" and "unless they pay {N}" to `cost`; "that player" to `{ kind: 'controllerOf', of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' } }` in a `castSpell` trigger and to the selector the trigger names elsewhere; "unless that player pays 2 life" to `payLife`; "unless that player discards a card" to a `discard` cost. "Whenever an opponent casts a spell" is `by: 'opponent'`.
- Notes: (1) "Any opponent may pay" (the first who pays stops it) and a payer who chooses among costs ("pays {1} or discards a card") are not built. (2) The web needs a wording for the `unlessPays` question and payment.

### Not built in this slice

- Panharmonicon ("triggers an additional time", D-108 point 8): see `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Modal spells and abilities (engine slice; D-105, D-114; CR 700.2, 700.2a-d, 601.2b-c, 602.2b, 603.3c, 608.2b-c, 903.3d)

Entries for "Choose one —" and the like, on spells, triggered abilities and activated abilities (about 0.10 of the corpus replay's unread weight, D-105). The engine side only. All tests use SYNTHETIC cards (`packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-modal.ts`, `SYNTHETIC Modal Choice` in `synthetic.ts`, and cards defined in the test files); run them with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-modal packages/engine/test/ast-modal-validate.test.ts packages/ai/test/casual-modes.test.ts packages/ai/test/modal-games.test.ts`. Code: `rules/modes.ts`, `rules/cast.ts`, `rules/activate.ts`, `rules/trigger-stack.ts`, `rules/stack.ts`, `rules/legality.ts`, `rules/activate-legality.ts`, `ast/validate-modes.ts`, `api.ts`, `legal-actions.ts`.

How modes are chosen, for every entry below. The controller answers a `chooseModes { source, sourceName, sourceOracleText, purpose, modes: { index, text, legal }[], min, max, sets, forTrigger? }` decision with `chooseModes { modes }`, one of `sets` (each in printed order, fewest modes first; any order is accepted). It comes first in the cast (before the kicker, X and the targets), first in an activation (before X, the targets and the costs) and first as a triggered ability goes on the stack (before its targets). Only the modes whose targets can all be chosen are in a set (CR 700.2a). With one legal set the engine chooses it (`modesChosen { automatic: true }`), with none the spell is not castable, the activated ability is not offered and the triggered ability is removed (`abilityRemoved { reason: 'noLegalModes' }`). `cancel` rewinds a cast or activation, never a trigger. The targets of the chosen modes are asked in one `targets` decision, mode by mode, each slot tagged with its `mode`; the target ids are unique across the modes. On resolution the chosen modes' effects run in printed order.

### "Choose one —", "Choose two —", "Choose one or both —", "Choose one or more —" (a modal spell)

- CR: 700.2 (modes, how many), 700.2a (chosen as the spell is cast; a mode that would be illegal cannot be chosen), 601.2b-c (announced first; the targets of the chosen modes follow), 700.2c (a mode not chosen has no targets), 700.2d (the same mode is not chosen twice), 608.2b-c (fizzling; effects in printed order)
- Gap class: S (a new step of the casting transaction; designed in D-105 and D-114)
- Code: see above
- AST node: `spell: { chooseModes: { min: 1, max: 1 }, modes: [{ text: 'Deals 3 damage to target creature.', targets: [{ id: 'kill', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'] } } }], effect: { kind: 'dealDamage', amount: 3, to: { kind: 'target', id: 'kill' } } }, { text: 'Draw a card.', effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } }, ...] }`; "choose one" is `{ min: 1, max: 1 }`, "choose two" `{ min: 2, max: 2 }`, "choose one or both" `{ min: 1, max: 2 }`, "choose one or more" `{ min: 1, max: <number of modes> }`. The spell has no `effect` and no `targets` of its own.
- Decision and action: `chooseModes` as above; `purpose: 'spell'`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-modal-choose.test.ts` (each mode of three alone; two modes with a target each, asked mode by mode; printed order whatever order they were named in; one or both; one or more with its seven sets; answers that are not a legal set are refused; the view shows the modes; the same actions give the same hash; every offered action is accepted), `scenario-xx-modal-legality.test.ts` (a mode with no legal target is not offered; one legal set is chosen without asking; no legal set means not castable, also for "choose two" with one legal mode; cancel at the mode stage, the targets and the payment rewinds exactly; every offered set of five cards runs to the end), `scenario-xx-modal-resolution.test.ts` (fizzling in full and in part, a mode with no target does not save a spell, the target of a mode not chosen does not count), `ast-modal-validate.test.ts`; AI: `modal-games.test.ts` (100 games with the rules restated from the texts at every step). Behaviours mutation-checked: see the slice report.
- Example cards: "Choose one — • ~ deals 3 damage to target creature. • Draw a card. • You gain 3 life." (the shape of the charms and commands; no real card was looked up for this slice, no Scryfall access). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Three Way Charm`, `Twin Charm`, `Both Charm`, `Split Strike`, `Many Charm`, `Drain Charm`, `SYNTHETIC Modal Choice`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser to read the bullets and its other text.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 19, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the Oracle layout is a lead line "Choose one —", "Choose two —", "Choose one or both —" or "Choose one or more —" followed by one line per mode starting with "• " (U+2022 and a space). The lead line gives `chooseModes` as above; each bullet line is one mode: its `text` is the bullet without "• ", its `targets` and `effect` are what the ordinary sentence rules lower from it, as a spell of its own, with target ids numbered across the whole spell (not restarting at `t1` in every mode: the ids must be unique). A card whose bullets cannot all be lowered is unsupported as a whole (a card is supported or not, never partly). Fewer than two or more than eight bullets, or a lead line the list above does not name ("Choose up to two", "Choose three"), is not read (`notRunnable`).
- Notes: (1) The modes are in printed order, which is the order they run in; do not reorder them. (2) "Choose one —" with modes that each read X uses the spell's one X (`spell.x: true`), as for any spell with X.

### "Choose one. If you control a commander as you cast this spell, you may choose both instead." (the widening)

- CR: 700.2 (how many modes), 903.3d (controlling a commander: a commander permanent on the battlefield), 601.2b (the choice is made as the spell is cast)
- Gap class: P (a condition on the number of modes)
- Code: `rules/modes.ts` (`modeLimit`), `ast/validate-modes.ts`
- AST node: `chooseModes: { min: 1, max: 1, maxIf: { condition: { kind: 'controlCommander' }, max: 2 } }`.
- Decision and action: as above; with a commander on the battlefield the decision has `max: 2` and the sets `[0], [1], [0, 1]`; without one (the commander in the command zone, or on the other side) `max: 1` and `[0], [1]`. The condition is read when the modes are chosen.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-modal-legality.test.ts` (with and without a commander on the battlefield, in the command zone, on the other side; both is refused without), `casual-modes.test.ts` (the agent takes both), `modal-games.test.ts` (the sets match the commander on the battlefield at every question), `ast-modal-validate.test.ts` (`maxIf.max` must be more than `max` and at most the number of modes).
- Example cards: the "Choose one. If you control a commander as you cast this spell, you may choose both instead." wording named in the task for this slice; no real card was looked up. SYNTHETIC fixture: `SYNTHETIC Command Charm`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 19, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the lead line "Choose one. If you control a commander as you cast ~, you may choose both instead." followed by bullets lowers to `chooseModes { min: 1, max: 1, maxIf: { condition: { kind: 'controlCommander' }, max: 2 } }` (with two modes; `max` is the number of modes for "choose both"). "~" or "this spell" for the card name.
- Notes: (1) The condition is evaluated for the controller, with the spell as the source.

### "When ~ enters, choose one —" and "Whenever ~ attacks, choose one —" (a modal triggered ability)

- CR: 603.3c and 700.2b (the modes are chosen as the ability goes on the stack; no legal mode removes it), 603.3d and 601.2c (then the targets of the chosen modes), 603.3b (several triggers: the order first, then each one's questions), 608.2b
- Gap class: S (the trigger flow of D-089 gains a step)
- Code: `rules/trigger-stack.ts` (`runQueue`, `answerTriggerModes`), `rules/modes.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [] }, chooseModes: { min: 1, max: 1 }, modes: [{ text: 'You gain 3 life.', effect: ... }, { text: 'It deals 2 damage to target creature an opponent controls.', targets: [...], effect: ... }] }`. The `effect` is the empty sequence and there are no `targets`; every effect and target is in a mode. The trigger can be any trigger the engine supports.
- Decision and action: when more than one set is legal a `chooseModes { purpose: 'trigger', forTrigger: true }` decision, the ability being on the stack as its `source` and `triggering.awaiting`; no cancel. Then, if the chosen modes have targets, the same `targets` decision as any trigger (`forTrigger`) with slots tagged by mode. With one legal set the engine chooses (and chooses forced targets too); with none the ability is removed and never goes on the stack.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-modal-triggers.test.ts` (the question and its legal actions, no cancel; each mode; one legal mode chosen automatically with its event; no legal mode: `abilityRemoved`, also for "choose two" with one legal mode; choose two with targets of both modes asked together; an attack trigger; two triggers at once: the order, then the modes, then the targets, and `TriggerChoice.trigger`), `modal-games.test.ts`.
- Example cards: the "When ~ enters, choose one —" and "Whenever ~ attacks, choose one —" wordings; no real card was looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Modal Greeter`, `Modal Hunter`, `Twin Greeter`, `Modal Raider`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 19, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: a triggered line whose clause after the trigger is "choose one —" (or two, one or both, one or more) followed by bullets lowers to a `triggered` ability with `effect` the empty sequence, `chooseModes` and `modes` as above; the intervening "if" and "you may" of the line keep their own fields. The bullets are lowered with the trigger's subject available (a mode may read "it" or "that creature").
- Notes: (1) A modal trigger with `optional: true` asks "you may" on resolution after the modes and targets were chosen, as any trigger.

### "{2}, {T}: Choose one —" (a modal activated ability)

- CR: 602.2b and 700.2a (the modes are chosen as the ability is activated, then X, targets and costs), 700.2a (an ability with no legal mode cannot be activated), 733 (cancel)
- Gap class: S (the activation transaction gains the step)
- Code: `rules/activate.ts` (`toActivationModes`, `toActivationX`, `toActivationTargets`), `rules/activate-legality.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }, { kind: 'tap' }], effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [] }, chooseModes: { min: 1, max: 1 }, modes: [...] }`; as for a trigger the `effect` is the empty sequence and the targets are in the modes. The ability may not be a mana ability.
- Decision and action: the ability goes on the stack, then `chooseModes { purpose: 'ability' }` (the ability's stack object is `source`; the name is its permanent's), then X, the targets and the costs; `cancel` rewinds.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-modal-activated.test.ts` (the question, cancel, each mode, a mode whose target is gone, an answer that is not a legal set, a mode with no target is not offered, an ability with no legal mode is not offered and says why, one legal mode chosen by the engine), `casual-modes.test.ts` (the agent activates it for a mode worth 3 or more and answers), `modal-games.test.ts`.
- Example cards: the "{2}, {T}: Choose one —" wording; no real card was looked up. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Modal Totem`, `Picky Totem`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 19, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: an activated line (cost, colon) whose effect clause is "Choose one —" with bullets lowers to an `activated` ability with `effect` the empty sequence, `chooseModes` and `modes`; "Activate only as a sorcery" and the other restrictions keep their own fields. Loyalty abilities with modes are not read (the engine has no loyalty abilities).
- Notes: (1) An {X} in the cost is read by every mode (`xAllowed` is the ability's own cost).


## Power and toughness from counts, X and characteristic-defining abilities (engine slice; D-109, D-115; CR 604.3, 208.2a, 613.4a-c, 611.2c, 611.3a, 608.2h, 613.8)

Entries for "gets +1/+1 for each ...", "~'s power and toughness are each equal to the number of ...", "All creatures get -X/-X until end of turn", "Target creature gets +X/+0 until end of turn" and the lords scaled by a count (about 0.08 of the corpus replay's unread weight, D-109). The engine side only. All tests use SYNTHETIC cards (`packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-pt.ts`, and cards defined in the test files); run them with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine/test/scenario-xx-pt packages/engine/test/ast-pt-validate.test.ts packages/ai/test/pt-games.test.ts`. Code: `rules/derive.ts`, `rules/derive-view.ts`, `rules/derive-count.ts`, `rules/derive-statics.ts`, `rules/continuous.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts` (`ptAmountOf`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkPtAmount`, `checkStaticPtAmount`, `checkStarPairing`), `ast/validate-continuous.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`.

The amount shapes, for every entry below (types in `ast/types.ts`):

- `PtAmount` (the `power` and `toughness` of the effects `modifyPt` and `setPt`): a number (negative allowed), any `Amount` (`x`, `countOf`, `powerOf`, `manaValueOf`, `timesKicked`), or `{ kind: 'times', factor, of: Amount }`, a whole non-zero multiple. "-X/-X" is `times` of -1 of `{ kind: 'x' }`. It is worked out once, as the effect resolves, and the numbers are what the continuous effect stores (CR 611.2c, 608.2h); X is the X announced for the resolving spell or activated ability (CR 107.3a).
- `StaticPtAmount` (the `power` and `toughness` of the static `modifyPt`): a number, a `countOf`, or a `times` of a `countOf`. It is worked out every time the object is derived (CR 611.3a). Never `x`, `powerOf`, `manaValueOf` or `timesKicked`, and the counted filter never has `power` or `toughness`; `validateCard` refuses them.
- `DefinedPtAmount` (the `power` and `toughness` of the static `characteristicPt`): a `countOf` or a positive `times` of one.
- A `countOf` in a static ability counts for the ability's source: `controller: 'you'` is the source's controller (its owner outside the battlefield and the stack), `other: true` leaves the source out, the zone is the battlefield unless `zone` says otherwise.

### "Equipped creature gets +1/+1 for each artifact you control." and "Enchanted creature gets -1/-1 for each creature card in your graveyard."

- CR: 611.3a (not locked in), 613.4c (layer 7c), 613.1d (types read after layer 4, so an artifact creature counts), 109.5 ("you" is the source's controller), 613.8 (a count reads no power or toughness)
- Gap class: P (an amount in a static ability)
- Code: `rules/derive-statics.ts`, `rules/derive-count.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'attached' }, power: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you' } }, toughness: <the same> } }`; for the negative form `power: { kind: 'times', factor: -1, of: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } } }`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pt-static-counts.test.ts` (the Equipment and the Aura: the Equipment counts itself, the bonus follows an artifact entering and being destroyed with no continuous effect stored, an opponent's artifacts are not counted, "you" is the Equipment's controller and not the host's, an artifact creature and a creature that became an artifact are counted, the negative multiple kills at 0 toughness, the view and the hash), `ast-pt-validate.test.ts`.
- Example cards: the two wordings above (no real card was looked up for this slice, no Scryfall access). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Artisan Blade`, `Artisan Aura`, `Grave Chill Aura`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; about 10 cards by the D-109 count.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 20, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the static line of an Aura or Equipment "Equipped creature gets +N/+M for each <filter>." lowers to the static `modifyPt` with subject `attached` and, for each of power and toughness, a `countOf` of the filter when N is 1, a `times` of N of the `countOf` when N is another whole number (negative for "-N/-M for each"), and the number 0 where the text says "+X/+0" style zero. The filter is the ordinary object filter of "for each <filter>" ("artifact you control": `types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you'`; "creature card in your graveyard": `zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you'`). A filter with a power or toughness comparison ("for each creature with power 3 or greater") is not read (`notRunnable`, CR 613.8).
- Notes: (1) The count is by the controller of the Equipment or Aura. (2) The permanent itself counts when it matches (an Equipment is an artifact).

### "~ gets +1/+1 for each creature you control." and "Creatures you control get +1/+1 for each ..." (a creature or a lord scaled by a count)

- CR: 611.3a, 613.4c, 613.7a (a static ability has its permanent's timestamp), 613.1f (a permanent that loses all abilities gives nothing)
- Gap class: P
- Code: `rules/derive-statics.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'this' }, power: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', other: true } }, toughness: <the same> } }` for "for each other creature you control"; a lord is `subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }, other: true }` with the amount a `countOf` ("Other creatures you control get +1/+1 for each Elf you control": `filter: { types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Elf'], controller: 'you' }`, which counts the lord itself, since it is an Elf).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pt-static-counts.test.ts` (alone it is 1/1; it counts the others and not the opponent's; a creature that dies takes its bonus with it; it adds to a counter and an Aura; the lord counts itself and gives nothing to itself; two lords; Dress Down: a creature or a lord that loses all abilities gives and gets nothing), `ast-pt-validate.test.ts`.
- Example cards: the wordings above. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Pack Wolf`, `Elf Chanter`, `Elf Scout`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 20, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "~ gets +1/+1 for each <filter>" lowers to a static `modifyPt` with subject `this` and a `countOf` for both; "<subject> get +1/+1 for each <filter>" lowers to the subject of the existing "creatures you control get +1/+1" rule with the `countOf` amounts. "Each other creature" in the filter is `other: true`.
- Notes: (1) "As long as" conditions are not read in the same line.

### "~'s power and toughness are each equal to the number of cards in your hand." (a characteristic-defining ability, printed */*)

- CR: 604.3 and 604.3a (a static ability that defines power or toughness; it functions in all zones), 208.2 and 208.2a (a printed `*`; "use 0 for a number that cannot be determined"), 613.4a (layer 7a, before 7b and 7c), 613.1f (it is lost with all abilities), 707.2 (a copy has it), 208.3 (a noncreature permanent has none), 113.7a and 608.2h (last-known information of a creature that left)
- Gap class: S (a new layer, 7a, with a rule that works in every zone; `derive` reads it for any object, the amount reads the board without `derive`: D-115)
- Code: `rules/derive.ts` (layer 7a), `rules/derive-count.ts` (`characteristicPtOf`), `rules/derive-view.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkStarPairing`)
- AST node: the face has `power: '*'` and `toughness: '*'` and the ability `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'characteristicPt', power: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { zone: 'hand', controller: 'you' } }, toughness: <the same> } }`. `validateCard` accepts a `'*'` only together with exactly one such ability of the same face that defines that stat, and refuses the ability for a stat the card prints as a number.
- Zones and counts: the number is worked out for the object wherever it is. In the hand it counts itself ("cards in your hand"); on the stack and the battlefield it does not; "you" outside the battlefield and the stack is the owner. A creature card in a graveyard that counts "creature cards in your graveyard" counts itself. Nothing is stored.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pt-cda.test.ts` (hand: follows the board, 0/0 dies as state-based actions are checked, only your own hand counts; lands; power only with the printed toughness; combat damage; hand, library, stack and graveyard; a dies trigger reading "its power" from last-known information after the hand changed; layer order: 7a under a base-4/4 effect under a counter and a counted Aura; base values from a count; a noncreature permanent has none; loses all abilities: a star is 0 and kills a `*/*`, the printed toughness stays for a power-only definition, "loses all abilities and becomes 1/1" gives 1/1, the definition is back when the effect ends; a token copy has it; the view for both seats and the opponent's hand hidden; legal actions all accepted; the hash), `ast-pt-validate.test.ts`, `ast-features.test.ts`; AI: `pt-games.test.ts` (100 games with the layers restated from the card texts at every step, in every zone the view shows).
- Example cards: the wordings above. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Hand Hydra`, `Land Lurker`, `Grave Ghoul`, `Lifeline Hydra`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 20, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the card's printed power and toughness `*` stay `'*'` (the converter already reads a bare `*` and marks `1+*`, `*+1` and the like as unsupported: those are not read). The line "~'s power and toughness are each equal to the number of <filter>." (or "This creature's ...") lowers to `characteristicPt` with `power` and `toughness` both the `countOf`; "<filter>" is the ordinary filter of the noun phrase ("cards in your hand": `zone: 'hand', controller: 'you'`; "lands you control": `types: ['land'], controller: 'you'`; "creature cards in your graveyard": `zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you'`). If the line says "twice the number of" the amount is a `times` of 2. The parser must not drop the line and keep the `*`: `validateCard` refuses a printed `*` with no ability.
- Notes: (1) A copy of the object with an exception ("except it's 7/7") is not built. (2) The defining line must be the card's own ability; a CDA granted to a token is a token definition with a numeric P/T and is refused.

### "~'s power is equal to the number of creatures you control." (power only, printed */N)

- CR: 604.3, 208.2a, 613.4a
- Gap class: P (a `characteristicPt` that defines only one stat)
- Code: as above
- AST node: the face has `power: '*'` and `toughness: 3` and `{ kind: 'characteristicPt', power: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } } }` with no `toughness`. Supported: the toughness is the printed number; likewise "~'s toughness is equal to ..." with a printed `N/*`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pt-cda.test.ts` (3 creatures make a 3/3, it counts itself; losing all abilities leaves 0/3), `ast-pt-validate.test.ts` (power only, toughness only, a CDA for a stat printed as a number is refused).
- Example cards: the wording above. SYNTHETIC fixture: `SYNTHETIC Horde Hound`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 20, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "~'s power is equal to ..." lowers to `characteristicPt { power }`; "~'s toughness is equal to ..." to `characteristicPt { toughness }`; the other stat stays a printed number.
- Notes: (1) Printed `*/1+*` ("Tarmogoyf" style) is `'*'` with a problem in the converter and stays unsupported.

### "All creatures get -X/-X until end of turn." and "Target creature gets +X/+0 until end of turn." (X in a pump)

- CR: 107.3a (X is announced and is the same everywhere), 611.2a and 514.2 (until end of turn), 611.2c (the set and the numbers are fixed when it resolves), 704.5f (toughness 0 or less)
- Gap class: P (an `Amount` where a number was)
- Code: `rules/continuous.ts` (`changeOf`), `rules/effect-support.ts` (`ptAmountOf`), `ast/validate-continuous.ts`
- AST node: spell `x: true`, cost `{X}{B}`, effect `{ kind: 'modifyPt', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'] } }, power: { kind: 'times', factor: -1, of: { kind: 'x' } }, toughness: <the same>, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }`; "+X/+0": `power: { kind: 'x' }, toughness: 0` with a target. An activated ability with {X} in its cost ("{X}{R}: ~ gets +X/+0 until end of turn") is the same node with `objects: { kind: 'this' }` on an `activated` ability.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pt-effects.test.ts` (X = 2 kills toughness 2 on both sides and leaves a 2/4 at 0/2, X = 3, X = 0, it ends at cleanup, the stored effect is `-2/-2`; +X/+0 with a combat; two spells with their own X; the activated {X}{R} ability), `ast-pt-validate.test.ts` (an X with no X in the cost is refused, a multiple needs a whole non-zero factor, `times` is refused in a draw count).
- Example cards: the two wordings above. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Withering Surge`, `Surge Strike`, `Rage Bear`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only; about 91 cards by the D-109 count, with the parser.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 20, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "-X/-X" lowers to `times` factor -1 of `{ kind: 'x' }` for power and toughness; "+X/+0" to `{ kind: 'x' }` and 0; "+X/+X" to `{ kind: 'x' }` for both; "-X/-0" to `times` -1 of x and 0. The spell must set `x: true` and have {X} in its cost (or an activated ability {X} in its cost). Where X is defined by the text ("where X is the number of creatures you control") use the `countOf` in place of `x`; "where X is ..." with any other definition is not read.
- Notes: (1) The numbers are fixed when the effect resolves: the creatures that enter later are not affected and the bonus does not follow the board (CR 611.2c).

### "Creatures you control get +1/+1 until end of turn for each creature you control." and "Target creature has base power and toughness each equal to the number of lands you control until end of turn." (a count in a resolving effect)

- CR: 611.2c and 608.2h (the answer is determined once, when the effect is applied), 608.2c (instructions in order: the count is taken as it runs, after anything put on the stack above it), 613.4b and 613.4c
- Gap class: P
- Code: `rules/continuous.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'modifyPt', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } }, power: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } }, toughness: <the same>, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }`; base values are `{ kind: 'setPt', ..., power: <countOf>, toughness: <countOf> }`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pt-effects.test.ts` (counted as it resolves: three creatures give +3/+3 and the opponent's creature nothing; a creature that dies afterwards does not shrink it, one that enters is not given it; the count is taken after a spell above it resolved; the base values stay when the lands change; the stored change is the number), `ast-pt-validate.test.ts`.
- Example cards: the wordings above. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `SYNTHETIC Mass Rally`, `Land Shape`.
- Cards unlocked: the engine side only.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 20, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: the "for each" and "equal to the number of" amounts of a pump or a base-value effect lower to a `countOf` (or a `times` of one) in the `modifyPt` or `setPt` node, as for a static ability but with no restriction on the filter (a `powerOf` is allowed in an effect).
- Notes: (1) A static ability's count follows the board and an effect's does not: the wording "gets ... until end of turn" is an effect, "gets ..." with no duration on a permanent's own line is a static.

### Not built in this slice

A CDA with an offset ("1+*"), a CDA counting a chosen player's permanents, a static ability that reads X or another object's power, and layer 7d (switch). See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Keyword batch 2 (D-116, D-120)

Engine slice of 2026-10-22 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 21, shapes in D-120). Example cards: wordings from D-116; no real card was looked up for this slice (no network), so no count is claimed. SYNTHETIC fixtures are in `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-keywords-2.ts`. Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after this one) to read its other abilities. AI soak: `packages/ai/test/keyword-batch2-games.test.ts` (100 seeded games in the `keywordBatch2` deck style, replay hashed; no other deck style holds these cards, so older seeded games are unchanged).

### "Skulk"

- CR: 702.118a-c (Skulk), 509.1b
- Gap class: K (an evasion keyword read by the block legality check)
- Code: `rules/combat-legality.ts` (`evades`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'skulk' } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. A creature with greater power than the attacker's can't be declared as a blocker for it; the engine refuses the block and the legal actions never offer it. Power is read as blockers are declared (counters and pumps count); a block already declared stays when power changes later. Equal or lower power may block. Two instances are redundant (CR 702.118c). It can be granted (`GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS`).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-combat.test.ts` (greater, equal, less power; counters; a shrink spell before blockers; refusal and legal actions; two instances), AI soak (no blocker with greater power is ever offered). Mutation-checked: `>` read as `>=` fails 5 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Skulk" (alone or in a keyword line, reminder text in parentheses dropped) lowers to the keyword above. "Creatures you control have skulk" and similar grants lower to the existing `grantKeyword` with `{ kind: 'skulk' }`.
- Notes: The comparison uses derived power, so a creature with no power (a noncreature that is somehow a blocker) counts as 0.

### "Battle cry"

- CR: 702.91a-b (Battle cry), 611.2c, 608.2c
- Gap class: K (expands in `derive` to a triggered ability)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`BATTLE_CRY_ABILITY`), filter `attacking` in `rules/select.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'battleCry' } }`, which derives `triggered { trigger: attacks this, effect: modifyPt { each { attacking, other }, +1/+0, untilEndOfTurn } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision; the trigger goes on the stack as attackers are declared. The attacking creatures other than itself are read as it resolves and then fixed (CR 611.2c): a creature that left combat is not pumped, one put onto the battlefield attacking later is not either. The battle cry creature may be gone when it resolves. Two instances on one creature trigger twice, and two creatures with battle cry pump each other (CR 702.91b).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-combat.test.ts` (others only, alone, two creatures, two instances, source gone, set fixed); AI soak (every other attacker gets exactly +1 power). Mutation-checked: dropping `other` fails 4 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Battle cry" (a keyword line; reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above. The spelled-out ability "Whenever this creature attacks, each other attacking creature gets +1/+0 until end of turn." is already the trigger and effect above and needs no keyword.
- Notes: The filter `attacking: true` is new (an object filter read by `select`); it is refused in static abilities (a static count of attackers would need layer-7 dependencies, D-115).

### "Mentor"

- CR: 702.134a-b (Mentor), 603.3d, 608.2b
- Gap class: K (expands to a triggered ability with a target)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`MENTOR_ABILITY`), filters `attacking` and `powerLessThanSource` (`rules/select.ts`, `rules/filter-match.ts`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'mentor' } }`, which derives `triggered { attacks this, targets: [t1: creature, attacking, powerLessThanSource], effect: addCounters t1 +1/+1 }`.
- Decision and action: A `targets` decision when more than one attacking creature has less power than the mentor; one legal target is chosen by the engine and none removes the ability (D-107 rules for forced choices, CR 603.3d). The target is checked again on resolution against the mentor's power then, or its last-known power if it is gone (CR 608.2b, 113.7a): a target that has grown to equal or more power is illegal and the ability fizzles.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-combat.test.ts` (not itself, not equal, not at home; one and no target; mentor shrinks or is gone; target grows; the legal targets listed are exactly the accepted ones); AI soak (every mentor target attacks and has less power). Mutation-checked: dropping `powerLessThanSource` fails 6 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Mentor" (a keyword line) lowers to the keyword above. "Put a +1/+1 counter on target attacking creature with power less than this creature's power" in a trigger of another wording lowers to the same filter `{ types: ['creature'], attacking: true, powerLessThanSource: true }` only when the source is "this creature"; other sources are not read.
- Notes: `powerLessThanSource` is read as the power of the source of the ability, so it is only valid in a target filter of a triggered or activated ability, refused elsewhere.

### "Evolve"

- CR: 702.100a-d (Evolve), 603.4, 113.7a
- Gap class: K (expands to a triggered ability with an intervening if)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`EVOLVE_ABILITY`), `rules/conditions.ts` (`subjectGreaterPt`), `rules/trigger-subject.ts`, `rules/triggers.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'evolve' } }`, which derives `triggered { entersBattlefield { creature, you, other }, interveningIf: subjectGreaterPt, effect: addCounters this +1/+1 }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. When a creature enters under the controller's control, the trigger is put on the stack only if its power or its toughness is greater than the evolving creature's (CR 702.100a "and/or"); the "if" is read again as the ability resolves, the entering creature as it is now or as it last was (CR 603.4, 113.7a), the evolving creature as it is now. Two instances trigger separately and the second reads the first's counter (CR 702.100d). An opponent's creature and the creature itself do not trigger it.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-combat.test.ts` (bigger, equal or smaller, either stat alone, opponent's, itself, recheck on resolution, a second creature entering while the first trigger waits, two instances). Mutation-checked: removing the intervening if fails 4 tests; dropping `other` is an equivalent mutant (a creature is never greater than itself).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Evolve" (a keyword line) lowers to the keyword above. The spelled-out text is not lowered: it needs the condition `subjectGreaterPt`, which the grammar does not read yet.
- Notes: `triggerSubject` is kept on the stack object so the condition can be read on resolution (`rules/trigger-subject.ts`).

### "Bushido 1"

- CR: 702.45a-b (Bushido), 509.1g, 509.1h
- Gap class: K (expands to two triggered abilities)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`bushidoAbilities`), `rules/triggers.ts` (occurrences for `blocks` and `becomesBlocked`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'bushido', count: 1 } }` (`count` at least 1), which derives two triggers, `blocks this` and `becomesBlocked this`, each with `modifyPt this +N/+N untilEndOfTurn`. The trigger kinds `blocks` and `becomesBlocked` take a `what: TriggerSubject` as `attacks` does and are available to other cards.
- Decision and action: No decision. The triggers are found in the `blockersDeclared` event: `blocks` once for each creature declared as a blocker, `becomesBlocked` once for each blocked attacker, however many creatures block it (CR 509.1h). They resolve before damage, so first strike and regular damage see the bonus. Two instances give +2/+2 (CR 702.45b).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-combat.test.ts` (blocking, becoming blocked once with several blockers, unblocked and non-blocking creatures, other creatures, two instances, both roles in one combat); AI soak (every bushido trigger accounts for one blocker or blocked attacker). Mutation-checked: counting a blocked attacker once for each blocker fails 1 test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Bushido N" (N a number, at least 1; reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword with `count: N`. "Whenever this creature blocks or becomes blocked, it gets +N/+N until end of turn" is the two triggers above; "Whenever ~ blocks" alone lowers to `blocks`, "Whenever ~ becomes blocked" to `becomesBlocked`.
- Notes: `blocks` and `becomesBlocked` take a `TriggerSubject` like `attacks`; the grammar reads only the `this` forms for now.

### "Extort"

- CR: 702.101a-b (Extort), 603.3b, 119.3, 405.5, 107.4e
- Gap class: K (expands to a triggered ability with a may-pay)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`EXTORT_ABILITY`), `rules/effects.ts` (`mayPay`), `rules/pay-unless.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts` (`lifeLostThisWay`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'extort' } }`, which derives `triggered { castSpell { stackKind: spell } by you, effect: mayPay { cost: [{W/B}], effect: sequence [ loseLife eachOpponent 1, gainLife you lifeLostThisWay ] } }`. The effect `mayPay` is new and reusable: "You may pay [cost]. If you do, [effect]."
- Decision and action: The spell goes on the stack and the trigger above it, so it resolves first (CR 405.5). The controller is asked `confirmOptional` (purpose `'mayPay'`); accepting opens the usual `payMana` decision (purpose `'mayPay'`), which `cancel` backs out of (not paying does nothing). {W/B} is paid with a white or a black mana (CR 107.4e). Each opponent loses 1 life and the controller gains the life actually lost. Two instances trigger separately, each with its own question (CR 702.101b); the question is not asked when nothing can pay it. Every spell the controller casts triggers it, creature spells too, and the extort creature does not trigger itself being cast.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-extort.test.ts` (ask, pay, decline, cancel, hybrid payment, `payManual`, legal actions agree with `applyAction`, opponents, itself, two instances, two creatures, nothing to pay with, life gained equals life lost). Mutation-checked: a decline that still runs the effect fails 5 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Extort" (a keyword line) lowers to the keyword above. "You may pay {W/B}. If you do, ..." in any other trigger lowers to `mayPay` with the printed cost and the effect after "If you do"; "each opponent loses 1 life and you gain life equal to the total life lost this way" is the sequence above (the amount `lifeLostThisWay` is valid only after a `loseLife` in the same effect).
- Notes: The AI pays when it has spare mana after what it plans to cast (`CasualAgent`).

### "Affinity for artifacts"

- CR: 702.41a-b (Affinity), 601.2f, 118.7, 113.6d
- Gap class: K (expands to a static cost reduction of the spell itself)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`affinityAbility`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`reductionFor` reads `expandKeywords`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'affinity', filter: { types: ['artifact'] } } }` (a filter of battlefield permanents the caster controls; `controller` other than you, a zone other than the battlefield, `attacking`, `powerLessThanSource`, `owner` and `stackKind` are refused), which derives `static costReduction { spells: this, amount: countOf { filter, controller: you } }`.
- Decision and action: No decision. {1} less of the generic part for each matching permanent the caster controls as the cost is determined (CR 601.2f); colored mana is not reduced (CR 118.7a) and the cost never goes below its colored part. The artifacts are not tapped. The reduced cost shows in the view of the card in hand (D-095) and the legal actions offer the spell exactly when it can be paid. Two instances each apply (CR 702.41b). An artifact creature with affinity counts itself once on the battlefield, not while being cast.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-keywords2-affinity.test.ts` (reduction, no tapping, colored mana, floor, legal actions, view, opponent's artifacts, two instances with cancel, itself); AI soak (the cost in the view equals the printed cost less the count). Mutation-checked: dropping `controller: you` fails 1 test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Affinity for artifacts" lowers to the keyword with filter `{ types: ['artifact'] }`; "Affinity for [subtype]" (such as "Affinity for Plains" or "Affinity for Equipment") to `{ subtypes: [..] }` (the grammar's own name-to-filter reading, as for "for each" counts, D-115); "Affinity for artifact creatures" to `{ types: ['artifact', 'creature'] }` both required, read as the existing type filter does.
- Notes: A cost reduction with a count of something other than permanents is not affinity.

### "{1}{B}: Regenerate ~."

- CR: 701.19a-c (Regenerate), 704.5g, 704.5h, 614.8, 506.4
- Gap class: P (a new effect, a replacement shield, read in two places)
- Code: `rules/regenerate.ts`, `rules/effects.ts` (`destroy`), `rules/sba.ts`, `view.ts` (`regenerationShields`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'regenerate', objects: ObjectSelector, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }` as an ability's or spell's effect; ability: `activated { cost: [mana 1, B], effect: regenerate this untilEndOfTurn }`.
- Decision and action: Resolving makes one shield per permanent (a `ContinuousEffect` with change `regenerate`, until end of turn). The next time the permanent would be destroyed (the `destroy` effect, lethal damage, deathtouch) nothing is destroyed: damage is removed, it is tapped, removed from combat if attacking or blocking (a blocker regenerating in the first-strike step deals no damage after), a `regenerated` event is published and the shield is used up. A second destruction the same turn is not replaced unless another shield exists. Not replaced: sacrifice, 0 toughness, the legend rule, exile. The shield ends with the turn.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-regenerate.test.ts` (lethal damage, deathtouch, destroy effects and "destroy all", a shield spell, one shield used once, two shields, the shield ends with the turn and belongs to the permanent, sacrifice, 0 toughness and the legend rule not replaced, a blocker regenerating in the first-strike step, tapped status, legal actions, determinism); AI: `casual-keyword-batch2.test.ts` (activates in response to a destroy spell and in combat when it would lose, once, not for burn that cannot kill it). Mutation-checked: the state-based action not reading the shield fails 6 tests; the AI activating while a shield exists fails 1 test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "{1}{B}: Regenerate ~." (`~` or the card name or "this creature") lowers to an `activated` ability with the cost parsed as costs and effect `regenerate` on `{ kind: 'this' }`, duration `untilEndOfTurn`. "Regenerate" with another cost shape ("{B}: ...", "Sacrifice..." and so on) lowers the same way. "Regenerate each creature you control." to `{ kind: 'each', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } }`.
- Notes: The wording on a card has no duration: "this turn" is understood.

### "Regenerate target creature." and "Destroy target creature. It can't be regenerated."

- CR: 701.19a, 701.19c (Regenerate), 614.8
- Gap class: P (a spell effect, and a flag on `destroy`)
- Code: `rules/regenerate.ts`, `rules/effects.ts` (`destroy` with `cantBeRegenerated`)
- AST node: spell effect `regenerate { objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }`; `destroy { objects, cantBeRegenerated: true }`.
- Decision and action: The first creates a shield on the target (a target no longer a permanent on the battlefield is skipped, CR 608.2b). The second destroys the target even if it has a shield; the shield is not used up and stays on the permanent (CR 701.19c). A destroy without the flag is replaced by a shield.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-regenerate.test.ts` ("It can't be regenerated" kills the creature and leaves the shield; activating under it is allowed; the shield spell then Murder). Mutation-checked: ignoring `cantBeRegenerated` fails 1 test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Regenerate target creature." lowers to the spell above with a creature target. "Destroy target creature. It can't be regenerated." (also "Destroy target creature. It can't be regenerated this turn", "Destroy all creatures. They can't be regenerated.") lowers to one `destroy` with `cantBeRegenerated: true`, the second sentence attached to the first and not a separate effect.
- Notes: A static "creatures can't be regenerated" is not built.

### "Convoke"

- CR: 702.51a-d (Convoke), 601.2f-h, 302.6
- Gap class: S-lite (a cost-payment step of casting; D-120)
- Code: `rules/pay-options.ts`, `rules/cast.ts` (stage `payOptions`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`canPaySpellCost` with virtual sources), `mana/payment.ts` (`genericOnly` sources), `legal-actions.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'convoke' } }`. Decision `choosePayOptions { source, mechanism: 'convoke', cost, candidates, colors, maxUsable, suggested, choices }`, action `choosePayOptions { objects }`.
- Decision and action: After the targets and before the mana, when at least one untapped creature the caster controls could pay (a creature of the color of a colored symbol, or any creature for generic mana; summoning-sick creatures may, CR 302.6). Each creature tapped pays one mana; the engine works out which, colors first; an answer that leaves a cost nothing can pay is refused and the question stays open; an empty list pays all in mana; `cancel` rewinds the cast (CR 733). The cost paid for is the total after reductions and increases and the commander tax. The legal actions offer the spell exactly when the creatures make it payable, and a creature that could also tap for mana counts once.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pay-options.test.ts` (over all three keywords: candidates, colors, hybrid, commander tax, refusal, cancel, partial use, planner agreement, determinism); AI: `casual-keyword-batch2.test.ts` (keeps attackers untapped on its own turn, does not tap mana creatures when an equivalent one exists), soak (the cost left equals the cost less the number chosen). Mutation-checked: tapped creatures allowed fails 3, any color pays colored mana fails 6, tapping skipped fails 4.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Convoke" (a keyword line, reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above.
- Notes: {C} and {S} cost symbols are not paid by convoke and stay in the cost.

### "Improvise"

- CR: 702.126a-c (Improvise), 601.2f-h
- Gap class: S-lite (the same step as Convoke)
- Code: as Convoke; `mechanism: 'improvise'`.
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'improvise' } }`.
