- Decision and action: As Convoke, with untapped artifacts the caster controls, paying generic mana only (never a colored symbol). Artifacts are tapped, not sacrificed; a Sol Ring is listed but counted once by the planner. A creature that is also an artifact is tapped once and is not offered to improvise after it convoked.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pay-options.test.ts` (artifacts pay the generic part only, five artifacts refused for four generic, tapped artifacts not offered, Sol Ring counted once, both keywords on one card). Mutation-checked with the Convoke ones above.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Improvise" lowers to the keyword above.
- Notes: As for Convoke.

### "Delve"

- CR: 702.66a-c (Delve), 601.2f-h, 400.2
- Gap class: S-lite (the same step as Convoke)
- Code: as Convoke; `mechanism: 'delve'`.
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'delve' } }`.
- Decision and action: As Convoke, with cards in the caster's graveyard, paying generic mana only. The cards chosen are exiled as the cost is paid (public cards, new objects, the opponent sees them in exile); `cancel` puts them back. Fewer cards than the generic mana, or none, is allowed when the rest can be paid. The opponent's graveyard is not offered. The AI exiles lands, instants and sorceries before creatures and other permanents.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-pay-options.test.ts` (listing, exile, partial, opponent's graveyard, legal actions, public exile, cancel, order of the three questions); AI: `casual-keyword-batch2.test.ts` (graveyard order of exile).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Delve" lowers to the keyword above.
- Notes: "As an additional cost, exile N cards from your graveyard" is an additional cost, not delve.

### "Cascade"

- CR: 702.85a-c (Cascade), 603.3b, 608.2g, 118.9, 202.3e, 107.3b, 903.9b
- Gap class: P (a new effect; the free cast of D-101 during a resolution)
- Code: `rules/cascade.ts`, `rules/cascade-cast.ts`, `rules/cast.ts` (`beginCast` with `resolving`), `rules/effects.ts`, `api.ts` (`resumeResolution`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`CASCADE_ABILITY`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'cascade' } }`, which derives `triggered { castSpell { stackKind: spell } by you self: true, effect: cascade }`. The effect `cascade` takes no fields and is valid only in the cascade trigger (`validateCard` refuses it in spell text).
- Decision and action: When the spell is cast the trigger goes on the stack above it (CR 603.3b). Resolving exiles cards from the top of the library one at a time (each a public zone change) until a nonland card with a lower mana value than the spell's (X as announced on the stack, 0 in the library, CR 202.3e) is exiled; the library running out ends it. If it could be cast (a legal target for each target, additional costs payable) the controller is asked `confirmOptional` (purpose `'cascade'`); yes casts it as CR 608.2g says (steps of CR 601.2a-i, no priority, the cascade ability goes on resolving, X = 0, `StackData.byEffect`), no or an uncastable card goes on. Then every exiled card not cast goes to the bottom of the library in a random order from the seeded generator; only `cardsBottomed { seat, count }` is reported. A commander card is offered the command zone first (CR 903.9b). Only the front face is cast.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-cascade.test.ts` (the trigger above the spell and only while it is on the stack, the first lower nonland, an equal value passed over, a library of lands or empty, decline, an uncastable card, targets and cancel, the free cast as topmost object with no priority first, the bottom in a seeded random order with only a count reported, public exile, last-known mana value, two instances, a cascade card cast by cascade, legal actions, a commander, determinism); AI: `casual-keyword-batch2.test.ts` (accepts), soak (the cast spell is cheaper, no mana added, bottom order not leaked). Mutation-checked: `<` read as `<=` fails 1 test; the AI declining fails 1 test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 21, 2026-10-22
- What the parser must produce: "Cascade" (a keyword line, reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above. The long form "When you cast this spell, exile cards from the top of your library until ..." is not lowered separately.
- Notes: Several instances trigger separately (CR 702.85c). A cascade card that exiles a split card or the back face of a double-faced card casts only the front face (`docs/ROADMAP.md`).

### Not built in this slice

Morph and Ninjutsu, convoke for {C} and {S}, a static "can't be regenerated", cascade of a non-front face, and the other D-120 limits listed in `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Named alternative costs: Overload, Evoke, Dash (D-117, D-121)

Engine slice of 2026-10-23 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 22, shapes in D-121). Real wordings and counts are from `reports/backlog.md` and `reports/templates.json` (oracle_cards 2026-10-22): `Overload {M}` 26 cards (12 unlocked by the line alone), `Evoke {M}` 30 cards (17), `Dash {M}` 22 cards (15); the three are lines of the 355-card "Alternative costs of a spell" gap of D-117, whose other members (Blitz, Bestow, Spectacle, Madness, Miracle, ...) are later steps. SYNTHETIC fixtures are in `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-alt-costs.ts`. Cards unlocked: the engine side only; each card needs the parser (a stage after this one) to read its other abilities. AI soak: `packages/ai/test/alt-cost-games.test.ts` (100 seeded games in the `altCosts` deck style, replay hashed; no other deck style holds these cards, so older seeded games are unchanged); heuristics: `packages/ai/test/casual-alt-costs.test.ts`.

All three are entries of `CardFace.alternativeCosts` (a list, at most one of each kind): `{ kind: 'overload' | 'evoke' | 'dash', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols }], condition? }`. The keyword line is not an ability; the parser lowering declares `needs: ['castAlternative:<kind>']` (`isFeatureSupported`). The engine lists one `castSpell { object, alternative }` option for each alternative whose cost (plus commander tax and additional costs, minus reductions, CR 118.9d) can be paid, beside the ordinary cast; the alternative is chosen with the action (CR 601.2b) and recorded on the stack (`StackData.alt`, `spellCast.alternative`, `ObjectView.alternative`); `ObjectView.alternativeCosts` shows each cost for a card in hand.

### "Overload {6}{U}"

- CR: 702.96a-c (Overload), 118.9, 118.9a, 118.9d, 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 612, 702.11b
- Gap class: K (an alternative cost with a text change)
- Code: `ast/overload.ts` (`overloadedText`, `overloadIssue`), `rules/legality.ts` (`targetSpecsOf`, `targetsPossible`, `alternativeCostAllowed`, `alternativeCastObjects`), `rules/stack.ts` (`resolveTop`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`spellCost`, `alternativeCostsOf`), `rules/cast.ts`, `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'overload', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['6', 'U'] }] }]` on an instant or sorcery whose spell text names its targets. The text change is not data: the engine replaces each object `target` selector of the effect (`objects` and `to` of an effect) by `each` with the filter of that target slot, on the battlefield, and drops the slots.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object, alternative: 'overload' }`; no targets decision follows (CR 702.96b), the cost is paid, the spell resolves as the same spell with "each" for "target": it does as much as it can to every object that matches, whether or not it could have been chosen (hexproof, shroud, protection do not matter; nothing "becomes the target", so Ward does not trigger); it cannot fizzle. It is offered even when the ordinary cast is not (no legal target), and not when its own cost cannot be paid. A spell with no matching objects still resolves.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-overload.test.ts` (both options and only one of them, no target, several targets and none of the caster's, a filter without a controller reaching the caster's own creatures, a two-instruction text, bounce, hexproof and shroud, the only creature leaving in response, cost paid and view, cancel, counterspell, commander tax, timing, validation refusals, the pure text change, determinism and replay); AI soak and `casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: the slot filter dropped from `each` fails 5 tests, a spell that still asks for targets 16, an overload offered only with a legal single target 2, the mana cost paid instead of the overload cost 4, the condition of the alternative cost ignored 1, the printed text resolved instead of the changed one 6; the AI never overloading fails 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 22, 2026-10-23
- Web: the card's menu lists one button per option, "Cast X" and "Overload {3}{R}{R}" (the cost is `ObjectView.alternativeCosts`, after tax and reductions); the spell on the stack carries an "overloaded" badge (hover: "target" reads "each", no targets) and the preview panel says "Cast for its overload cost"; the log reads "You cast X for its overload cost." No targets question follows. Code: `ui/decisions/alt-cost.ts` (`alternativeLabel`), `ui/decisions/flashback.ts` (`castLabel`), `game/alt-cost.ts` (`alternativeBadge`, `alternativeLogWords`), `game/describe.ts`. Tests: `apps/web/test/alt-costs-model.test.ts`, `alt-costs-host.test.ts`, `e2e/alt-costs.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-overload`).
- What the parser must produce: `Overload {6}{U}` (a keyword line; the reminder text in parentheses, `(You may cast this spell for its overload cost. If you do, change "target" in its text to "each.")`, is dropped; Cyclonic Rift in `packages/cards/test/fixtures/corpus/golden.jsonl` reads "Return target nonland permanent you don't control to its owner's hand." and then that line) lowers to the entry above, with the spell text lowered as usual from the ordinary wording ("Return target nonland permanent you don't control to its owner's hand."). The word "each" is never read from the text. A card whose spell text the engine cannot overload (modal, a player or "any target" slot, a target read for an amount, no target at all) is unsupported with the reason `overloadIssue` gives.
- Notes: Real cards with a second target mention or a follow-up instruction ("For each creature exiled this way, its controller searches ...", Winds of Abandon) need the parser and engine support for that text on its own.

### "Evoke {2}{U}"

- CR: 702.74a (Evoke), 118.9, 118.9a, 118.9d, 603.3, 603.3b, 701.21, 400.7, 700.4, 603.6c
- Gap class: K (an alternative cost with a trigger the engine adds)
- Code: `rules/derive-alt.ts` (`EVOKE_ABILITY`, `alternativeAbilitiesOf`), `rules/derive.ts`, `rules/zones.ts` (`GameObject.alt`), `rules/cast-cost.ts`, `rules/legality.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'evoke', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2', 'U'] }] }]` on a permanent card. The sacrifice trigger is not data: a permanent that entered from an evoked spell has (`GameObject.alt = 'evoke'`) the triggered ability `entersBattlefield this: sacrifice this`.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object, alternative: 'evoke' }` (the creature's own timing, from the hand or the command zone). The spell resolves, the creature enters, and the sacrifice trigger goes on the stack with the creature's other enters triggers: a controller with several non-identical triggers orders them (`orderTriggers`), so the other enters ability resolves before or after the sacrifice as chosen. While the trigger is on the stack the creature is on the battlefield and either player may respond (bounce it: the trigger does nothing and a creature cast again is a new object that was not evoked; take away its abilities: the trigger is already on the stack and still sacrifices). Sacrificing it makes its "dies" and "leaves the battlefield" abilities trigger.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-evoke.test.ts` (options, cost and view, tax, sorcery timing, zones that cannot cast it, the trigger on the stack and a response, a bounce in response, loses all abilities in response, ordering with another enters ability and with a targeted one, dies and leaves abilities, a commander, validation refusals, determinism and replay); AI soak and `casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: the sacrifice trigger skipped fails 11 tests, the permanent never remembering it 12, the flag surviving a trip through the hand 11, a sacrifice of any permanent instead of itself 10; commander tax dropped for alternative costs fails 3 (one in each of the three files); the AI never evoking fails 2.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 22, 2026-10-23
- Web: "Evoke {1}{U}" is a button beside "Cast X"; when it is the only option (the ordinary cast cannot be paid) a click still opens the menu, so the card is never evoked unasked. The log reads "You cast X for its evoke cost."; the spell on the stack has an "evoked" badge and the permanent a flag "evoked" (hover: "Evoked: sacrificed when it enters.", also in the preview panel and the tile's accessible name). The sacrifice trigger is on the stack as "Whenever X enters, sacrifice this creature" (the ordinary trigger wording). Tests: `alt-costs-host.test.ts`, `e2e/alt-costs.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-evoke`).
- What the parser must produce: `Evoke {2}{U}` (also `Evoke {1}{B}`, reminder text dropped) lowers to the entry above; the other abilities of the card (Mulldrifter's "When this creature enters, draw two cards.") lower as usual. `Evoke—Exile a white card from your hand.` (Solitude, Fury, Grief, Endurance, Subtlety) has a cost that is not mana: refuse it (`validateCard` does).
- Notes: There is no `evoked` condition node; a card that asks "if it was evoked" needs one added. A creature whose control changes before the trigger resolves is not sacrificed (`docs/ROADMAP.md`).

### "Dash {1}{R}"

- CR: 702.109a (Dash), 118.9, 118.9a, 118.9d, 603.7, 603.7c, 603.7d, 603.7e, 702.10, 302.6, 400.7, 903.9b
- Gap class: K plus S-light (the first delayed triggered ability)
- Code: `rules/derive-alt.ts` (`DASH_HASTE_ABILITY`, `DASH_RETURN_ABILITY`), `rules/stack.ts` (the creation), `rules/triggers.ts` (`findDelayedTriggers`, `collectTriggers`), `state/types.ts` (`DelayedTrigger`, `GameState.delayedTriggers`), `rules/zones.ts`, `rules/setup.ts`, `testing/assemble.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'dash', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1', 'R'] }] }]` on a creature card. Haste and the return are not data: a dashed permanent (`GameObject.alt = 'dash'`) has the keyword `haste`, and the resolving spell adds to `GameState.delayedTriggers` the ability "at the beginning of the next end step, return this to its owner's hand" for the permanent it became.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object, alternative: 'dash' }` (sorcery timing for a creature). The creature enters with haste and can attack at once. At the beginning of the first end step after it entered (either player's) the delayed trigger goes on the stack, where it can be responded to; resolving it returns the creature to its owner's hand if it is the same permanent (a commander asks the owner for the command zone, CR 903.9b). A creature that died or was bounced earlier is not returned and the trigger does nothing; one cast again is a new object (CR 603.7c). A countered dash spell creates no trigger. A creature that lost its abilities (Frog Hex) loses haste but is still returned (CR 603.7e).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-dash.test.ts` (options, cost and view, tax, timing, haste and attack, return at the end step with the trigger on the stack, once only, cast after combat, an enters ability each time, died, bounced, bounced and cast again, countered, loses all abilities, a commander, validation refusals, determinism and replay); AI soak and `casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: the delayed trigger never created fails 7 tests, no haste 2, the delayed trigger never removed (returns every turn) 3, the creature returned to the graveyard 6; the AI dashing into untapped blockers fails 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 22, 2026-10-23
- Web: "Dash {1}{R}" is a button beside "Cast X" (asked about even when it is the only option, as for Evoke); the log reads "You cast X for its dash cost."; badges "dashed" on the spell and a flag "dashed" on the permanent (hover: it has haste and returns to its owner's hand at the beginning of the next end step). The delayed trigger has `abilityIndex` -1 and no printed text; the stack shows it as "At the beginning of each end step, return this creature to its owner's hand" (`triggerCondition` and `triggerGist` read the ability's AST, `moveToZone` to the hand is worded for it and for bounce effects; no code reads `abilityTriggered.abilityIndex`). A dashed commander returned at the end step asks "Move X to the command zone?" like any commander (the autopilot stops at that question); Undo takes the dash cast back before the turn is passed, and at the question the history is already empty. Tests: `alt-costs-host.test.ts`, `e2e/alt-costs.e2e.ts` (hidden decks `synthetic-dash`, `synthetic-dash-commander`).
- What the parser must produce: `Dash {1}{R}` (reminder text dropped) lowers to the entry above; Ragavan, Zurgo Bellstriker, Goblin Heelcutter and the other dash cards need their own abilities read as well.
- Notes: The first delayed trigger exists to serve Dash; other delayed abilities (Blitz's draw and sacrifice, Unearth, "at the beginning of the next end step, sacrifice it") would reuse `DelayedTrigger` with another event and effect.

### Not built in this slice

Evoke and other alternative costs with a cost that is not mana, Overload on modal text, Blitz, Bestow, Miracle and the casts from other zones of D-117, and the limits listed in `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Named alternative costs: Spectacle, Escape, Unearth (D-117, D-124)

Engine slice of 2026-10-23, step 2 of D-117 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 23, shapes in D-124). Real wordings and counts are from `reports/backlog.md` and `reports/templates.json` (oracle_cards 2026-10-22): `Spectacle {M}` 11 cards (4 unlocked by the line alone), `Escape—{M}, Exile N other cards from your graveyard.` 30 cards (6), `Unearth {M}` 55 cards (31), and `When ~ enters, sacrifice it unless it escaped.` 3 cards (Uro, Kroxa, Phlage). The shapes file keeps the reminder text out, so none is quoted here. SYNTHETIC fixtures: `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-alt-costs.ts`. AI soak: `packages/ai/test/alt-cost-games.test.ts` (the `altCosts` deck style holds the new cards, so no older seeded game changed); heuristics: `packages/ai/test/casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. The new nodes are `SUPPORTED_FEATURES.castAlternatives` `spectacle` and `escape`, `keywordKinds` and `effectKinds` `unearth`, `conditionKinds` `escaped`; a lowering declares `castAlternative:spectacle`, `castAlternative:escape`, `keyword:unearth` and `condition:escaped`.

### "Spectacle {R}"

- CR: 702.137a (Spectacle), 118.9, 118.9a, 118.9d, 119.3, 119.4, 120.3a, 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 102.2
- Gap class: K plus a record in the state (`SeatState.lifeLostThisTurn`)
- Code: `state/ops.ts` (`loseLifeBy`), `rules/flow.ts` (the reset in `startTurn`), `rules/legality.ts` (`opponentLostLifeThisTurn`, `alternativeCostAllowed`), `rules/damage.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts`, `rules/activate-costs.ts`, `rules/cast.ts`, `rules/mana-abilities.ts`, `rules/mana-engine.ts`, `rules/pay-unless.ts`, `rules/zones.ts` (every life loss goes through `loseLifeBy`), `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'spectacle', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['R'] }] }]` on a card that is not a land. The condition is not data: the engine reads it at the first casting step.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object, alternative: 'spectacle' }`, offered beside the ordinary cast only while an opponent still in the game has lost life this turn (combat damage, damage from a spell, "loses life", life paid for a cost or by a mana ability, all count; gaining life, damage that is prevented or dealt as poison, life the caster lost and life lost by a player who left the game do not) and the spectacle cost, commander tax and additional costs can be paid. The record is per player and per game turn: life lost in an opponent's turn counts for a spell cast in that turn, and every seat's record is reset as each turn begins. Timing is the spell's own (a creature stays sorcery speed, an instant an instant).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-spectacle.test.ts` (no option before any loss; after a Bolt, after combat damage, after "each opponent loses 1 life", after an opponent paid life as a cost; blocked damage and gaining life do not count; the caster's own loss does not count while the opponent of the one who lost may use it; reset at the next turn; opponent's turn; commander tax; sorcery and instant timing; cost and view; validation refusals for a land, a non-mana cost, X and a second spectacle; an action naming the option without the condition is refused; determinism and replay); AI soak and `casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: the condition never read fails 10 tests, damage to a player not recorded 10, "loses life" not recorded 1, life paid not recorded 1, the caster's own loss counted 1, no reset at the turn 1, the mana cost paid instead of the spectacle cost 11; the AI never choosing it fails 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 23, 2026-10-23
- Web: "Spectacle {1}{R}" is a button in the card's menu, only while the engine lists the option (an opponent has lost life this turn; the web reads no life total, it shows what the engine offers), and it is asked about even when it is the only option, as for Evoke and Dash; a card that cannot be cast any other way is not clickable before the life loss. The log reads "You cast X for its spectacle cost."; the spell on the stack has a "spectacle" badge (hover: an opponent lost life this turn) and the preview panel says "Cast for its spectacle cost"; the permanent has no flag (it does not remember the cost). `SeatView.lifeLostThisTurn` is not shown. Code: `game/alt-cost.ts` (`alternativeBadge`, `alternativeLogWords`), `ui/decisions/alt-cost.ts` (`alternativeLabel`). Tests: `apps/web/test/alt-costs2-model.test.ts`, `alt-costs2-host.test.ts`, `e2e/alt-costs2.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-spectacle`).
- What the parser must produce: `Spectacle {R}` (also `Spectacle {1}{R}`, `Spectacle {B}`; a keyword line, reminder text dropped) lowers to the entry above, declaring `castAlternative:spectacle` and `cost:mana`. The other abilities of the card (Light Up the Stage, Skewer the Critics, Rix Maadi Reveler) lower as usual; the word "spectacle" is never read from anywhere but this line.
- Notes: The engine reads "an opponent" as a player still in the game (`opponentsOf`); in a two-player game that is the other seat.

### "Escape—{G}{G}{U}{U}, Exile five other cards from your graveyard."

- CR: 702.138a-d (Escape), 118.9, 118.9a, 118.9d, 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 903.8, 614.12, 400.7, 733
- Gap class: K plus S-light (the first cast from the graveyard other than Flashback, the first cost that exiles other cards)
- Code: `rules/legality.ts` (`castZone`, `escapeObjects`, `canCast`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`SpellCost.exile`, `exilable`, `canPaySpellCost`, `alternativeCostsOf`), `rules/cast.ts` and `rules/cast-additional.ts` (stage `'exile'`), `rules/exile-cost.ts`, `api.ts` (the answer), `rules/priority.ts`, `rules/conditions.ts` (`escaped`), `rules/enters.ts` and `rules/zones.ts` (`ifEscaped`), `rules/derive-alt.ts` (`permanentAlternative`), `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'escape', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['G', 'G', 'U', 'U'] }], exile: 5 }]` on the front face of a card that is not a land and has no Delve. Nothing about the permanent is data except what the card says: "sacrifice it unless it escaped" is `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: entersBattlefield this, interveningIf: { kind: 'not', term: { kind: 'escaped' } }, effect: sacrifice this }`, and "This creature escapes with a +1/+1 counter on it" is `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'entersWithCounters', counter: '+1/+1', count: 1, ifEscaped: true } }`; `validateCard` refuses both on a card with no escape cost.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object, alternative: 'escape' }` for a card in its owner's graveyard (the option is listed after flashback). The escape mana, commander tax (none from the graveyard), additional costs and the exile must all be payable, with the card itself not counting among the cards exiled. The cast pays the mana, the sacrifice and the discard, then exiles the other cards: with exactly as many as the cost asks they are exiled without a question; otherwise `ChooseCardsDecision { purpose: 'exile', zone: 'graveyard', min = max = n, forCost: true }` is answered by `chooseCards` (exactly n distinct cards of `legal`); `cancel` rewinds the cast and returns the cards. The spell is an ordinary spell: it resolves or is countered into the graveyard (never exiled), so the card can escape again when enough other cards are there. The permanent has `GameObject.alt = 'escape'` (`ObjectView.alternative`) until it changes zones; from hand or any other cast it has none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-escape.test.ts` (the option with three, two and zero other cards and with the escape mana, not from the hand, exile or an opponent's graveyard, sorcery timing, the view; the question with more cards than needed, no question with exactly enough, the card itself is not offered, wrong answers refused, cancel, public cards; the creature remembers it; escape twice after a death, a sorcery goes to the graveyard and can escape again, a countered one goes to the graveyard; "escapes with" counters only when it escaped and as a replacement, "unless it escaped" for a hand cast and an escape, a bounced and recast permanent did not escape; a commander in the graveyard pays no tax and one in the command zone has no option; validation refusals; determinism and replay); AI soak and `casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: the card itself exilable fails 1 test, one card too few required 15, the spell exiled when it leaves the stack as with flashback 8, "escapes with" counters always 1, the permanent forgetting that it escaped 4, tax paid from the graveyard 1, the other cards never exiled 10, an unchecked answer 2, the `escaped` condition always false 3; castable from the hand fails 1 only when both its guards are removed (the zone test in `castZone` and the graveyard list in `escapeObjects` each cover the other); the AI never escaping fails 1 and exiling the cards it values most 2.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 23, 2026-10-23
- Web: a card in the viewer's graveyard with an `escape` option gets a chip above the hand ("Name Escape {G}{G}{U}{U}, exile 5 other cards", the cost from `ObjectView.alternativeCosts` after reductions and the number from `exile`) and the same text as a badge in the graveyard viewer, whose cards are clickable like any card the engine offers something for; a click opens the menu (a single named alternative is asked about, never cast unasked) and sends `castSpell { object, alternative: 'escape' }`. The question is the `chooseCards` of `purpose: 'exile'`, `zone: 'graveyard'`, `forCost: true`: the dock panel "Exile to cast X" lists the legal cards of the viewer's graveyard (cards of one name are one row with a count, a click adds the next copy), the bar says "Exile 3 other cards from your graveyard to cast X." (the count is the decision's `max`), "Exile" is enabled at exactly the count, and Cancel rewinds the whole cast (the card is in the graveyard again and nothing is exiled). The other seat sees "Opponent is choosing cards to exile from their graveyard (X)." (`waitingOn.purpose: 'exile'`, no card named). The log reads "You cast X for its escape cost." and the exiled cards are the ordinary zone-change lines; the spell has an "escaped" badge and the permanent a flag "escaped" (also in the preview panel and the tile's accessible name). Code: `ui/decisions/flashback.ts` (`graveyardLabels`, `graveyardBadge`), `ui/board/OtherZonesStrip.tsx`, `ui/decisions/cards-picker.ts` (`pickerCards`, `cardsPrompt`, `discardTitle`), `ui/decisions/CardPickerPanel.tsx`, `ui/decisions/alt-cost.ts`, `game/alt-cost.ts`. Tests: `apps/web/test/alt-costs2-model.test.ts`, `alt-costs2-host.test.ts`, `e2e/alt-costs2.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-escape`: the script keeps its lands and lets the end-of-turn discard fill the graveyard).
- What the parser must produce: `Escape—{G}{G}{U}{U}, Exile five other cards from your graveyard.` (the real line of Uro, Titan of Nature's Wrath and the shape's example; the number is a word: one to nine) lowers to the entry above (`cost` the mana symbols, `exile` the number), declaring `castAlternative:escape` and `cost:mana`; `When ~ enters, sacrifice it unless it escaped.` lowers to the triggered ability above, declaring `condition:escaped` (and `condition:not`); an "escapes with" sentence, when a card has one, lowers to the `ifEscaped` replacement (the shapes file holds no real line of it, so the wording is to be taken from the owner's run). `Each nonland card in your graveyard has escape. ...` (Underworld Breach) is not read.
- Notes: A card with Delve and Escape is refused (`validateCard`). "Escapes with [ability]" (CR 702.138d) and a trigger linked to "enters this way" (CR 702.138c) are not built.

### "Unearth {R}"

- CR: 702.84a (Unearth), 602.2, 113.6b, 602.5d, 603.7, 603.7c, 603.7d, 603.7e, 614.6, 400.7, 700.4, 702.10, 903.9a
- Gap class: K plus S-light (the first ability that functions from the graveyard; the second delayed trigger)
- Code: `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`unearthAbility`), `rules/activate-legality.ts` (`activatableAbilities`, `hasGraveyardAbility`), `rules/effects.ts` (the `unearth` effect, the creation of the delayed trigger), `rules/zones.ts` (`MoveRequest.alt`, the exile replacement), `rules/derive-alt.ts` (`UNEARTH_EXILE_ABILITY`, haste), `state/types.ts` (`Instruction` `move.alt`, `GameObject.alt`), `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`Scope.fromGraveyard`), `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`, `invariants.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'unearth', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['R'] }] } }]` on a permanent card (one per face; mana only, no X). The engine expands it into the activated ability `{ cost, effect: { kind: 'unearth' }, timing: 'sorcery', functionsIn: 'graveyard' }`. The effect `unearth` is valid only in an ability that functions from the graveyard.
- Decision and action: `activate { object, abilityIndex }` for the card in its owner's graveyard, at sorcery speed (the owner's turn, a main phase, an empty stack, nothing else). It is an activated ability, not a spell: the card stays in the graveyard while the ability is on the stack and no `spellCast` is emitted. As it resolves, if the card is still the same object in the graveyard it returns to the battlefield as a new object, untapped, under its owner's control (so its enters triggers trigger), with haste and `GameObject.alt = 'unearth'`; a card that left the graveyard in the meantime is not returned. At the beginning of the next end step a delayed triggered ability (on the stack, where it can be responded to) exiles it, even if it lost its abilities; and if it would leave the battlefield in any way (destroyed, bounced, sacrificed, killed in combat) it is exiled instead, so it does not die and a commander so exiled is offered the command zone (CR 903.9a). A card that dies without having been unearthed goes to the graveyard and can be unearthed again; an exiled one cannot.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-unearth.test.ts` (the option from the graveyard only and with the mana, sorcery speed in the opponent's turn, with something on the stack and in combat; the ability on the stack with the card still in the graveyard; the new object with haste, attacking the turn it returns, its enters triggers; cancel; the card exiled in response; the delayed exile at the end step in either main phase, responded to with a bounce, after the creature lost its abilities; "exile instead" for destroy, bounce, combat damage and a creature with no abilities, and its "dies" trigger not triggering; a commander; a creature cast normally dies normally; unearth again after a normal death; validation refusals; determinism and replay); AI soak and `casual-alt-costs.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: no haste fails 3 tests, no delayed exile 4, no "exile instead" 6, not sorcery speed 1, activated from any zone 1, the permanent forgetting it 8, a delayed trigger that exiles nothing 4, the replacement applied to every permanent with a remembered alternative cost (so to dashed and evoked ones) 19 across the unearth, dash and evoke tests; the AI never unearthing fails 2. One mutation survives and is equivalent: dropping the "still in the graveyard" test of the effect changes nothing, because every zone change makes a new object (CR 400.7) and the old id is already gone.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 23, 2026-10-23
- Web: a card in the viewer's graveyard with an `activate` option gets the same chip and badge, "Name Unearth {R}" (the label reads the ability from `ObjectView.activated`: the effect `unearth` is worded "Unearth" plus the cost, as Cycling and Equip are); one click activates it, since it is the only thing to do. It is an ability, not a spell, so the log has no "cast" line and the stack shows the ability under the card's name with its words, now also for an activated ability (`StackPanel` shows the gist of an activated ability, which it did not before): "Return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield. It gains haste. Exile it at the beginning of the next end step. If it would leave the battlefield, exile it instead of putting it anywhere else" (written from D-124). The permanent has the flag "unearthed" (hover: exiled at the beginning of the next end step; if it would leave the battlefield it is exiled instead; also in the preview panel and the tile's accessible name) and the keyword haste, so it can attack the turn it returns. The delayed trigger has `abilityIndex` -1 and no printed text; the stack shows it as "At the beginning of each end step, exile this creature" (the AST's `endStep` of each player, as for Dash, and an `exile` of `this`). A commander exiled by the replacement is asked about the command zone with `from: 'exile'`: "X is in exile. You may put it into the command zone instead." (it read "the exile" before). Tests: `apps/web/test/alt-costs2-model.test.ts`, `alt-costs2-host.test.ts`, `e2e/alt-costs2.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-unearth`: a creature discarded at the end of turn is unearthed, attacks and is exiled at the end step).
- What the parser must produce: `Unearth {R}` (the real line, a keyword line of its own, also `Unearth {2}{B}`; reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above, declaring `keyword:unearth` and `cost:mana`. A card with a different unearth cost (an {X} or a cost that is not mana) is unsupported. The other lines "{M}, {T}: Return target creature card from your graveyard to the battlefield. It gains haste. Exile it at the beginning of the next end step. ..." (one card in the shapes file) are not unearth and are not read.
- Notes: Haste is a derived keyword of the permanent, so a later effect that takes all its abilities away takes the haste too; the exile at the end step and "exile instead" stay (they belong to the delayed trigger and to the object). A copy of an unearthed permanent was not unearthed.

### Not built in this slice

A non-mana cost in Spectacle, Escape or Unearth; two unearth instances on a card; a grant of unearth or escape by another card; "escapes with [ability]" and the trigger linked to it; Blitz, Bestow, Miracle, Encore, Plot and Rebound (D-117); and the limits listed in `docs/ROADMAP.md`.


## Play rules: additional land drops, the top card, the cost of attacking, the optional untap (D-125)

Engine slice of 2026-10-26 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 24, shapes in D-125). Four static abilities that change what a player may do in a turn. Real wordings and counts are from `reports/backlog.md` and `reports/templates.json` (oracle_cards 2026-10-22). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-play-rules.ts` (names start with "SYNTHETIC "; their text describes the AST, not a real card). AI soak: `packages/ai/test/play-rule-games.test.ts` (the `playRules` deck style: 100 whole games, random and CasualAgent seats, every game replayed to the same hash; it asserts each mechanic occurs) and the hidden-information audit in `packages/ai/test/hidden-info.test.ts`. Feature ids: `static:additionalLandDrops`, `static:lookAtTopOfLibrary`, `static:attackTax`, `static:optionalUntap` (`SUPPORTED_FEATURES.staticKinds`).

### "You may play an additional land on each of your turns."

- CR: 305.2, 305.2a, 305.2b, 305.3, 604.1, 613.1f
- Gap class: P (a static ability read where the rule acts)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` and `ast/validate.ts` (`additionalLandDrops`), `rules/statics.ts` (`landPlaysAllowed`), `rules/legality.ts` (`canPlayLand`), `view.ts` (`SeatView.landPlaysAllowed`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'additionalLandDrops', count: 1 } }`; `count: 2` for "two additional lands"; `eachPlayer: true` for "Each player may play an additional land on each of their turns."
- Decision and action: none new. `playLand` stays on offer while the lands played this turn are below `landPlaysAllowed`: 1, plus the extra drops of effects this turn (`turn.extraLandDrops`, for the active player), plus `count` of every battlefield permanent the seat controls with the ability (any permanent with `eachPlayer`). Several add up; the limit follows the board (a source that dies lowers it, one that arrives raises it for the rest of the turn); an opponent's permanent gives nothing; only the active player ever plays a land (CR 305.3).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-additional-land-drops.test.ts`; `packages/ai/test/casual-play-rules.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 24, 2026-10-26
- Web: nothing was needed to play the extra land (the land is clickable whenever the engine lists `playLand`). A seat whose `SeatView.landPlaysAllowed` is above 1 shows a chip "Land plays 1/2" (`landsPlayedThisTurn` over `landPlaysAllowed`, both seats, public) beside the library count; there is no chip at the ordinary single drop, and no log line (the land play is the ordinary "You play Forest."). The permanent says what it does in the preview panel, "Play rules: You may play an additional land on each of your turns." (written from the node by `game/play-rule-text.ts`, see the note under the four sections below). Code: `ui/board/PlayerStrip.tsx`. Tests: `apps/web/test/play-rules-host.test.ts`, `play-rules-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/play-rules.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-extra-land`).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `You may play an additional land on each of your turns.` (17 cards: Exploration, Oracle of Mul Daya, The Gitrog Monster, ...) lowers to the node above, declaring `ability:static` and `static:additionalLandDrops`; `You may play N additional lands on each of your turns.` (Azusa, Lost but Seeking; N a word) to `count: N`; `Each player may play an additional land on each of their turns.` (Rites of Flourishing, Ghirapur Orrery) to `eachPlayer: true`. `You may play an additional land this turn.` (5 cards) is a different thing, an effect of a spell, and is not this node.
- Notes: The CasualAgent casts a permanent that grants a drop before other spells while a land waits in the hand.

### "You may look at the top card of your library any time."

- CR: 401.5 (Library), 401.2, 604.1
- Gap class: P plus a view (hidden information)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` and `ast/validate.ts` (`lookAtTopOfLibrary`), `rules/statics.ts` (`topCardVisibleTo`), `view.ts` (`SeatView.libraryTop`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'lookAtTopOfLibrary' } }`
- Decision and action: none. `getView(state, viewer).seats[viewer].libraryTop` is an `ObjectView` of the top card while the viewer controls a battlefield permanent with the ability (and the library is not empty); it is absent in every other seat's view and in every view without the ability. It is a view only: the state, the events and the action log never hold the card, so nothing is stored or hashed, replays and hashes are unchanged, and the other player can learn it from nothing the engine emits. While a spell is being cast or an ability activated, a top card that changed since the transaction began is not shown until it is done (CR 401.5). A permanent that lost its abilities shows nothing.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-look-at-top.test.ts` (no ability, own view, the opponent's view by id and by name, both players, several sources, other zones, an empty library, the next card after a draw, the source leaving, the source arriving, the cast rule, the hash, the events and the log of a turn, validation); `packages/ai/test/hidden-info.test.ts` (play-rule decks: the viewer's own top card is the only library card in any view); `packages/ai/test/casual-play-rules.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 24, 2026-10-26
- Web: `seats[viewer].libraryTop` is a chip beside the viewer's library count (a colour band, the name and the cost, like a commander chip in the command zone; "Top card of your library: Name" as its accessible name and tooltip); it is drawn only in the strip of the viewer's own seat and only while the field is present, so the opponent's strip never gets one (the engine puts no such field in another seat). Hovering it, or right-clicking to pin, shows the card in the preview panel like any card (`findObject` looks at the viewer's `libraryTop`). Nothing is sent back. Code: `ui/board/PlayerStrip.tsx` (`LibraryTop`), `ui/board/resolve.ts` (`findObject`). Tests: `apps/web/test/play-rules-host.test.ts`, `play-rules-model.test.ts`, `play-rules-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/play-rules.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-top-card`, both seats hold the card).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `You may look at the top card of your library any time.` (44 cards: Bolas's Citadel, Mystic Forge, Realmwalker, The Reality Chip, ...) lowers to the node above, declaring `ability:static` and `static:lookAtTopOfLibrary`. Cards that also let the player play or cast from the top (Bolas's Citadel, Mystic Forge) have more lines that are not read yet, so they stay unsupported; "Play with the top card of your library revealed." is a different ability and is not this node.
- Notes: The CasualAgent does not typecycle for a land it knows it will draw (`knownTopCard`, `landIsOnTop`).

### "Creatures can't attack you unless their controller pays {2} for each creature they control that's attacking you."

- CR: 508.1d, 508.1f, 508.1h, 508.1k, 506.3, 605.3a, 107.4f, 733
- Gap class: S-light (a cost on a declaration)
- Code: `ast/types.ts`, `ast/validate-cost.ts` (`checkAttackTax`), `rules/attack-tax.ts` (the taxes on the board, the cost of a declaration, `payableAttackTax`, `attackCostsFor`), `rules/combat-legality.ts` (`validateAttacks`, `requiredAttackers`), `rules/combat-flow.ts` (`declareAttackersDecision`, `answerAttackers`, `answerAttackPayment`), `api.ts` (the `payMana` answer), `legal-actions.ts`, `state/decisions.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'attackTax', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }] } }`; with `planeswalkers: true` for the form that names planeswalkers. The cost is mana only (no X); hybrid and Phyrexian symbols are paid as everywhere (`{W/P}` with W or 2 life).
- Decision and action: `DeclareAttackersDecision.attackCosts?` lists each taxed target with `cost` (the symbols one attacker pays) and `affordable` (the most attackers that can be paid for now; a floor). `declareAttackers { attacks }` is answered as before; when a tax covers the chosen attackers the engine validates them (a cost that cannot be paid is refused: the player declares fewer) and then asks `payMana` for seat = the attacker with `purpose: 'attackTax'`, `attacks` (the declaration), `totalCost` (every attacker's tax) and `source` (the first taxing permanent); `manaSources` is read with the attackers already tapped. `payAuto` or `payManual` taps the attackers, pays, declares them and gives priority; `cancel` asks `declareAttackers` again. The tax is on attacking its controller (and with `planeswalkers` that controller's planeswalkers; never a battle); several taxes add up and each attacker pays all of them; a creature that attacks someone else pays nothing; a creature that attacks each combat if able is not required to pay (CR 508.1d): `mustAttack` leaves it out when every target of it is taxed, and the legal actions send it to a free target when there is one. The legal action list holds only declarations within `affordable`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-attack-tax.test.ts` (no tax, the decision and its numbers, one and two attackers, several sources, wrong color, a tapped attacker cannot pay with its own mana ability, not enough mana, fewer attackers, manual taps, cancel, the opponent's view and answers, whose permanent, a planeswalker, a battle, split attackers, Phyrexian mana, commander tax untouched, `mustAttack`, legal actions agree with the engine, the hash, validation); the soak in `play-rule-games.test.ts` (taxed declarations, paid for, held back); `casual-play-rules.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 24, 2026-10-26
- Web: before the declaration the dock has a panel "Cost of attacking" with a row for each taxed target of `attackCosts`: "Attacking Opponent costs {2} for each creature. Your mana covers 1 creature now.", and with attackers chosen "2 chosen: {4}" (generic amounts are added, coloured ones kept); the bar's detail adds "This attack costs {4}.". More attackers than `affordable` is not blocked, because `affordable` is a floor (the engine may accept it): the panel warns ("You can pay for 1 creature attacking Opponent; 2 are chosen, and the engine refuses an attack that cannot be paid.", or "You cannot pay to attack Opponent now" at 0), and a refused declaration shows the engine's error as the usual toast. The payment (`payMana` with `purpose: 'attackTax'`) reads "Pay {2} per attacking creature (4 total) so these creatures can attack: tap your mana sources." ("Pay {2} so this creature can attack" for one; only the total when the attackers pay different amounts, which is a guess from `totalCost` alone: with several taxes and a partial attack the per-creature part can be off), the dock names the attackers from `attacks` and the source ("Goblin 1, Goblin 2 will attack once this is paid. The cost is from Tax Gate."), it is the same mana panel as a spell, and Cancel reads "Choose other attackers" (it sends `cancel`, which changes nothing and asks `declareAttackers` again with a new id, so the choice starts afresh). With Manual mana off the autopilot pays with `payAuto` as it does for a spell, so the dialog shows only in Manual mana mode. The host never leaves an undo point at this payment and a cancel there drops none (the attack is declared by the payment and clears the history). Code: `ui/decisions/attack-tax.ts`, `ui/decisions/payment.ts` (`payBarPrompt`, `payCancelLabel`), `ui/decisions/Overlays.tsx` (`DockPanel`), `ui/decisions/model.ts`, `game/host.ts` (`recordUndo`). Tests: `apps/web/test/play-rules-model.test.ts`, `play-rules-host.test.ts`, `play-rules-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/play-rules.e2e.ts` (hidden decks `synthetic-hasty-goblins` against `synthetic-attack-tax`).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Creatures can't attack you unless their controller pays {2} for each creature they control that's attacking you.` (4 cards: Propaganda, Ghostly Prison, Windborn Muse, Koskun Falls; the cost is any mana, `{M}`) lowers to the node above, declaring `ability:static`, `static:attackTax` and `cost:mana`. The planeswalker form `Creatures can't attack you or planeswalkers you control unless their controller pays {W/P} for each of those creatures.` (the real example of 3 cards: Norn's Annex, Baird, Steward of Argive, Archon of Absolution) lowers to the same with `planeswalkers: true`. The one with a counted cost (`pays {X} for each of those creatures, where X is the number of enchantments you control.`, Sphere of Safety) is not this node and stays unsupported.
- Notes: The AI sends as many creatures as it can pay for, the strongest first, only those that deal at least the mana they cost (or all when lethal), and sends the rest at a planeswalker that costs nothing when there is one.

### "You may choose not to untap ~ during your untap step."

- CR: 502.3, 502.4, 613.1f
- Gap class: P plus a turn-based decision
- Code: `ast/types.ts` and `ast/validate.ts` (`optionalUntap`), `rules/restrictions.ts` (`hasOptionalUntap`), `rules/flow.ts` (the untap step, `answerUntapChoice`), `api.ts` (the `choosePermanent` answer), `state/decisions.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'optionalUntap' } }`, on the permanent itself (the text says "this permanent": the engine reads the ability on the object that has it).
- Decision and action: as the untap step begins the active player's permanents untap, except the tapped ones whose derived abilities include `optionalUntap` and that do not also keep from untapping (`doesntUntap`). If there are any, their controller gets one `choosePermanent` decision with `purpose: 'untap'`, `legal` those permanents, `min` 0, `max` `legal.length` and `source` the first of them; the answer `choosePermanents { objects }` names the permanents that untap, the rest stay tapped. It is an ordinary action in the log. No priority is given in the untap step (CR 502.4): the upkeep follows the answer. A permanent that is untapped, that the opponent controls, or that cannot untap anyway is not offered; every turn it is tapped again it is asked again. With nothing to ask there is no decision.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-optional-untap.test.ts` (nothing asked without the ability, the list, untap and stay tapped, every turn, several permanents, lands and artifacts, the opponent's turn, the other seat's view and answers, refused answers, the legal actions, the interplay with `doesntUntap`, the log and the hash, validation); the soak in `play-rule-games.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 24, 2026-10-26
- Web: a `choosePermanent` decision with `purpose: 'untap'` is a modal checklist (`UntapDialog`), not the sacrifice panel: "Untap during your untap step: choose which of these stay tapped.", a check box per `legal` permanent (named as everywhere, "Name 1", "Name 2" when they share a name; hovering shows the card), all checked at the start (`ui.permanents` holds the ones that untap; the answer `choosePermanents` names them), a status "untaps" or "stays tapped" on each row, and a button "Untap all", "Untap 1 of 2" or "Leave all tapped" with "1 of 2 stays tapped." under the title. There is no Cancel (the engine has none) and nothing on the board is clickable. The log reads "You left X tapped." for the permanents that were offered and not named ("X and Y" for several; nothing when they all untap; "Opponent left ..." for the other seat), before the ordinary "X untaps." lines; the question is read from the pending decision before the answer. The answer clears the undo history (it is not undoable, as every mid-turn question). Code: `ui/decisions/untap.ts`, `ui/decisions/UntapDialog.tsx`, `ui/decisions/model.ts`, `ui/decisions/ui-state.ts`, `game/describe.ts` (`untapChoiceLine`). Tests: `apps/web/test/play-rules-model.test.ts`, `play-rules-host.test.ts`, `play-rules-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/play-rules.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-optional-untap`).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `You may choose not to untap ~ during your untap step.` (44 cards: Vedalken Shackles, Coffin Queen, Immovable Rod, The Pandorica, Helm of Possession, Bottomless Vault, Ice Floe, ...; `~` is the card itself) lowers to the node above, declaring `ability:static` and `static:optionalUntap`. Shackles-like cards have an activated ability that depends on the permanent staying tapped; a card is supported only when its other lines are read too. A line that names another permanent (`You may choose not to untap target ...`) is not this node.
- Notes: The AI untaps everything. The ability is read from the derived abilities, as `doesntUntap` is.

### Web: the words of the four abilities in the preview panel

`ObjectView` carries no static abilities, so the host reads the printed face of each visible battlefield permanent (`game/static-notes.ts`, as `definedPt` does) and sends the page `Snapshot.staticNotes`, the sentences of its play-rule statics by object id (`useStaticNotes`); the preview panel lists them under "Play rules". The sentences are composed from the node, never from a card name or the Oracle text, by `staticWording` in `game/play-rule-text.ts` (re-exported from `ui/decisions/ability-label.ts`): "You may play an additional land on each of your turns" ("two additional lands", "Each player may play an additional land on each of their turns"), "You may look at the top card of your library any time", "Creatures can't attack you unless their controller pays {2} for each creature they control that's attacking you" ("you or planeswalkers you control ... for each of those creatures"), "You may choose not to untap this permanent during your untap step". Limit: a permanent that lost its abilities still lists its printed ones (the view does not say; the derived abilities are not exported). Tests: `apps/web/test/play-rules-model.test.ts`, `play-rules-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/play-rules.e2e.ts`.

### Not built in this slice

"Play with the top card of your library revealed" and playing or casting cards from the top; an attack tax whose cost is not mana or counts something; a cost to block; the optional untap of a permanent other than the one with the ability; a test that steers a lose-all-abilities effect across an untap step; and the limits listed in `docs/ROADMAP.md`.


## Echo, Cumulative upkeep and the monarch (D-127)

Engine slice of 2026-10-26 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 25, shapes in D-127). Three mechanics of the upkeep and the end step. Real wordings and counts are from `reports/coverage.md`, `reports/backlog.md` and `reports/templates.json` (oracle_cards 2026-10-22). The monarch is CR 725 (the brief said 724; CR 724 is "Ending Turns and Phases"). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-upkeep.ts` (names start with "SYNTHETIC "; their text describes the AST, not a real card). AI soak: `packages/ai/test/upkeep-games.test.ts` (the `upkeep` deck style: whole games, random and CasualAgent seats, every game replayed to the same hash; it asserts each mechanic occurs; 20 games per run, D-126). Feature ids: `keyword:echo`, `keyword:cumulativeUpkeep`, `condition:cameUnderControlSinceLastUpkeep`, `effect:cumulativeUpkeep`, `effect:becomeMonarch` (`SUPPORTED_FEATURES`).

### "Echo {3}{W}{W}"

- CR: 702.30a, 702.30b, 603.4, 118.12a, 118.3, 400.7, 302.6, 502.4
- Gap class: K (a keyword whose expansion needs one new condition)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `echo`, `Condition` `cameUnderControlSinceLastUpkeep`, `unlessPays.upkeep`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`echoAbility`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/pay-unless.ts` (`upkeepField`), `rules/flow.ts` (`startTurn` keeps `previousTurnStartTimestamp`), `state/types.ts` (`SeatState.previousTurnStartTimestamp`), `state/decisions.ts` (`upkeep`), `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/validate.ts` (the condition needs "your upkeep"), `ast/supported.ts` (mana only, no X), `testing/assemble.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'echo', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['3', 'W', 'W'] }] } }]` (mana only, no X; one per instance). The engine expands it into `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'upkeep', whose: 'you' }, interveningIf: { kind: 'cameUnderControlSinceLastUpkeep' }, effect: { kind: 'unlessPays', player: { kind: 'you' }, cost, effect: { kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'this' } }, upkeep: 'echo' } }`.
- Decision and action: at the beginning of its controller's upkeep, a permanent that came under that player's control after the beginning of their previous turn triggers (the intervening "if" is checked again as the ability resolves). On resolution the controller is asked `confirmOptional { purpose: 'unlessPays', upkeep: 'echo', cost }` (`acceptOptional` or `declineOptional`), then `payMana { purpose: 'unlessPays', upkeep: 'echo', totalCost }` (`payAuto`, `payManual`, or `cancel` for "do not pay"). Declining, cancelling or being unable to pay (nobody is asked then, CR 118.3) sacrifices the permanent; paying keeps it and it is not asked again at the next upkeep. A permanent that left the battlefield (bounced in response: a new object, CR 400.7) fails the check, so the ability is removed (`fizzled`, reason `interveningIf`) and nobody is asked. Two instances trigger separately and the controller orders them.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-echo.test.ts` (the trigger for a permanent that came after the last upkeep and the absence of one for a permanent that has been there since before it, the opponent's upkeep, a noncreature permanent, a creature cast in the main phase and its first and second upkeep after it, the question and its shape, paying and the next upkeep free, declining, cancel, nothing to pay with, a manual payment with the wrong and the right land, the legal actions against `applyAction`, bounce in response, recast, a permanent controlled by the other seat, two instances and their ordering, payment of each, one paid and one not, mana for one only, features, validation refusals for life, X, an empty cost and the wrong trigger, determinism); the soak and `ast-supported.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: no timestamp test in the condition fails 2 tests, no intervening "if" 3, an expansion that never sacrifices 5, the previous turn start read as the current one 18, the `upkeep` marker dropped from the decisions 2, a trigger at every upkeep 3. Two mutations survive and are equivalent today: dropping the controller comparison (the ability's controller and the permanent's can only differ after a change of control, which no effect makes yet) and dropping the zone test (a permanent that left is a new object and the old id is gone, CR 400.7).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 25, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-127, web step). The question and the payment say why and what declining does, from `upkeep` on the decision: "Echo: pay {1}{R} or sacrifice Name?" (`ui/decisions/optional.ts`, `sacrificeUnlessPrompt`; the payment bar adds ": tap your mana sources."); the buttons are "Pay" and "Sacrifice it" (the same decline button for any "unless you pay" whose effect sacrifices the permanent itself), and the Cancel of the manual payment reads "Sacrifice it" (`payCancelLabel`). The ability on the stack reads "At the beginning of your upkeep, sacrifice this creature unless you pay {1}{R} if this permanent came under your control since the beginning of your last upkeep" (`ability-label.ts`, `conditionText`). The keyword chip reads "Echo {1}{R}", one for each instance, from the Oracle text (`ui/board/keywords.ts`). Tests: `apps/web/test/upkeep-model.test.ts`, `e2e/upkeep.e2e.ts` (paid by hand, declined).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Echo {3}{W}{W}` (47 cards: Karmic Guide, Mogg War Marshal, Bone Shredder, Deranged Hermit, Extruder, Stingscourger, Yavimaya Granger, Crater Hellion, ...; a keyword line of its own, the reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above, declaring `keyword:echo` and `cost:mana`. A card whose echo cost is not mana (the corpus lists the mana form only) or has X is unsupported; the engine refuses it with a reason.
- Notes: The engine has no effect that changes control, so a change of control is tested by placing the permanent under the other seat's control (`controller` option); the condition also compares the controller. In a scenario a permanent placed with `fresh: true` is due at its controller's next upkeep and one placed plainly is not (the scenario's previous turn start lies between them). The AI pays while the permanent is worth more than the payment (`upkeepAnswer`).

### "Cumulative upkeep {1}"

- CR: 702.24a, 702.24b, 118.12a, 118.3, 119.4, 701.9, 122
- Gap class: K plus one effect
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `cumulativeUpkeep`, `Effect` `cumulativeUpkeep`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`cumulativeUpkeepAbility`), `rules/effects.ts` (the `cumulativeUpkeep` case, `AGE_COUNTER`), `rules/pay-unless.ts`, `state/types.ts` (`Instruction` `unlessPays.upkeep`), `state/decisions.ts`, `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (not in a spell), `ast/supported.ts` (mana, life and discard; no X)
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'cumulativeUpkeep', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }] } }]`; the cost may also be `[{ kind: 'payLife', amount: 1 }]` or `[{ kind: 'discard', count: 1 }]` ("Cumulative upkeep—Pay 1 life.", "Cumulative upkeep—Discard a card."). The engine expands it into `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'upkeep', whose: 'you' }, effect: { kind: 'cumulativeUpkeep', cost } }`.
- Decision and action: at every upkeep of its controller. As the ability resolves: if the permanent is still on the battlefield under the controller's control it gets an age counter (`counterAdded`, counter `age`), then the controller is asked `confirmOptional { purpose: 'unlessPays', upkeep: 'cumulativeUpkeep', cost }` where `cost` is the printed cost repeated once for each age counter now on it ({1}, then {1}{1}; 1 life, then 2; ...), then the payment (`payMana`, `chooseCards` for a discard, a life payment is immediate). The whole cost is paid or none of it. Declining, cancelling or being unable to pay (nobody is asked) sacrifices the permanent. A permanent that left before the ability resolved gets no counter and no question. Two instances each count all the age counters there are when they resolve ({1}, then {1}{1}).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-cumulative-upkeep.test.ts` (the trigger every upkeep and not the opponent's, a noncreature permanent, the counter put on before the question, paying 1 then 2 then failing without a question, declining, cancel, bounce in response, the legal actions, life 1 then 2 then 3 and too little life, discard with the choice and too few cards, two instances, features, validation refusals for sacrifice, counters, tap, X, an empty cost and a spell); the soak. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: no age counter fails 8 tests, a cost that is not repeated 5, one repetition too few 8, an expansion that never sacrifices 6, a trigger at every upkeep 3, the `upkeep` marker dropped 1. One survives and is equivalent today: reducing the battlefield-and-controller guard to "the object exists" (a permanent that left is a new object, CR 400.7, and control cannot change yet).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 25, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-127, web step). "Cumulative upkeep: pay {1}{1} (2 age counters) or sacrifice Name?" on the question and the payment (the cost is the printed cost repeated, so repeated mana and life are merged into one phrase, `mergeRepeated` in `game/cost-text.ts`; the age counters are read from `counters.age` of the permanent the ability belongs to, and left out when it is gone); "Pay" ("Discard" for a discard cost) and "Sacrifice it". The age counters show as the ordinary counter chip "age N" on the tile and in the preview panel. The stack gist reads "Put an age counter on this creature. Sacrifice it unless you pay {1} for each age counter on it"; the keyword chip "Cumulative upkeep {1}". Limit: the discard of a cumulative upkeep cost keeps the engine's own prompt ("Discard a card."), since that question carries no `upkeep` marker and the ability on the stack is not an `unlessPays` to read it from. Tests: `apps/web/test/upkeep-model.test.ts`, `e2e/upkeep.e2e.ts` (the counter grows, `{1}` then `{1}{1}`, backing out of the payment).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Cumulative upkeep {1}` (57 cards, 14 of them blocked by nothing else: Mystic Remora, Illusions of Grandeur, Illusionary Wall, Survivor of the Unseen, Yavimaya Ants, Mesmeric Trance, Arnjlot's Ascent, Arctic Wolves, Soldevi Simulacrum, Fyndhorn Pollen, ...) lowers to the keyword with the mana cost, declaring `keyword:cumulativeUpkeep` and `cost:mana`. `Cumulative upkeep—Pay 1 life.` and `Cumulative upkeep—Discard a card.` lower with `payLife` and `discard` (declaring `cost:payLife`, `cost:discard`); the reports list only the mana shape, so the share of the other forms is not known here. Costs the question cannot pay (`Cumulative upkeep—Sacrifice a creature.`, putting a counter, a choice of costs such as `{W} or {U}`) are refused with a reason; the parser should declare the cost kind and leave the card unsupported.
- Notes: The counter is the engine's `age` counter by name, as `loyalty` and `lore` are; two instances count the same counters (CR 702.24b).

### "When ~ enters, you become the monarch."

- CR: 725.1, 725.2, 725.3, 725.4, 603.3, 603.10, 608.2h, 510.2, 113.8
- Gap class: S-light (the first player designation, and abilities with no source)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `becomeMonarch`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `rules/monarch.ts` (`becomeMonarch`, `monarchAfterLoss`, `findMonarchTriggers`, the two abilities, `MONARCH_TEXT`), `rules/effects.ts`, `rules/triggers.ts` (`collectTriggers`), `rules/trigger-stack.ts` (sourceless triggers), `rules/ability-object.ts`, `rules/sba.ts` and `api.ts` (`concede`) for CR 725.4, `state/types.ts` (`GameState.monarch`, `PendingTrigger.inherent`, `StackData.inherent`), `state/events.ts` (`monarchChanged`), `state/decisions.ts` (`TriggerChoice.source` may be null), `view.ts` (`GameView.monarch`, "The monarch" on the stack), `invariants.ts`, `testing/` (`at(step, { monarch })`, `expect.monarch`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'becomeMonarch', player: { kind: 'you' } } }` for the permanent; a spell is `spell: { effect: { kind: 'becomeMonarch', player: { kind: 'you' } } }`. The player is one player (`eachPlayer` and `eachOpponent` are refused).
- Decision and action: no new decision. The effect gives the designation to the player (`monarchChanged { from, to }`; nothing when they already have it); the old monarch ceases to be it. The designation stays when the permanent leaves. The monarch's two abilities are triggers with no source that the engine finds itself: at the beginning of the end step of the monarch's own turn (draw a card), and for each creature that deals combat damage to the monarch (its controller becomes the monarch, from last-known information if the creature is gone). They go on the stack as "The monarch" (an ability object with no `abilitySource`, with the CR 725.2 text), controlled by the player who was the monarch when they triggered, so they can be responded to and a monarch who lost the designation in response still draws; several of them are ordered by their controller (the ordering decision lists "The monarch" with `source: null`). When the monarch leaves the game (state-based action or concession) the active player becomes the monarch, else the next player in turn order still in the game, else nobody (CR 725.4). `getView(state, seat).monarch` shows it to both seats; `monarchChanged` is in the event stream.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-monarch.test.ts` (no monarch at the start, the enters trigger and the view, a spell, two sources, becoming it again, the source dying or staying, an enchantment source, validation of `eachPlayer`; the end-step draw, the stack object's name, text and controller, only on the monarch's turn, every turn, the designation moving in response, the ordering with another end-step trigger; combat damage to the monarch through the stack, the new monarch drawing that turn, damage to a non-monarch, to a creature (a block) and non-combat damage doing nothing, the creature removed in response, two creatures and their ordering, the hash; CR 725.4 for a concession of either seat and for state-based losses; features, fixtures and the legal actions); the soak and `ast-supported.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: becoming the monarch again emitting an event fails 1 test, a draw at every end step 3, non-combat damage by a creature stealing it 1, damage to any player stealing it 1, no hand-on when the monarch leaves 3, the steal giving the designation to the wrong player 4, the monarch's triggers controlled by the active player 2, `becomeMonarch` doing nothing 10, the triggers not collected 9. One survives and is equivalent today: dropping the "is a creature" test of the steal (only creatures deal combat damage in this engine; the test stays because the rule says "a creature").
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 25, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-127, web step). A crown chip "Monarch" on the player strip of `GameView.monarch` (`ui/board/PlayerStrip.tsx`, `data-monarch`; the life button's accessible name adds ", the monarch"); the log reads "You become the monarch." / "Opponent becomes the monarch." / "There is no monarch." from `monarchChanged` and "The monarch triggers." for the two abilities (`game/describe.ts`; `nameIn` names a stack object with `inherent` "The monarch"); no separate toast (the log line is the notice). The monarch's stack object has no `abilitySource`, so the stack panel tells it as an ability with no source (`game/monarch-text.ts`, `isInherentAbility`), shows no hover preview (there is no card) and words it from the kind of trigger, relative to the viewer: "At the beginning of your end step, draw a card." / "Whenever a creature deals combat damage to you, its controller becomes the monarch." ("the opponent's" and "to the opponent" when the other player is the monarch). The trigger-ordering rows with `source: null` use the same words. `becomeMonarch` reads "You become the monarch" in any gist (`ability-label.ts`). Tests: `apps/web/test/upkeep-model.test.ts`, `e2e/upkeep.e2e.ts` (the crown, the end-step draw on the stack and the extra card, a creature taking the crown in combat). Limit: the ObjectView does not carry `inherent`, so an ability on the stack with no source is taken to be the monarch's (the only kind there is).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `When ~ enters, you become the monarch.` (38 cards: Court of Grace, Court of Garenbrig, Regal Behemoth, Court of Ambition, Queen Marchesa, Court of Cunning, Court of Vantress, Court of Ire, Court of Locthwain, Skyline Despot, ...; 4 of them need nothing else) lowers to the enters trigger above, declaring `trigger:entersBattlefield` and `effect:becomeMonarch`. The Courts' other lines are separate and must be read too. Other wordings (`You become the monarch.` as a spell, `Whenever ~ deals combat damage to a player, you become the monarch.`) lower to `becomeMonarch` with the matching trigger; the reports do not rank them, so they are not listed. A condition `if you're the monarch` is not built, so a card that has one stays unsupported.
- Notes: The inherent abilities are the engine's, not a card's: no card needs to say them. The designation is not an object: nothing in a zone, nothing a `moveToZone` or a copy touches.

### Not built in this slice

"If you're the monarch" and "the monarch" as a selector (e.g. "target opponent who is the monarch"), "you can't become the monarch" and the initiative; Echo and Cumulative upkeep costs the question cannot pay (sacrifice, put a counter, tap, a choice of costs: `{W} or {U}`, which CR 702.24a makes separately for each counter); the same keywords granted by another card; a change of control during the game (the engine has no such effect: the Echo condition reads the controller, and is tested only by placing a permanent under the other seat's control); See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.


## Cast later: delayed draw, Suspend, Madness and Foretell (D-128)

Engine slice of 2026-10-26 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 26, shapes in D-128). Four mechanics that act on a card or an effect at a later time. Real wordings and counts are from `reports/backlog.md` and `reports/templates.json` (oracle_cards 2026-10-22). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-later.ts` (names start with "SYNTHETIC "; their text describes the AST, not a real card). AI soak: `packages/ai/test/later-games.test.ts` (the `later` deck style: whole games, random and CasualAgent seats, every game replayed to the same hash, a check in every step that a foretold card is face down to the other seat; it asserts each mechanic occurs; 20 games per run, D-126); heuristics: `packages/ai/test/casual-later.test.ts`; the hidden-information audit `packages/ai/test/hidden-info.test.ts` (a test with the `later` style). Feature ids: `effect:delayedTrigger`, `keyword:suspend`, `castAlternative:madness`, `castAlternative:foretell` (`SUPPORTED_FEATURES`); `effect:removeTimeCounter` and `effect:castExiled` are listed too as the engine's own effects (a card cannot write them: `validateCard` refuses them). The engine side only: each card needs the parser (a later stage) to read its other lines. Checked by mutation on a scratch copy of the repository (27 mutations of the engine rules: five or more for each mechanic; every one failed at least one test; the counts are with each mechanic below).

### "You draw a card at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep."

- CR: 603.7, 603.7a, 603.7b, 603.7c, 603.7d, 603.7e, 603.3b, 121.1
- Gap class: P (an effect that creates a delayed triggered ability; `GameState.delayedTriggers` existed for Dash and Unearth)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `delayedTrigger`), `ast/validate-later.ts` (`checkDelayedTrigger`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `rules/effects.ts` (the `delayedTrigger` case), `rules/triggers.ts` (`findDelayedTriggers`, unchanged), `state/types.ts` (`DelayedTrigger`, unchanged)
- AST node: `spell: { effect: { kind: 'delayedTrigger', at: { step: 'upkeep', whose: 'each' }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } } }`. `at.step` is any `TriggerStep` and `at.whose` is `each` (the next such step, whoever's turn) or `you` ("your next upkeep"). The inner effect is `draw`, `gainLife` or `loseLife` with the player selector `you`, `eachPlayer` or `eachOpponent` and a fixed count of at least 1 (nothing that names an object of the spell, a target or an X, and no nested delayed trigger: `validateCard` says why).
- Decision and action: no decision. As the spell resolves it adds `{ source: the spell, controller: its controller, ability: { trigger: { kind: 'step', step, whose }, effect } }` to `GameState.delayedTriggers`. The first time the step begins (a step that began before the spell resolved does not count) the ability triggers, is removed from the list and goes on the stack like any triggered ability: it shows under the spell's name, is controlled by the player who controlled the spell, can be responded to and is ordered with that player's other triggers. A spell that was countered creates nothing. It still resolves when the spell is long in the graveyard.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-delayed-draw.test.ts` (the creation and the empty hand, the next turn's upkeep whoever's it is, once only, cast during an upkeep, "your next upkeep" waiting for its controller's turn, another step, another player effect, the source in the graveyard, a countered spell, two at once and their ordering, both seats, the hash, validation refusals for an object, a nested trigger, a target player, an X, the controller of an object, a zero amount, an unknown step and turn). Mutation-checked: a delayed trigger that is never removed fails 2 tests, one controlled by the other seat 8, a spell that creates nothing 10, "each turn" read as "your turn" 6, validation that allows any effect 2.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 26, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-128, web step). The delayed ability on the stack reads "At the beginning of the next turn's upkeep, draw a card" ("At the beginning of your next upkeep" for `whose: 'you'`): the engine's delayed ability is a `step` trigger like a permanent's own, and the view does not mark it as delayed, so `game/later-text.ts` `delayedWording` takes the cue from the source's own text saying "next" (a cosmetic read; any other step trigger keeps the ordinary words, `game/trigger-text.ts`). `GameView` still does not list the delayed triggers waiting for their step, so nothing shows between the cast and the upkeep. Tests: `apps/web/test/later-model.test.ts`, `later-host.test.ts`, `e2e/later.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-delayed`).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `You draw a card at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep.` (Arcane Denial; 1 card, none supported by this sentence alone: the card's other sentence is not built) lowers to the node above, declaring `effect:delayedTrigger` and `effect:draw`. The sentence `Its controller may draw up to two cards at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep.` (Arcane Denial's second, template `Its controller may draw up to N cards at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep.`) is not lowered: it needs the controller of the target spell and a number chosen by that player (ROADMAP).
- Notes: the engine's `at.whose: 'you'` reads the controller of the delayed ability, which is the controller of the spell.

### "Suspend 2—{1}{B}"

- CR: 702.62a, 702.62b, 702.62c, 702.62d, 116.1, 116.2f, 603.4, 603.3b, 608.2g, 601.2a-i, 702.10, 302.6, 400.7, 903.9a, 903.8
- Gap class: K plus a special action, two abilities that work from exile and a cast during a resolution
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `suspend`, `Effect` `removeTimeCounter` and `castExiled`), `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts` (not on a land, one per face), `ast/validate-parts.ts` and `ast/validate-later.ts` (the engine-only effects), `ast/supported.ts` (mana only), `rules/special-legality.ts` (`canSuspend`, `suspendableObjects`), `rules/special-actions.ts` (the action and its payment), `rules/priority.ts` (the options), `rules/later-triggers.ts` (the two abilities), `rules/cast-exiled.ts` and `rules/cast-exiled-answer.ts` (the cast), `rules/cast.ts` (`beginCast` takes `suspended`), `rules/zones.ts` (`GameObject.alt` `suspend` for a creature), `rules/derive-alt.ts` (haste), `rules/effects.ts` (`removeTimeCounter`, `castExiled`), `rules/stack.ts`, `rules/time-counter.ts`, `api.ts`, `state/actions.ts`, `state/decisions.ts`, `state/events.ts`, `state/types.ts` (`StackData.suspended`, the `castExiled` instruction), `invariants.ts`, `view.ts` (`ObjectView.specialActions`, `suspended`), `testing/` (`suspend` verb)
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'suspend', count: 2, cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1', 'B'] }] } }]` (a positive count, mana only, no X; `{0}` is allowed; one per face; not on a land).
- Decision and action: a priority option and action `suspend { object }` for a card in the player's hand that they could begin to cast now (the card's timing: an instant or a card with flash any time they have priority, anything else in their main phase with the stack empty; no split second on the stack) and whose suspend cost they can pay; no legal target and no mana for the spell's own cost are needed.
