Taking it asks `payMana { purpose: 'suspend', source: the card, totalCost }` (`payAuto`, `payManual`, or `cancel`, which gives priority back and changes nothing; a cost of {0} is taken at once); then the card goes to exile face up with N counters named `time` (events `zoneChanged`, `suspended { seat, object, count }`, `counterAdded`) and the same player has priority again: no stack object. At the beginning of the owner's upkeep each suspended card (exile, a `time` counter, the keyword) triggers an ability "remove a time counter" (a stack object whose `abilitySource` is the card; the owner orders several): resolving it removes one counter (`counterRemoved`) unless the card is no longer suspended. When the last counter has been removed from a card with suspend that is in exile a second ability triggers; as it resolves, if the card could be cast now (a legal target for each target, additional costs payable, no timing) its owner is asked `confirmOptional { purpose: 'suspend', subject: the card }`; accepting casts it for free from exile as the ability resolves (targets chosen then, no payment, no priority until the spell is on the stack: `spellCast { fromZone: 'exile', alternative: 'free', suspended: true }`; `cancel` rewinds to the question); declining, or a card that could not be cast ("if able"), leaves it in exile with no counters, for good. A creature spell cast this way makes a permanent with `alternative: 'suspend'` that has haste.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-suspend.test.ts` (the option beside the ordinary cast, the payment and the exile with N counters and no stack, both players seeing the card, the view's `specialActions`, cancel, a manual payment and one that does not cover the cost, a cost of {0}, no mana, a card without suspend or not in the hand, the other seat; sorcery timing against an instant and a card with flash, no legal target needed, split second on the stack; the tick at the owner's upkeep and not the opponent's, an instant suspended in the opponent's upkeep, two cards and their ordering, the intervening "if" checked again at resolution; N = 3 counter by counter, N = 1, the question, accepting (free, from exile, no priority), declining, a target chosen as it is cast and the cast taken back, no legal target when the last counter comes off, a target that appears later, a sorcery cast in the upkeep, a countered spell, a noncreature permanent; haste, the same creature cast from the hand, the permanent's mark; a commander with suspend (the command-zone question, no tax); the hash; validation refusals for no counters, X, a cost that is not mana, an empty cost, a land, two abilities and the engine-only effects). Mutation-checked: a tick that removes two counters fails 13 tests, one at every upkeep 16, a suspend with no timing 2, no haste mark on the spell 4, a question asked when it cannot be cast 1, one counter too many 19, the cast ability never triggered 13, no haste from the mark 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 26, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-128, web step). A card with `ObjectView.specialActions` gets a button in its menu (a click always asks, even when it is the only thing to do): "Suspend 2 — {1}{B}" with the line "Exile it with 2 time counters. Cast it for free when the last is gone." (`game/later-text.ts`, `ui/decisions/abilities.ts`, `priority.ts`). The payment is the usual mana panel worded "Pay {1}{B} to suspend X with 2 time counters: tap your mana sources." with a dock line (`data-special-pay`) and a plain Cancel that gives priority back (`ui/decisions/payment.ts`; `game/host.ts` keeps the undo history across the open and the cancel). A suspended card is a chip "X time 2" in the strip above the hand (both players', the opponent's dashed and marked), a "time 2" badge on its card in the exile viewer (which also says whose card it is) and a line in the preview (`ui/board/OtherZonesStrip.tsx`, `ZoneViewer.tsx`, `CardFace.tsx` `LaterBadges`). The two abilities are worded on the stack from `ability.effect.kind`: "At the beginning of your upkeep" / "Remove a time counter from X." and "When the last time counter is removed from X" / "Cast X without paying its mana cost." (`laterAbilityWords`, `ui/panels/StackPanel.tsx`, `data-later-wording`); the order panel words the upkeep tick of a card with time counters (`laterOrderWording`). The question is "Cast X now without paying its mana cost? (its last time counter was removed)" with "Cast it" and "Don't cast it (it stays in exile)" (`ui/decisions/optional.ts`). The spell has the badge "suspended" (`ObjectView.suspended`, `game/alt-cost.ts`), the creature a "suspended" flag whose hover and preview line say it has haste; the log reads "You suspended X with 3 time counters." and "You cast X without paying its mana cost (suspend)." (`game/describe.ts`, `describe-later.ts`). Tests: `later-model.test.ts`, `later-host.test.ts`, `later-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/later.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-suspend`).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Suspend 2—{1}{B}` (65 cards, 25 unlocked by the line alone: Profane Tutor, Search for Tomorrow, Sol Talisman, Rousing Refrain, Resurgent Belief, Ancestral Vision, Inevitable Betrayal, Wheel of Fate, Lotus Bloom, Inspiring Refrain, ...; a line of its own on a card whose spell or permanent text is other lines, the reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above, declaring `keyword:suspend` and `cost:mana`. The count is a digit (`Suspend 4—{0}` is a cost of {0}, which the engine takes without payment). A suspend line whose cost has an {X} or is not mana is refused by `validateCard` with a reason.
- Notes: the time counter is the counter named `time`, as `age`, `loyalty` and `lore` are by name. "Play it" is "cast it": a card with suspend is never a land here. "Until you lose control of the spell or the permanent it becomes" is not modelled (the engine has no effect that changes control): the haste follows the permanent that spell became. The AI suspends in its own main phase once nothing worth casting is left, and answers the question by whether the card is worth casting now.

### "Madness {1}{B}{B}"

- CR: 702.35a, 702.35b, 702.35c, 701.9, 118.9, 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 608.2g, 400.7, 903.9a, 514.1, 702.29
- Gap class: K plus a replacement of where a discarded card goes and a cast during a resolution
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`NamedAlternativeKind` `madness`), `ast/features.ts`, `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts` (not on a land), `rules/madness.ts` (`discardOne`, `hasMadness`), `rules/search.ts`, `rules/flow.ts`, `rules/discard-cost.ts`, `rules/activate-costs.ts` (the five discards), `rules/later-triggers.ts` (the ability, from `madnessExiled`), `rules/cast-exiled.ts`, `rules/cast-exiled-answer.ts`, `rules/legality.ts` (`canCastDuringResolution` with the madness cost, `castZone` never for `madness`), `rules/cast-cost.ts`, `state/events.ts` (`madnessExiled`), `state/decisions.ts` (purpose `madness`)
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'madness', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1', 'B', 'B'] }] }]` (mana only, no X, not on a land).
- Decision and action: whenever a player discards a card with madness (a spell or ability effect, the cleanup step, a discard paid as a cost, Ward's or an "unless pays" discard, Cycling's "discard this card") it goes to exile instead of the graveyard: events `zoneChanged` (hand to exile), `discarded` (with the id the card had in the hand), `madnessExiled { seat, object }`. The exile is not a choice. An ability of the card goes on the stack (controlled by its owner, the one who discarded it; several are ordered by their owner); as it resolves, if the card could be cast now the owner is asked `confirmOptional { purpose: 'madness', subject: the card, cost: the madness cost }`; accepting casts it from exile for that cost (`payMana`, then the spell: `spellCast { fromZone: 'exile', alternative: 'madness' }`, no priority until it is on the stack; `cancel` rewinds to the question); declining, or a card that could not be cast (a target missing, the mana missing), puts it into its owner's graveyard. A commander discarded this way is in exile first: its owner may send it to the command zone (the usual question), and then there is nothing to cast. The madness cast is never a priority option from a hand.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-madness.test.ts` (the replacement by an effect, the events, a card without madness, the owner's choice of the discarded card, no hand cast; the ability on the stack and its controller, the question with the cost, accepting and paying, declining, taking the cast back, no mana, no legal target, a legal target chosen as it is cast, two cards and their ordering, the new object; a discard cost, the cleanup step, Cycling; a commander in either way; the hash; validation refusals for X, a cost that is not mana, an exile count and a land; no madness cast from a hand). Mutation-checked: no replacement fails 13 tests, a cleanup discard that goes to the graveyard 1, Cycling's 1, a discard cost's 1, the discard effect's 10, a card that cannot be cast staying in exile 2, declining leaving it in exile 2, no `madnessExiled` event 13.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 26, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-128, web step). The question is "Cast X for its madness cost {1}{B}?" with "Cast it for {1}{B}" and "Don't cast it (it goes to the graveyard)", then the ordinary payment (`optionalAsk`); the ability on the stack reads "When X is exiled with madness" / "Cast X for its madness cost."; the spell has the badge "madness" and the log "X was exiled with madness.", "You choose to cast X for its madness cost {1}{B}." (or "You don't cast X; it goes to the graveyard.") and "You cast X for its madness cost." (`game/describe-later.ts`). No madness button is ever offered from a hand (the engine lists none). Tests: `later-model.test.ts`, `later-host.test.ts`, `e2e/later.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-madness`, a cycled card).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Madness {1}{B}{B}` (56 cards, 27 unlocked by the line alone: Necrogoyf, Markov Baron, Big Game Hunter, Stensia Masquerade, Curse of Fool's Wisdom, Call to the Netherworld, Asylum Visitor, Terminal Agony, Circular Logic, Anje's Ravager, ...; a keyword line, the reminder text dropped) lowers to the alternative cost above, declaring `castAlternative:madness` and `cost:mana`. A madness cost with an {X} or that is not mana is refused with a reason.
- Notes: the brief called the exile optional ("may exile it instead"); CR 702.35a says "exiles it instead", so the engine exiles and lets the owner choose the cast (D-128). The AI answers yes when the card is worth casting and discards a card with madness first at the cleanup step.

### "Foretell {4}{R}{R}"

- CR: 702.143a, 702.143b, 702.143c, 702.143e, 116.2h, 116.1, 118.9, 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 400.2, 406.3, 903.9a, 903.8, 733
- Gap class: K plus a special action and hidden information (a face-down card in exile)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`NamedAlternativeKind` `foretell`), `ast/features.ts`, `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts` (not on a land), `rules/special-legality.ts` (`canForetell`, `foretellableObjects`, `isForetold`, `FORETELL_ACTION_COST`), `rules/special-actions.ts`, `rules/priority.ts`, `rules/legality.ts` (`castZone` for `foretell`: exile, foretold, a later turn; `foretellCastObjects`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`alternativeCostsOf` lists the foretell cost of a foretold card), `state/types.ts` (`GameObject.foretoldTurn`), `state/events.ts` (`foretold`), `view.ts` (`faceDownView`, `ObjectView.specialActions`, `faceDown`, `foretold`, `foretoldTurn`), `invariants.ts`, `testing/` (`foretell` verb)
- AST node: `faces[n].alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'foretell', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['4', 'R', 'R'] }] }]` (the foretell cost: what casting the card from exile costs; mana only, no X; not on a land). The {2} of the action is the engine's.
- Decision and action: a priority option and action `foretell { object }` for a card with a foretell cost in the hand of the active player, any time they have priority, when they can pay {2} (no other timing). It asks `payMana { purpose: 'foretell', source: the card, totalCost: ['2'] }` (`payAuto`, `payManual`, `cancel`), exiles the card with `foretoldTurn` set to the number of the turn (`foretold { seat, object }`: no card, no name) and gives the player priority again. In a later turn the owner may cast it with the option `castSpell { object, alternative: 'foretell' }` for the foretell cost instead of the mana cost, with the spell's own timing (`spellCast { fromZone: 'exile', alternative: 'foretell' }`, no commander tax: it is not cast from the command zone); never in the turn it was foretold, never as an ordinary cast, never by the other player. The spell is a new object, public and not foretold. Hidden information: the owner's view shows the card in exile in full (`faceDown`, `foretold`, `foretoldTurn`, `alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'foretell', cost }]`); every other view shows an object with the id and the owner and `name: 'Face-down card'`, no types, text, cost or counters, `faceDown: true` and `isCommander: false`; the `foretold` and `zoneChanged` events and the action log name no card (the opaque card id every event carries is not a name, as for a card drawn); the payment decision is in its own seat's view only.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-foretell.test.ts` (the option in every step of the player's own turn and with the stack busy, not in the opponent's turn, the payment of {2} and the exile with no stack, no mana, a card without foretell or not in the hand, cancel, the `specialActions` of the card in the hand; the owner's view, the opponent's view (name, text, types, cost, commander flag, the whole view as text), the events and the log, the pending payment, two foretold cards as two objects; not the turn it was foretold, the cast for the foretell cost at instant speed in the opponent's turn, no ordinary cast, a sorcery's timing, a creature, no mana for the foretell cost, only the owner, cancelling the cast, the new object; a commander with foretell in either way; the hash and the foretold turn; validation refusals for X, a cost that is not mana and a land); the soak (every step checks the placeholder) and the hidden-information audit. Mutation-checked: a foretold card castable the same turn fails 1 test, the other seat seeing the card 2, foretelling in the opponent's turn 1, a cost of {1} 2, no foretold turn recorded 10, the mana cost paid instead of the foretell cost 3.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 26, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-128, web step). "Foretell {2}" is a button in the hand card's menu (line: "Exile it face down. Cast it from a later turn for {1}{G}."), paid as "Pay {2} to foretell X: tap your mana sources." with a plain Cancel. The owner's foretold card is the full card (name, art lookup, text) with a "Foretold" badge in the exile viewer and a chip "X Foretold" in the strip; from the next turn a chip "Cast for {1}{G}" appears above the hand, and a click casts it at once (`castSpell` with `alternative: 'foretell'`; `ui/decisions/alt-cost.ts` `foretellCastLabel`, `layouts.ts` `exileBadge`; `policy/should-stop.ts` counts a foretold instant like a hand card). The spell has the badge "foretold"; the log reads "You foretold X." for the owner and "Opponent foretold a card." for the other player. The other player's view of the card is a placeholder (`faceDown` without `foretold`, `name` "Face-down card"): `CardFace` draws a card back without a name, art lookup or cost, the strip chip reads "Face-down card (foretold) (opponent's)", the viewer button is "Face-down card (foretold)", the preview says "A face-down card in exile"; the move to exile that a foretell makes is left out of the log (`isForetellMove`), because the log is worded from the full state and would name the card. Tests: `later-model.test.ts`, `later-host.test.ts` (the other player's view and log never hold the name), `later-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/later.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-foretell`, also as the AI's deck: the page never contains the name while the card is face down). Limit: the engine does not export the type of `ObjectView.specialActions`' entries (`SpecialActionView`), so the web names it from the view.
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Foretell {4}{R}{R}` (50 cards, 18 unlocked by the line alone: Delayed Blast Fireball, Ravenform, Ultimate Magic: Holy, Lifestream's Blessing, Saw It Coming, Sozin's Comet, Haunting Voyage, Behold the Multiverse, Mystic Reflection, Cosmic Intervention, ...; a keyword line, the reminder text dropped) lowers to the alternative cost above, declaring `castAlternative:foretell` and `cost:mana`. Text about a foretold spell ("If this spell was foretold, ...") and costs that reduce a foretell cost are other lines and are not read.
- Notes: the {2} is fixed by the rules (CR 702.143a), so it is not data. A card put into exile "foretold" by another effect (CR 702.143d) is not built.

### Not built in this slice

"Its controller may draw up to N cards at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep." and any delayed effect that names an object, a target, an X or a number chosen by a player; Suspend or Foretell granted by another card, with a cost that is not mana, or put onto a card by an effect (CR 702.143d); the reveal of foretold cards when a player leaves the game (CR 702.143f); "until you lose control" for the haste of Suspend; conditions on "foretold" or "suspended" spells; Miracle and Plot (the other D-117 steps); the engine's list of delayed triggers waiting for their step in `GameView` (so the web shows the delayed draw only once it is on the stack). See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Copies of spells, Storm and Replicate (D-129)

Engine slice of 2026-10-26 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 27, shapes in D-129). A copy of a spell on the stack, and the two keywords that make copies of the spell being cast. Real wordings and counts are from `reports/backlog.md` and `reports/templates.json` (oracle_cards 2026-10-22). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-copies.ts` (names start with "SYNTHETIC "; their text describes the AST, not a real card). AI soak: `packages/ai/test/copy-games.test.ts` (the `copies` deck style: whole games, random and CasualAgent seats, every game replayed to the same hash, rules restated from the events in every step: a copy is a spell with no card, controlled and owned by the player who put it on the stack, is never cast and is never counted; the number of spells the seats counted equals the casts in the events; each storm trigger makes exactly as many copies as spells were cast before its spell and each replicate trigger as many as the cost was paid; a copy of a permanent spell becomes a token with no card; a copy is on the stack or nowhere; it asserts each path occurs and every card of the style is cast; 20 games per run, D-126); heuristics: `packages/ai/test/casual-copies.test.ts`. Feature ids: `effect:copySpell`, `keyword:storm`, `effect:storm`, `keyword:replicate`, `effect:replicate`, `condition:replicated` (`SUPPORTED_FEATURES`); `effect:storm` and `effect:replicate` are the engine's own effects of the expansions (`validateCard` refuses them anywhere else). The engine side only: each card needs the parser (a later stage) to read its other lines. Checked by mutation on a scratch copy of the repository (25 mutations of the engine rules: 17 for the copy and Storm, 8 for Replicate; every one failed at least one test, after the tests that kill the three that first survived were added; the counts are with each mechanic below).

### "Copy target instant or sorcery spell. You may choose new targets for the copy."

- CR: 707.10, 707.10a, 707.10c, 707.10f, 707.2, 704.5e, 608.2b, 608.3c, 608.3f, 113.7a, 601.2i, 603.2, 702.21a
- Gap class: P (a primitive: the first copy of a spell) with a new object model (`GameObject.copyOf`)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `copySpell`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/features.ts`, `state/types.ts` (`GameObject.copyOf`, the `copySpell` instruction), `state/ops.ts` (`printedCard`), `state/decisions.ts` (`TargetsDecision.forCopy`, `current`), `state/events.ts` (`spellCopied`), `rules/copy-source.ts` (`copiedSpell`, `copyInstructions`), `rules/copy-spell.ts` (the copy and the question), `rules/copy-spell-answer.ts` (the answer), `rules/effects.ts` (the `copySpell` case), `rules/stack.ts`, `rules/zones.ts` (a copy leaves the stack: ceases, or becomes a token), `rules/copiable.ts`, `rules/derive.ts`, `rules/derive-view.ts`, `rules/derive-statics.ts`, `rules/legality.ts` (`faceOf`), `rules/cast-cost.ts`, `api.ts`, `legal-actions.ts`, `view.ts` (`ObjectView.copy`), `invariants.ts`, `testing/describe.ts`
- AST node: `spell: { targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { stackKind: 'spell', anyOf: [{ types: ['instant'] }, { types: ['sorcery'] }] } } }], effect: { kind: 'copySpell', spell: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, newTargets: 'may' } }`. `spell` is a `target` slot or `{ kind: 'triggerSubject' }` (the spell whose casting caused the trigger); `newTargets` is `'may'` (the controller of the copy may choose new targets) or `'no'` (the copy has the targets of the original).
- Decision and action: the copy is a spell with no card on top of the stack, controlled and owned by the player who controls the effect, with the original's face, targets, X, modes, kicked fact and count, replicate count and alternative cost (`spellCopied { seat, original, copy }`; no `spellCast`: a copy is not cast, triggers no "whenever you cast" ability, is not counted by Storm and is not a spell cast for any count). With `newTargets: 'may'` and something to choose, its controller is asked `targets { source: the copy, forCopy: true, current: the targets it has, specs }` before anything else happens: each slot lists every legal target and the one the copy has there even if it is now illegal (CR 707.10c: any number may stay as they are, illegal or not; new ones must be legal); `chooseTargets` answers, there is no `cancel`; nothing is asked when the answer is forced (no targets, one possible choice). Then `becameTarget` for each object the copy targets (Ward triggers for a copy) and the next copy is made. When it resolves a copy with an illegal target does what it can; when it leaves the stack any other way (countered, fizzled, an instant or sorcery that resolved) it ceases to exist: no zone change, no graveyard (CR 707.10a, 704.5e). A copy of a permanent spell that resolves becomes a token permanent (`zoneChanged` onto the battlefield, no `tokenCreated`: CR 707.10f, 608.3f) with its copiable values, its kicked fact and its counters, asking what a permanent entering asks. A `triggerSubject` spell that was countered in response to the trigger is copied from its last-known information.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-copy-spell.test.ts` (the copy above the original with the same targets, controlled by the copier, the spell with no card; not cast: no `spellCast`, not counted, prowess untouched; the event and the view in both seats; both resolve, the copy first, only the card in the graveyard; a countered copy ceases to exist with no zone change; a spell with no targets copied without a question; X, kicked and modes copied; the question with the legal targets and what the copy has, leaving the target, choosing a new one, an illegal target refused unless it is the copy's own, the original keeping its targets, the effect going on after the question, "copy it" with no question, an illegal target kept and the copy doing what it can; the opponent copying my spell (their copy, their choice); Ward triggering for a copy; a trigger copying with and without new targets, a spell countered in response to the trigger copied from last-known information; an activated ability copying only a spell its controller controls; a target that left leaves nothing to copy). Mutation-checked: kicked not copied fails 2 tests, X not copied 2, modes not copied 3, a permanent copy that ceases instead of becoming a token 1, a copy that always becomes a token 1, an illegal current target not offered 1, the question never asked 17, a copy controlled by the original's controller 1, last-known information ignored 2, no `becameTarget` for a copy 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 27, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-129, web step). A copy on the stack has the badge "copy" (`ObjectView.copy`, `data-copy`, `CastBadges` in `ui/board/CardFace.tsx`) and the name, text and art of the card it copies (the hover preview reads them by name); the preview says "A copy of a spell: not a card". The `targets` decision with `forCopy` reads "Choose new targets for the copy of X." (`ui/decisions/target-prompt.ts`), starts with `current` selected (`ui/decisions/ui-state.ts`), the confirm button reads "Keep the targets" while the picks equal `current` (`sameTargets` in `targeting.ts`) and "Confirm" otherwise, and there is no Cancel (as for a trigger). The legal targets panel lists the copy's own target too; to change a target the player clicks the chosen chip off and another on. The log reads "You copy X: the copy is put on the stack." (`game/describe.ts`). Tests: `apps/web/test/copies-model.test.ts`, `e2e/copies.e2e.ts` (hidden decks `synthetic-storm`, `synthetic-replicate`: a copy keeps a target, another goes to the player).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Copy target instant or sorcery spell. You may choose new targets for the copy.` (6 cards, 2 of them unlocked by the line alone; the template's cards are Flare of Duplication, Reverberate, Reiterate, Twincast, Fury Storm and Refuse // Cooperate) lowers to the node above, declaring `effect:copySpell`; `{7}, {T}: Copy target instant or sorcery spell you control. You may choose new targets for the copy.` (2 cards, Twinning Staff and Lithoform Engine: an activated ability whose target slot has `controller: 'you'`), `When ~ enters, copy target instant or sorcery spell. You may choose new targets for the copy.` (Dualcaster Mage: an enters trigger with that target slot) and `Whenever you cast a spell of the chosen type, copy that spell.` (Reflections of Littjara: a `castSpell` trigger, `{ kind: 'copySpell', spell: { kind: 'triggerSubject' }, newTargets: 'no' }`, the filter `chosenType` and `by: 'you'`) lower to the same effect. "You may choose new targets for the copy" is `newTargets: 'may'`; its absence is `'no'`. Not read: `Copy target instant or sorcery spell, then return it to its owner's hand. You may choose new targets for the copy.` (1 card), "Whenever you cast or copy an instant or sorcery spell, ..." (6 cards in three shapes: needs a trigger that also sees `spellCopied`), "copy ~ for each creature sacrificed this way" and the copies of abilities (ROADMAP).
- Notes: the copy keeps the alternative cost the spell was cast for (overload changes the text; evoke and dash marks go to the token); it never keeps `flashback`, `free` or the cast count. A copy of a split card's half is that half.

### "Storm"

- CR: 702.40a, 702.40b, 603.3b, 603.4, 113.7a, 601.2i, 707.10, 707.10c, 707.10f, 500.5
- Gap class: K (a keyword) plus a count of the spells cast this turn
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `storm`, `Effect` `storm`), `ast/features.ts`, `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/validate.ts` (the effect only in a self cast trigger), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`STORM_ABILITY`), `rules/effects.ts` (the `storm` case), `rules/cast.ts` (`finishCast` counts the spell and records the count), `rules/flow.ts` (`startTurn` resets), `state/types.ts` (`SeatState.spellsCastThisTurn`, `StackData.spellsCastBefore`), `view.ts` (`SeatView.spellsCastThisTurn`), `invariants.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'storm' } }]`, which derives `triggered { castSpell by you self: true, effect: storm }`. The effect `storm` takes no fields and is valid only as the whole effect of that trigger. Several instances trigger separately.
- Decision and action: when the spell is cast the trigger goes on the stack above it (CR 603.3b). Resolving it makes one copy for each spell cast this turn before the storm spell, by every player (spells countered or still on the stack count, copies never do): the number is `StackData.spellsCastBefore`, written into the spell as it was cast, so a spell cast in response to the trigger does not add and a storm spell countered in response is still copied from its last-known information. Each copy may get new targets (the `forCopy` question, one for each copy, in turn). The copies are on the stack above the original, so they resolve before it; a copy of a permanent spell becomes a token. A copy is not cast: it triggers no Storm of its own.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-storm.test.ts` (the trigger above the spell; no copy of a copy; no earlier spell, one, three; the opponent's spells counted; the number fixed as cast; reset each turn; a spell countered in response copied from last-known information; one question for each copy, each going somewhere else; X, kicker and mode copied; two instances making four copies; a permanent spell making tokens with their enter triggers, a countered copy of it; a copy made by another effect not counted and not storming; the supported features and validation: the effect refused outside "when you cast this spell", unknown variants of `copySpell` refused with their messages); `ast-supported.test.ts`. Mutation-checked: a count one too high fails 14 tests, a count that includes copies 13, no new targets 6, the opponent's spells not counted 1, no reset each turn 1, the count read when the trigger resolves 2, no trigger for the keyword 16.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 27, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-129, web step). The chip "Stm" ("Storm", `ui/board/keywords.ts`); the trigger on the stack reads "When you cast this spell" / "Copy this spell for each other spell cast before it this turn." (`game/copy-text.ts`, `data-copy-wording`); the player strip shows "Spells cast this turn N" (`SeatView.spellsCastThisTurn`, `data-spells-cast`) only while a storm card is in the viewer's hand. Tests: `copies-model.test.ts`, `e2e/copies.e2e.ts` (three storm spells: two copies, one question each). Limit: the number of spells cast before a storm spell (`StackData.spellsCastBefore`) is not in the view, so the trigger cannot say "(N)"; the counter of the strip is the count so far this turn.
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Storm` (33 cards blocked, 16 unlocked by the line alone: Flusterstorm, Grapeshot, Empty the Warrens, Chatterstorm, Stormscale Scion, Tempest Technique, Tendrils of Agony, Weather the Storm, Temporal Fissure, Sprouting Vines, ...; a keyword line, the reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above, declaring `keyword:storm`. The long form "When you cast this spell, copy it for each other spell cast before it this turn. ..." is not lowered separately.
- Notes: the AI casts a Storm spell after every other spell worth casting. A turn in which the other spells use up the mana leaves the storm spell for a later turn (ROADMAP).

### "Replicate {2}"

- CR: 702.56a, 702.56b, 601.2b, 601.2f-h, 603.4, 113.7a, 707.10, 733
- Gap class: K (a keyword) with a count question as the spell is cast
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `replicate`, `Effect` `replicate`, `Condition` `replicated`), `ast/features.ts`, `ast/validate-keywords.ts` (mana only, no X, not empty), `ast/validate-cast.ts` (the condition needs the keyword), `ast/supported.ts` (one per face), `rules/alt-costs.ts` (`replicateCostOf`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`CostRequest.replicas`), `rules/cast.ts` (`toReplicate`, `answerReplicas`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`REPLICATE_ABILITY`), `rules/effects.ts`, `api.ts`, `state/decisions.ts` (`ChooseXDecision` purpose `replicate`), `state/types.ts` (`StackData.replicated`), `state/events.ts` (`spellCast.replicas`), `view.ts` (`ObjectView.replicated`), `invariants.ts`, `testing/` (`replicas` verb and cast option)
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'replicate', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }] } }]` (mana only, no X, not empty, one per face), which derives `triggered { castSpell by you self: true, interveningIf: replicated, effect: replicate }`.
- Decision and action: as the spell is cast, after the kicker and before X and the targets, the caster is asked `chooseX { purpose: 'replicate', min: 0, max, cost, perX: { mana: 0, life: 0 } }`, `max` being the most times the whole cost (with the kicker as it was answered) could be paid; nothing is asked when it could not be paid even once; `chooseX` answers, `cancel` rewinds (CR 733). The cost is charged once for each time (the total shown, the largest X, the legal actions and the payment all use the same number). The count is `StackData.replicated` and `spellCast.replicas` (absent for none). With a count of at least 1 the trigger goes on the stack above the spell (with none it does not trigger, CR 603.4); it makes one copy for each time the cost was paid, from last-known information if the spell was countered in response, each with the new-targets question. A copy has the count too but only casting triggers Replicate.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-replicate.test.ts` (the question with its range and the legal answers, none when it cannot be paid once, the order count then targets then payment, the total cost for 0 to 3 times, cancel; no trigger when not paid, two copies with a question each, a spell with no targets, a copy that does not replicate again and a copy of a replicated spell made by another effect, a spell countered in response; the kicker asked first and the copies kicked with the cost of both, the kicker declined, X after the count with the copies having the same X, a permanent spell making tokens; the supported features and validation refusals: X, a cost that is not mana, an empty cost, two abilities, the effect outside "when you cast this spell", the condition without the keyword); the AI (`casual-copies.test.ts`: as many times as it can pay, half for an {X} spell, the copies aimed at the opponent). Mutation-checked: a cost not charged fails 7 tests, the question skipped 12, one copy too many 7, the trigger without its condition 1, the condition ignoring last-known information 1, the count computed without the kicker 1, a copy that forgets the count 1, X not reduced by the replicate cost 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 27, 2026-10-26
- Web: done (D-129, web step). The question is the `XDialog` count dialog (title "Replicate", "Replicate {R}: how many times? You can afford up to 3.", steppers "Fewer copies" / "More copies", starts at 0, "Not replicated." / "Replicated 2 times: {R} × 2 added to the cost.", the confirm button "Don't replicate" / "Replicate once" / "Replicate 2 times"; `ui/decisions/x-picker.ts` `isReplicate`, `replicatePrompt`). The spell has the badge "replicated 2 times" (`ObjectView.replicated`, `replicatedWords` in `ui/format.ts`) and the log "You cast X, replicated 2 times." (`spellCast.replicas`); the chip "Rep" reads "Replicate {R}" from the Oracle text; the trigger on the stack reads "Copy this spell 2 times." while the spell is on the stack (else "... for each time its replicate cost was paid."). Tests: `copies-model.test.ts`, `e2e/copies.e2e.ts` (the dialog, two copies).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Replicate {M}` (16 cards, 7 unlocked by the line alone; the most popular are Changing Loyalty, Hatchery Sliver, Consign to Memory, Shattering Spree, Super Combo, Mists of Lórien, Lose Focus, Gigadrowse, Stream of Thought and Pyromatics; the example `Replicate {2}`; a keyword line, the reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above, declaring `keyword:replicate` and `cost:mana`. A replicate cost with an {X} or that is not mana is refused by `validateCard` with a reason.
- Notes: several instances of Replicate on one card are not run (`isSupported` says so). The AI pays as many times as it can; half for a spell with an {X}.

### Not built in this slice

Casualty (CR 702.153; 14 cards, 5 by it alone: it needs an optional additional cost that chooses a permanent as the spell is cast and a reflexive trigger; see D-129 and `docs/ROADMAP.md`); copies of activated and triggered abilities; "except" copies; "may cast the copy" (CR 707.12); copies "for each player or object it could target" (CR 707.10d, 707.10e); "Whenever you cast or copy an instant or sorcery spell" (6 cards); "Copy target instant or sorcery spell, then return it to its owner's hand"; several instances of Replicate; a Storm count as an `Amount` or a condition on the number of spells cast. See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Counted amounts that read the game (D-131)

Engine slice of 2026-10-26 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 28, shapes in D-131). The amounts, the filter and the conditions that the largest family of blockers of the owner's five decks (`reports/decks.md`, parser version 32) needs. Real wordings are from that report's "Blockers across the decks" table (row numbers `#` in D-131). SYNTHETIC fixtures: `packages/engine/src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-amounts.ts` (names start with "SYNTHETIC "; their text describes the AST, not a real card). AI soak: `packages/ai/test/amount-games.test.ts` (the `amounts` deck style, which also holds the cards of the next sections: whole games, random and CasualAgent seats, every game replayed to the same hash, rules restated from the events and the board before each action; D-126, 20 games per run). Engine tests: `test/scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts`, and `test/scenario-xx-amounts-rules.test.ts` for what the mutation check found untested (clamps, zones and counter kinds of the amounts, the three comparisons of `amountIs`, a tapped token copy that asks its own question, a count of creatures to return, `all` as the recipient of damage and of a prevention shield, a creature of a spell that is gone, `eachPlayer` in turn order on the opponent's turn, what the validator refuses). Mutation-checked on a scratch copy of the engine (78 mutations of the rules and the validator for this slice and the next sections, about five for each primitive, in two passes). First pass, 28 mutations: every one was killed, after the two that first survived got a test (a sum in an effect that reads only its first part, and a player counted once for "who drew a card this way" however many cards they drew). Second pass, 50 mutations: 21 survived, 17 got a test that kills them (the clamp of a total, the zone of an aggregate, the counter kind, `atMost` and `equal`, the source of a condition, a tapped token that asks a question, `eachPlayer` in turn order, `all` as a recipient, the creature that deals damage being gone, a returned count that is an amount, and five refusals of the validator); one was dead code and was removed (the condition on a designation passed a trigger subject that the validator never lets it read); three are equivalent and stay: `eachOpponent` in turn order (the engine has exactly two seats, so there is one opponent; `apnapOrder` itself is tested with four seats), the `excluded` object of an aggregate (it is the spell being cast, which is not on the battlefield, so only a filter on the stack could see it) and an attacker that left the battlefield but is still listed in the combat (it is no longer an object that any filter reads). Engine side only: each line needs the parser (a later stage).

### "X is ..." text: "you draw X cards and lose X life, where X is the number of Zombies you control"

- CR: 107.3c, 107.3a, 608.2c, 611.2c
- Gap class: P (a plumbing decision, no new node)
- Code: none new: `rules/amounts.ts` `amountOf` reads the definition wherever it stands; slots that held only numbers take an `Amount` now (`Replacement` `entersWithCounters.count`, `ChooseSelector.count` of `sacrifice` and `moveToZone`)
- AST node: the definition is written into each slot that reads X: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'draw', player: you, count: <definition> }, { kind: 'loseLife', player: you, amount: <definition> }] }`. There is no `defineX` node and nothing stored: CR 107.3c says the value is the definition and "may change while that spell or ability is on the stack", so each instruction reads it as it runs (CR 608.2c). The announced `Amount` `x` (D-103) stays for X in a cost only.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("where X is ...": draw and lose by Zombies; "sacrifice a Zombie, then draw X" reads the Zombies left; damage and life from Swamps; X damage to each creature, X the creatures; -1/-1 for each Swamp; enters with a counter for each Zombie card in your graveyard, counted as it enters and zero when there is none).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: the words of every amount kind in `game/amount-text.ts` (`amountPhrase`: "the number of Zombies you control", "the greatest power among creatures you control", "the total power of creatures you control", "your devotion to black", "the number of colors in your commander's color identity", "half the number of ..., rounded down", "twice ...", "... plus ...", "that creature's toughness", "the number of age counters on it"), the filters in `game/filter-text.ts`, and the gist in `ui/decisions/ability-label.ts` (the stack panel, the target prompt and the ability chooser): a slot that reads the game says X and the effect ends with "where X is ..." once (`createDefinitions`; two different amounts are X and Y), a multiple of a count reads "2 life for each Zombie you control". The preview panel shows the card's Oracle text; the view carries the AST of an ability on the stack, never of a spell, so a spell on the stack is worded by its text. An amount or a filter the words cannot say reads "a number", never a guess.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `At the beginning of your upkeep, you draw X cards and lose X life, where X is the number of Zombies you control.` (Graveborn Muse), `~ deals X damage to each creature, where X is the number of creatures on the battlefield.` (Chain Reaction), `~ deals X damage to target creature and you gain X life, where X is the number of Swamps you control.` and the `or planeswalker` form (Tendrils of Corruption, Consuming Corruption), `When ~ enters, put X +N/+N counters on target creature, where X is that creature's power.` (Thickest in the Thicket: `addCounters` with `count: powerOf target`) and `~ enters with a +N/+N counter on it for each Zombie card in your graveyard.` (Diregraf Colossus: the replacement `entersWithCounters` with `count: countOf { zone: 'graveyard', subtypes: ['Zombie'], controller: 'you' }`). The parser binds "X" to the definition of the same sentence ("where X is ...") and puts a copy of the lowered amount in every slot that says X; X that is not defined by the text stays the announced X.
- Notes: "X ... as you cast ~" (Monstrous Onslaught, a value fixed at announcement) is not built.

### "the greatest power among creatures you control", "the total power of creatures you control"

- CR: 208.1, 202.3, 107.1b, 601.2f, 603.4
- Gap class: P (an amount over a filter)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Amount` `aggregate`), `rules/amounts.ts` (`aggregateOf`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkAmount`, `checkStateAmount`), `ast/validate-cost.ts` (a cost reduction reads it), `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'aggregate', how: 'greatest' | 'total', stat: 'power' | 'toughness' | 'manaValue', filter: ObjectFilter }`: over the objects the filter matches (its zone, the battlefield by default; `controller` relative to the ability's controller), the largest or the sum of the stat as `derive` gives it now; an object with no such stat is left out; nothing matched is 0; a negative result is 0 (CR 107.1b). Valid anywhere an amount is, as the amount of a condition (`amountIs`) and of a cost reduction; not in a static power or toughness (it would read layer 7, CR 613.8).
- Decision and action: none; read as the instruction runs.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("the greatest and the total of a stat": +X/+X and trample with X the greatest power; read as it resolves, the 3/1 killed in response; no creature is 0; the opponent's creatures not counted; total toughness; a noncreature adds nothing; the greatest mana value of artifacts; a cost reduced by total power, castable and not). Mutation-checked: greatest as total fails 3 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: the words of the aggregate are in `amountPhrase` (see the first section); the stack shows them for an ability that reads one.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Until end of turn, creatures you control gain trample and get +X/+X, where X is the greatest power among creatures you control.` (Overwhelming Stampede: a `grantKeyword` and a `modifyPt` of the same each-selector, `power` and `toughness` the same aggregate), `Draw cards equal to the greatest power among creatures you control.` (Rishkar's Expertise: `draw` with the aggregate as `count`), `~ costs {X} less to cast, where X is the total power of creatures you control.` (Ghalta: a `static` `costReduction` with `spells: { kind: 'this' }` and the aggregate as `amount`) and the condition of `At the beginning of combat on your turn, if creatures you control have total power N or greater, target creature you control gains haste until end of turn.` (the `interveningIf` is `amountIs` of the total power with `compare: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: N }`; the trigger is `step beginCombat whose you`). Not read: `... where X is the greatest power among creatures you control as you cast ~.` (133), "the creature with the greatest power or tied" (19, 150), "the greatest mana value among your commanders" (161). The declared needs: `amount:aggregate`, `condition:amountIs`, `static:costReduction`.

### "your devotion to black", "the number of colors in your commander's color identity"

- CR: 700.5, 700.5a, 903.4
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Amount` `devotion`, `commanderColors`), `rules/amounts.ts` (`devotionSymbols`, `devotionOf`), `rules/mana-outcomes.ts` (`commanderIdentity`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'devotion', colors: ['B'] }` (one or more of W, U, B, R, G): the mana symbols that are one of those colors among the mana costs of the permanents the controller of the ability controls, as `derive` gives them now; a hybrid symbol counts once if any of its colors is named, a Phyrexian symbol counts for its color, a generic, colorless or snow symbol for none. `{ kind: 'commanderColors' }`: how many of the five colors the controller's commanders have in their color identity (0 with no commander).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("devotion and the colors of the commanders": the drain by devotion with the life gained "this way"; a land counts nothing; only the controller's permanents; hybrid, monocolored hybrid and Phyrexian symbols; the colors of a two-color commander; no commander). Mutation-checked: hybrid not counted fails 1 test, commander colors off by one 2.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: devotion and the commanders' colors in `amountPhrase` (see the first section).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `When ~ enters, each opponent loses X life, where X is your devotion to black. You gain life equal to the life lost this way.` (Gray Merchant of Asphodel: an enters trigger whose effect is `sequence [loseLife eachOpponent amount devotion ['B'], gainLife you amount lifeLostThisWay]`). `{M}, {T}, Pay life equal to the number of colors in your commanders' color identity: Draw a card.` (War Room, four of the five decks) has the amount but its cost does not: "pay life equal to an amount" is a cost with an amount, which the engine does not have (`payLife.amount` is a number, D-107), so the line stays unsupported (ROADMAP). The declared needs: `amount:devotion`, `amount:commanderColors`.

### "half the number of ..., rounded down", "N life for each ...", "... plus ..."

- CR: 107.1a, 604.3, 613.4a
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Amount` `half`, `multiple`, `sum`; `StaticPtAmount` and `DefinedPtAmount` gain `sum`), `rules/amounts.ts`, `rules/derive-count.ts` (`staticAmountOf`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkStaticPtAmount`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'half', of: Amount, round: 'down' | 'up' }` (the rounding is always stated, CR 107.1a), `{ kind: 'multiple', factor: 2 or more, of: Amount }`, `{ kind: 'sum', of: Amount[] }` (two or more). `times` stays the negative-capable shape of a power or toughness. In a characteristic-defining ability or a static "+N/+N" a `sum` is of counts only (`countOf`).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("half the number ...": 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 7 Zombies make 0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 3 tokens; 2 life for each creature with power 4 or more; a sum in an effect; power and toughness equal to a sum over several zones and following the board). Mutation-checked: rounding up fails 3 tests, a multiple that forgets its factor 1, a sum in an effect that reads only its first part 2, a static sum that reads only its first part 2.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: half, multiple and sum in `amountPhrase`; "N life for each ..." for a multiple of a count (`quantity` in `game/amount-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `At the beginning of your upkeep, create X N/N black Zombie creature tokens, where X is half the number of Zombies you control, rounded down.` (Endless Ranks of the Dead: an upkeep trigger, `createToken` with a custom 2/2 black Zombie definition and `count: half of countOf { types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Zombie'], controller: 'you' } round down`), `You gain N life for each creature you control with power N or greater.` (Shamanic Revelation: `gainLife` with `multiple` of the count, the filter `power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: N }`) and `~'s power and toughness are each equal to the number of Zombies on the battlefield plus the number of Zombie cards in all graveyards.` (a `characteristicPt` with the `sum` of a battlefield count and `countOf { zone: 'graveyard', subtypes: ['Zombie'] }` for both stats, the face printing `'*'`). The declared needs: `amount:half`, `amount:multiple`, `amount:sum`.

### "that creature's toughness", "for each +1/+1 counter on ~"

- CR: 113.7a, 608.2h, 122.1, 601.2f
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Amount` `toughnessOf`, `countersOn`), `rules/amounts.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkAmountObject`: a target, `this` or the trigger's permanent; `powerOf` accepts the same now)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'toughnessOf', of: ObjectRef }` and `{ kind: 'countersOn', of: ObjectRef, counter: '+1/+1' }`; `of` may be `{ kind: 'triggerSubject' }` in a trigger about a permanent (last-known information once it left). A cost reduction or a condition reads the counters on `this` only.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("an object's toughness and counters": life equal to the toughness of the creature that entered; last-known information when it died in response; a cost reduced for each counter on the permanent, with and without counters). Mutation-checked: toughness read as power fails 1 test, counters read from last-known information only 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: `toughnessOf` and `countersOn` in `amountPhrase` ("that creature's toughness", "the number of age counters on it"); "its power" when the effect already names the creature (a bite).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Whenever ~ or another creature you control enters, you gain life equal to that creature's toughness.` (Verdant Sun's Avatar: an `entersBattlefield` trigger on the filter `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }`, which includes the source, with `gainLife` of `toughnessOf triggerSubject`), `Whenever another Angel or Cleric you control enters, you gain life equal to that creature's toughness.` (Righteous Valkyrie: the filter `other: true`, `anyOf` of the two subtypes) and `Angel spells and Human spells you cast cost {M} less to cast for each +N/+N counter on ~.` (Herald of War: a static `costReduction` whose `spells` is the filter with `anyOf` of the subtypes, `controller: 'you'`, and `amount: countersOn this '+1/+1'`). The declared needs: `amount:toughnessOf`, `amount:countersOn`, `selector:triggerSubject`.

### "the number of creatures attacking you", "for each opponent who has more cards in hand than you"

- CR: 508.1b, 506.4, 109.5
- Gap class: P (a filter flag and an amount over the players)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.attackingYou`, `Amount` `opponentsWithMore`), `rules/select.ts` (`attackersOfPlayer`), `rules/filter-match.ts`, `rules/amounts.ts` (`playerStatOf`, `opponentsWithMore`)
- AST node: `countOf { types: ['creature'], attackingYou: true }`: the creatures whose attack target is the controller of the ability as a player, not those attacking a planeswalker or battle that player controls; refused where `derive` matches filters (a static ability, a static count). `{ kind: 'opponentsWithMore', stat: 'life' | 'cardsInHand' | 'creatures' }`: the opponents still in the game whose stat is greater than the controller's.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("creatures attacking you; opponents with more cards in hand": two Spiders for two attackers; an attacker of the planeswalker is not counted; none; a Clue for the one opponent with more cards, none for one with as many). Mutation-checked: counting planeswalker attackers fails 1 test, "more" read as "at least as many" 3.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: `attackingYou` in the filter words ("the number of creatures attacking you", `game/filter-text.ts`) and "the number of opponents with more cards in hand than you" in `amountPhrase`.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Create X N/N green Spider creature tokens with reach, where X is the number of creatures attacking you. Prevent all combat damage that would be dealt this turn by non-Spider creatures.` (Arachnogenesis: the first sentence lowers to `createToken` of a custom 1/2 Spider with the `reach` keyword and the count above; the second sentence needs a prevention shield limited by a creature type of the source, which `preventDamage.by` (one object) cannot say, so the card stays unsupported) and `At the beginning of your upkeep, investigate once for each opponent who has more cards in hand than you.` (Wojek Investigator: `createToken` of the Clue preset with `count: opponentsWithMore cardsInHand`). The declared needs: `selector:attackingYou`, `amount:opponentsWithMore`.

### "if you have N or more creature cards in your graveyard": a condition on an amount

- CR: 603.4, 608.2c, 107.1b
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Condition` `amountIs`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkStateAmount`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'amountIs', amount: Amount, compare: { cmp: 'atLeast' | 'atMost' | 'equal', value: number } }`: any amount that reads the game now (a count, an aggregate, devotion, the commander's colors, the counters on this permanent, opponents with more, or arithmetic on those; `validateCard` refuses X, a target, the trigger subject and `thisWay` there with a reason). As an intervening "if" (CR 603.4: as the ability triggers and as it resolves), in a `conditional` effect, in an `activateOnlyIf` and in an alternative cost.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("an amount compared with a number": exactly 8 triggers, 7 does not; checked again as it resolves; three creature cards in the graveyard against two). Mutation-checked: "at least" read as "more than" fails 5 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: the condition words in `game/condition-text.ts` ("if the number of creature cards in your graveyard is 3 or greater", "if the total power of creatures you control is 8 or greater"), used by the intervening "if" of a trigger on the stack and by a conditional effect ("If ..., ...; otherwise, ..."); "if an opponent has more life than you" for `opponentsWithMore` of at least 1.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `At the beginning of your upkeep, if you have N or more creature cards in your graveyard, you may return target creature card from your graveyard to your hand.` (Oversold Cemetery: `step upkeep whose you`, `interveningIf: amountIs countOf { zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } atLeast N`, `optional: true`, a `moveToZone` of the target card to the hand; the old feature id `condition:cardsInGraveyard` is replaced by `condition:amountIs`), `At the beginning of combat on your turn, if creatures you control have total power N or greater, ...` (Surrak, the Hunt Caller) and the conditions of `{M}, {T}: You may play the exiled card without paying its mana cost if creatures you control have total power N or greater.` (not run: it also needs the play of an exiled card). The declared needs: `condition:amountIs`.

### Not built in this slice

"Pay life equal to an amount" as a cost (War Room), "add mana for each" as a production with an amount (Mana Geyser), comparing two amounts ("the artifact with the greatest mana value or tied", "the creature with the greatest power or tied"), a filter compared with an amount (Loot's "mana value less than or equal to the number of lands you control"), life above the starting total, "that many" (a count chosen by a player: "discard any number of cards, then draw that many"), the power of a creature sacrificed as a cost ("where X is the sacrificed creature's power"), "greatest mana value among your commanders", an amount of power or toughness in a static ability other than a count or a sum of counts, "X as you cast ~". See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Tokens that enter tapped (D-131)

### "Create thirteen tapped N/N black Zombie creature tokens."

- CR: 110.5b, 111.1, 111.2, 614.1c, 603.2e
- Gap class: P (a flag)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `createToken.tapped`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `rules/tokens.ts` (`createTokens(..., enterTapped)`), `rules/enter-token.ts`, `rules/effects.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`token:tapped`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'createToken', token, count: Amount, tapped: true }`: the tokens enter the battlefield tapped; nothing "becomes tapped" (no tap event, no trigger of "whenever this becomes tapped", CR 603.2e). The count may be any amount, the controller any player selector.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-amounts.test.ts` ("tokens that enter tapped": thirteen tapped Zombies; a tapped Treasure for each Zombie, which stays tapped; a token not asked to enter tapped enters untapped; no tap event for the tokens). Mutation-checked: ignoring the flag fails 3 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: `game/token-text.ts` words the token from its definition ("Create 13 tapped 2/2 black Zombie creature tokens", "Create X tapped Treasure tokens, where X is ...", "a token that's a copy of target creature"); the token is drawn tapped like any tapped permanent.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Create thirteen tapped N/N black Zombie creature tokens.` (Army of the Damned: `createToken` of a custom Zombie definition with `count: 13` and `tapped: true`; the flashback line is read separately), `Whenever you cast a Zombie spell, create a tapped N/N black Zombie creature token.` (Diregraf Colossus: a `castSpell` trigger with the Zombie filter and `by: 'you'`), `Whenever ~ deals combat damage to a player, that player discards a card and you create a tapped N/N black Zombie creature token.` (Liliana's Reaver: a `dealsCombatDamage` trigger; "that player discards a card" needs a selector for the player that was dealt the damage, which the engine does not have, so the card stays unsupported) and `Each player sacrifices N creatures of their choice. You create N tapped N/N black Zombie creature tokens.` (Necrotic Hex: a `sequence` of the sacrifice below and `createToken` with `tapped: true`). The declared needs: `token:tapped`, `token:custom`.

## Several players, each player sacrifices, mill and what happened this way (D-131)

Engine tests: `test/scenario-xx-player-effects.test.ts`; fixtures: `synthetic-player-effects.ts`.

### "You and target opponent each draw N cards": several players in one selector

- CR: 101.4, 121.2, 608.2c
- Gap class: P (a selector, used by every effect that takes a `player`)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`PlayerSelector` `all`), `rules/select.ts` (`apnapOrder`, `resolvePlayers`, `isPlayerSelector`), `rules/effect-support.ts` (`PLAYER_ONLY_KINDS`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkPlayerSelector`, `PLAYER_ONLY`), `ast/features.ts` (`selector:all`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'all', of: PlayerSelector[] }`: the players the members name, each once, in the order of CR 101.4 (the active player first, then turn order). `eachPlayer` and `eachOpponent` follow that order too now (they started with the controller). `validateCard` refuses fewer than two members, a nested list, and a list where one player is needed (`becomeMonarch`); scry and surveil stay the controller's alone.
- Decision and action: none of its own; a `sacrifice` with `all` asks each player in that order (`choosePermanent`, purpose `sacrifice`).
- Tests: ("several players in one selector": both draw two; the target must be an opponent; a player named twice acts once; the opponent acts first on their own turn and the active player on theirs; four seats in turn order; each player chooses their sacrifice, the active player first). Mutation-checked: a player named twice acting twice fails 2 tests, turn order ignoring the active player 3.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: `game/player-text.ts` words the selector ("You and target opponent each draw 2 cards", "Deal 2 damage to you and target opponent"); the same words serve every effect that takes a `player`.
- What the parser must produce: the real line `You and target opponent each draw N cards.` (Secret Rendezvous: a sorcery with the target slot `{ kind: 'player', filter: 'opponent' }` and `draw` with `player: { kind: 'all', of: [you, target t1] }`).

### "Each player sacrifices N creatures of their choice", "for each creature sacrificed this way"

- CR: 701.21, 701.21a, 608.2d, 101.4
- Gap class: P (an `Amount` count on the existing node, and what an earlier instruction did)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ChooseSelector.count: Amount`, `Amount` `thisWay`), `rules/effects.ts` (the `sacrifice` and `destroy` cases), `rules/step-outcome.ts` and `rules/stack.ts` (`thisWay` added up on the resolution), `state/types.ts` (`ResolutionContext.thisWay`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`thisWay` available after a `sacrifice`, `destroy` or `draw` of the same sequence)
- AST node: the existing `{ kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter, count }, player: { kind: 'eachPlayer' } }` with `count` a number or an `Amount`; and `{ kind: 'thisWay', what: 'sacrificed' | 'destroyed' | 'playersDrew' }`: the permanents sacrificed (or destroyed) by the earlier effects of this resolution, and the players who drew at least one card in the earlier draw effects, added up over the resolution. Valid only after an effect of that kind in the same sequence (a `conditional` may not have run, so it does not count).
- Decision and action: each player chooses among their own permanents (`choosePermanent`, purpose `sacrifice`, `permanentsChosen` event) in the order of CR 101.4; a player with no more candidates than the number loses them all without being asked; the AI answers as it always did. The sacrifices follow each player's choice one after the other, not together (ROADMAP).
- Tests: ("each player sacrifices": a single candidate chosen for the player, a choice for the other; none; two each and a tapped Zombie for each sacrificed; none sacrificed, no token; each other player sacrifices and the controller gets a token; the number is an amount; X = 0 asks nothing; a counter for each creature destroyed this way, tokens included; a card for each opponent who drew, once however many they drew, and not for one whose draw went to the command zone). Mutation-checked: not counting the sacrificed fails 2 tests, not counting the destroyed 1, counting every card a player drew 1, a count that ignores the amount 3.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: "Each player sacrifices 2 creatures of their choice" and "Each opponent sacrifices X creatures of their choice, where X is ..." in the gist; each player is asked once in turn order (CR 101.4), and the sacrifice dialog of D-110 says whose sacrifice it is and what caused it (`sacrificeCause` in `ui/decisions/permanents.ts`, shown in `PermanentPanel.tsx`: "Your sacrifice, from your Name: Each player sacrifices a creature of their choice. Each player chooses their own, the active player first."; for a spell the view has no AST, so the line names the card and the panel's rules text says the rest). The log of `sacrificed` already names the player.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `When ~ enters, each player sacrifices a creature of their choice.` (Fleshbag Marauder: an enters trigger, `sacrifice` of a `choose` of creatures with `player: eachPlayer`), `Each player sacrifices N creatures of their choice. ...` (the same with `count: N`), `Each other player sacrifices a creature of their choice. You create a N/N black Zombie creature token for each creature sacrificed this way.` (Syphon Flesh: `sacrifice` with `player: eachOpponent`, then `createToken` with `count: thisWay sacrificed`), `Each opponent draws a card, then you draw a card for each opponent who drew a card this way.` (Cut a Deal: `draw eachOpponent 1`, then `draw you count thisWay playersDrew`) and `When ~ enters, destroy all creatures with flying. Put a +N/+N counter on ~ for each creature destroyed this way.` (Whiptongue Hydra: `destroy` then `addCounters` of `this` with `count: thisWay destroyed`; the filter "with flying" is not built, so the card stays unsupported). Not read: `Target opponent sacrifices a creature with the greatest power among creatures they control.` (171), "Each player sacrifices all permanents they control that are N or more colors." The declared needs: `amount:thisWay`, `selector:all`, `selector:choose`.

### "Mill N cards"

- CR: 701.17, 701.17a, 701.17b, 121.4, 903.9a
- Gap class: P (a keyword action)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `mill`), `rules/mill.ts` (`millCards`), `rules/effects.ts`, `state/events.ts` (`milled`), `ast/features.ts` (`effect:mill`), `apps/web/src/game/describe.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'mill', player: PlayerSelector, count: Amount }`: each player the selector names puts the top `count` cards of their library into their graveyard, one after another, top first, as many as it holds when fewer (CR 701.17b), and loses nothing for an empty library (CR 121.4 is about drawing); 0 does nothing. Each card moves through the zone-change pipeline (a new object, CR 400.7; a commander is put into the command zone afterwards by its owner's choice, CR 903.9a, as for any card going to a graveyard).
- Decision and action: none. Event: `milled { seat, objects }` (the new objects, in the order milled; none when nothing was milled).
- Tests: ("mill": two cards and 2 life; a library with fewer cards than the number, all of them, no loss; an empty library, no event; each player mills X; X = 0; the top cards first). Mutation-checked: milling from the bottom fails 1 test, one card too many 4, an event for nothing 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: the log line ("You mill 2 cards.", `game/describe.ts`, level `major`) and the gist of a `mill` ("Each player mills X cards, where X is ...").
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `When ~ enters, mill N cards and you gain N life.` (Mire Triton: an enters trigger with `sequence [mill you N, gainLife you N]`) and `When ~ enters, mill N cards.` (Undead Butler: a `mill` with `player: you`). "Each player mills ..." and "target player mills ..." use the same node with that selector. The declared needs: `effect:mill`.

## A creature that deals damage (D-131)

### "Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to target creature or planeswalker you don't control."

- CR: 120.1, 120.3, 701.14, 608.2b, 113.7a, 615, 903.10a
- Gap class: P (a damage source other than the spell)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `dealDamage.from`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkDamageSource`), `rules/effects.ts` (the `dealDamage` case), `ast/features.ts` (`selector:damageSource`), `packages/ai/src/casual-bite.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'dealDamage', from: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, amount: { kind: 'powerOf', of: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }, to: { kind: 'target', id: 't2' } }`: the creature is the source (CR 120.1): its lifelink, deathtouch, infect and wither apply (CR 120.3), a prevention effect for that source applies (CR 615), the damage is never combat damage (so no commander damage, CR 903.10a), and the creature takes none back (a bite, not a fight, CR 701.14). `from` is a `target`, `this` or the `triggerSubject` permanent (validated). A `from` that is no longer a legal target deals no damage (CR 608.2b: the spell does what it can; with every target gone it is countered); a `triggerSubject` or `this` that left deals the damage as it last existed (CR 113.7a, 608.2h).
- Decision and action: none; the AI picks the agent's strongest creature for the slot that deals the damage.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-player-effects.test.ts` ("a creature deals damage equal to its power, and takes none back": the Giant kills the Spider and has no damage; not combat damage with the creature as the source; lifelink; deathtouch; wither counters; loyalty of a planeswalker; the power read as it resolves after a pump; the creature gone in response, no damage; their creature gone in response; the creature as its own recipient; the creature that entered as the source and its last-known power; no commander damage; X damage by the number of Dragons), `packages/ai/test/casual-bite.test.ts`. Mutation-checked: damage dealt by the spell instead of the creature fails 4 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: the log names the creature as the source of the damage ("Name deals 3 damage to Name.") and the gist words it: "Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to target creature or planeswalker an opponent controls" (a bite), "Target creature you control deals 3 damage to ..." (`damageText` in `ui/decisions/ability-label.ts`); a spell's own damage stays "Deal 3 damage to ...".
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to target creature or planeswalker you don't control.` (Bite Down: two target slots, the first `{ filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } }`, the second with `controller: 'opponent'` and `anyOf` of creature and planeswalker; the node above), `Whenever a creature you control enters, it deals damage equal to its power to any target.` (Warstorm Surge: an enters trigger with an `anyTarget` slot, `from: triggerSubject`, `amount: powerOf triggerSubject`), `Whenever ~ or another Dragon you control enters, it deals X damage to any target, where X is the number of Dragons you control.` and `Whenever a Dragon you control enters, it deals X damage to any target, where X is the number of Dragons you control.` (Scourge of Valkas and Dragon Tempest: a Dragon trigger with `from: triggerSubject` and the count as the amount). Not read: `Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to target creature you don't control. If the creature you control has trample, excess damage is dealt to that creature's controller instead.` (Ram Through: the trample clause), `Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to each other creature and each opponent.` (Chandra's Ignition: "each other creature" cannot exclude the damage source). The declared needs: `selector:damageSource`, `amount:powerOf`, `selector:triggerSubject`.

## Designations and conditions about players (D-131)

### "If you're the monarch", "if an opponent has more life than you"

- CR: 725.1, 603.4, 608.2c
- Gap class: P (a generic condition on a player)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Condition` `playerHasDesignation`, `Designation`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkConditionPlayers`), `ast/features.ts` (`condition:playerHasDesignation`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'playerHasDesignation', player: PlayerSelector, designation: 'monarch' }`: holds when the holder of the designation is one of the players the selector names ("if you're the monarch" is `you`, "if an opponent is the monarch" `eachOpponent`; `not` for the negative). The designation is closed to `'monarch'` for now (the monarch is a designation, CR 725.1, not an emblem; the initiative and the like can join the union). A condition names its players with `you`, `eachPlayer`, `eachOpponent`, `defendingPlayer`, `all` of those, or the controller or owner of `this`; a target or the trigger subject is refused with a reason. "If an opponent has more life than you" is `amountIs` of `opponentsWithMore life` at least 1, not a condition of its own.
- Decision and action: none. As an intervening "if" it is read as the ability triggers and as it resolves (CR 603.4); in a `conditional` effect as the instruction runs.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-player-effects.test.ts` ("conditions about players": the monarch gets the Dragon; not the monarch, nothing triggers; an opponent is the monarch; lost in response, nothing is created; "... instead" as a `conditional` with an `else`; "an opponent is the monarch"; more, equal and fewer life; more creatures). Mutation-checked: an inverted designation fails 6 tests, "nobody is the monarch" counted as holding 3.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 28, 2026-10-26
- Web: `playerHasDesignation` in `game/condition-text.ts` ("if you're the monarch", "if an opponent is the monarch", "you're not the monarch" under `not`); the monarch itself is already in the view and the log (D-127).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `At the beginning of your upkeep, if you're the monarch, create a N/N red Dragon creature token with flying.` (Skyline Despot: `step upkeep whose you` with the `interveningIf` above and `createToken` of a custom 4/4 red Dragon with flying), `At the beginning of your upkeep, create a N/N white Spirit creature token with flying. If you're the monarch, create a N/N white Angel creature token with flying instead.` (Court of Grace: one `conditional` with the condition, the Angel as `effect` and the Spirit as `else`: "instead" is the else branch), `When ~ enters, if an opponent controls more creatures than you, create a N/N white Angel creature token with flying.` and `When ~ enters, if an opponent has more life than you, you gain N life.` (both Linvala, the Preserver: an enters trigger with `interveningIf: amountIs opponentsWithMore creatures/life`). Not read: `Enchanted creature doesn't untap during its controller's untap step unless that player is the monarch.` (158: a static ability with a condition on a player). The declared needs: `condition:playerHasDesignation`, `condition:amountIs`, `amount:opponentsWithMore`.

### Not built in this slice

Statics that read a designation or a life total ("as long as you're the monarch", "as long as you have 40 life", 147, 219, 158); the initiative and the other designations; the simultaneous sacrifices of CR 101.4; "whenever you mill" and "whenever you investigate" triggers; a bite that excludes its source from an "each other creature" recipient; trample excess; a keyword filter ("creatures with flying", "without flying"); preventing damage by creatures that are not of a type. See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## Filter parts (D-133)

### "nontoken", "non-Zombie", "colorless", "with flying", "without flying"

- CR: 111.1, 205.3d, 105.2c, 702, 613.8
- Gap class: P (parts of the generic object filter)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter` `token`, `notSubtypes`, `colorless`, `keywords`, `notKeywords`), `rules/filter-match.ts` (`matchesFilterWith`, `FilterSubject.keywords`), `rules/select.ts` (`describeObject` gives the keywords of the derived abilities, or of the last-known abilities of a permanent that left), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkFilter`, `readsKeywords`), `ast/validate-attach.ts` and `ast/validate-keywords.ts` (a static subject, a count in a static ability and Affinity may not name a keyword), `ast/features.ts` (`selector:token`, `selector:nontoken`, `selector:notSubtypes`, `selector:colorless`, `selector:keywords`)
- AST node: `token: false` is "nontoken", `token: true` "token" (a permanent that is not represented by a card, including a permanent that was a copy of a spell); `notSubtypes: ['Zombie']` is "non-Zombie"; `colorless: true`; `keywords: ['flying']` is "with flying" (all of them) and `notKeywords: ['flying']` "without flying" (none of them), only keywords without parameters. They read anywhere a filter is read except where `derive` reads it: a static ability's subject and the objects a static amount counts refuse a keyword (it would depend on the layer it applies in, CR 613.8); every other field works there.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-filter-parts.test.ts` (a nontoken lord and a lord of tokens, "other nontoken", the opponent's creatures, "all non-Zombie creatures get -1/-1" killing a 1/1, a colorless artifact creature and not a black one, "with flying", "without flying", two keywords needing both, and `validateCard` refusing a keyword in a static subject and a keyword with a parameter). Mutation-checked: dropping the token test, `notSubtypes`, `colorless`, `notKeywords`, turning `every` into `some` for two keywords and dropping the static-subject refusal each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` and `game/filter-text.ts` word `token: false` ("nontoken"), `notSubtypes` ("non-Zombie"), `colorless` and the keywords (" with flying", " without flying"); a counted noun of tokens (`token: true`) is not worded yet (the phrase is `null`, never a guess).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Nontoken creatures you control get +N/+N and have vigilance.` (row 32: two statics over `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', token: false }`), `Other nontoken creatures you control get +N/+N and have vigilance.` (34: the same with `other: true`), `Colorless creatures you control get +N/+N.` (122: `colorless: true`), `Whenever you cast a colorless spell, you gain N life.` (153: `castSpell` with `{ stackKind: 'spell', colorless: true }`), `~ deals N damage to each non-Dragon creature.` (112: `dealDamage` to an `each` of `{ types: ['creature'], notSubtypes: ['Dragon'] }`), `~ deals X damage to each creature without flying and each planeswalker.` (114: an `each` with an `anyOf` of `{ types: ['creature'], notKeywords: ['flying'] }` and `{ types: ['planeswalker'] }`), `When ~ enters, destroy all creatures with flying. Put a +N/+N counter on ~ for each creature destroyed this way.` (49: `destroy` of an `each` with `keywords: ['flying']`, then `addCounters` with `countOf`-free `thisWay destroyed`), `When ~ enters, you may exile target nontoken creature.` (147: an optional `exile` of a `target` of `{ types: ['creature'], token: false }`). The declared needs are the `selector:` ids above.

## Triggers on a filtered subject and the player an event is about (D-133)

### "Whenever a nontoken creature you control dies", "Whenever ~ or another nontoken Zombie you control dies", "Whenever another nontoken creature you control enters"

- CR: 603.2, 603.10a, 111.7, 700.4
- Gap class: P (the subject of a trigger is an `ObjectFilter`; the parts are the filter parts above)
- Code: `rules/triggers.ts` (`matchesWhat`: the filter is read against the object as it is, or as it last was for a look-back trigger), `ast/types.ts` (`TriggerSubject`)
