- AST node: `{ kind: 'dies', what: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', token: false } }`, and the same `what` for `entersBattlefield`, `attacks`, `blocks`, `becomesBlocked`, `becomesTapped`, `leavesBattlefield` and `dealsCombatDamage`; `castSpell` has `filter` and `by`. "~ or another Zombie ..." is two triggered abilities (the second has `other: true`), which cannot both trigger for one event.
- Decision and action: none. A token that dies still triggers "dies" abilities that do not say nontoken (it ceases to exist after, CR 111.7); creatures that die together see each other die (CR 603.10a).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-trigger-subjects.test.ts` ("dies: a filtered subject, tokens and last-known information": a nontoken creature of mine makes a Zombie, a token dying triggers nothing, the opponent's creature does not, "~ or another nontoken Zombie" once for itself, another nontoken Zombie, not a token or a non-Zombie, and Wrath kills a Rider, a Zombie and an Open Graves together and the abilities of all of them see each other die; "enters": another nontoken Zombie, a +1/+1 counter and haste "on it" until end of turn, not a token, a creature with flying gets haste, "it" gone before the ability resolves; "blocks, leaves the battlefield, is cast and deals combat damage").
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` (`filterPhrase` with the new parts; the controller before what the creature has, "a creature you control with flying"; a creature type names its type, "another nontoken Zombie you control"), `ui/decisions/ability-label.ts` ("it" for the trigger subject of an effect, "that player", "you gain that much life", the group of an `each` selector with its controller, and the gist of `grantKeyword` and `restrict`).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Whenever a nontoken creature you control dies, create a N/N black Zombie creature token.` (64), `Whenever a nontoken creature you control dies, ~ deals N damage to you and you draw a card.` (65), `Whenever a nontoken creature you control dies, investigate.` (149), `Whenever ~ or another nontoken Zombie you control dies, create a N/N black Zombie creature token.` (85: two abilities, `dies` of `this` and `dies` of the filter with `other`), `Whenever another nontoken Dragon you control enters, create a N/N red Dragon creature token with flying.` (150), `Whenever ~ or another Zombie enters, all non-Zombie creatures get -N/-N until end of turn.` (83: two `entersBattlefield` abilities, `modifyPt` of an `each` with `notSubtypes`), `Whenever ~ or another artifact you control enters, put a +N/+N counter on ~. It can't be blocked this turn.` (84: `addCounters` and `restrict` on `this`), `Whenever a Dragon you control enters, put a +N/+N counter on ~. Until end of turn, ~ becomes a Dragon in addition to his other types and gains flying.` (148: `addCounters` on `this`, an additive `setTypes` and `grantKeyword` on `this`), `Whenever you cast a noncreature or Dragon spell, ~ deals N damage to each opponent.` (71: `castSpell` with `{ stackKind: 'spell', anyOf: [{ notTypes: ['creature'] }, { subtypes: ['Dragon'] }] }`).

### "Whenever a creature attacks you or a planeswalker you control", "that creature's controller"

- CR: 508.1b, 506.3, 603.2
- Gap class: P (a field of the attack trigger and an existing player reference)

"That creature's controller" is `{ kind: 'controllerOf', of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' } }` (D-111).

### "Whenever ~ is dealt damage", "you gain that much life"

- CR: 120.3, 603.2, 603.2g

One trigger for each source that damages the permanent, at the moment the damage is dealt: it triggers for the damage that kills it, and damage that is entirely prevented triggers nothing (CR 603.2g).

- Tests: `scenario-xx-trigger-subjects.test.ts` ("is dealt damage": Lightning Bolt, two blockers that deal damage separately make two triggers even though the creature dies, "that much" is each source's own damage, a prevented Bolt triggers nothing, "a creature you control is dealt combat damage" not for noncombat damage). ### "that player": the player the event is about

- CR: 603.2, 121.1, 120.2a, 601.2i, 702.21a
- Gap class: P (a player selector)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`PlayerSelector` `triggerPlayer`), `rules/select.ts` (`resolvePlayers`), `rules/triggers.ts` (`Occurrence.player` for `attacks`, `castSpell`, `drawn`, `dealsCombatDamage` to a player, `becomesTarget`, `permanentSacrificed` and `dealtDamage`), `rules/trigger-subject.ts` (`readsTriggerPlayer`), `ast/validate.ts` (`hasPlayer`), `rules/effect-support.ts` (`PLAYER_ONLY_KINDS`)

### "it" for the creature that entered: a continuous effect or a counter on the trigger subject

If the creature is gone when the ability resolves nothing happens to it (CR 608.2b: it is a new object).

It gains haste until end of turn.` (67: `addCounters` and `grantKeyword` on `triggerSubject`) and `Whenever a creature you control with flying enters, it gains haste until end of turn.` (62: `entersBattlefield` of `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', keywords: ['flying'] }`).

### Not built in this group

Triggers for several creatures at once ("whenever N or more artifact creatures you control deal combat damage to a player", 56; "whenever you attack with N or more creatures", 152; "whenever another player attacks with N or more creatures", 151; "choose one that hasn't been chosen this turn", 55); a trigger of a card in a graveyard (58); the commander as a filter (59, 73); a condition on what the permanent that left had ("if it had counters on it", 68); stun counters (74). ## Lords and grants until end of turn to a filter (D-133)

### "Other Zombie creatures get +N/+N.", "Skeletons you control and other Zombies you control get +N/+N and have deathtouch.", "All Zombies gain menace until end of turn."

- CR: 611.3a, 613.4c, 613.1f, 611.2c, 613.7
- Gap class: none for the engine (the nodes ran since D-094 and D-115); the parser is the gap
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `modifyPt` and `grantKeyword` with a `filter` subject, `Effect` `modifyPt` and `grantKeyword` with an `each` selector), `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`affects`, `staticEffectsOn`), `rules/continuous.ts` (`runContinuous`)
- AST node: a static lord is `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter }, power: N, toughness: N } }` and, for "and have deathtouch", a second `grantKeyword` static with the same subject; "other" is `other: true`, "Skeletons you control and other Zombies you control" is an `anyOf` with `other` on the second member. A grant until end of turn is `grantKeyword` or `modifyPt` of `{ kind: 'each', filter }` with `duration: 'untilEndOfTurn'`: the set is the creatures there are as the effect resolves, a creature that enters later is not in it (CR 611.2c).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-lords.test.ts` (it affects both players' Zombies and not itself or a non-Zombie, two lords each affect the other, a Zombie that enters later is affected at once and the bonus ends when the lord is destroyed mid-turn, marked damage stays so a lord leaving can kill, the `anyOf` union with `other`, a grant to every Zombie of both players that ends with the turn and misses a Zombie that came later, and the set of a "power 3 or greater" grant read as it resolves).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29 (tests only), 2026-10-27
- Web: none.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Other Zombie creatures get +N/+N.` (33), `Skeletons you control and other Zombies you control get +N/+N and have deathtouch.` (39), `{M}, {T}: All Zombies gain menace until end of turn.` (96: an activated ability with a mana and a tap cost), `{M}: Dragons you control get +N/+N until end of turn.` (170) and `Whenever ~ attacks, each creature you control with power N or greater gets +N/+N and gains trample until end of turn.` (158: `modifyPt` and `grantKeyword` of an `each` with `power`).

## Conditional static abilities (D-133)

### "As long as you control your commander, ~ gets +N/+N and creatures you control have vigilance.", "~ gets +N/+N as long as you have N or more life.", "~ has hexproof as long as it's untapped."

- CR: 611.3a, 613.8, 903.3, 903.3d, 119.1, 725
- Gap class: P (an optional condition on a static ability, read by a derive-safe evaluator)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Ability` `static.condition`, `Condition` `lifeTotal`, `thisMatches`, `controlCommander.yours`), `rules/static-conditions.ts` (`staticConditionHolds`: `controlsAtLeast`, `controlCommander`, `lifeTotal`, `thisMatches`, `playerHasDesignation`, `and`, `or`, `not`), `rules/condition-reads.ts` (the life and commander reads shared with `rules/conditions.ts`), `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`Modifier.condition`, `conditionHolds`: the answers are kept per state), `rules/attack-tax.ts` (the condition of an `attackTax`), `ast/validate-static-condition.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkCondition`), `ast/features.ts` (`ability:staticCondition`, `condition:lifeTotal`, `condition:thisMatches`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', condition, static }`. The statics that go through `derive` (`modifyPt`, `grantKeyword`, `grantAbility`, `restriction` except `cantBeCountered`) and `attackTax` take one; any other static ability with a condition is refused with a reason. Conditions: `controlsAtLeast` (a count by type, subtype, color, controller or tapped status), `controlCommander` (`yours: true`: a commander its controller owns; a commander that is in the command zone is not controlled), `lifeTotal { compare, aboveStarting? }` (the life total, or its difference from the starting life total, `GameSettings.startingLife`), `thisMatches { filter }` (this permanent against a filter, `tapped: false` for "untapped"), `playerHasDesignation`, `and`, `or`, `not`. A condition that reads power, toughness, keywords, the combat, X, kicks or counters is refused (CR 613.8). The same `lifeTotal`, `thisMatches` and `controlCommander` run as an intervening "if", in a `conditional` effect and as "activate only if".
- Decision and action: none. The condition is read each time the effects on a permanent are worked out (CR 611.3a), so a life total that changes, a commander that leaves or a creature that taps changes the board at once.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-conditional-statics.test.ts` (the commander: none, mine on the battlefield, in the command zone, another player's that I control, mine controlled by the opponent, and the bonus ends when it is destroyed; life: 29 and 30, 46 and 47 above a starting life of 40, gaining life turns it on; untapped hexproof, tapped, two Druids read each on their own, untapping next turn; three artifacts, the opponent's do not count; a Zombie that counts "another Zombie" does not loop; a restriction with a condition; an attack tax that applies only while untapped; the monarch; `activate only if` at 47 and 46 life; the validator: the accepted conditions, the refused ones, only the statics that act in a layer, the life numbers). Mutation-checked: dropping the condition, `yours`, the starting-life difference, the controller of the count, the per-source key of the answers, the validator's refusal and the attack tax condition each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: done (D-133, web step). The preview panel's "Rules in words" (`data-play-rules`) says each static ability of a visible permanent: `game/static-text.ts` words a lord, a grant of a keyword and a restriction from the AST ("Other Zombies get +1/+1.", "Skeletons you control and other Zombies you control have deathtouch.", "This creature gets +2/+2 as long as you control your commander."), the group from its filter with `pluralSubtype` in `game/filter-text.ts` (any creature type: Elves, Wolves, Merfolk; a unit test loops over every type of CR 205.3m), and the condition with `game/condition-text.ts` (`controlsAtLeast` "you control 3 or more artifacts", `controlCommander` "you control your commander", `lifeTotal` "you have 30 or more life" / "at least 7 life more than your starting life total", `thisMatches` "it's untapped"). The host sends the notes (`game/static-notes.ts`); a shape the words do not cover gives no note, never a guess. The view carries no static abilities, so the words come from the printed face of the card, as for the play rules. Tests: `apps/web/test/subjects-model.test.ts`, `filter-text.test.ts`, `e2e/subjects.e2e.ts` (a Skeleton lord on the board).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `As long as you control your commander, ~ gets +N/+N and creatures you control have vigilance.` (8: two statics with `condition: { kind: 'controlCommander', yours: true }`, a `modifyPt` of `this` and a `grantKeyword` over `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }`), `As long as you have at least N life more than your starting life total, creatures you control get +N/+N.` (10: `lifeTotal` with `aboveStarting`), `~ gets +N/+N as long as you have N or more life.` (174: `lifeTotal`), `~ has hexproof as long as it's untapped.` (115: `thisMatches { tapped: false }`), `As long as you control N or more artifacts, ~ has hexproof.` (118: `controlsAtLeast`), `As long as ~ is untapped, creatures can't attack you or planeswalkers you control unless their controller pays {M} for each of those creatures.` (120: an `attackTax` with `planeswalkers` and a `thisMatches` condition) and the activation restriction `Activate only if you have at least N life more than your starting life total and only as a sorcery.` (the end of row 108, Speaker of the Heavens: `activateOnlyIf` with `lifeTotal`, `timing: 'sorcery'`).

### Not built in this group

A static ability that grants an ability that is not a mana ability (9, Tyrant's Familiar), grants a keyword that has a parameter or is not in `GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS` (121), sets base power and toughness or creature types over a filter (133), names a keyword in its subject (134), or is an Aura's "unless that player is the monarch" (127); "can't attack or block unless you control another creature with power N or greater" (136); statics read outside `derive` other than the attack tax and the cast permission with a condition; the condition on the other statics (hand size, land drops, look at the top card, optional untap, cost reductions). See `docs/ROADMAP.md`.

## A limit on blockers (D-133)

### "Each creature you control with power N or greater can't be blocked by more than one creature."

- CR: 509.1b, 509.1a, 702.111b, 613.8
- Gap class: P (a restriction, read where blockers are declared)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Restriction` `cantBeBlockedByMoreThanOne`), `rules/combat-legality.ts` (`validateBlocks`, `evades`), `rules/combat-flow.ts` and `state/decisions.ts` (`DeclareBlockersDecision.attackers[].maxBlockers`), `ast/features.ts` (`RESTRICTIONS`), `ast/supported.ts` (a restriction's static subject may compare power)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'restriction', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } } }, restriction: 'cantBeBlockedByMoreThanOne' } }`. The power is read with every effect that changes it (counters, pumps, other statics; the restriction is read after the layers).
- Decision and action: `declareBlockers` lists `maxBlockers: 1` for an attacker with the restriction; an answer with two blockers for it is refused ("That attacker cannot be blocked by more than one creature"); with menace nothing can block it (two blockers needed, one allowed), so no question is asked about it.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-block-limits.test.ts` (a creature with power 4 or greater: one blocker is legal, two are refused, `maxBlockers` in the decision, power 3 may be blocked by two, the opponent's big creature is not "you control", no limit without the Banner, a creature pumped to 5 and one with a +1/+1 counter are limited, menace with the limit, and flying with the limit). Mutation-checked: dropping the count check, the menace rule, `maxBlockers` and the power read after the layers each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: done (D-133, web step). `ui/board/keywords.ts` has the chip "Blk≤1"; the block dialog refuses a second blocker on an attacker whose `maxBlockers` is reached (`atBlockLimit`, `clickAttacker` and `blockableAttackers` in `ui/decisions/block.ts`, also for a drag), says why ("X can't block A (can't be blocked by more than one creature).", `blockRefusals`), and the attacker's badge reads "at most 1 blocker" / "1 blocker, the most". Moving the blocker that holds the place is allowed. The random agent keeps within `maxBlockers`. Tests: `subjects-model.test.ts`.
- What the parser must produce: the real line `Each creature you control with power N or greater can't be blocked by more than N creature.` (24, Challenger Troll: the static above, N as 4 and 1). Not built: `~ can't be blocked by creatures with power N or less.` (111) and `Juggernauts you control can't be blocked by Walls.` (132), which name the blockers.

## Costs that tap or sacrifice other permanents and the sacrificed creature (D-133)

### "Tap three untapped Zombies you control", "Tap two untapped artifacts you control", "Tap another untapped creature you control"

- CR: 118.3, 602.2, 302.6, 603.2e, 733
- Gap class: P (a cost kind and a choice)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Cost` `tapPermanents`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`breakDown`, `costRefusal`), `rules/activate.ts` (`payTap`, `tapChosen`, `answerTapCost`), `rules/sacrifice.ts` (`tapCandidates`), `state/decisions.ts` (`ChoosePermanentDecision.purpose` `tap`), `state/events.ts` (`permanentsChosen` purpose `tap`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkCost`), `ast/supported.ts` (`activationCostIssues`), `ast/features.ts` (`cost:tapPermanents`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'tapPermanents', filter, count }`: `count` untapped permanents the player controls that match `filter` (`controller` is `you` or left out, the zone is the battlefield, `tapped` is not said). The source may be one of them unless the filter says `other`; summoning sickness does not apply (only a creature's own {T} ability is held back, CR 302.6). It is paid before the sacrifice and the discard, and `validateCard` refuses it together with a mana cost, another tap cost or a crew cost (the permanents could have been tapped for mana first).
- Decision and action: with exactly `count` candidates the engine taps them and says so (`permanentsChosen`, `automatic: true`); with more it asks a `choosePermanent` decision (`purpose: 'tap'`, `forCost: true`, `min` = `max` = `count`, `legal` the candidates), answered by `choosePermanents`, and `cancel` rewinds the activation (CR 733).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-creature-costs.test.ts` ("Tap three untapped Zombies you control": three pay it with the source among them, fresh Zombies may be tapped, two are not enough, a tapped Zombie does not count, the opponent's and non-Zombies do not, a choice among four with the purpose `tap`, wrong counts refused, cancel restores the position, three `tapped` events; "Tap two untapped artifacts": a non-creature artifact and the artifact creature itself, a card with the cost and no creature of its own; "Tap another untapped creature you control, {T}": the source is left out). Mutation-checked: dropping the untapped test, the candidate count and the automatic tap each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: done (D-133, web step). `game/cost-text.ts` words the cost ("Tap two untapped artifacts you control", from the filter, so "three untapped Elves you control" and "another untapped creature you control" read right). The `choosePermanent` of purpose `tap` is the permanent panel ("Tap to pay for Name", region "Permanents to tap", badge "Tap") with the prompt "Choose 2 untapped Zombies you control to tap as a cost." read from the tap cost of the ability on the stack (`tapPrompt` in `ui/decisions/permanents.ts`; "Choose 2 permanents to tap as a cost for Name." when the view does not show it), Confirm needs exactly the count, and Cancel rewinds the activation. The log reads "You tap A and B to pay for Name." ("You must tap ...: no other choice." when automatic; `game/describe.ts`, CR 602.2b), then each permanent's own "taps" line. Tests: `subjects-model.test.ts`, `e2e/subjects.e2e.ts` (hidden deck `synthetic-subjects`: the picker, Cancel, two artifacts tapped, a card drawn).
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `Tap N untapped Zombies you control: You draw a card and lose N life.` (40: `tapPermanents` of `{ types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Zombie'] }` with count 3), `Tap N untapped artifacts you control: Target creature can't be blocked this turn.` (41: `restrict` of a target with `cantBeBlocked`) and `Tap N untapped artifacts you control: Draw a card.` (137). Not built: `You may pay {M} and tap N untapped creatures you control with flying rather than pay ~'s mana cost.` (162: an alternative cost) and `Whenever ~ attacks, you may tap X untapped Myr you control. ...` (78: an effect with a chosen number).

### "Sacrifice two artifacts", "Sacrifice another creature ... where X is the sacrificed creature's power"

- CR: 701.21, 602.2, 113.7a, 608.2h
- Gap class: P (a count on the sacrifice cost and a reference to what was sacrificed)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Cost` `sacrifice.count`, `ObjectRef` `sacrificedCost`), `rules/activate.ts` (`paySacrifice`, `sacrificeChosen`, `rememberSacrificed`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`CostBreakdown.sacrificeCount`), `rules/select.ts` (`resolveObjects`), `state/types.ts` (`StackData.sacrificedAsCost`, `ResolutionContext.costSacrificed`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkSacrificedCost`: valid as the object of `powerOf`, `toughnessOf`, `manaValueOf` or `countersOn` in an activated ability whose cost sacrifices something), `ast/validate-cost.ts` and `ast/validate-mana-ability.ts` (no count for a spell or a mana ability), `ast/features.ts` (`selector:sacrificedCost`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sacrifice', what: filter, count: 2 }` (2 or more, an activated ability only, not with `this`) and `{ kind: 'powerOf' | 'toughnessOf' | 'manaValueOf' | 'countersOn', of: { kind: 'sacrificedCost' } }`. The amount reads the last-known information of the sacrificed permanent (its power with every effect it had, a token's too, an object that left is read through its old id); for a cost of two permanents the first is read.
- Decision and action: `choosePermanent` with `purpose: 'sacrifice'`, `forCost`, `min` = `max` = `count` unless exactly that many qualify (then the engine sacrifices them all); `cancel` rewinds.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-creature-costs.test.ts` ("Sacrifice two artifacts": two are sacrificed, one is not enough, a choice among three, two `sacrificed` events; "Sacrifice another creature": X is the power of a 5/5, of a Zombie with a lord and a counter, of a token, 0 for a 0/8 Wall, the creature is gone before the ability resolves and the ability still reads it, cancel restores the position, and "another" leaves the source out; toughness and mana value of what was sacrificed, a token's mana value is 0, sacrificing the source itself; the validator). Mutation-checked: forgetting the sacrificed ids, a reference that resolves to nothing, the count check and the automatic choice each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: `game/cost-text.ts` ("Sacrifice two artifacts"), `game/amount-text.ts` ("the sacrificed creature's power"); the sacrifice dialog already asks for `min`..`max` permanents.
- What the parser must produce: the real lines `{M}, Sacrifice N artifacts: Draw a card.` (93, Sai: the number words are 2 here: `sacrifice` of `{ types: ['artifact'] }` with `count: 2`, a mana cost and a `draw`) and `{M}, {T}, Sacrifice another creature: Create X N/N black Zombie creature tokens, where X is the sacrificed creature's power.` (95, Ghoulcaller Gisa: a mana, tap and `sacrifice` of `{ types: ['creature'], other: true }`, then `createToken` with `count: { kind: 'powerOf', of: { kind: 'sacrificedCost' } }`; "where X is" writes the amount into the slot as in D-131). Not built: `{M}, {T}, Return an artifact you control to its owner's hand: You may put an artifact card from your hand onto the battlefield.` (94).

## If a creature would die (D-133)

### "If a nontoken creature an opponent controls would die, instead exile that card and create a N/N black Zombie creature token."

- CR: 614.1a, 614.6, 614.12, 700.4, 704.3, 903.9a, 603.10a
- Gap class: P (a replacement kind in the zone-change pipeline)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Replacement` `wouldDie`), `rules/die-replacement.ts` (`replacementSources`, `dieReplacementFor`), `rules/zones.ts` (`moveObject`: before the commander replacement, after Unearth's; the tokens are created after the exile), `rules/sba.ts` and `rules/effects.ts` (`destroy`) and `rules/effect-support.ts` (`withReplacers`): the permanents with the replacement as the event began are carried by `MoveRequest.replacers`, `ast/validate.ts` (`checkReplacement`), `ast/features.ts` (`replacement:wouldDie`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'wouldDie', what: filter, instead: 'exile', alsoCreate?: { token: preset | custom, count } }` on a permanent: each creature that `what` matches (relative to the controller of the permanent: `controller: 'opponent'` is its controller's opponents) that would be put into a graveyard from the battlefield by any means is exiled instead, it never dies, and the controller of the permanent creates the tokens. It is lost with "loses all abilities". Creatures that die together replace each other's deaths: a Kalitas that is destroyed with the opponent's creatures still replaces theirs. Several replacements for one creature: the oldest permanent's applies and nothing is asked (CR 616.1).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-die-replacement.test.ts` (destruction, lethal damage, zero toughness and a sacrifice are replaced; no "dies" ability triggers while "leaves the battlefield" does; my own creature and a token of the opponent die as usual; a commander may go to the command zone from exile; "if a creature would die, exile it instead" for every creature, a token exiled ceases to exist; Wrath and a state-based action kill the Collector and the opponent's creatures together and every replacement still applies; when the Collector is gone nothing is replaced; two Collectors each replace the other side; the validator). Mutation-checked: ignoring the batch, the state-based action batch, the destroy batch, the filter's controller read from the wrong side and the token's controller each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: done (D-133, web step). "If a nontoken creature an opponent controls would die, exile it instead and create a 2/2 black Zombie creature token." in the preview's "Rules in words" (`replacementWording` in `game/static-text.ts`, the creature group from `filterPhrase`, the token from `game/token-text.ts`); the log shows the creature going to exile as before. Tests: `subjects-model.test.ts`.
- What the parser must produce: the real line `If a nontoken creature an opponent controls would die, instead exile that card and create a N/N black Zombie creature token.` (30, Kalitas, Traitor of Ghet: the replacement above with `what: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'opponent', token: false }` and a custom 2/2 black Zombie). Not read: `When ~ dies or is put into exile from the battlefield, you may put her into her owner's library third from the top.` (141: a trigger, not a replacement).

## Casting a card from the graveyard (D-133)

### "You may cast this card from your graveyard as long as you control a Zombie."

- CR: 113.6b, 601.2a, 604.2, 903.8
- Gap class: P (a static ability of a card that works in the graveyard; the casting-zone machinery of D-124 fits)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `castFromGraveyard`), `rules/legality.ts` (`mayCastFromGraveyard`, `castZone`, `castableObjects`), `ast/validate.ts` (a face has it once and is not a land), `ast/validate-static-condition.ts`, `testing/refs.ts` (the DSL's `cast` finds a card of that name in the graveyard when none is in the hand), `ast/features.ts` (`static:castFromGraveyard`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'castFromGraveyard' }, condition? }`: while the card is in its owner's graveyard its owner may cast it, at the timing its type allows, for its ordinary mana cost and additional costs (no alternative cost is combined with it, no commander tax); the condition is read as it is cast with the owner as "you" and the card as "this". The spell is an ordinary spell: it triggers "whenever you cast", goes to the graveyard again if it does not resolve onto the battlefield, and `cancel` puts the card back.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object }` of a card in the graveyard, listed in the priority options after the ordinary casts.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-cast-from-graveyard.test.ts` (with a Zombie the card is castable and returns, without one it is not, the opponent's Zombie does not count, a card in hand is cast as always, only the owner, a card without the permission is not castable, creature timing, the Zombie leaving afterwards does not stop the spell, a countered spell goes to the graveyard, a sorcery without a condition resolves and is castable again, cancel restores the position, a new object each time; the validator: a land, a condition a static cannot read, two permissions). Mutation-checked: dropping the condition, the graveyard candidates and the one-permission rule each fail a test.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 29, 2026-10-27
- Web: done (D-133, web step). A plain `castSpell` option (no `alternative`) for a card in the graveyard is the card's own permission: the chip above the hand and the badge in the graveyard viewer read "Cast from graveyard", the menu item "Cast Name from graveyard" (`isGraveyardPermission`, `GRAVEYARD_CAST` in `ui/decisions/flashback.ts`, `OtherZonesStrip.tsx`); flashback and escape keep their own labels. The preview of a graveyard card says "You may cast this card from your graveyard as long as you control a Zombie." (the host reads the face of the public graveyards, `game/static-notes.ts`). Tests: `subjects-model.test.ts`, `e2e/subjects.e2e.ts` (a sorcery cast from the hand and again from the graveyard: two life lost).
- What the parser must produce: the real line `You may cast this card from your graveyard as long as you control a Zombie.` (92, Gravecrawler: `{ kind: 'static', condition: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Zombie'] }, count: 1 }, static: { kind: 'castFromGraveyard' } }`). Not built: `As long as this card is in your graveyard and you control a Mountain, creatures you control have haste.` (7: a static ability that works from the graveyard) and `{T}: You may cast target Zombie creature card from your graveyard this turn.` (109: an effect that gives a permission for the turn).

## Recursion, Gift and graveyard abilities (D-135)

The nine primitives behind the last cards of the Zombies precon and War Room. Every shape is tested over several creature types in `scenario-xx-recursion.test.ts`, `scenario-xx-gift.test.ts` and `scenario-xx-graveyard-permissions.test.ts`, and the node checks are in `ast-recursion-validate.test.ts`. The Oracle lines are the real ones extracted for the golden corpus (`packages/cards/test/fixtures/corpus/golden.jsonl`).

### "Return target creature card from your graveyard to your hand. If it's a Zombie card, draw a card."

- CR: 608.2c, 113.7a, 608.2h, 400.7
- Gap class: P (a condition that reads the card an effect moved)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Condition` `objectMatches`), `rules/conditions.ts` (`ConditionContext.targets`, `moved`), `rules/effects.ts` (`conditional` passes them), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkEffectCondition`), `ast/features.ts` (`condition:objectMatches`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'objectMatches', of: { kind: 'target', id } | { kind: 'moved' }, filter }` inside a `conditional` effect. The object is read as it is or, once it has left the zone it was in (the target of a spell that returned it to the hand is a new object there), from last-known information; the filter has no zone, controller or owner. The same node reads "If it's an Angel, put two +1/+1 counters on it" after a `moveToZone` to the battlefield (`addCounters` of `moved`).
- Decision and action: none; the targets decision of the spell is the ordinary one.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-recursion.test.ts` ("a condition on the card that was moved, for any creature type": Zombie, Goblin and Angel cards draw, another type does not, the answer is read from the card as it was, a negated condition, a target that is gone; counters if it is an Angel, Zombie), `ast-recursion-validate.test.ts`. Mutation checks (scratch copy): the condition always true, no last-known information, the filter zone fixed, the targets dropped.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: none (a spell is shown by its name and text).
- What the parser must produce: `Return target creature card from your graveyard to your hand. If it's a Zombie card, draw a card.` (Cemetery Recruitment, a sorcery): `spell.targets` `[{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } } }]` and `effect` `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, to: 'hand' }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'objectMatches', of: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, filter: { subtypes: ['Zombie'] } }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } }] }`. The same shape reads the Angels line of the Angels deck (`Return target creature card from your graveyard to the battlefield. If it's an Angel, put N +1/+1 counters on it.`: `to: 'battlefield'`, the condition's `of` is `{ kind: 'moved' }` and the effect an `addCounters` of `{ kind: 'moved' }`).

### "You may discard a card. If you do, draw a card.", "When you do, ..."

- CR: 603.12, 608.2c, 701.48a (Learn reads the same way), 603.3d
- Gap class: P (a counted "did it" and a reflexive triggered ability)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `may`, `reflexive`; `Condition` `didPrevious`), `rules/effects.ts` (`may`, the `performed` of the `move`, `sacrifice` and `mill` instructions), `rules/draw.ts` and `rules/search.ts` (`runDiscard`: the cards actually drawn and discarded), `rules/stack.ts` (`ResolutionContext.performed`), `rules/step-outcome.ts` (`performed`, `resetPerformed`), `rules/may-effects.ts` (`runReflexive`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`MAY_EFFECT_KINDS`, `Scope.mayAvailable`), `ast/supported.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'may', effect }` (the effect is a `discard`, `sacrifice`, `exile`, `moveToZone`, `draw` or `mill`), then `{ kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'didPrevious' }, effect }` or the last effect `{ kind: 'reflexive', targets?, effect }`. The controller is asked as the `may` comes up (the existing `confirmOptional` question); declining drops only that effect. "If you do" is true when the instructions since the `may` did something: a card discarded or drawn (an empty hand or library is not), a creature sacrificed, an object moved, a card milled. The reflexive trigger (CR 603.12) is a triggered ability of the source of the ability, created at once when the `may` was done; it goes on the stack the next time a player would receive priority and its controller chooses its targets then (CR 603.3d), so a card the first part put into the graveyard can be named, an ability with no legal target is removed, and the exiled source cannot be returned by it. It knows nothing of the targets of the ability that made it, and its controller is the controller of that ability.
- Decision and action: `confirmOptional` (no purpose), then `acceptOptional` or `declineOptional`; the reflexive ability's `targets` decision when it has several legal targets.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-recursion.test.ts` ("you may [do something]. If you do": discard then draw, declining, an empty hand, a cast trigger, milling, "what an earlier instruction did does not count", "the cards of one may count together"; the reflexive trigger of a dying creature and of a sacrificed one, the target chosen as it goes on the stack, no target removes it, declining creates nothing). Mutation checks: the reflexive trigger always created, a `may` that does not reset, a discard that always counts, the counts not accumulated, a move or a sacrifice not counted.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: the `confirmOptional` question and the stack entry are the existing ones; the reflexive ability is shown under its source like any triggered ability.
- What the parser must produce: `When ~ enters, you may discard a card. If you do, draw N cards.` (Bitter Reunion) and `Whenever you cast a creature spell, you may discard a card. If you do, draw a card.` (Hazoret's Monument): a triggered ability (`entersBattlefield` of `this`, `castSpell` of a creature spell by `you`) with `effect` `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'discard', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'didPrevious' }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: N } }] }`. `When this creature dies, you may exile it. When you do, return target creature card from your graveyard to your hand.` (Undead Butler): trigger `{ kind: 'dies', what: { kind: 'this' } }` with the effect `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'exile', objects: { kind: 'thisCard' } } }, { kind: 'reflexive', targets: [<creature card in your graveyard, id t1>], effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, to: 'hand' } }] }`; its other line, `When this creature enters, mill three cards.`, is the existing `mill`. The existing `mayPay` ("You may pay {4}. If you do, ...") stays as it is.

### "Sacrifice any number of other permanents, then draw that many cards."; "a creature with the greatest power among creatures they control"

- CR: 701.21, 608.2d, 608.2c
- Gap class: P (a sacrifice choice with no minimum, and a maximum of a stat)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ChooseSelector` `anyNumber`, `greatest`), `rules/sacrifice-choice.ts` (`greatestAmong`, `runChooseSacrifice`), `rules/effects.ts` (`sacrifice`), `state/types.ts` (the `chooseSacrifice` instruction), `ast/validate-parts.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { other: true }, anyNumber: true } }` followed by `{ kind: 'draw', player: you, count: { kind: 'thisWay', what: 'sacrificed' } }` (the permanents sacrificed by this resolution, D-131); `{ kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { types: ['creature'] }, greatest: 'power' }, player: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }` (a filter that names `controller: 'you'` means the player who sacrifices). The decision is `choosePermanent` with `purpose: 'sacrifice'`, `min` 0 and `max` the number of candidates for "any number" (asked even with one candidate, never for none); with `greatest` the candidates are those with the greatest power (or toughness or mana value, as `derive` gives it as the choice is made) and the sacrificing player chooses among the tied ones.
- Decision and action: `choosePermanent` (purpose `sacrifice`), answered with `choosePermanents` (none is legal for "any number").
- Tests: `scenario-xx-recursion.test.ts` ("sacrifice any number of other permanents, then draw that many cards": the player chooses which and how many, none, the permanent itself is not a choice, exactly one candidate is still asked), `scenario-xx-gift.test.ts` ("a creature with the greatest power": no choice with a single maximum, the opponent chooses among tied creatures only). Mutation checks: a minimum of 1, no question for one candidate, `greatest` ignored, `greatest` picking the least.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: the sacrifice dialog already asks for `min`..`max` permanents; its prompt has to say "any number" when `min` is 0.
- What the parser must produce: `When ~ enters, sacrifice any number of other permanents, then draw that many cards.` (God-Eternal Bontu): an `entersBattlefield` trigger of `this` whose effect is the sequence above. `Target opponent sacrifices a creature with the greatest power among creatures they control.` (Consumed by Greed, with its gift lines below): the `sacrifice` with `greatest: 'power'`, `player` the target.

### "When ~ dies or is put into exile from the battlefield, you may put her into her owner's library third from the top."

- CR: 603.6c, 603.10a, 400.7, 401.7
- Gap class: P (a trigger over several destination zones and a library position)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`leavesBattlefield.to` a zone or a list; `moveToZone.position` a number), `rules/triggers.ts` (`matches`), `state/ops.ts` (`addToZone`), `rules/zones.ts`, `rules/effect-support.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`trigger:leavesToAnyOf`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'leavesBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' }, to: ['graveyard', 'exile'] }` (a list has two or more distinct zones; a creature that dies goes to the graveyard) with the effect `{ kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'thisCard' }, to: 'library', position: 3 } }` (or the ability's own `optional: true` with the bare `moveToZone`: "you may" at the start of the effect and at the head of the ability both work). `position` is `'top'`, `'bottom'` or a number of 2 or more: the card goes below the first N - 1 cards, or last when the library has fewer than N cards (CR 401.7). `thisCard` is the card the permanent became in the graveyard or in exile, as long as it is still there.
- Decision and action: `confirmOptional`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-recursion.test.ts` (dies: third from the top; exiled: the same ability from exile; declining; a library of one card and of two: last). Mutation checks: an off-by-one position, only the first zone of the list.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` words a list of zones ("is put into a graveyard or is put into exile").
- What the parser must produce: `When ~ dies or is put into exile from the battlefield, you may put her into her owner's library third from the top.` (God-Eternal Bontu): the trigger above (the `trigger.or` rule lowers "dies or is put into exile" to `to: ['graveyard', 'exile']`; "her" and "it" are `thisCard`) and the effect above.

### Gift a card

- CR: 702.174 (a to m), 601.2b, 603.4, 707.10, 733
- Gap class: K (a keyword with a cast-time choice and two abilities of its own)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `gift`, `Condition` `giftPromised`, `PlayerSelector` `giftRecipient`, `TargetSpec.ifGiftPromised`), `rules/cast-gift.ts` (`toGift`, `answerGift`, `giftEffectOf`), `rules/cast.ts` (`answerGiftChoice`, `toReplicate` hands on to `toGift`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`GIFT_ABILITY`), `rules/stack.ts` (the gift draw before the other effects), `rules/legality.ts` (`targetSpecsOf`), `rules/zones.ts` (the permanent remembers `giftTo`), `rules/copy-spell.ts`, `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/select.ts`, `state/types.ts` (`StackData.giftTo`, `GameObject.giftTo`), `state/decisions.ts` (`ConfirmOptionalDecision` purpose `gift`, `giftTo`), `state/events.ts` (`spellCast.gift`), `api.ts` and `legal-actions.ts` (the answer and the `cancel`), `ast/validate-cast.ts`, `testing/driver.ts` (`cast(..., { gift })`, `CastHandle.gift`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'gift', gift: 'card' } }`; `{ kind: 'giftPromised' }` as a condition (an intervening "if", a `conditional`); `{ kind: 'giftRecipient' }` as a player; a target slot with `ifGiftPromised: true`. The question is asked after the kicker and replicate questions and before X and the targets, only when a promise could be made (an opponent in the game and every `ifGiftPromised` slot fillable); the opponent is kept on the spell, the permanent and last-known information. A permanent: "When this enters, if its gift cost was paid, the chosen player draws a card" is derived by the engine (it and the card's own "if the gift was promised" trigger are ordered by the player). An instant or sorcery: the chosen player draws as it resolves, before every other effect, and never if it is countered. `validateCard`: one gift per face, not on a land, not on a modal spell, the conditions and the selector need the keyword, a gift-only target is used only in a `giftPromised` conditional.
- Decision and action: `confirmOptional` with purpose `gift` and `giftTo`; `acceptOptional` promises, `declineOptional` casts with no gift (with several opponents the next is offered), `cancel` rewinds.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-gift.test.ts` (a permanent: the opponent draws and the destroy trigger runs, not promised, nothing to destroy, the question and its three answers, cancel, the event, a creature that left still gives the gift; an instant or sorcery: the draw is first, not promised, countered; a target only if promised, the gift not offered without a legal target; the greatest power; validation). Mutation checks: no gift effect, the effect also for a permanent, the gift target always or never declared, the permanent forgetting the opponent, the recipient the caster, no last-known information, decline accepting, no cancel, no event field, no question.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: not done (list in D-135): the dialog label is `Gift`; the prompt for the question names the opponent (`giftTo`); the log line of `spellCast` says to whom the gift was promised.
- What the parser must produce: `Gift a card (You may promise an opponent a gift as you cast this spell. If you do, when it enters, they draw a card.)` (Scrapshooter) and `Gift a card (... If you do, they draw a card before its other effects.)` (Consumed by Greed): the keyword node (the reminder text is read as reminder). `When this creature enters, if the gift was promised, destroy target artifact or enchantment an opponent controls.`: an `entersBattlefield` trigger of `this` with `interveningIf: { kind: 'giftPromised' }`, `targets` `[{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { controller: 'opponent', anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }] } } }]` and a `destroy` of the target. `Target opponent sacrifices a creature with the greatest power among creatures they control. If the gift was promised, return target creature card from your graveyard to your hand.` (Consumed by Greed): `targets` `[{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'player', filter: 'opponent' } }, { id: 't2', ifGiftPromised: true, what: <a creature card in your graveyard> }]` and a sequence of the `sacrifice` with `greatest: 'power'` and `player` the target, then `{ kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'giftPromised' }, effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't2' }, to: 'hand' } }`.

### "You may cast target Zombie creature card from your graveyard this turn."

- CR: 601.2, 611.2a, 514.2, 400.7, 117.1a
- Gap class: P (an effect that opens a window to cast a card, with a duration)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `mayCast`), `state/types.ts` (`ContinuousChange` `castPermission`), `rules/may-effects.ts` (`runMayCast`), `rules/legality.ts` (`mayCastFromGraveyard`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/supported.ts` (the target is a graveyard card)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'mayCast', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }`; until the end of the turn its controller may cast that card from their graveyard at the timing its type allows, for its ordinary cost (no alternative cost, no commander tax, no exile afterwards); a card that leaves the graveyard is a new object and has lost it; only a card of the controller's own graveyard gets it.
- Decision and action: `castSpell { object }` of a card in the graveyard, listed after the ordinary casts.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-graveyard-permissions.test.ts` (Zombie, Goblin and Angel chanters: the card can be cast and enters; only the targeted card; a type the ability does not name cannot be targeted; gone next turn; sorcery timing; the ordinary cost; the target gone in response; nothing is exiled; another player cannot cast it). Mutation checks: the permission forever, for every card, for no card (the seat and owner checks are equivalent mutants of each other).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: the graveyard cast chip and menu of D-133 apply (a plain `castSpell` option of a card in the graveyard).
- What the parser must produce: `{T}: You may cast target Zombie creature card from your graveyard this turn.` (Zul Ashur, Lich Lord): an activated ability with `cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }]`, `targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Zombie'], controller: 'you' } } }]` and `effect: { kind: 'mayCast', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }`; its `Ward—Pay 2 life` is the existing keyword.

### "{4}{B}{B}: Return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield with a +1/+1 counter on it. This ability costs {4} less to activate if an opponent controls four or more nonbasic lands."

- CR: 113.6b, 602.2, 601.2f, 118.7, 122.6, 614.1c, 205.4
- Gap class: P (an activated ability of a card in the graveyard, a conditional reduction of its cost, a filter part)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Ability` `activated.costReducedIf`, `Condition` `opponentControlsAtLeast`, `ObjectFilter.notSupertypes`), `rules/activate-adjust.ts` (`adjustedAbility`), `rules/activate-legality.ts` (`hasGraveyardAbility`, the cost as it stands), `rules/activate.ts` (the ability put on the stack is the adjusted one), `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/filter-match.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`ability:costReducedIf`, `condition:opponentControlsAtLeast`, `selector:notSupertypes`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['4', 'B', 'B'] }], functionsIn: 'graveyard', costReducedIf: { generic: 4, condition: { kind: 'opponentControlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['land'], notSupertypes: ['basic'] }, count: 4 } }, effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'this' }, to: 'battlefield', controller: 'owner', withCounters: { counter: '+1/+1', count: 1 } } }`. As the ability is activated the condition is read (for the controller as "you": some one opponent controls four or more permanents that are lands without the supertype basic) and `generic` is taken off the generic mana of the cost, never below zero and never colored mana; what was paid is what the stack object shows. It works only from the graveyard of its owner, at any speed unless `timing` says otherwise; if the card is gone when it resolves nothing is returned.
- Decision and action: `activate { object, abilityIndex }` of a card in the graveyard, then the ordinary payment.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-graveyard-permissions.test.ts` (paid in full: returns with a counter and the right size; with four nonbasic lands under an opponent's control it costs {B}{B}; with three it does not; basic lands and your own lands do not count; the cost is worked out once and the stack object shows it; only from the graveyard, only the owner's; at instant speed; the card gone in response). Mutation checks: the reduction always, one less, "more than" four, your own lands counted, nonbasic ignored, graveyard abilities not listed, the counter not put on, the stack ability not the adjusted one, the legality not using the adjusted cost.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: a graveyard `activate` option is not worded yet (the chooser lists abilities of permanents; the cost shown is the printed one, the payment prompt shows the reduced total).
- What the parser must produce: `{4}{B}{B}: Return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield with a +1/+1 counter on it. This ability costs {4} less to activate if an opponent controls four or more nonbasic lands.` (Razorlash Transmogrant; its first line, `This creature can't block.`, is the existing `restriction` `cantBlock` of `this`): the activated ability above; "nonbasic land" is `{ types: ['land'], notSupertypes: ['basic'] }`.

### "When this land enters untapped, ..."

- CR: 603.6a, 614.12, 614.1c
- Gap class: P (how a permanent entered as a trigger condition)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`entersBattlefield.tapped`), `rules/triggers.ts` (`matches`), `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`trigger:entersUntapped`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } | <filter>, tapped: false }` ("enters untapped"; `true` is "enters tapped"). It is read as the event is noticed, the state the permanent's own replacement effects (`entersTapped`, "unless you control three or more other Swamps") left it in; a permanent that entered tapped never has the trigger, and one tapped later is still one that entered untapped. With a filter it reads each permanent that enters ("whenever a land enters untapped under your control").
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-graveyard-permissions.test.ts` (three other Swamps: untapped, the trigger puts a creature card on top of the library; declining; two Swamps: tapped and no trigger; the land does not count itself; no target removes the trigger; "enters tapped" the other way round; a filter). Mutation checks: how it entered ignored, "untapped" meaning tapped.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: none.
- What the parser must produce: `This land enters tapped unless you control three or more other Swamps.` (existing `entersTapped` with `unless: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { subtypes: ['Swamp'] }, count: 3 }`; the entering land is not on the battlefield, so "other" needs no filter field) and `When this land enters untapped, you may put target creature card from your graveyard on top of your library.` (Witch's Cottage): `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' }, tapped: false }, optional: true, targets: [<creature card in your graveyard, id t1>], effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, to: 'library', position: 'top' } }`. Its `({T}: Add {B}.)` reminder is the mana ability of the basic land type Swamp: the card's own abilities must list it (`{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'fixed', mana: ['B'] } }`).

### "{M}, {T}, Pay life equal to the number of colors in your commanders' color identity: Draw a card."

- CR: 118.3, 119.4, 903.4
- Gap class: P (a life payment whose amount is a game amount)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Cost` `payLife.amount: Amount`), `rules/activate-adjust.ts` (`adjustedAbility` works the amount out once), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`breakDown`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkStateAmount`), `ast/supported.ts` (only an activated ability's cost takes an amount), `rules/mana-abilities.ts`, `rules/enters.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'payLife', amount: { kind: 'commanderColors' } }` (or any amount `amountIs` reads: a count of permanents, a greatest or total, devotion) in the `cost` of an activated ability; the amount is read once as the ability is activated (0 with no commander: the ability still works) and the payment needs that much life (CR 119.4).
- Decision and action: none beyond the ordinary activation.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-recursion.test.ts` ("pay life equal to the number of ...": the colors of the commanders' identity, no commander, a count of creatures, the cost paid once when a creature leaves in response, too little life); `ast-recursion-validate.test.ts` (refused in a mana ability, an additional cost and a keyword cost). Mutation checks: the amount not worked out, the activation not using the adjusted ability, legality not using it.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 30, 2026-10-27
- Web: `game/cost-text.ts` words it ("life equal to the number of colors in your commanders' color identity").
- What the parser must produce: `{M}, {T}, Pay life equal to the number of colors in your commanders' color identity: Draw a card.` (War Room): an activated ability with `cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['3'] }, { kind: 'tap' }, { kind: 'payLife', amount: { kind: 'commanderColors' } }]` and `effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 }`; its `{T}: Add {C}.` is the existing colorless mana ability.

## The Artifacts slice (D-139)

The engine half of the owner's Artifacts precon (20 cards). Each entry says what the parser must produce for the real Oracle line (the exact text is in the golden corpus). All shapes are generic over the filter, the stat, the creature type or the amount; the tests run each over two or three of them. The `Added in` of every entry is `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28. The parser reads every line since stage P31 (D-140; `PARSER_VERSION` 36), the golden snapshot marks the twenty cards supported, and the web words the slice and has its dialogs (the "Web" lines below describe the engine step's state).

### "Return all attacking creatures to their owner's hand.", "Destroy all artifacts, creatures, and enchantments.", "Exile each opponent's graveyard.", "Each player sacrifices all permanents they control that are one or more colors."

- CR: 701.8, 701.21a, 101.4, 105.2, 308, 506.4, 400.7
- Gap class: P (the effects existed; a color count, the Kindred type and the sacrifice of every named permanent by its own controller are new)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.colorCount`, the `kindred` card type, `Effect` `sacrifice` with an `each` selector), `rules/filter-match.ts`, `rules/effects.ts` (`sacrifice` each by controller, in APNAP order), `characteristics.ts`
- AST node: Aetherize `spell: { effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { types: ['creature'], attacking: true } }, to: 'hand' } }`; Nevinyrral's Disk `activated` with `cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }, { kind: 'tap' }]` and `{ kind: 'destroy', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['creature'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }] } } }`; Soul-Guide Lantern `cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }, { kind: 'sacrifice', what: { kind: 'this' } }]` and `{ kind: 'exile', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', owner: { kind: 'eachOpponent' } } } }`; All Is Dust (types `['kindred', 'sorcery']`) `{ kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { colorCount: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 1 } } } }` ("two or more colors" is `value: 2`, "monocolored" `{ cmp: 'equal', value: 1 }`).
- Decision and action: none (a sacrifice by each controller never asks).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mass-effects.test.ts` (attackers bounced and not the others, a union of types with indestructible and regeneration honored, graveyards by owner, the color counts over three comparisons, a Kindred card, a stolen attacker returned to its owner). Mutation checks: the count ignored, `atLeast` off by one, `equal` read as `atMost`, indestructible ignored (all killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `ObjectFilter` wording does not say `colorCount` yet (`game/filter-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: the four lines above exactly as in the shapes; the type line `Kindred Sorcery — Eldrazi` is types `['kindred', 'sorcery']`; "Each player sacrifices all permanents they control that are one or more colors" lowers to the `sacrifice`/`each` node with no `player` (each permanent goes with its own controller). Needs `selector:colorCount`, `cardType:kindred`.

### "At the beginning of your upkeep, if you control the artifact with the greatest mana value or tied for the greatest mana value, draw a card."

- CR: 603.4, 202.3
- Gap class: P (a condition over a stat among all permanents)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Condition` `controlsGreatest`), `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/amounts.ts` (`statOf`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'controlsGreatest', filter: { types: ['artifact'] }, stat: 'manaValue' }` as the `interveningIf` of `{ kind: 'upkeep', whose: 'you' }` with `{ kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 }`. `stat` is `power`, `toughness` or `manaValue`; the filter sets no `controller` (it is the condition that names "you"); a tie counts for each tied permanent; nothing matched is false.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-controls-greatest.test.ts` (mana value, power, toughness and a creature type; ties, the opponent greater, no artifacts, a creature that is not an artifact, +1/+1 counters). Mutation checks: min for max, any controller, empty is true (killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/condition-text.ts` has no words for it (null, never a guess).
- What the parser must produce: Padeem's second line as above; its first line "Artifacts you control have hexproof." is the existing group grant. Needs `condition:controlsGreatest`.

### "Living weapon (When this Equipment enters, create a 0/0 black Phyrexian Germ creature token, then attach this to it.)"

- CR: 702.92, 111.2, 301.5a, 704.5f, 400.7j
- Gap class: K (a keyword that stands for a trigger)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `livingWeapon`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`LIVING_WEAPON_ABILITY`, `GERM_TOKEN`), `rules/effects.ts` (`createToken` sets `moved`), `ast/validate-artifacts.ts` (only an Equipment)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'livingWeapon' } }` on a card with the subtype Equipment. The keyword stands for `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'createToken', token: <the Germ>, count: 1 }, { kind: 'attach', object: { kind: 'this' }, to: { kind: 'moved' } }] } }`; the Germ is a 0/0 black Phyrexian Germ.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-living-weapon.test.ts` (the Germ is made, black, 0/0 and attached; a bonus of +2/+2 and a counted bonus keep it alive, none lets state-based actions kill it, Equip moves the Equipment, a keyword on a non-Equipment is refused). Mutation checks: no attach, two Germs, not expanded (killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: the keyword chip is plain; the reminder text is not shown.
- What the parser must produce: the keyword line `Living weapon (...)` (reminder dropped) as the keyword node; Nettlecyst's second line is the existing attached `modifyPt` with `{ kind: 'countOf', filter: { anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }], controller: 'you' } }` for both power and toughness ("for each artifact and/or enchantment you control"), its third `Equip {2}` the existing keyword. Needs `keyword:livingWeapon`.

### "{5}{U}, {T}: Draw a card. This ability costs {1} less to activate for each other artifact you control."

- CR: 601.2f, 602.2b
- Gap class: P (a cost reduction by an amount)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`activated.costReducedBy`), `rules/activate-adjust.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['5', 'U'] }, { kind: 'tap' }], effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 }, costReducedBy: { amount: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you', other: true } } } }`. "{2} less for each" is `{ kind: 'multiple', factor: 2, of: <the count> }`; the amount is read once as the ability is activated, for its controller with the source as "this"; only the generic part of the first mana cost goes down (never below 0); it applies after `costReducedIf`.
- Decision and action: none beyond the ordinary activation.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-cost-reduced-by.test.ts` (artifacts, Zombies twice each, cards in a graveyard; the floor at the colored symbols; the other permanents counted, the source not; the opponent's not; the cost shown on the stack). Mutation checks: never applied, one too many, read for the opponent, not generic only (killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `ability-label.ts` shows the printed cost, not the reduction.
- What the parser must produce: Tamiyo's Logbook as above; the sentence "This ability costs {1} less to activate for each ..." follows the cost line (as `costReducedIf` does, D-135). Needs `ability:costReducedBy`.

### "{T}, Put a page counter on this artifact: Scry 1.", "When there are four or more page counters on this artifact, exile it. If you do, you gain 4 life."

- CR: 118.3, 122.1, 603.8, 603.12, 701.22
- Gap class: P (a counter cost and a state trigger)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Cost` `putCounters`, `Trigger` `state`), `rules/activate-costs.ts`, `rules/state-triggers.ts` (`findStateTriggers`), `rules/settle.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`didPrevious` after any effect that reports what it did)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'putCounters', counter: 'page', count: 1 }` in the `cost` of an activated ability of a permanent (`[{ kind: 'tap' }, { kind: 'putCounters', counter: 'page', count: 1 }]`, effect `{ kind: 'scry', count: 1 }`; `[{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }, { kind: 'tap' }, ...]` with a draw); and `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'state', condition: { kind: 'amountIs', amount: { kind: 'countersOn', of: { kind: 'this' }, counter: 'page' }, compare: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'exile', objects: { kind: 'this' } }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'didPrevious' }, effect: { kind: 'gainLife', player: { kind: 'you' }, amount: 4 } }] } }`. A state trigger triggers once when the condition becomes true and not again until it has left the stack; any plain condition works (a life total, a count).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-counter-costs.test.ts` (the counter paid as a cost, a tapped Tome, the second ability and its {2}, cancel takes the counter back, the fourth counter triggers and exiles, three do not, a Tome that enters with the counters, once each time the condition becomes true, "if you do" after a Tome that left, a sacrifice of another object, a life-total state trigger). Mutation checks: the counter not added, retrigger while waiting, no dedupe on the stack, the condition ignored (killed); the check for a counter cost off the battlefield is unreachable (the validator refuses it).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/cost-text.ts` words `putCounters` ("Put a page counter on this permanent"); the state trigger has a generic line only (`game/trigger-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: Mazemind Tome's three lines as above (the reminder text of Scry is dropped). Needs `cost:putCounters`, `trigger:state`.

### "{U}, {T}, Return an artifact you control to its owner's hand: You may put an artifact card from your hand onto the battlefield."

- CR: 118.3, 108.3, 111.7, 903.9b, 701.21a, 608.2d
- Gap class: P (a return cost and a chosen card put onto the battlefield)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Cost` `returnToHand`, `ChooseSelector.optional`), `rules/activate-bounce.ts`, `rules/activate.ts`, `rules/move-choice.ts` (`runChooseMove`), `state/decisions.ts` (`choosePermanent` purpose `bounce`, `chooseCards` purpose `move`)
- AST node: cost `{ kind: 'returnToHand', what: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you' } }` (`what: { kind: 'this' }` returns the permanent itself, `count` more than one asks for that many); effect `{ kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { zone: 'hand', types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you' }, optional: true }, to: 'battlefield' }` (`tapped: true` for "tapped"; `zone: 'graveyard'` for a card from the graveyard). With more candidates than are needed a `choosePermanent` (purpose `bounce`, `forCost`) asks; a token returned ceases to exist; a commander's owner is asked (CR 903.9b) in the middle of the activation.
- Decision and action: `choosePermanent` (purpose `bounce`) answered with `choosePermanents`; `chooseCards` (purpose `move`, `min` 0) answered with `chooseCards`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-return-costs.test.ts` (the choice among several artifacts and the card put onto the battlefield, none put, only artifact cards offered, nothing to put, the opponent's artifact not a candidate, cancel, the returned permanent put back as a new object, a commander to the command zone or the hand, creatures with a tapped entry, a count of two, a token that ceases to exist, `this`). Mutation checks: to the graveyard instead of the hand (killed); a return of a permanent of another controller is unreachable (the candidates are the controller's).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/cost-text.ts` words `returnToHand`; no prompt for `chooseCards` purpose `move` (the generic one is shown) and no `waitingOn` wording.
- What the parser must produce: Master Transmuter as above (an artifact creature with the activated ability). Needs `cost:returnToHand`, `selector:optional`.

### "Juggernauts you control attack each combat if able.", "Juggernauts you control can't be blocked by Walls.", "Other creatures you control have base power and toughness 5/3 and are Juggernauts in addition to their other creature types.", "You may cast artifact spells as though they had flash."

- CR: 508.1d, 509.1b, 613.4b, 613.1d, 205.1, 601.3b, 702.8
- Gap class: P (statics for a creature type, a block limit and a cast permission)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `setPt`, `setTypes` with `subtypes`/`types` for a subject, `cantBeBlockedBy`, `castAsThoughFlash`), `rules/derive-statics.ts`, `rules/derive-group-types.ts`, `rules/static-subject.ts`, `rules/combat-legality.ts`, `rules/legality.ts` (`flashPermitted`)
- AST node (Graaz): `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'restriction', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Juggernaut'], controller: 'you' } }, restriction: 'attacksEachCombat' } }` (the existing group restriction); `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'cantBeBlockedBy', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Juggernaut'], controller: 'you' } }, blockers: { subtypes: ['Wall'] } } }` (`subject: { kind: 'this' }` for "This creature can't be blocked by creatures with power 2 or less", `blockers: { power: { cmp: 'atMost', value: 2 } }`); `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'setPt', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }, other: true }, power: 5, toughness: 3 } }` and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'setTypes', subject: <the same>, additive: true, subtypes: ['Juggernaut'] } }`. Shimmer Myr: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'flash' } }` and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'castAsThoughFlash', spells: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you' } } }` (the filter describes the spell on the stack; it is the controller's permission and does not give the spell flash).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-lords-and-blocking.test.ts` (three creature types and three blocker filters, the layers with +1/+1 on top and a timestamp, the opponent's creatures, the lord itself, `this`), `scenario-xx-aura-flash-linked.test.ts` (flash for artifact, creature and mana value filters, only the controller). Mutation checks: power and toughness swapped, the blocker filter emptied, the limit never read, the subject ignored, additive off, flash for everyone, the spell filter ignored (killed); a limit whose filter names a controller and group types of a card not on the battlefield are unreachable for every shape of the parser.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/static-text.ts` has no words for them yet.
- What the parser must produce: the four lines as above. Group types are read from a view before the other statics (ROADMAP: no dependency chains, no condition on them). Needs `static:setPt`, `static:cantBeBlockedBy`, `static:castAsThoughFlash`, `static:setTypes` with a subject.

### "When this creature enters, you may exile target nontoken creature. As long as a card exiled with this creature is a creature card, this creature has the power, toughness, and creature types of the last creature card exiled with it. It's still a Shapeshifter."; "Enchant creature. When this Aura enters, tap enchanted creature and you become the monarch. Enchanted creature doesn't untap during its controller's untap step unless that player is the monarch."

- CR: 607.2a, 613.4b, 205.1a, 303.4, 701.26a, 725.2, 502.3
- Gap class: P (linked exile and "attached" as a reference)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `exile.linked`, `StaticEffect` `takeFromLinkedExile`, `ObjectRef` `attached`), `rules/effects.ts` (`exile`, the link kept as `GameObject.linkedExile`), `rules/derive-group-types.ts` (`linkedCardOf`), `rules/derive-statics.ts`, `rules/select.ts`, `ast/validate-artifacts.ts` (a linked exile and its reader come in pairs)
- AST node (Duplicant): `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, optional: true, targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { types: ['creature'], token: false } } }], effect: { kind: 'exile', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, linked: true } }` and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'takeFromLinkedExile', subject: { kind: 'this' }, from: { types: ['creature'] }, alsoSubtypes: ['Shapeshifter'] } }`. (Fall from Favor) `{ kind: 'tap', objects: { kind: 'attached' } }` then `{ kind: 'becomeMonarch', player: { kind: 'you' } }` as the enters trigger of an Aura, and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'restriction', subject: { kind: 'attached', types: ['creature'] }, restriction: 'doesntUntap' }, condition: { kind: 'not', term: { kind: 'playerHasDesignation', player: { kind: 'controllerOf', of: { kind: 'attached' } }, designation: 'monarch' } } }`.
- Decision and action: the optional trigger's `confirmOptional`; none otherwise.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-aura-flash-linked.test.ts` (imprint, declined, the last of three, a card that is not a creature card, counters on top, a new object after it leaves, a Mimic with an activated version, the Aura that taps and the monarch cases). Mutation checks: first instead of last, the link not remembered, the link to the wrong object, power and toughness not taken, the old types kept (killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: none yet.
- What the parser must produce: Duplicant and Fall from Favor as above; the text "Imprint —" is an ability word and is dropped. Needs `effect:exile` with `linked`, `static:takeFromLinkedExile`, `ref:attached`.

### "Draw three cards. Then discard two cards unless you discard an artifact card.", "Look at the top six cards of your library. You may reveal an artifact card from among them and put it into your hand. Put the rest on the bottom of your library in a random order."

- CR: 701.9, 701.20, 401.4, 701.24, 603.2, 508.1
- Gap class: P (a discard alternative and a look at the top cards)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `discard.unless`, `lookAtTop`), `rules/search.ts` (`runDiscard`), `rules/look-take.ts`, `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionCards`), `api.ts` and `legal-actions.ts` (`orSingle`), `view.ts` (`pendingCards` for `lookTake`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 3 }, { kind: 'discard', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 2, unless: { types: ['artifact'] } }] }` (an answer is the full count, or one card the `unless` filter names; with none in hand it is the full count); `{ kind: 'lookAtTop', count: 6, take: { filter: { types: ['artifact'] }, max: 1 }, rest: 'bottomRandom' }` (`rest: 'graveyard'` for the other wording; the player may take none; a commander left goes to the bottom with its owner's question). For Adaptive Omnitool the trigger is `{ kind: 'attacks', what: { kind: 'attached' } }` with that effect.
- Decision and action: `chooseCards` (purpose `discard` with `orSingle` and `min` 1, or purpose `lookTake` with `looked` and `min` 0), answered with `chooseCards`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-discard-unless-look.test.ts` (an artifact alone or two cards, none in hand, over a creature filter, the top six with one artifact or none, fewer cards than six, another count and the graveyard, the order of the bottom from the seed, an attack trigger of the equipped creature). `casual-artifacts.test.ts` and `artifacts-games.test.ts` (the AI answers). Mutation checks: no single answer, the filter ignored, the minimum wrong, the rest on top, the order not random, one card too many looked at (killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: the discard dialog does not word the single-card alternative (`orSingle`); no dialog for `lookTake` (the cards looked at are in `pendingCards`; `looked` lists all of them).
- What the parser must produce: Thirst for Knowledge and the Omnitool's second line as above; the Omnitool's first line is the existing attached `modifyPt` with `{ kind: 'countOf', filter: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'you' } }`. Needs `effect:lookAtTop`, `effect:discard` with `unless`.

### "Whenever you tap a permanent for {C}, add an additional {C}."

- CR: 605.1b, 605.4a, 106.12, 106.4, 601.2g
- Gap class: P (a triggered mana ability)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `tappedForMana`), `rules/mana-triggers.ts` (`additionalManaFor`), `rules/mana-sources.ts`, `rules/mana-engine.ts`, `ast/validate.ts` (the shape check)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'tappedForMana', what: {}, by: 'you', mana: 'C' }, effect: { kind: 'addMana', mana: { kind: 'fixed', mana: ['C'] } } }` (`what` a filter of the permanent tapped, `mana` the type the tapped ability must have made, left out for "tap a creature for mana, add {R}"). It uses no stack, resolves as soon as the mana ability that triggered it has and adds the mana to the same pool; only for the abilities with {T} in their cost; the payment planner counts the extra mana as the source's.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mana-triggers.test.ts` (Sol Ring and the Monument make three, a Forest makes {G} and not {C}, the opponent's Monument, a land type, any mana, the pool, automatic payment taps no more than it must, Command Tower, an ability without {T}). Mutation checks: another controller counted, the mana type ignored, not only {T}, the filter ignored (killed).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` words it ("Whenever you tap a permanent for {C}").
- What the parser must produce: Forsaken Monument's second line as above (`what: {}` for "a permanent"); the first line is the existing lord with `colorless: true`, the third the existing `castSpell` trigger with a `colorless` filter. Needs `trigger:tappedForMana`.

### "Whenever one or more artifact creatures you control deal combat damage to a player, draw a card."

- CR: 603.2c, 510.2, 603.2g, 702.7
- Gap class: P (a batch trigger)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `combatDamageBatch`), `rules/triggers.ts` (`batchTriggers`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'combatDamageBatch', what: { types: ['artifact', 'creature'], controller: 'you' }, to: 'player' }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } }` (`to: 'opponent'` for "to an opponent", `atLeast: 2` for "two or more"); it triggers once for each player damaged in one damage event, `triggerPlayer` is the player damaged; the first strike step and the regular step are two events.
- Decision and action: none (several triggers ask `orderTriggers`).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-combat-batch.test.ts` (two artifact creatures one draw, a creature that is not an artifact, a blocked creature, a double striker, `atLeast`, an opponent, two sources of the trigger, noncombat damage). Mutation checks: no per-player count, `atLeast` ignored, the opponent not checked, noncombat counted, the filter ignored (all killed but the opponent check, which needs a creature controlled by another player in a 2-player game).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` words it.
- What the parser must produce: Thopter Spy Network's second line as above; its first line is the existing upkeep trigger with an intervening "if you control an artifact". Needs `trigger:combatDamageBatch`.
