### "{X}: Destroy each nonland permanent with mana value X whose controller was dealt combat damage by this creature this turn. Activate only once each turn."

- CR: 107.3a, 202.3, 510.2, 602.5b, 400.7
- Gap class: S-lite (a small log of the turn in the state) with three P readers
- Code: `state/types.ts` (`TurnLog`, `GameState.turnLog`), `rules/flow.ts` (emptied as each turn begins), `rules/damage.ts` (combat damage to players), `rules/activate.ts` and `rules/activate-legality.ts` (`oncePerTurn`), `rules/filter-match.ts` and `rules/select.ts` (`manaValueX`, `controllerDealtDamageBySource`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['X'] }], oncePerTurn: true, effect: { kind: 'destroy', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { notTypes: ['land'], manaValueX: true, controllerDealtDamageBySource: true } } } }`. `oncePerTurn` counts the object's own ability (a new object after a zone change is a new count) when the activation is complete; `manaValueX` compares with the announced X; `controllerDealtDamageBySource` matches the permanents of a player the ability's source dealt combat damage to this turn (damage prevented entirely is not in the log); both flags are read only in an effect's `each` selector.
- Decision and action: `chooseX` as for any X ability.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-turn-log.test.ts` (X 2 and X 4 over four permanents, not lands, not my own, no damage, a blocked attacker, the second activation refused and the next turn, the log emptied, a second ability of the same permanent, damage from an ability is not combat damage, damage by another creature). Mutation checks: the flag ignored, the log not kept or not reset, any ability of the object, noncombat counted, any source, X not equal or at most (killed); the fallback that reads X from the stack object is equivalent while the resolution gives X.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: nothing shows the turn log; `oncePerTurn` has no words.
- What the parser must produce: Steel Hellkite's third line as above; "Activate only once each turn." after the effect sets `oncePerTurn: true`; its first two lines are the existing flying and `{2}: gets +1/+0 until end of turn`. Needs `ability:oncePerTurn`, `selector:manaValueX`, `selector:controllerDamagedBySource`.

### "Whenever this creature attacks, you may tap X untapped Myr you control. If you do, this creature gets +X/+0 until end of turn and deals X damage to the player or planeswalker it's attacking."

- CR: 701.26a, 608.2d, 608.2c, 302.6, 508.1b, 120.3, 120.3c
- Gap class: P (a chosen number tapped, what was done this way, and a damage recipient)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `tap` with a `choose` selector, `Amount` `thisWay` `tapped`, `dealDamage.to` `attackedBy`), `rules/tap-choice.ts` (`runChooseTap`), `rules/effects.ts`, `rules/stack.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkChooseTap`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attacks', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'tap', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { subtypes: ['Myr'], controller: 'you' }, anyNumber: true } }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'didPrevious' }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'modifyPt', objects: { kind: 'this' }, power: { kind: 'thisWay', what: 'tapped' }, toughness: 0, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' }, { kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'thisWay', what: 'tapped' }, to: { kind: 'attackedBy', of: { kind: 'this' } } }] } }] } }`. `choose` takes `anyNumber: true` (none to all) or `count` (exactly that many, or all that there are); the filter says `controller: 'you'` and no `tapped` (the untapped are chosen from); summoning sickness does not matter; the tapped are what `thisWay` `tapped` counts and what "if you do" reads (tapping none is not doing it). The enters trigger "create four 1/1 colorless Myr artifact creature tokens" is the existing `createToken` with `count: 4`.
- Decision and action: `choosePermanent` (purpose `effect`, `min` 0) answered with `choosePermanents`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-tap-choice.test.ts` (the legal set, two tapped and +2/+0 and 2 damage and combat damage, none, an already tapped Myr, a planeswalker as the target, another creature type, a count, a single candidate, "if you do" after none, the number tapped by a plain tap effect, two attackers at different targets, the four tokens). Mutation checks: not tapped, performed constant, the count off, the minimum wrong, the damage target taken from the first attacker, the tap effect's count (killed); tapping with `anyNumber` auto-choosing is equivalent (count is 0).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `ability-label.ts` words `tap` with a choice and `attackedBy` generically; the `choosePermanent` panel for purpose `effect` shows the generic prompt.
- What the parser must produce: Myr Battlesphere's second line as above ("tap X untapped Myr" is `anyNumber`; "X" is the number chosen, never an announced X). Needs `selector:anyNumber`, `amount:thisWay`, `selector:attackedBy`.

### "When this artifact enters during the declare attackers step, you may reselect which player or permanent target attacking creature is attacking."

- CR: 508.7, 508.7a, 508.7b, 508.7c, 506.3, 603.4, 702.8
- Gap class: P (an effect on what an attacker attacks and a condition on the step)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `reselectAttack`, `Condition` `duringStep`), `rules/reselect-attack.ts`, `rules/attack-targets.ts`, `rules/conditions.ts`, `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionReselect`), `api.ts` (`chooseOption` purpose `attackTarget`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, interveningIf: { kind: 'duringStep', step: 'declareAttackers' }, optional: true, targets: [{ id: 'a', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { types: ['creature'], attacking: true } } }], effect: { kind: 'reselectAttack', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 'a' } } }`. `step` is a combat step (`beginningOfCombat`, `declareAttackers`, `declareBlockers`, `firstStrikeDamage`, `combatDamage`, `endOfCombat`); as an `interveningIf` it holds as the event happens and as the ability resolves (one step, because the stack empties before a step ends). The controller of the effect chooses among the attack targets of the creature's controller's opponents (players, planeswalkers, battles; the current target is among them, so keeping it is declining); with nothing else to attack nothing is asked.
- Decision and action: `chooseOption` (purpose `attackTarget`, `attacker`, `attackTargets`, options `player:<seat>` and `permanent:<object id>`) answered with `chooseOption`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-reselect-attack.test.ts` (flash in during declare attackers and move the attack to a planeswalker, keep, decline, still in combat and the same attack, cast in another step does nothing, a flash enchantment on another step, the mana ability), `ast-artifacts-validate.test.ts`. Mutation checks: the target unchanged, the options for the wrong player, an unlisted option accepted, the wrong player asked, removed from combat, the step condition always true or inverted (killed). The options for three or more players are not tested in a scenario (the DSL has two seats).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 31, 2026-10-28
- Web: `game/chosen.ts` words the option text only minimally; the dialog (`OptionDialog`) is the creature type picker and must get a dialog of its own for this purpose.
- What the parser must produce: Misleading Signpost's second line as above (the parenthetical is dropped); "Flash" is the existing keyword and "{T}: Add {U}." the existing mana ability. Needs `effect:reselectAttack`, `condition:duringStep`.

## The Dinosaurs slice (D-145)

The engine half of the owner's Dinosaurs precon (20 cards). Each entry says what the parser must produce for the real Oracle line (the exact text is in the golden corpus, `packages/cards/test/fixtures/corpus/golden.jsonl`). All shapes are generic over the filter, the stat, the creature type or the amount; the tests run each over two or three of them, with SYNTHETIC cards (`src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-*.ts`). The `Added in` of every entry is `ENGINE_VERSION` 32, 2026-10-29. Run the checks with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine` (the scenario tests are `scenario-xx-<name>.test.ts`) and `SOAK_GAMES=100 pnpm exec vitest run packages/ai/test/dinosaurs-games.test.ts`. The "Web" line of each entry lists what the web has to word or ask; the web step is done (D-148: wording in `game/dinosaurs-text.ts`, the division and the other dialogs; see `docs/ROADMAP.md`).

| Card | Covered by |
| --- | --- |
| Arachnogenesis | `preventDamage.byFilter` (new); `createToken` with `countOf` `attackingYou` (existing) |
| Clifftop Lookout | `revealUntil` (new) |
| Collective Resistance | `escalate` (new) with the existing modal spell (`chooseModes`, `modes`) |
| Ezuri's Predation | `fight` with `pairing` (new); `createToken`, `moved` (existing) |
| Goreclaw, Terror of Qal Sisma | parser only: `costReduction` for creature spells with power 4 or greater (existing, `SYNTHETIC Titan Tamer`), `attacks` trigger with `modifyPt` and `grantKeyword` over an `each` (existing, `SYNTHETIC Surging Brute`) |
| Ilysian Caryatid | `ManaAbility.instead` and `anyColor.count` (new) |
| Kenrith's Transformation | `loseAllAbilities`, `setColors`, non-additive `setTypes` (new) with `setPt` and the enters trigger `draw` (existing) |
| Loot, Exuberant Explorer | `lookAtTop.take` `to`, `tapped`, `manaValueAtMost` (new); the extra land play (existing) |
| Monstrous Onslaught | `TargetSpec.divide`, `ChosenTarget.share`, `dealDividedDamage` (new) |
| Mosswort Bridge | `hideaway` and `castFree` with `linkedExile` (new); the condition `amountIs` over `aggregate` (existing) |
| Pugnacious Hammerskull | `triggered.triggerCondition` (new) and stun counters (new) |
| Ram Through | `dealDamage.trampleExcess` (new) over the bite (existing) |
| Rhonas the Indomitable | `restrictionUnless` and `cantAttack` (new); the pump is parser only (existing, `SYNTHETIC Trampling Sage`) |
| Rishkar's Expertise | `castFree` (new); `draw` with `aggregate` (existing) |
| Rishkar, Peema Renegade | parser only: `addCounters` on a target slot with `min` 0 `max` 2, `grantAbility` to a group with `hasCounter` (new filter part; `SYNTHETIC Counter Mentor`, `SYNTHETIC Counter Lord`) |
| Scavenging Ooze | parser only: `exile` then `conditional` `objectMatches` (existing, `SYNTHETIC Graveyard Scavenger`) |
| Tangleweave Armor | `commanderManaValue` as a static `modifyPt` amount (new); living weapon, equip (existing) |
| Thickest in the Thicket | parser only: `addCounters` with `powerOf`, `controlsGreatest` (existing, `SYNTHETIC Thicket Growth`) |
| Unnatural Growth | `PtAmount` `affected` (new) |
| Witch's Clinic | `ObjectFilter.commander` (new) with `grantKeyword` (existing) |

### "Target commander gains lifelink until end of turn.", "Equipped creature gets +X/+X, where X is the greatest mana value among your commanders.", "Each creature you control with a counter on it has '{T}: Add {G}.'"

- CR: 903.3, 202.3, 122.1, 613.4c, 604.1
- Gap class: P (two filter parts and an amount)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.commander`, `ObjectFilter.hasCounter`, `Amount` and `StaticPtAmount` `commanderManaValue`), `rules/filter-match.ts` (`FilterSubject.commander`), `rules/select.ts`, `rules/amounts.ts`, `rules/derive-count.ts` (`commandersManaValue`, `staticAmountOf`), `rules/commander-identity.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts`
- AST node: Witch's Clinic `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }, { kind: 'tap' }], targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { commander: true } } }], effect: { kind: 'grantKeyword', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, keyword: { kind: 'lifelink' }, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' } }` (add `types: ['creature']` when the text says "target creature commander"); Tangleweave Armor `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'attached' }, power: { kind: 'commanderManaValue' }, toughness: { kind: 'commanderManaValue' } } }` with the existing `livingWeapon` and `equip` keywords; Rishkar's second line `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantAbility', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', hasCounter: true } }, ability: { kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'fixed', mana: ['G'] } } } }`. `hasCounter` is `true` (any counter) or a counter name (`'+1/+1'`, `'stun'`). `commander` is a card a player designated (CR 903.3), wherever it is: a token or a copy of one is not; whose commander it is does not matter (`controller` says whose it must be to control). `commanderManaValue` is the greatest among all the controller's commanders, in any zone, as printed (a copy effect on the battlefield is not read, ROADMAP); 0 with none. A static ability may name `hasCounter` in its subject (counters belong to no layer); it may not name `commander` (refused, as `attacking` is).
- Decision and action: none (a target slot is the usual `targets` decision).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-commanders-counters.test.ts` (the commander as a target, a token or non-commander refused, two commanders and the greatest value, the value from the command zone, the battlefield and the graveyard, the equipment bonus following the value, counters of any kind and of a kind in a group grant, a counter count of 0 is no counter). Mutation checks: a handful of targeted mutants per mechanic on a scratch copy, each killed by these tests, except the defensive guards named under `docs/ROADMAP.md`.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 32, 2026-10-29
- Web: `game/filter-text.ts` has no words for `commander` and `hasCounter`; `game/amount-text.ts` none for `commanderManaValue` (they must say nothing rather than guess).
- What the parser must produce: the three lines above. Needs `selector:commander`, `selector:hasCounter`, `amount:commanderManaValue`.

### "Whenever this creature attacks while you don't control another Dinosaur, put a stun counter on it."

- CR: 122.1d (in `docs/rules/`), 603.2, 603.4, 502.3, 701.26b
- Gap class: P (a stun counter as a replacement of untapping, and a condition that is part of the trigger event)
- Code: `rules/untap.ts` (`untapPermanent`, `STUN_COUNTER`), `rules/flow.ts` (untap step), `rules/effects.ts` (`untap` effect), `ast/types.ts` (`Ability.triggered.triggerCondition`), `rules/triggers.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`triggerFeatures`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attacks', what: { kind: 'this' } }, triggerCondition: { kind: 'not', term: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Dinosaur'], other: true }, count: 1 } }, effect: { kind: 'addCounters', objects: { kind: 'this' }, counter: 'stun', count: 1 } }`. `triggerCondition` is read once as the event happens (controller as "you", the source as "this"); an `interveningIf` is the "if" of CR 603.4 and is read again as the ability resolves: "while" and "as long as" in a trigger are `triggerCondition`, "if" is `interveningIf`. A stun counter needs no node: the counter is called "stun" and every untap (the untap step, the `untap` effect) loses one counter instead of untapping.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-stun-counters.test.ts` (the counter removed instead of untapping and the permanent stays tapped, one counter each untap step, the `untap` effect also removed one, a permanent not tapped keeps its counter, a second counter, the condition read at the event and not again, the intervening version checked again, the trigger not put on the stack when the condition is false). Mutation checks: a handful of targeted mutants per mechanic on a scratch copy, each killed by these tests, except the defensive guards named under `docs/ROADMAP.md`.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 32, 2026-10-29
- Web: the `counterRemoved` log line already exists; `game/trigger-text.ts` must word `triggerCondition` (it is not in the text of an intervening "if").
- What the parser must produce: the Hammerskull line above ("while you don't control another Dinosaur" is the `triggerCondition`, the `other: true` is "another"). Needs `trigger:triggerCondition`.

### "Rhonas the Indomitable can't attack or block unless you control another creature with power 4 or greater."

- CR: 508.1a, 509.1a, 113.6, 613.8
- Gap class: P (a restriction that depends on a condition)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Restriction` `cantAttack`, `StaticEffect` `restrictionUnless`), `rules/restrictions.ts` (`restrictedUnless`), `rules/combat-legality.ts`, `ast/validate-dinosaurs.ts` (`checkDinosaursStatic`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'restrictionUnless', subject: { kind: 'this' }, restrictions: ['cantAttack', 'cantBlock'], unless: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['creature'], other: true, power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } }, count: 1 } } }`. `restrictions` is `cantAttack`, `cantBlock` or both; `unless` is any condition read for the controller with the permanent as "this" (so `other` means another creature) when the attack or block is declared and in the legal-action lists, never in a layer; the subject is `this` only; the restriction goes when the permanent loses its abilities. The pump "{2}{G}: Another target creature gets +2/+0 and gains trample until end of turn" is the existing `activated` with `targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { types: ['creature'], other: true } } }]` and a `sequence` of `modifyPt` (`power: 2`, `toughness: 0`) and `grantKeyword` trample, both `untilEndOfTurn` (`SYNTHETIC Trampling Sage`); the first line "Deathtouch, indestructible" are the existing keywords.

### "Monstrous Onslaught deals X damage divided as you choose among any number of target creatures, where X is the greatest power among creatures you control as you cast this spell."

- CR: 601.2d, 601.2c, 608.2b, 120.3, 107.1b
- Gap class: P (a division announced with the targets)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`TargetSpec.divide`, `Effect` `dealDividedDamage`), `state/chosen.ts` (`ChosenTarget.share`), `state/decisions.ts` (`TargetChoiceSpec.divide`), `rules/target-choice.ts` (`divideTotal`, `slotCanBeFilled`, `targetAnswerError`), `rules/select.ts` (`targetBounds`), `rules/legality.ts` (`targetsPossible`), `rules/effects.ts`, `legal-actions.ts`, `testing/driver.ts` (`divide`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`declareTargets`), `ast/validate-dinosaurs.ts` (`checkDividedDamage`)
- AST node: `spell: { targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { types: ['creature'] } }, divide: { total: { kind: 'aggregate', how: 'greatest', stat: 'power', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } } } }], effect: { kind: 'dealDividedDamage', to: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } } }`. "Any number" is `min` left out (0 here) and `max` left out (at most the total, each target needs 1); "one, two or three targets" is `min: 1, max: 3` (`SYNTHETIC Arc Volley`, `what: { kind: 'anyTarget' }`, `divide: { total: 3 }`). The total is an amount that reads the game (no X, no target) and is read once, as the spell is cast; with a total of 0 no target can be chosen, and a slot whose `min` exceeds the total makes the spell uncastable. The division is announced with the targets in one `chooseTargets` action: each `ChosenTarget` carries `share` (a whole number of at least 1), the shares add up to `divide` exactly when any target is chosen; zero targets (when `min` is 0) is allowed and does nothing. On resolution each target still legal is dealt its share by the spell; a target that became illegal gets none and the others keep theirs (CR 608.2b); with every target illegal the spell is removed.
- Decision and action: `targets` (`TargetChoiceSpec.divide` is the total, `max` already limited by it) answered with `chooseTargets` whose `targets` carry `share`. `legalActionsForDecision` lists, for each set of targets, the rest on the last target and the rest on the first.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-divided-damage.test.ts` (the decision, the shares dealt, a share smaller than toughness, all to one target including my own creature, no target, a division that does not add up or gives zero, a target twice, X read at cast against a response that kills the biggest creature, an illegal target in the middle, every target illegal, X = 0, a minimum above the total, one to three targets of any kind, a share on a spell that divides nothing, every listed legal action accepted, the validator), `packages/ai/test/casual-divide.test.ts`. Mutation checks: the exact sum, the minimum of 1, the cap by the total, the total read at resolution, the share ignored, the slot check of the effect (killed); a negative total is clamped as defensive.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 32, 2026-10-29
- Web: **the targets dialog must ask the division** (it answers `chooseTargets` without `share` today, which the engine refuses for a divided slot, so Monstrous Onslaught cannot be cast from the UI until then); the log and `ability-label.ts` have no words for `dealDividedDamage`; the stack item should show each target with its share (`ObjectView.targets` carries `share`).
- What the parser must produce: the line above as the spell's `targets` slot with `divide` and the `dealDividedDamage` effect ("where X is ... as you cast this spell" is the `total`; "any number of target creatures" leaves `min` and `max` out). Needs `effect:dealDividedDamage`.

### Parser-only shapes: "{G}: Exile target card from a graveyard. If it was a creature card, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature and you gain 1 life.", "put X +1/+1 counters on target creature, where X is that creature's power", "put a +1/+1 counter on each of up to two target creatures", "Creature spells you cast with power 4 or greater cost {2} less to cast", "each creature you control with power 4 or greater gets +1/+1 and gains trample until end of turn"

- CR: 406.3, 608.2h, 603.2, 205.3m, 611.2a, 702.19, 601.2f
- Gap class: T (the primitives exist; the tests pin the shapes the parser must write)
- Code: none new (`synthetic-parser-shapes.ts`, `synthetic-big-discount.ts` and the tests).
- AST node: Scavenging Ooze `activated` with `cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['G'] }]`, `targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'graveyard' } } }]` and `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'exile', objects: T1 }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'objectMatches', of: T1, filter: { types: ['creature'] } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'addCounters', objects: THIS, counter: '+1/+1', count: 1 }, { kind: 'gainLife', player: YOU, amount: 1 }] } }] }`; Thickest in the Thicket `triggered` `entersBattlefield` with `targets` one creature and `addCounters` with `count: { kind: 'powerOf', of: T1 }`, and `triggered` `{ kind: 'endStep', whose: 'you' }` with `conditional` `controlsGreatest { filter: { types: ['creature'] }, stat: 'power' }` then `draw` 2 (a tie counts); Rishkar's enters trigger `targets: [{ id: 't1', what: ..., min: 0, max: 2 }]` with `addCounters` 1 on `T1`; Goreclaw's attack trigger `trigger: { kind: 'attacks', what: THIS }` with a `sequence` of `modifyPt` (+1/+1) and `grantKeyword` trample over `{ kind: 'each', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } } }`, both `untilEndOfTurn`, and its cost line a `costReduction` as `SYNTHETIC Titan Tamer` has it (creature spells you cast with power 4 or greater, generic 2).
- Decision and action: the usual targets decisions.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-parser-shapes.test.ts`, `scenario-xx-big-discount.test.ts` (a 4/4 for {3}{G} costs {1}{G}, a 5/5 for {5} costs {3}, a 3/3 is not reduced, only your own spells, two reductions, never below the colored part, a permanent on the battlefield is not a spell, the same shape with a mana value comparison and a type left out).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 32, 2026-10-29 (the shapes; the primitives are older)
- Web: none new.
- What the parser must produce: the shapes above exactly.

## The Angels slice (D-149)

The engine half of the owner's Angels precon (23 cards). Each entry says what the parser must produce for the real Oracle line (the exact text is in the golden corpus, `packages/cards/test/fixtures/corpus/golden.jsonl`). All shapes are generic over the filter, the amount, the creature type or the cost; the tests run each over two or three of them, with SYNTHETIC cards (`src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-*.ts`). The `Added in` of every entry is `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29. Run the checks with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine` (the scenario tests are `scenario-xx-<name>.test.ts`) and `SOAK_GAMES=100 pnpm exec vitest run packages/ai/test/angels-games.test.ts`. The "Web" line of each entry lists what the web has to word or ask; the web step is not done (only what the typecheck and `trigger-text.ts` needed: the wording of the trigger kinds `lifeGained`, `countersAdded` and `attackBatch`, the log line of `proliferated` and the name "Tap creatures" of the `tapPermanents` alternative cost).

| Card | Covered by |
| --- | --- |
| Angel of Vitality | `lifeGainBonus` (new) with the existing conditional static `modifyPt` over `lifeTotal` |
| Angel of the Ruins | parser only: a target slot with `min` 0 `max` 2 over an `anyOf` filter, `typecycling` (existing, `SYNTHETIC Ruin Angel`) |
| Angelic Destiny | parser only: attached `modifyPt`, `grantKeyword` twice, additive `setTypes`, `dies` trigger on `attached` with `moveToZone` of `thisCard` (existing, `SYNTHETIC Angelic Mantle`) |
| Angelic Sleuth | parser only: `leavesBattlefield` with `hasCounter: true`, `createToken` Clue (existing, `SYNTHETIC Counter Sleuth`) |
| Archangel of Tithes | the existing `attackTax` with a `thisMatches` condition and `blockTax` with `whileAttacking` (new) |
| Emeria Shepherd | `objectMatches` over `triggerSubject` (new `of`) with `conditional`, `may`, `didPrevious` (existing); an Aura card entering by `moveToZone` (new, `auraEntry`) |
| Endless Atlas | `Condition.sameNameAtLeast` (new) in `activateOnlyIf` |
| Exemplar of Light | `lifeGained` and `countersAdded` triggers, `oncePerTurn` (new) |
| Exorcise | parser only: one target slot over an `anyOf` filter with a power comparison (existing, `SYNTHETIC Banishing Light Sorcery`) |
| Firemane Commando | `attackBatch` trigger, `attackersAttackedYou`, `triggerPlayer` (new) |
| Giada, Font of Hope | `othersEnterWithCounters` (new) with a `countOf`; `ManaAbility.spendOnly` (new) |
| Grasp of Fate | `exile.until`, `TargetSpec.onePerOpponent` (new), Auras returned by the delayed trigger (new) |
| Herald of Eternal Dawn | `cantLoseGame`, `opponentsCantWin` (new); `flash`, `flying` (existing) |
| Heraldic Banner | `ManaProduction` `chosenColor` (new) with `asEntersChoose` and the filter `chosenColor` (existing) |
| Merchant of Truth | granted `exalted` (new: a grant adds an instance and its triggered ability); `dies` trigger with `createToken` Clue (existing) |
| Metallic Mimic | `othersEnterWithCounters` with `chosenType` (new); `asEntersChoose`, additive `setTypes` (existing) |
| Metropolis Reformer | `playerHexproof` (new); `dealtDamage` trigger with `triggerDamage` (existing) |
| Norn's Choirmaster | `ObjectFilter.commander` with `controller: 'you'` (existing) and `proliferate` (new) |
| Sephara, Sky's Blade | `alternativeCosts` kind `tapPermanents` (new); a `grantKeyword` whose subject names a keyword (new) |
| Seraph of the Sword | `preventDamageToThis` (new) |
| Serra Avenger | `cantCastThis` and `Condition.ownTurnNumber` (new) |
| Tome of Legends | `ObjectFilter.commander: 'yours'` (new value); `entersWithCounters`, `addCounters`, `removeCounters` cost (existing) |
| Vanguard Seraph | `lifeGained` trigger with `firstEachTurn` (new); `surveil` (existing) |

### "If you would gain life, you gain that much life plus 1 instead.", "Whenever you gain life, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature.", "Whenever you gain life for the first time each turn, surveil 1."

- CR: 119.3, 119.9, 119.10, 614.1a, 702.15
- Gap class: P (a replacement of the amount and a trigger on the event)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Replacement` `lifeGainBonus`, `Trigger` `lifeGained`), `rules/life-gain.ts` (`gainLife`: the one place life is gained, by the `gainLife` effect and by lifelink), `rules/triggers.ts` (`occurrences` of `lifeGained`, `nth` from `TurnLog.lifeGained`), `state/events.ts` (`lifeGained`), `ast/validate-angels.ts` (`checkAngelsTrigger`, `checkAngelsReplacement`)
- AST node: Angel of Vitality `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'lifeGainBonus', amount: 1 } }` and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'this' }, power: 2, toughness: 2 }, condition: { kind: 'lifeTotal', compare: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 25 } } }`; Exemplar of Light's first line `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'lifeGained', who: 'you' }, effect: { kind: 'addCounters', objects: { kind: 'this' }, counter: '+1/+1', count: 1 } }`; Vanguard Seraph `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'lifeGained', who: 'you', firstEachTurn: true }, effect: { kind: 'surveil', count: 1 } }`. `who` is `'you'`, `'opponent'` or `'any'`; `firstEachTurn` is true or left out. The replacement applies to its controller's every gain (the effect and lifelink alike), several add up, a gain of 0 is no event and is not replaced (CR 119.10). The trigger fires once for each gain, with the amount after the replacement in the event (`lifeGained.amount`, and `nth` the player's gains this turn); no `Amount` reads that number yet.
- Decision and action: none (a surveil asks its own question).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-life-gain.test.ts` (a gain of 3 is 4 with a bonus of 1, the number is the card's (3 makes 6), two bonuses each apply once and add up, a gain of 0 is no event and is not replaced, the controller's gains only and not the opponent's permanent's, lifelink is a gain too, the ability goes with the permanent, the conditional +2/+2 at 25 life; the trigger "for the first time each turn": one gain, two gains in a resolution trigger it once, a second spell in the same turn does not and the next turn does, an opponent's gain is not mine, surveil 1 asks the controller; "a player" and "an opponent" gains). Mutation checks: a handful of targeted mutants per mechanic on a scratch copy, each killed by these tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` words `lifeGained` (done, minimal); the `lifeGained` event has no log line of its own (the `lifeChanged` line it comes with is the line); no words for the `lifeGainBonus` replacement.
- What the parser must produce: the three shapes above. Needs `replacement:lifeGainBonus`, `trigger:lifeGained`.

### "Whenever you put one or more +1/+1 counters on this creature, draw a card. This ability triggers only once each turn."

- CR: 122.6, 122.6a, 603.2
- Gap class: P (a trigger on counters put, and a limit on how often an ability triggers)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `countersAdded`, `Ability.triggered.oncePerTurn`, `GameEvent` `counterAdded.by`), `rules/triggers.ts` (`collectTriggers` with `limitOncePerTurn`), `state/types.ts` (`TurnLog.triggered`), `rules/effects.ts`, `rules/zones.ts`, `rules/tokens.ts` and `rules/proliferate.ts` (the `counterAdded` events and who put the counters), `ast/validate-angels.ts`
- AST node: Exemplar of Light's second line `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'countersAdded', what: { kind: 'this' }, counter: '+1/+1', by: 'you' }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 }, oncePerTurn: true }`. `what` is any trigger subject (`this`, or a filter such as `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }`); `counter` is the kind as the engine writes it (`'+1/+1'`, `'page'`, `'stun'`), left out for any kind; `by` is `'you'` (the controller of this ability put them) or `'any'`. One event for one instance of putting, however many counters. Counters a permanent enters with are put by its controller; proliferate counts as put by its controller. `oncePerTurn` makes the second event of a turn not noticed at all; it is the object's own count (a permanent that left and came back is new, CR 400.7), and it is `limitOncePerTurn` in the single and the batch trigger paths.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-life-gain.test.ts` (a life gain puts a counter and the counter draws a card, two gains put two counters and draw once, another source of counters in the same turn does not draw again and the next turn does; "you put" counts my counters on my creature and not the opponent's, any kind of counter, "by any player" with only +1/+1 counters).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` words `countersAdded` (done, minimal); `oncePerTurn` is not said.
- What the parser must produce: the line above (the sentence "This ability triggers only once each turn." sets `oncePerTurn` on the ability before it). Needs `trigger:countersAdded` and `ability:oncePerTurn` (the feature the activated ability already has, now valid on a triggered one).

### "Whenever you attack with two or more creatures, draw a card.", "Whenever another player attacks with two or more creatures, they draw a card if none of those creatures attacked you."

- CR: 508.1, 508.1b, 603.2, 603.4
- Gap class: P (a batch trigger on the declaration, a condition on it, and a player who is the subject)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `attackBatch`, `Condition` `attackersAttackedYou`), `rules/triggers.ts` (the declaration as one event), `rules/trigger-subject.ts` (the attacking player, `triggerPlayer`, and the declared attackers), `rules/conditions.ts` (`attackersAttackedYou`), `ast/validate-angels.ts` (the condition is valid only in the effect of an `attackBatch` trigger)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attackBatch', who: 'you', atLeast: 2 }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } }` and `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attackBatch', who: 'opponent', atLeast: 2 }, effect: { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'not', term: { kind: 'attackersAttackedYou' } }, effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'triggerPlayer' }, count: 1 } } }`. `who` is the attacking player, relative to the controller of the ability (`'you'` or `'opponent'`: "another player"); `atLeast` is the number of declared attackers that match `what` (all of them when `what` is left out: `what: { subtypes: ['Angel'] }` is "three or more Angels"). It triggers once for a declaration, whatever it holds; the attackers are read as they are declared. `attackersAttackedYou` is true when at least one declared attacker was declared as attacking the controller as a player (not a planeswalker or a battle they control), read from the declaration (a creature removed since still counts); "none of those creatures attacked you" is its `not`. The player who attacked is `triggerPlayer`.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-attack-batch.test.ts` (two attackers draw one card, three attackers still one, one attacker or none nothing, the opponent's declaration is not mine; "none of those creatures attacked you" with both at my planeswalker, one at me and one at the planeswalker, both at me, and a creature removed from combat before the ability resolves; "three or more Angels" with two Angels and a Bear and with three; "that player" is the attacking player; the validator refuses `attackersAttackedYou` outside an `attackBatch` trigger and an `attackBatch` of fewer than two).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: `game/trigger-text.ts` words `attackBatch` (done, minimal); no words for `attackersAttackedYou`.
- What the parser must produce: the two lines above. Needs `trigger:attackBatch`, `condition:attackersAttackedYou`.

### "Each other Angel you control enters with an additional +1/+1 counter on it for each Angel you already control.", "Each other creature you control of the chosen type enters with an additional +1/+1 counter on it."

- CR: 614.1c, 614.12, 122.6, 122.6a
- Gap class: P (a replacement for a group of entering permanents)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Replacement` `othersEnterWithCounters`), `rules/enters-group.ts` (`groupEntryCounters`, `addCounterMaps`), `rules/zones.ts` and `rules/tokens.ts` (the counters are there when anything triggers from the entering), `ast/validate-angels.ts`
- AST node: Giada `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'othersEnterWithCounters', what: { subtypes: ['Angel'], controller: 'you', other: true }, counter: '+1/+1', count: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { subtypes: ['Angel'], controller: 'you' } } } }`; Metallic Mimic `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'othersEnterWithCounters', what: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', other: true, chosenType: true }, counter: '+1/+1', count: 1 } }` with its `asEntersChoose` (`creatureType`) and additive `setTypes` with `chosenType: true` (existing). `what` is the entering permanent as it would be on the battlefield (`controller: 'you'` is the controller of the permanent with the ability, `other` leaves that permanent out, `chosenType` and `chosenColor` read its choice; `token: false` is "nontoken"); `count` is a number or a `countOf`, counted for the controller of the permanent with the ability without the entering permanent. The counters are in addition to those the permanent enters with otherwise; cards, tokens and copies that become tokens are covered; it does nothing for the permanent itself unless the filter lets it match; it goes with the permanent's abilities.
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-enters-group.test.ts` (the Chief alone gives one counter, two Angels there give two, the Chief gets none from its own ability, a creature that is not an Angel none, an opponent's Angel none, a token Angel and one from the graveyard get them, it stops when the Chief leaves; the chosen type, another type none, the choice read from the permanent; two counters for a nontoken creature, a -1/-1 counter on the opponent's creatures).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: no words for the replacement.
- What the parser must produce: the two replacements above. Needs `replacement:othersEnterWithCounters`.

### "Whenever your commander enters or attacks, put a page counter on this artifact.", "Whenever a commander you control enters or attacks, proliferate."

- CR: 903.3, 603.2, 701.34a
- Gap class: P (a filter value) and T (the triggers exist)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.commander: true | 'yours'`), `rules/select.ts` (`isCommanderObject`, `yours` reads the owner), `rules/filter-match.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts`
- AST node: Tome of Legends, two abilities, `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { commander: 'yours' } }, effect: { kind: 'addCounters', objects: { kind: 'this' }, counter: 'page', count: 1 } }` and the same with `{ kind: 'attacks', what: { commander: 'yours' } }`; its other lines are `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'entersWithCounters', counter: 'page', count: 1 } }` and `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }, { kind: 'tap' }, { kind: 'removeCounters', counter: 'page', count: 1 }], effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 } }`; Norn's Choirmaster the same two triggers with `what: { commander: true, controller: 'you' }` and `effect: { kind: 'proliferate' }`. "Your commander" is a commander card the controller of the ability owns and designated, wherever it is controlled now; "a commander you control" is `commander: true` with `controller: 'you'`. A static ability may not name `commander`.
- Decision and action: none (the proliferate asks, see below).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-commander-triggers.test.ts` (your commander cast from the command zone gives a counter and attacking another, a creature that is not a commander nothing, another player's commander that I control is not mine, a page counter is spent to draw a card; "a commander you control": mine gain 1 life, an opponent's commander that I control counts, the opponent's under their control does not, a creature that is not a commander does not), `scenario-xx-proliferate.test.ts` (the commander entering and attacking proliferate).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: `game/filter-text.ts` has no words for `commander: 'yours'`.
- What the parser must produce: the lines above. Needs `selector:commander` (the same feature for both values).

### "Proliferate." (the keyword action, as a spell or an effect)

- CR: 701.34, 701.34a, 122.6, 104.3d
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `proliferate`), `rules/proliferate.ts` (`runProliferate`, `proliferateCandidates`), `rules/effects.ts`, `rules/stack.ts` (the instruction and its answer), `api.ts` (`choosePermanents` with `players`), `legal-actions.ts` (none, all and each one), `state/decisions.ts` (`ChoosePermanentDecision.players`), `state/events.ts` (`proliferated`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'proliferate' }`, alone as a spell effect (`spell: { effect: { kind: 'proliferate' } }`) or as the effect of an ability or a trigger, or one in a `sequence`. The controller chooses any number of permanents with a counter (anyone's) and players with poison counters, and each chosen permanent and player is given one more counter of each kind it has; counters are put by the controller. With nothing to choose from nothing happens and nobody is asked.
- Decision and action: `choosePermanent` with `purpose: 'proliferate'`, `legal` the permanents with a counter, `players` the players with poison counters, `min` 0, `max` `legal.length`; the answer is `{ kind: 'choosePermanents', objects, players? }`, any subset (the legal-action list holds none, all and each single one; the engine takes any subset). The events `counterAdded` (with `by`), `poisonChanged` and then `proliferated` follow.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-proliferate.test.ts` (only permanents with a counter and players with poison are offered, everything chosen gives each kind one more, nothing chosen changes nothing, a subset (my counter and the opponent's poison, not the opponent's -1/-1), a permanent with no counter, a player with no poison and a repeated choice are refused, the legal actions list the empty choice, everything and each alone, nothing to choose asks nobody, ten poison counters lose the game, "whenever you put one or more +1/+1 counters" triggers for the permanent proliferated and not for the opponent's proliferate).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: a dialog for `choosePermanent` purpose `proliferate` (the permanents and the players, any number); the log line of `proliferated` is done (minimal).
- What the parser must produce: `{ kind: 'proliferate' }`. Needs `effect:proliferate`.

### "You have hexproof.", "You can't lose the game and your opponents can't win the game.", "You can't cast Serra Avenger during your first, second, or third turns of the game.", "Prevent all combat damage that would be dealt to this creature."

- CR: 702.11b, 104.2, 104.2a, 104.3, 104.3a, 101.2, 601.2, 615.1, 615.6, 113.6
- Gap class: P (four statics and one condition)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `playerHexproof`, `cantLoseGame`, `opponentsCantWin`, `cantCastThis`, `preventDamageToThis`; `Condition` `ownTurnNumber`; `SeatState.turnsTaken`), `rules/player-protection.ts` (`playerCanBeTargetedBy`, `cannotLoseGame`), `rules/select.ts` and `rules/legality.ts` (targets), `rules/sba.ts` (704.5a-c, 704.6c), `rules/cast-restrictions.ts` (`castForbiddenByOwnAbility`, read by `canCast`, `canCastDuringResolution`, the legal-action lists and the cast itself), `rules/prevention.ts` (prevention to the permanent), `rules/conditions.ts` (`ownTurnNumber`), `rules/flow.ts` and `rules/setup.ts` (`turnsTaken`), `ast/validate-angels.ts` (`checkAngelsStatic`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'playerHexproof' } }`; `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'cantLoseGame' } }` and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'opponentsCantWin' } }`; Serra Avenger `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'cantCastThis' }, condition: { kind: 'ownTurnNumber', compare: { cmp: 'atMost', value: 3 } } }` with `flying` and `vigilance`; Seraph of the Sword `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'preventDamageToThis', subject: { kind: 'this' }, scope: 'combat' } }` (`scope` is `'combat'`, `'noncombat'` or `'all'`). `playerHexproof`: the controller can't be the target of spells and abilities its opponents control (its own can); read when targets are chosen and as the spell or ability resolves. `cantLoseGame`: the numbers stay what they are and the player loses when the ability is gone and a state-based action still finds the condition; a concession still works (CR 104.3a). `opponentsCantWin`: a no-op today (CR 104.2a, the last player in the game wins, overrides it), and the card needs the node to be supported. `cantCastThis`: the condition is required; it is read for the player who would cast the card with the card as "this", for every way to cast (alternative costs and free casts too, CR 101.2); `ownTurnNumber` is true only on the turn of the player and when the turns they have had, this one included, satisfy the comparison (`atMost` 3 is the first three).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-angels-statics.test.ts` (an opponent's Bolt can't target me and can target the opponent and the creature, my own Bolt can, a spell that can only target me can't be cast, it works only while the creature is there, the dealt-damage life gain; 0 life, negative life, ten poison, 21 commander damage and an empty library do not lose the game and do without the creature, the loss when it leaves, a concession, the opponent of the Herald's controller loses normally; prevention of combat damage to the creature, damage that is not combat damage is dealt, a lifelink creature gains nothing, "all damage" stops a Bolt, combat damage to the player is not prevented; the first three turns cannot cast and the fourth can, a flash creature cast in the opponent's turn and not in my own early turn).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: no words for these five nodes; `SeatState.turnsTaken` is not shown.
- What the parser must produce: the four lines above (Serra Avenger's text names the card: "You can't cast ~ during your first, second, or third turns of the game" is the one `cantCastThis` with the condition). Needs `static:playerHexproof`, `static:cantLoseGame`, `static:opponentsCantWin`, `static:cantCastThis`, `static:preventDamageToThis`, `condition:ownTurnNumber`.

### "As long as this creature is attacking, creatures can't block unless their controller pays {1} for each of those creatures."

- CR: 509.1b, 509.1c, 509.1d, 118.12a, 506.4
- Gap class: P (the mirror of the attack tax of D-125)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `blockTax`), `rules/block-tax.ts` (`blockTax`, `blockTaxSymbols`, `payableBlockTax`, `affordableBlockers`), `rules/combat-flow.ts` (`answerBlockPayment`), `rules/combat-legality.ts`, `api.ts` (route), `legal-actions.ts` (the block lists trimmed to what can be paid), `state/decisions.ts` (`DeclareBlockersDecision.blockCost`, `PayManaDecision` purpose `blockTax`), `ast/validate-angels.ts` (`checkBlockTax`)
- AST node: Archangel of Tithes' second line `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'blockTax', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }], whileAttacking: true } }`; the first line is the existing `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'attackTax', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['1'] }], planeswalkers: true }, condition: { kind: 'thisMatches', filter: { tapped: false } } }`. Without `whileAttacking` the tax is always on ("Creatures can't block unless ..."). Several taxes add up. The cost is mana only, no X. The tax is paid after the blockers are chosen: a `payMana` decision with purpose `blockTax` follows the declaration; the blocking creatures may tap for mana (blocking does not tap them), so `blockCost.affordable` is exact for mana from lands and a floor for mana from the blockers; a declaration with more blockers than `affordable` is refused and the legal-action list holds none (a player is never required to pay, CR 509.1c).
- Decision and action: `declareBlockers` with `blockCost: { cost, affordable, source }` when some permanent taxes blocking; then `payMana` purpose `blockTax` (`payAuto` or `payManual`; no cancel).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-block-tax.test.ts` (the decision says what blocking costs and how many can be paid for, one blocker is paid with a land, two blockers need two lands and one land is refused, no block costs nothing, with no mana no block is declared or listed, cancelling the payment takes the declaration back, paying by hand and a payment that does not cover the cost is refused, only while the Angel is attacking, the Angel leaving combat, a creature that taps for mana pays for its own block, the Bridge and the Angel together cost {1}{1}, the Bridge alone).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: a dialog for the block cost (the decision says `affordable`), and for the `payMana` purpose `blockTax`; no words for `blockTax`.
- What the parser must produce: the static above, with the sentence "As long as this creature is attacking," as `whileAttacking` and any other "as long as" as the ability's `condition`. Needs `static:blockTax`.

### "Spend this mana only to cast an Angel spell.", "{T}: Add one mana of the chosen color."

- CR: 106.6, 106.4, 614.12, 607.2d
- Gap class: P (restricted mana, a production)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ManaAbility.spendOnly`, `ManaProduction` `chosenColor`), `mana/types.ts` (`ManaRestriction` `spellMatch`, `SpendTarget.subtypes`), `mana/pool.ts` (only mana that may pay is spent), `rules/mana-outcomes.ts` (`spendRestrictionOf`, `productionOutcomes`), `rules/mana-sources.ts`, `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`castManaPlan` passes the spell's types and subtypes), `view.ts` (restricted mana in the pool)
- AST node: Giada `{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'fixed', mana: ['W'] }, spendOnly: { subtypes: ['Angel'] } }`; with a type `spendOnly: { types: ['artifact', 'creature'] }` or both (`{ types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Elf'] }`: a spell must be of one of the types and of one of the subtypes); Heraldic Banner's third line `{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], production: { kind: 'chosenColor' } }` after `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'asEntersChoose', what: 'color' } }` and the existing `modifyPt` over `{ types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', chosenColor: true }`. Restricted mana can pay only a spell (never an activation or a cost of an ability) that matches; it stays in the pool as restricted mana until the end of the step or phase. `chosenColor` makes nothing when the permanent made no choice.
- Decision and action: none new (`payMana`; the pool lists the restricted mana).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mana-restricted.test.ts` (it pays for an Angel spell and not for a creature that is not an Angel, not for an ability, tapped by hand it stays in the pool as restricted mana and an Angel spell uses it, a spell it can't pay does not use it; "artifact or creature spells": a creature and an artifact are paid, a Bolt and a sorcery are not; "an Elf creature spell" needs the type and the subtype; the chosen color W and G, the +1/+0 for creatures of the chosen color and not the opponent's, the choice made as it enters).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: the mana pool must show restricted mana and what it can pay (`ManaObject` has the restriction); no words for `spendOnly`.
- What the parser must produce: the two shapes above; "Spend this mana only to cast ..." attaches to the mana ability before it. Needs `ability:manaSpendOnly` and `manaProduction:chosenColor`.

### "When this enchantment enters, for each opponent, exile up to one target nonland permanent that player controls until this enchantment leaves the battlefield."

- CR: 610.3, 610.3a, 610.3b, 610.3c, 115.1, 601.2c, 400.7
- Gap class: P (an "until" exile, a slot for each opponent, and the return)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `exile.until`, `TargetSpec.onePerOpponent`), `rules/effects.ts` and `rules/move-choice.ts` (the exile and its return), `rules/exile-return.ts` (`releaseExiled`, D-154: the return is a second one-shot effect, not a trigger), `state/types.ts` (`ExileReturn`, `GameState.exileReturns`), `rules/target-choice.ts` (`targetChoiceSpecs`, `targetAnswerError`: `onePerGroup`), `state/chosen.ts` (`ChosenTarget.group`), `legal-actions.ts` (a target for each opponent at most), `ast/validate-angels.ts`
- AST node: Grasp of Fate `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, targets: [{ id: 't1', onePerOpponent: true, what: { kind: 'object', filter: { notTypes: ['land'], controller: 'opponent' } } }], effect: { kind: 'exile', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, until: 'sourceLeaves' } }`; an activated version `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [...], targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'opponent' } } }], effect: { kind: 'exile', objects: T1, until: 'sourceLeaves' } }`. The slot has at most one target for each opponent still in the game; `min` 0 or left out ("up to one"); `max` left out is one for each opponent. The cards return to the battlefield under their owners' control (CR 610.3c) when the source leaves, as a second one-shot effect run right after the leaving (CR 610.3, D-154: nothing goes on the stack, and cards returned by simultaneous leavings come back together); if the source left already as the ability resolves, nothing is exiled (CR 610.3a, 610.3b); a card that left exile meanwhile is a new object and is not returned (CR 400.7); a token has ceased to exist. An Aura that returns chooses what it enchants, see the next entry. Only in an ability of a permanent.
- Decision and action: `targets` with a slot that has `onePerGroup: true` (the engine refuses two targets of one player's); the return (D-154, no trigger) asks nothing but the host of an Aura.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-exile-until.test.ts` (the slot has none at least and one at most and says it is grouped, the controller's own permanents and lands are not offered, none may be chosen, two targets for one opponent are refused; the permanent comes back when the Grasp is destroyed as a new object, a noncreature permanent, under its owner's control, nothing is exiled when the Grasp left while its ability was on the stack, two Grasps return their own; the activated version with a Warden that dies or leaves in response; an Aura that returns asks what it enchants with two creatures, chooses by itself with one, "enchant creature you control" offers only mine, a non-Aura card enters as always, a Grasp that exiled an Aura returns it; several opponents: one target of each, two of one refused).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: the targets panel must show the slot as "up to one for each opponent" (the decision has `onePerGroup`); no words for `until` and `onePerOpponent`.
- What the parser must produce: the trigger above. Needs `effect:exile`; `until` and `onePerOpponent` are fields with no feature name of their own (`validateCard` refuses them where they are not allowed).

### "Return target nonland permanent card from your graveyard to the battlefield." (an Aura among them), "Landfall: ... If that land is a Plains, you may return that nonland permanent card to the battlefield instead."

- CR: 303.4f, 303.4g, 614.12, 603.4, 608.2c
- Gap class: P (an Aura entering by an effect, a condition on the triggering object)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Condition.objectMatches.of` may be `triggerSubject`), `rules/aura-entry.ts` (`isAuraEntering`, `runAuraEntry`), `rules/stack.ts` (the `auraEntry` instruction and its answer), `rules/effects.ts` (`moveToZone`, `move.attachTo`), `state/decisions.ts` (`ChoosePermanentDecision` purpose `auraHost`, `subject`), `ast/supported.ts` (`auraEntryIssue`: only a `choose` selector is refused)
- AST node: Emeria Shepherd `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'landfall' }, targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', controller: 'you', anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['creature'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }, { types: ['planeswalker'] }, { types: ['battle'] }] } } }], effect: { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'objectMatches', of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' }, filter: { subtypes: ['Plains'] } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: T1, to: 'battlefield' } }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'not', term: { kind: 'didPrevious' } }, effect: { kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: T1, to: 'hand' } } }] }, else: { kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: T1, to: 'hand' } } } }`. An Aura card that a `moveToZone` of a target, `moved`, `this` or another object selector puts onto the battlefield is not cast: its controller chooses what it enchants among the permanents it can enchant (its enchant ability, protection and hexproof as the rules ask); with nothing to enchant, or when only one can be chosen, the engine says so or chooses itself, and with none the Aura stays where it was. A `choose` selector (a card chosen from a zone by the player) to the battlefield is still refused for an Aura. A token copy of a land or an Aura stays refused.
- Decision and action: `choosePermanent` with `purpose: 'auraHost'`, `subject` the Aura, `min` and `max` 1; the answer is `choosePermanents` with one permanent.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-exile-until.test.ts` (Auras returning), `scenario-xx-landfall-return.test.ts` (a land that is not a Plains: accept to the hand, decline leaves it; a Plains: accept the battlefield, decline it and accept the hand, decline both, an artifact enters too, an Aura enters attached to a creature its controller chooses; lands, instants and sorceries are not offered, only my graveyard's cards, an opponent's land does not trigger it).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: a dialog for `choosePermanent` purpose `auraHost` (choose what the Aura enchants); `objectMatches` over `triggerSubject` has no words.
- What the parser must produce: the trigger above (the "If that land is a Plains, you may ... instead" is the `conditional` with `else`; the "you may" of the first `may` and the "if you do" of the second are the existing `may` and `didPrevious`). Needs `condition:objectMatches` (it was supported; `of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' }` is new and checked by `validateCard`).

### "Clues you control have exalted.", "Creatures you control have exalted." (any grant of a keyword whose every instance counts)

- CR: 702.83, 702.83a, 702.83b, 702.108, 702.108b, 613.1f
- Gap class: K (a keyword the engine expands only from a printed instance)
- Code: `ast/features.ts` (`GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS` gains `exalted` and `prowess`), `rules/derive-layers.ts` (`INSTANCE_KEYWORDS`, `applyAbilityLayer`, exported `expansionOf`: every granted instance adds the keyword and the triggered ability it stands for), `rules/derive-keywords.ts`
- AST node: Merchant of Truth `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantKeyword', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { subtypes: ['Clue'], controller: 'you' } }, keyword: { kind: 'exalted' } } }`; "Equipped creature has prowess" and "target creature gains prowess until end of turn" are the existing `grantKeyword` with `{ kind: 'prowess' }`. Each grant adds one instance, whatever the object already has, and its triggered ability (so a permanent with printed exalted and one grant has two, and exalted counts instances among permanents the controller controls, CR 702.83b).
- Decision and action: none.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-granted-exalted.test.ts` (no Clue nothing is exalted, a nontoken creature of mine dies and the Clue has exalted, one Clue is one instance (+1/+1 for the lone attacker), two Clues +2/+2, two attackers are not alone, the Clues stop when the Merchant leaves; printed and granted exalted add up (two instances on the Lord's creature, one on each other), the opponent's creatures are not affected, it stops when the Lord leaves); `ast-supported.test.ts` and `ast-features.test.ts`.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: none new (keyword chips already exist for both).
- What the parser must produce: the static above; the parser grammar already reads a granted prowess and exalted (the tests that said otherwise were changed). Needs `static:grantKeyword` with `keyword:exalted` or `keyword:prowess` (both are in `GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS` now).

### "Other creatures you control with flying have indestructible." and "You may pay {W} and tap four untapped creatures you control with flying rather than pay this spell's mana cost."

- CR: 613.1f, 613.8a, 613.8b, 118.9, 118.9a, 118.9d, 601.2f-h, 118.3, 302.6, 702.12
- Gap class: P (a grant whose subject reads keywords) and P (an alternative cost with a tap)
- Code: for the grant: `ast/validate-attach.ts` (`checkStaticSubject(..., keywords)`: only `grantKeyword` may name a keyword), `rules/derive.ts` (`keywordsWith`), `rules/derive-statics.ts` (`dependsOnKeywords`, the `later` passes until none more applies), `rules/filter-match.ts` (`FilterSubject.keywords`). For the cost: `ast/types.ts` (`AlternativeCost` kind `tapPermanents`, `NamedAlternativeKind`), `ast/validate-cost.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`, `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`TapRequirement`, `SpellCost.tap`, `castManaPlan` leaving enough permanents, `AlternativeCostInfo.tap`), `rules/mana-sources.ts` (`PlanOptions.avoidObjects`), `rules/cast-additional.ts` (`payAdditionalCosts`: the tap before the sacrifice, the discard and the exile; `answerTap`), `rules/cast.ts` (`answerCastTap`, the manual payment refused when it would leave too few), `api.ts`, `rules/priority.ts` (the option), `view.ts` (`AlternativeCostView.tap`), `state/types.ts` (`CastingContext.extra.tap`)
- AST node: Sephara `alternativeCosts: [{ kind: 'tapPermanents', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['W'] }], filter: { types: ['creature'], keywords: ['flying'] }, count: 4 }]` on the face, `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'flying' } }`, `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'lifelink' } }` and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantKeyword', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], keywords: ['flying'], controller: 'you' }, other: true }, keyword: { kind: 'indestructible' } } }`. `keywords` and `notKeywords` ("creatures you control without flying have flying") are read after the effects that do not read keywords, over the keywords the target has with the grants applied so far, and the effects that did not apply are looked at again until none more does; two effects that depend on each other are not put in dependency order (ROADMAP). The alternative cost's filter has a controller of `you` or none, the battlefield and no `tapped` (the engine adds untapped); `count` is at least 1; the mana cost is mana only with no X; the engine pays the mana first, plans it to leave `count` permanents to tap, then asks which (none when exactly `count` qualify); a creature that came under control this turn may be tapped for it (CR 302.6). Not with Convoke, Improvise or Delve, and not on a land.
- Decision and action: the `castSpell` action with `alternative: 'tapPermanents'`; `choosePermanent` with `purpose: 'tap'`, `forCost` true, `min` and `max` `count`; `cancel` takes the whole cast back (CR 733). `PriorityOption` `castSpell` has `alternative: 'tapPermanents'` and `ObjectView.alternativeCosts` lists `{ kind: 'tapPermanents', cost: ['W'], tap: { filter, count: 4 } }`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-tap-alt.test.ts` (what is offered and what is not, the cost with exactly four fliers and with five, a choice of three refused, a cancel, a flier that makes mana left to tap, a payment by hand refused, summoning-sick creatures, the grant for a flier, for the opponent's flier, in every order with a creature that gets flying from another effect, an indestructible flier surviving Murder), `ast-tap-alt-validate.test.ts` (the checks of the cost and of the subject). Mutation checks: a handful of targeted mutants per mechanic on a scratch copy, each killed by these tests, except the guard of `canPaySpellCost` that `castManaPlan` repeats for a cost with mana (`docs/ROADMAP.md`).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: a pick of the permanents to tap (the existing purpose `tap` panel may serve) and the wording of the option "Cast for {W} and tap four creatures"; the alternative cost's name is "Tap creatures" for now (`game/alt-cost.ts`, typecheck fix).
- What the parser must produce: the face fields and the static above. Needs `castAlternative:tapPermanents`, `static:grantKeyword` and `selector:keywords`.

### "Exile target artifact, enchantment, or creature with power 4 or greater.", "When this creature enters, exile up to two target artifacts and/or enchantments.", "Enchanted creature gets +4/+4, has flying and first strike, and is an Angel in addition to its other types. When enchanted creature dies, return this card to its owner's hand.", "Whenever another permanent you control leaves the battlefield, if it had counters on it, investigate.", "{2}, {T}: Draw a card. Activate only if you control three or more lands with the same name."

- CR: 115.1, 601.2c, 702.29e, 303.4, 603.10a, 122.1, 701.16, 602.5b
- Gap class: T (the primitives exist; the tests pin the shapes the parser must write), and one condition
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Condition.sameNameAtLeast`), `rules/conditions.ts`; the rest none new (`synthetic-angels-shapes.ts`, `synthetic-angels-statics.ts` and the tests).
- AST node: Exorcise `spell: { targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }, { types: ['creature'], power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } }] } } }], effect: { kind: 'exile', objects: T1 } }`; Angel of the Ruins `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, targets: [{ id: 't1', min: 0, max: 2, what: { kind: 'object', filter: { anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }] } } }], effect: { kind: 'exile', objects: T1 } }` and `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'typecycling', filter: { subtypes: ['Plains'] }, cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }] } }`; Angelic Destiny `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'enchant', filter: { types: ['creature'] } } }`, `modifyPt` `+4/+4`, two `grantKeyword` (flying, first strike) and `setTypes` `{ subtypes: ['Angel'], additive: true }` all over `{ kind: 'attached', types: ['creature'] }`, and `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'dies', what: { kind: 'attached' } }, effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'thisCard' }, to: 'hand' } }`; Angelic Sleuth `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'leavesBattlefield', what: { controller: 'you', other: true, hasCounter: true } }, effect: { kind: 'createToken', token: { kind: 'preset', preset: 'Clue' }, count: 1 } }`; Endless Atlas `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }, { kind: 'tap' }], effect: { kind: 'draw', player: { kind: 'you' }, count: 1 }, activateOnlyIf: { kind: 'sameNameAtLeast', filter: { types: ['land'] }, count: 3 } }` (`sameNameAtLeast` reads the battlefield permanents the controller controls that match; two basic Plains share a name).
- Decision and action: the usual targets decisions.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-angels-shapes.test.ts` (Exorcise: an artifact, an enchantment and a creature with power 4 are legal targets and are exiled, power 2 is not; up to two targets exiled, none allowed and a creature that is not an artifact is not legal, Plainscycling {2} finds a Plains; the Aura makes the creature a 6/6 with flying, first strike and the Angel type and returns to its owner's hand when the creature dies; a creature with a +1/+1 counter dies and a Clue is made, none without counters, an opponent's permanent and the Sleuth itself do not trigger it), `scenario-xx-angels-statics.test.ts` (the Atlas: three Plains draw a card, two Plains and two Forests and four different names do not, the opponent's lands do not count).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29 (the shapes; the primitives are older)
- Web: no words for `sameNameAtLeast`.

### Polish from the Dinosaurs slice: copies of divided spells and Hideaway after a control change

- CR: 707.10, 707.10c, 115.7f, 601.2d, 702.75a, 608.2g, 305.1, 305.3
- Gap class: P (two defects, not new cards)
- Code: `rules/copy-spell.ts` (`copyDecision`), `rules/copy-spell-answer.ts` (`keepShares`), `state/decisions.ts` (`TargetChoiceSpec.keepsDivision`), `rules/legality.ts` (`canCastDuringResolution`, `canPlayLandByEffect`)
- AST node: none.
- Decision and action: for a copy of a spell that divides damage (`forCopy`), a slot has `min` = `max` = the copy's number of targets, `keepsDivision: true` and no `divide`; the answer is the usual `chooseTargets` with no share on a target (a share is refused): a target the copy already has keeps its own share, a new target takes the share of a target the answer dropped, in the order of `current`. A free cast or a play by an effect from exile is offered to the player who controls the ability, whoever owns the card; a card in a hand stays its owner's.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-copy-divided.test.ts` (the same targets with the same shares, the answer shapes, the order the targets are named in, one and two new targets, the damage dealt, a different number of targets refused, a share refused, the listed actions, an illegal target left), `scenario-xx-hideaway-control.test.ts` (a spell cast and a land played by the new controller, a land refused off-turn, who sees the card, a card in another player's hand), and the AI tests `casual-copy-divided.test.ts`.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 33, 2026-10-29
- Web: a divided copy's targets panel asks no division (`keepsDivision`: show the shares of `current` and take targets only); the `cast` question needs no change.
- What the parser must produce: nothing.

## The Dragons slice (D-151)

The engine half of the owner's Dragons precon (30 cards the engine did not run). Each entry says what the parser must produce for the real Oracle line (the exact text is in the golden corpus, `packages/cards/test/fixtures/corpus/golden.jsonl`). All shapes are generic over the filter, the amount, the creature type, the symbol or the cost; the tests run each over two or three of them, with SYNTHETIC cards (`src/cards/fixtures/synthetic-dragons-*.ts`). The `Added in` of every entry is `ENGINE_VERSION` 34, 2026-10-29. Run the checks with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine` (the scenario tests are `scenario-xx-<name>.test.ts`) and `SOAK_GAMES=100 pnpm exec vitest run packages/ai/test/dragons-games.test.ts`. Face-down cards (Morph) are not in this slice and not built.

| Card | Covered by |
| --- | --- |
| Anger | `functionsIn: 'graveyard'` on a static `grantKeyword` over a filter with `controlsAtLeast` (new) |
| Atsushi, the Blazing Sky | a modal `dies` trigger (existing) whose modes are `exileTop` + `mayPlay` `endOfYourNextTurn` (new) and `createToken` Treasure x3 (existing): `SYNTHETIC Dying Dragon` |
| Breaching Dragonstorm | `exileUntilCast` with `castAtMost` and `rest: 'stayExiled'` (new); the second ability is parser only (`entersBattlefield` of a Dragon you control, `moveToZone` of `this` to the hand) |
| Carnelian Orb of Dragonkind | `ManaAbility.spentRider` (new) |
| Chandra's Ignition | `each.except` (new) with `dealDamage.from` (existing) |
| Chaos Warp | `revealTop` (new) with `moveToZone` to the library, `shuffle` and `ownerOf` (existing) |
| Count on Luck | `exileTop` and `mayPlay` (new) |
| Cursed Mirror | `Replacement` `entersAsCopy` (new); the mana ability is existing |
| Dragonhawk, Fate's Tempest | `exileTop` with a `countOf` amount, `mayPlay` `yourNextEndStep` and a `delayedTrigger` with `exiledStillThere` (new); "enters or attacks" is two triggered abilities with one effect |
| Dragonstorm Globe | parser only: `othersEnterWithCounters` (D-149) and a `anyColor` mana ability (`SYNTHETIC Storm Globe`) |
| Drakuseth, Maw of Flames | `TargetSpec.differentFrom` (new) |
| Goddric, Cloaked Reveler | `Condition.enteredThisTurn` (new) on conditional layer 4, 5 and 7b statics and on a granted activated ability (new) |
| Goldlust Triad | keyword `myriad` (new, does nothing with two players); the Treasure trigger is existing |
| Haven of the Spirit Dragon | `ManaAbility.spendOnly` with a type and a subtype (new); the sacrifice-and-return ability is parser only (`SYNTHETIC Dragon Sanctum`) |
| Hellkite Charger | `extraCombat` (new) with `mayPay` and `untap` of an `each` attacking filter (existing) |
| Herald's Horn | `lookAtTop` with `rest: 'top'` (new), `asEntersChoose`, `costReduction` and the filter `chosenType` (existing) |
| Hit the Mother Lode | `exileUntilCast` (Discover) and the `shortfall` amount (new) with `createToken` `tapped` (existing) |
| Leyline Tyrant | `payAnyAmount` (new) and `unspentManaStays` (new) |
| Mana Geyser | `addMana` with `times` (new) |
| Minion of the Mighty | `Condition.attackersTotalPower` and `enterAttacking` (new) |
| Orb of Dragonkind | `anyCombination` and `spendOnly` with `alsoAbilities` (new); the sacrifice ability is parser only (`SYNTHETIC Dragon Seeker`) |
| Outpost Siege | `asEntersChoose` of `option` and `Condition.chosenOption` (new) |
| Parapet Thrasher | `ModeChoice.notRepeatedThisTurn`, `ControllerFilter` `triggerPlayer` and `eachOtherOpponent` (new) over `combatDamageBatch` (existing) |

### "Exile the top card of your library. You may play that card this turn.", "Until the end of your next turn, you may play those cards.", "You may play those cards until your next end step."

- CR: 701.13, 401, 601.2, 305.1, 611.2a, 610.3
- Gap class: P (two effects that give a card a permission)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `exileTop`, `mayPlay`, `PlayUntil`), `rules/impulse.ts` (`runExileTop`, `runMayPlay`, `playEnd`, `bakeStillExiled`), `rules/continuous.ts` (`expireEndOfTurnEffects`, `expireEndStepEffects`: a play permission ends with the turn, with the end of the controller's next turn or as their next end step begins), `rules/legality.ts` (`mayPlayFromExile`: castable and playable cards in exile), `state/types.ts` (`ContinuousChange` `playPermission`), `ast/validate-dragons.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'exileTop', count: 1 }, { kind: 'mayPlay', objects: { kind: 'moved' }, lasts: 'endOfTurn' }] }`; the count may be an `Amount` (`{ kind: 'countOf', filter: ... }` for Dragonhawk, 2 for Atsushi); `lasts` is `endOfTurn`, `endOfYourNextTurn` or `yourNextEndStep`. The permission names the cards that were exiled (`moved`), a card that leaves exile is a new object and has none (CR 400.7), a land needs a land drop and the active player's main phase (CR 305.1).
- Decision and action: none new (the card is cast or played through the ordinary priority options).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-impulse-play.test.ts` (a spell and a land from exile and the land drop, the mana a spell needs, the timing of the type, the opponent cannot cast the card, an empty library, the permission names the very card, two cards until the end of your next turn, an upkeep trigger, the three durations)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 34, 2026-10-29
- Web: nothing worded: `exileTop`, `mayPlay` and the play permission of a card in exile (the card is in the exile zone and playable) are to word.
- What the parser must produce: the sequence above for "Exile the top card of your library. You may play that card this turn." and its siblings (a count word, "those cards", "until the end of your next turn", "until your next end step"); Count on Luck is that sequence in an `upkeep` trigger of `whose: 'you'`.

- What the parser must produce: the two slots (an `id` for each "target"), `differentFrom` for "other".

### "Whenever one or more Dragons you control deal combat damage to an opponent, choose one that hasn't been chosen this turn - Destroy target artifact that opponent controls. / This creature deals 4 damage to each other opponent. / Exile the top card of your library. You may play it this turn."

- CR: 700.2, 603.3c, 510.2
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ModeChoice.notRepeatedThisTurn`, `ControllerFilter` `triggerPlayer`, `PlayerSelector` `eachOtherOpponent`), `state/types.ts` (`TurnLog.modesChosen`), `rules/modes.ts` (`legalModeSets`, `withModesChosen`), `rules/trigger-stack.ts`, `rules/filter-match.ts`, `rules/select.ts`, `rules/stack.ts` (the target is read against the player of the event as the ability resolves), `rules/trigger-subject.ts` (`readsTriggerPlayer` scans the targets), `ast/validate-dragons.ts` (`checkDragonsFace`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'combatDamageBatch', what: { subtypes: ['Dragon'], controller: 'you' }, to: 'opponent' }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [] }, chooseModes: { min: 1, max: 1, notRepeatedThisTurn: true }, modes: [{ text, targets: [{ id: 'art', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { types: ['artifact'], controller: 'triggerPlayer' } } }], effect: { kind: 'destroy', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 'art' } } }, { text, effect: { kind: 'dealDamage', amount: 4, to: { kind: 'eachOtherOpponent' } } }, { text, effect: ... exileTop and mayPlay } ] }`.
- Decision and action: `chooseModes` (a mode already chosen by this permanent this turn is `legal: false`; with one set left the engine takes it).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-dragons-damage.test.ts` (the modes offered, the one used is not offered again, one left is taken, "that opponent", "each other opponent" with two players hurts nobody, the record of the turn)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 34, 2026-10-29
- Web: `notRepeatedThisTurn`, `triggerPlayer` as a controller word ("that opponent controls", a typecheck fix) and `eachOtherOpponent` ("each other opponent", a typecheck fix) are to word.
- What the parser must produce: the modal trigger above.

### "The owner of target permanent shuffles it into their library, then reveals the top card of their library. If it's a permanent card, they put it onto the battlefield."

- CR: 701.20, 701.24, 110.2a, 400.7
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `revealTop`), `rules/reveal-top.ts`, `ast/validate-dragons.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'moveToZone', objects: T1, to: 'library' }, { kind: 'shuffle', player: { kind: 'ownerOf', of: T1 } }, { kind: 'revealTop', player: { kind: 'ownerOf', of: T1 }, ifMatches: { anyOf: [{ types: ['artifact'] }, { types: ['creature'] }, { types: ['enchantment'] }, { types: ['land'] }, { types: ['planeswalker'] }, { types: ['battle'] }] }, to: 'battlefield' }] }`.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-dragons-reveal.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 34, 2026-10-29
- Web: `revealTop` is to word; the `cardsRevealed` log line exists.
- What the parser must produce: the sequence ("permanent card" is the `anyOf` of the six permanent types).

### "When this enchantment enters, exile cards from the top of your library until you exile a nonland card. You may cast it without paying its mana cost if that spell's mana value is 8 or less. If you don't, put that card into your hand.", "Discover 10. If the discovered card's mana value is less than 10, create a number of tapped Treasure tokens equal to the difference."

- Decision and action: `confirmOptional` of purpose `cascade` with the card as `subject` (decline: the card goes into the hand).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-dragons-reveal.test.ts` (the card found, cast free and declined, over the limit goes to the hand, nothing found, the Treasures for the difference, no difference, the lands stay in exile for Breaching, the Dragon trigger returns the enchantment)

### "At the beginning of your upkeep, look at the top card of your library. If it's a creature card of the chosen type, you may reveal it and put it into your hand."

- CR: 701.20, 614.12
- Gap class: P
