### "When Sarkhan enters, you may behold a Dragon. If you do, create a Treasure token." and "Whenever a Dragon you control enters, put a +1/+1 counter on Sarkhan. Until end of turn, Sarkhan becomes a Dragon in addition to his other types and gains flying."

- CR: 701.4, 608.2c, 613.1d

### "When this creature dies, you may pay any amount of {R}. When you do, it deals that much damage to any target."

- CR: 118.1, 603.12, 603.7c

- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `payAnyAmount`), `rules/pay-amount.ts` (the question, then the payment as for "you may pay"), `rules/may-effects.ts` (`runPaidReflexive`: the reflexive ability with the amount paid as `triggerDamage`), `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionPayAmount`), `api.ts`, `state/decisions.ts` (`chooseX` purpose `payAnyAmount`), `legal-actions.ts`

- Decision and action: `chooseX` (purpose `payAnyAmount`, `min` 0, `max` the most it can pay, `cost` the symbol; no `cancel`), then `payMana` (purpose `mayPay`; `cancel` is not paying), then `targets` for the reflexive ability.

- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 34, 2026-10-29

- What the parser must produce: the trigger above.

### "Lieutenant - As long as you control your commander, this creature gets +2/+2 and has "Whenever this creature attacks, it deals 7 damage to target creature defending player controls.""

- CR: 903.3, 508.5, 613.4c, 613.1f
- Gap class: P

- Tests: `scenario-xx-pay-any-amount.test.ts` (with and without a commander, the legal targets are the defending player's creatures)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 34, 2026-10-29
- Web: `controller: 'defendingPlayer'` is to word ("a creature defending player controls", a typecheck fix).

### Parser only: "Each Dragon you control enters with an additional +1/+1 counter on it.", "{2}, {T}, Sacrifice this land: Return target Dragon creature card or Ugin planeswalker card from your graveyard to your hand.", "{R}, {T}, Sacrifice this artifact: Look at the top seven cards of your library. You may reveal a Dragon card from among them and put it into your hand. Put the rest on the bottom of your library in a random order."

- CR: 614.1c, 122.6, 701.20, 400.7
- Gap class: T (existing primitives, written here the way the parser must write them)
- Code: none new (`othersEnterWithCounters` of D-149, `moveToZone` with a graveyard filter with `anyOf`, `lookAtTop` of D-139)
- AST node: `SYNTHETIC Storm Globe` (`othersEnterWithCounters` with `what: { subtypes: ['Dragon'], controller: 'you' }`, no `other`), `SYNTHETIC Dragon Sanctum` (`activated` with `cost` mana 2, `tap`, `sacrifice` of `this`; a target `{ zone: 'graveyard', controller: 'you', anyOf: [{ types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Dragon'] }, { types: ['planeswalker'], subtypes: ['Ugin'] }] }`), `SYNTHETIC Dragon Seeker` (`lookAtTop` count 7, `take` `{ filter: { subtypes: ['Dragon'] }, max: 1 }`, `rest: 'bottomRandom'`).

### Polish from this slice

- `Instruction` `payAmount` runs in `rules/stack.ts` (it asks the mana engine, which `rules/effects.ts` may not import).
- `legalActionsForDecision` lists, for a `targets` decision with `differentFrom`, only the answers that keep the slots apart, and no `cancel` for `chooseX` of purpose `payAnyAmount`.
- The scenario DSL (`testing/refs.ts`): a land in exile that an effect lets the player play is found in exile, and a spell on the top of the library is cast from there (the hand, the command zone and exile first).

## The Korvold slice, text and tokens (D-175, D-176)

The twelve cards of slice 1 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29). Three engine pieces (D-175, `ENGINE_VERSION` 36) and the parser stage P35 (D-176, `PARSER_VERSION` 40); the other lines needed only the grammar, because the engine already ran what the parser lowers. The tests that pin each shape are named under it; `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each, `parser-p35.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs.

### "Create a Food token or a Treasure token."

- CR: 608.2d, 700.2, 111.10
- Gap class: P (the analysis said T: an inline choice had no engine node)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `chooseOne`, `InlineChoice`), `rules/choose-one.ts`, `rules/effects.ts`, `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionChooseOne`), `api.ts`, `state/decisions.ts` (`ChooseOptionDecision.purpose` `'effect'`), `state/events.ts` (`optionChosen`), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/supported.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`effect:chooseOne`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'chooseOne', options: [{ label: 'create a Food token', effect: { kind: 'createToken', token: { kind: 'preset', preset: 'Food' }, count: 1 } }, { label: 'create a Treasure token', effect: ... }] }` inside a landfall trigger; the decision is `chooseOption` with `purpose` `'effect'` and the labels as options, asked as the ability resolves, answered with `chooseOption`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-choose-one.test.ts` (SYNTHETIC `synthetic-choose-one.ts`: nothing asked as it is cast, the labels in order, each option, a wrong or another player's answer refused, the log event, an upkeep trigger that asks each time, an option that uses the spell's target, `validateCard`'s refusals), `casual-choose-one.test.ts`, `korvold-model.test.ts`, `korvold-render.test.tsx`, `e2e/choose-one.e2e.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 36, 2026-11-02
- Web: the dialog of the named options with the title "Choose one", the prompt "Choose one for X: a or b.", the log line "You choose to create a Food token (X)."
- What the parser must produce: `effect.create-token` with " or a Treasure token" after one predefined token of one lowers to `chooseOne` of two `createToken`, labels "create a <Name> token"; a trigger's "you may" before it is the ability's `optional`.
- Limits: two predefined tokens of one only; the casual agent reads the words of the options (a Treasure, then a card, else the first).

### "When this Aura is put into a graveyard from the battlefield, return it to its owner's hand."

- CR: 700.4, 603.10a, 400.7, 704.5m
- Gap class: P (the analysis said T: the engine's `died` event was for creatures only)
- Code: `rules/zones.ts` (the `died` event for any permanent put into a graveyard from the battlefield); the effect is `moveToZone` of `thisCard` to the hand (existing)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'dies', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'thisCard' }, to: 'hand' } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-dies-permanent.test.ts` (an artifact's and an Aura's own trigger, "whenever a creature dies" not triggered by an artifact, "a permanent you control dies" for both, the Aura back in the hand after its creature died or after it was destroyed), `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 36, 2026-11-02
- Web: nothing new (the log says "X dies.", the CR's word).
- What the parser must produce: `return it to its owner's hand` in a trigger of `~` dying or leaving is `thisCard`; where `~` is still on the battlefield it is `this`; for an object that is gone, or any other "it", the line is flagged.

### "Choose a Background"

- CR: 702.124k
- Gap class: K (a keyword with no effect in the game)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `chooseBackground`), `ast/features.ts`, `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `rules/keyword/vocabulary.ts`, `rules/keyword/index.ts` (the line `keyword.line`); the pair of commanders is `packages/cards/src/validate/partner.ts` (existing)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'chooseBackground' } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-choose-background.test.ts`, `ast-supported.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 36, 2026-11-02
- Web: the keyword chip is generic (nothing new).

### Tokens with an ability in quotes: "create a 1/1 black and green Pest creature token with \"When this token dies, you gain 1 life.\"", "create a 0/1 colorless Eldrazi Spawn creature token. It has \"Sacrifice this token: Add {C}.\"", "create a 0/2 red Dragon Egg creature token with defender and \"When this token dies, create a 2/2 red Dragon creature token with flying and '{R}: This token gets +1/+0 until end of turn.'\""

- CR: 111.3, 111.4, 113.1, 605.1a
- Gap class: T (the engine's `TokenDefinition.abilities` ran them; nothing in the engine changed)
- Code: `rules/token/index.ts`, `rules/static/quoted.ts` (`readQuotedAbility`, `TOKEN_QUOTED_ABILITY`), `rules/effect/korvold.ts` (`effect.it-has-ability`), `ir/fold-token.ts`, `lower/tokens.ts`, `lower/late.ts`, `lower/effects.ts`, `normalize.ts`, `sentence.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'createToken', token: { kind: 'custom', definition: { name: 'Pest', types: ['creature'], subtypes: ['Pest'], colors: ['B', 'G'], power: 1, toughness: 1, abilities: [{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'dies', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'gainLife', ... } }], ... } }, count: 1 }`; the Spawn's ability is `{ kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'sacrifice', what: { kind: 'this' } }], production: { kind: 'fixed', mana: ['C'] } }`; the Dragon Egg's death trigger makes a token whose own ability is an `activated` `modifyPt` of `this`
- Tests: `parser-p35.test.ts`, `parser-p35-needs.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts` (the Spawn is sacrificed for {C}, the Pest's death gives life, the Egg's gives a flying Dragon that {R} pumps), the rules' `examples` and `rejects`
- What the parser must produce: the ability in quotes is read as the ability of a card (`~` is the token; "this token" inside quotes is rewritten to `~`), an activated, a mana or a triggered ability, after keywords or alone, and "It has \"...\"" folds into the one described token before it.
- Limits: a quoted static ability or a keyword the engine cannot grant (`with "This creature gets +1/+1 for each artifact you control."`) stays unread; a card that names itself inside the quote is read as naming the token; "It has" only after one described token; a sentence with a quotation that is followed by another sentence of the same text is not split after the quote's period.

### "When this creature dies, create a number of tapped Treasure tokens equal to its power."

- CR: 111.2, 110.5b, 608.2h
- Gap class: T
- Code: `rules/token/index.ts` (`token.number-of`), `ir/bind.ts` (the count of a token is bound), `lower/effects.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'createToken', token: { kind: 'preset', preset: 'Treasure' }, count: { kind: 'powerOf', of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' } }, tapped: true }`
- Tests: `parser-p35.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts` (X = 3: three tapped Treasures as it dies, X = 0: none)
- What the parser must produce: the tokens are plural, a predefined token or one the text describes; the count is the amount after the tokens ("equal to its power", "the number of ...").

### "When this Aura enters, enchanted creature gains trample and indestructible until end of turn.", "That creature also gains trample until end of turn if you control a creature with power 4 or greater."

- CR: 303.4b, 611.2a, 608.2c
- Gap class: T
- Code: `rules/effect/pump.ts` (`GAIN_SUBJECTS`), `rules/effect/gain.ts` ("also"), `rules/noun/korvold.ts` (`noun.that-object`), `ir/bind.ts` (`BindScope.lastTarget`, `Antecedent.previousLine`)
- AST node: `grantKeyword` of `{ kind: 'attached' }` for the Aura; for the spell `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [grantKeyword doubleStrike of t1, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['creature'], power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } }, count: 1 }, effect: grantKeyword trample of t1 }] }`
- Tests: `parser-p35.test.ts`, `parser-diagnostics.test.ts` ("it" does not cross a line), `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts`
- What the parser must produce: "that creature" at the start of a line of a spell is the last single target of the line before; "it" is not.

### "Mill two cards, then you may return a creature card from your graveyard to your hand.", "Return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield, then shuffle your graveyard into your library."

- CR: 701.17, 608.2d, 701.24, 400.3, 903.9a
- Gap class: T (the analysis feared P for the graveyard: `moveToZone` of an `each` filter already moves a whole graveyard)
- Code: `rules/effect/korvold.ts` (`effect.return-chosen-card`, `effect.shuffle-graveyard`), `lower/effects.ts` (`putFromZone` to a hand), `lower/korvold.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'moveToZone', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }, optional: true }, to: 'hand' }`; `moveToZone` of `{ kind: 'each', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', controller: 'you' } }` to the library, then `{ kind: 'shuffle', player: { kind: 'you' } }`
- Tests: `parser-p35.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts` (chapter I with a creature card, none chosen, no creature card; chapter III with lands and other cards)

### "Equip—{2}, Pay 2 life.", "~ deals 2 damage to that permanent's controller."

- CR: 702.6a, 118.1, 109.4, 608.2h
- Gap class: T
- Code: `rules/keyword/attachments.ts`, `lower/abilities.ts` (`lowerEquipCosts`); `rules/effect/operands.ts` (`noun.its-controller` among the recipients of damage), `lower/nouns.ts` (`recipientOf`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'equip', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['2'] }, { kind: 'payLife', amount: 2 }] } }`; `{ kind: 'dealDamage', amount: 2, to: { kind: 'controllerOf', of: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } } }` after the destroy
- Tests: `parser-p35.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p35.test.ts` (the equip pays {2} and 2 life, is refused without the mana or at 1 life; the damage goes to the controller of the artifact that was destroyed, whoever it is)

### Status of the twelve cards at parser version 40

Awakening Zone, Beledros Witherbloom, Nesting Dragon, Goldvein Hydra, Tireless Provisioner, Rancor, Gilt-Leaf's Embrace, Temur Battle Rage, The Mending of Dominaria, My Precious // Allure of Power, Ganax, Astral Hunter and Cindervines read completely and pass `validateCard` (run from the owner's request file with a placeholder 1/1 for the creatures, because the file has no power or toughness). Whether the deck is fully supported is the owner's run of `pnpm corpus:decks` and `pnpm precon:snapshot` on `decks/korvold.txt`.

## The Korvold slice 2, fetch lands, reflexive sacrifice and Treasure grants (D-177, D-178)

The nine cards of slice 2 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29): Cabaretti Courtyard, Maestros Theater, Riveteers Overlook, Jund Panorama, Fabled Passage, Far Wanderings, Ziatora, the Incinerator, Goldspan Dragon and Bootleggers' Stash. Four engine pieces (D-177, `ENGINE_VERSION` 37) and the parser stage P36 (D-178, `PARSER_VERSION` 41). `convert-grammar-engine-p36.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each, `parser-p36.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs, `scenario-xx-fetch-grants.test.ts` pins the engine pieces with SYNTHETIC fixtures (`synthetic-fetch-grants.ts`).

### "Search your library for a basic land card, put it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle. Then if you control four or more lands, untap that land."

- CR: 701.23, 400.7j, 608.2c
- Gap class: P (the analysis said T + P: a search did not set what "it" means for the instruction after it)
- Code: `rules/effects.ts` (the `search` case starts `lastMoved` empty, each card the search puts somewhere is added as the move runs), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`movesObjects` counts `search`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'search', ... }, { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['land'] }, count: 4 }, effect: { kind: 'untap', objects: { kind: 'moved' } } }] }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-fetch-grants.test.ts` (three lands left and the land found is untapped, two and it stays tapped, only the found land is untapped, finding nothing, a card put into the hand is "it", two cards, a second search replaces what "it" is), `ast-fetch-grants-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p36.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 37, 2026-11-02
- Web: nothing new.
- What the parser must produce: the noun of the search is what "that land" and "it" mean in `tap` and `untap` after it (`LowerContext.searched`), lowered as `moved`; any other move, destroy or exile of the text takes it away. "Untap those lands" after one card and "untap two lands" are flagged.

### "When this land enters, sacrifice it. When you do, search your library for a basic Mountain, Forest, or Plains card, put it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle and you gain 1 life."

- CR: 603.12, 701.21, 205.4, 701.23
- Gap class: T + P (the reflexive trigger after a plain sacrifice was already valid in the engine, `performed` counts what a sacrifice did; the lowering did not read it; the list of types after "basic" was the template half)
- Code: `rules/noun/library.ts` (`noun.library-card`: "a basic Mountain, Forest, or Plains card" is `{ supertypes: ['basic'], anyOf: [{ subtypes: ['Mountain'] }, ...] }`), `lower/sentences.ts` (`lowerDoneReflexive`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'this' } }, { kind: 'reflexive', effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [search, { kind: 'gainLife', player: { kind: 'you' }, amount: 1 }] } }] } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-fetch-grants.test.ts` (SYNTHETIC "Passing Land": the land is sacrificed, the reflexive ability is an object of its own, it offers only the basic Forests and Islands, the life), `parser-p36.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p36.test.ts`
- What the parser must produce: the first sentence of the body is one plain instruction of a kind the engine can tell was done (`sacrifice`, a move, a draw, ...), the next sentence starts "When you do," and is the last; the engine creates the ability only when the instruction was done. A sentence before the sacrifice, or after the reflexive ability, is flagged.

### "At the beginning of your end step, you may sacrifice another creature. When you do, ~ deals damage equal to that creature's power to any target and you create three Treasure tokens."

- CR: 603.12, 608.2h, 113.7a, 701.21
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectRef` `sacrificed`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`Scope.sacrificedEarlier`, `checkSacrificedEarlier`), `rules/select.ts` (`SelectContext.sacrificed`), `rules/effects.ts` and `rules/stack.ts` (`StepOutcome.sacrificed`, `ResolutionContext.sacrificed`), `rules/may-effects.ts` (`runReflexive` carries the permanents to the pending trigger), `rules/trigger-stack.ts` and `rules/ability-object.ts` (`StackData.sacrificedBefore`), `state/types.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`selector:sacrificed`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { types: ['creature'], other: true } } } }, { kind: 'reflexive', targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'anyTarget' } }], effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'powerOf', of: { kind: 'sacrificed' } }, to: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }, { kind: 'createToken', ..., count: 3 }] } }] }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-fetch-grants.test.ts` (SYNTHETIC "Grim Tither", "Pyre Warden": the power of the creature that was sacrificed as it was on the battlefield, counters included, none sacrificed means no reflexive ability, declining), `ast-fetch-grants-validate.test.ts` (refused without a sacrifice before it, with another effect before the reflexive ability, as anything but an amount's object)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 37, 2026-11-02
- Web: the amount says "the sacrificed creature's power" (`game/amount-text.ts`, `ui/decisions/ability-label.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `sacrificedRef` of the lowering context is the reference of the noun of the last sacrifice of one chosen permanent; "that creature" or "its" bound to it in an amount is `sacrificed`. Two creatures sacrificed, or an unrelated "that creature", are not it.
- Limits: the first permanent sacrificed is read; `sacrificedCost` (the cost of an activated ability, D-133) is another reference.

### "Whenever ~ attacks or becomes the target of a spell, create a Treasure token."

- CR: 603.2, 601.2c
- Gap class: P (a spell-only field)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`becomesTarget` `spellOnly`), `rules/triggers.ts` (the object that targeted is a spell on the stack, not an ability), `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`trigger:becomesTargetSpell`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'becomesTarget', what: { kind: 'this' }, by: 'any', spellOnly: true }, effect: ... }` next to an `attacks` ability of its own
- Tests: `scenario-xx-fetch-grants.test.ts` ("Spell Magnet": a spell triggers it, an ability that targets it and a spell that does not do not), `ast-fetch-grants-validate.test.ts`, `fetch-grants-text.test.ts` (web)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 37, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Whenever this creature becomes the target of a spell" (`game/trigger-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `trigger.becomes-target` reads "of a spell" alone as `spellOnly` and "of a spell or ability" as before; `trigger.or` reads "becomes the target of a spell" as one of its events ("attacks or becomes the target of a spell"); "of an ability" alone is not read.

### "Treasures you control have \"{T}, Sacrifice this artifact: Add two mana of any one color.\"", "Lands you control have \"{T}: Create a Treasure token.\""

- CR: 613.1f, 605.1a, 602, 602.5
- Gap class: T (the analysis feared P for the planner: a mana ability that sacrifices its permanent is still a mana ability, and the planner already treats the sacrifice sources of a Treasure as a tier of their own; the engine's `grantAbility` already gave any ability to a group)
- Code: `rules/static/grants.ts` (`QUOTED_MANA` no longer refuses `~`), `lower/statics.ts` (`lowerGrantedAbility` takes a `filter` subject; `namedInQuotes` refuses a group), `normalize.ts` (`namesItselfInQuotes`), `parse-face.ts`, `compile.ts`, `lower/types.ts` (`namedInQuotes`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantAbility', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { subtypes: ['Treasure'], controller: 'you' } }, ability: { kind: 'mana', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }, { kind: 'sacrifice', what: { kind: 'this' } }], production: { kind: 'anyColor', count: 2 } } } }`; for the lands the `ability` is `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [{ kind: 'tap' }], effect: createToken Treasure }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-fetch-grants.test.ts` ("Gilded Banner": a Treasure has its own ability and the given one, two mana of the one color chosen, paid by hand and by the planner, a Treasure the opponent controls does not have it; "Coin Shrine": each land offers the ability, a tapped land does not, a land played later has it), `parser-p36.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p36.test.ts`
- What the parser must produce: `~` inside the quotes is the permanent that has the ability ("this artifact", "this land"), `this` in the granted ability; a card that names itself inside a quotation is not read for a group (the normalizer writes the name and "this artifact" alike as `~`, so the face is checked before it is rewritten).
- Limits: only a group (`filter`) and `this`; an Aura's "Enchanted creature has \"{T}: ...\"" is still read and not run; a rider or a restriction inside the quotes stays unread.

### "If <test>, instead ..." on a line of its own

- CR: 608.2c, 614.1a
- Gap class: T
- Code: `sentence.ts` (a leading "instead" after the test), `parse-face.ts` (`joinInsteadLines`: a line of a spell that starts "If ..., instead" or ends "... instead." after a line that ends in a period is read as one text with it), `rules/condition/game-state.ts` ("there are seven or more cards in your graveyard" beside "you have ...")
- AST node: `{ kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'amountIs', amount: { kind: 'countOf', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', controller: 'you' } }, compare: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 7 } }, effect: <the replacement>, else: <the line before> }`
- Tests: `parser-p36.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p36.test.ts` (six cards in the graveyard: one land; seven: up to three, and not the one-land search as well), the corpus lines of `convert-grammar-corpus-p2.test.ts`
- Limits: the line before is one instruction (as for a trailing "instead"); a keyword line before it ("Kicker {2}") is not what it replaces.

### Status of the nine cards at parser version 41

Cabaretti Courtyard, Maestros Theater, Riveteers Overlook, Jund Panorama, Fabled Passage, Far Wanderings, Ziatora, the Incinerator, Goldspan Dragon and Bootleggers' Stash read completely and pass `validateCard` (run from the owner's request file with a placeholder power and toughness for the creatures, because the file has none). Fabled Passage is in the golden corpus and changed from unsupported to supported. Whether the deck is fully supported is the owner's run of `pnpm corpus:decks` and `pnpm precon:snapshot` on `decks/korvold.txt`.


## The Korvold slice 4, triggers and small statics (D-179, D-180)

The seven cards of slice 4 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29): The Balrog of Moria, Kaervek the Merciless, Lavalanche, Lobelia Sackville-Baggins, Spelunking, Kardur, Doomscourge and Conqueror's Flail. Eight engine pieces (D-179, `ENGINE_VERSION` 38) and the parser stage P37 (D-180, `PARSER_VERSION` 42). `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each, `parser-p37.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs, `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` pins the engine pieces with SYNTHETIC fixtures (`synthetic-korvold-triggers.ts`).

### "Cycling {3}{R}", "When you cycle this card, create two Treasure tokens."

- CR: 702.29a, 702.29c, 702.29f, 702.35a
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Trigger` `cycled`, `Cost` `discardThis` with `cycling`), `state/events.ts` (`cycled`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (the expansion of Cycling and typecycling marks its `discardThis`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (`CostBreakdown.cycling`; paying the cost emits `cycled` after `discarded`, with the card where it wound up), `rules/triggers.ts` (the pool of a `cycled` event holds the cycled card; `triggeredOf` finds a `cycled` ability in any zone and only on that card), `ast/validate.ts` (a face with the trigger needs Cycling or typecycling), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (a card does not write `cycling`), `ast/features.ts` (`trigger:cycled`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'cycled' }, effect: { kind: 'createToken', token: { kind: 'preset', preset: 'Treasure' }, count: 2 } }` next to `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'cycling', cost: [...] } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` (SYNTHETIC "Treasure Cycler", "Landcycler Seer": the trigger goes above the cycling ability, a typecycling cost triggers it too, the cleanup discard does not), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts`, `scenario-xx-cycling.test.ts` (the marked cost), `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts`, `trigger-text.test.ts` and `korvold-triggers-text.test.ts` (web)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: "When you cycle this card" (`game/trigger-text.ts`) and a "cycles" line in the log (`game/describe.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `trigger.cycled` reads "you cycle this card" and "you cycle ~" (never "a card", "another card" or "or discard"); lowered to `cycled`.
- Limits: this card only.

### "Whenever an opponent casts a spell, ~ deals damage equal to that spell's mana value to any target."

- CR: 202.3, 601.2i, 608.2h
- Gap class: P (the analysis said T + P: the interpreter already read the mana value of the spell that caused a cast trigger; the validator refused it)
- Code: `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`checkAmountObject`: `manaValueOf` of `triggerSubject` when the trigger's subject is on the stack), `rules/amounts.ts` (unchanged)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'manaValueOf', of: { kind: 'triggerSubject' } }, to: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }` in a `castSpell` trigger with `by: 'opponent'`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` ("Spell Echo": a spell of mana value 1 and one of 5, a spell its own controller casts does not trigger it), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts` (the power of a spell is still refused), `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: nothing new.
- What the parser must produce: `amount.mana-value-of` reads "that spell's"; the lowering reads the object whose event caused the trigger (`triggerSubject`) beside a target.

### "~ deals X damage to target player or planeswalker and each creature that player or that planeswalker's controller controls."

- CR: 109.4, 120.2, 306.1
- Gap class: P (a filter on the controller by reference) and T (the target)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.controlledBy`), `rules/select.ts` (the `each` selector reads it, with the targets), `rules/filter-match.ts` (`FilterContext.controllers`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (read only in the `each` selector of an effect, never together with `controller`), `ast/features.ts` (`selector:controllerRef`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'x' }, to: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }, { kind: 'dealDamage', amount: { kind: 'x' }, to: { kind: 'each', filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'], controlledBy: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } } } }] }` with `t1` a player target
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` ("Rolling Blast": the opponent and their creatures only, yourself and your creatures), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts` (refused in a target slot and with `controller`), `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts`, `parser-p37.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: an `each` selector with `controlledBy` has no words (the filter is not one the wording says); the effect line says nothing rather than a wrong thing.
- What the parser must produce: `noun.player-or-planeswalker` is a player target (D-180: the engine has no planeswalker permanents), `noun.each-permanent-of-player` binds "that player" to it (`filter.controller = { noun }`), lowered with `controlledBy`.
- Limits: a planeswalker cannot be the target; "each creature they control" is not read.

### "When ~ enters, exile target creature card from an opponent's graveyard that was put there from the battlefield this turn, then create X Treasure tokens, where X is the exiled card's power."

- CR: 700.4, 400.7, 400.7j, 608.2h
- Gap class: P
- Code: `state/types.ts` (`TurnLog.graveyarded`), `rules/zones.ts` (a card put into a graveyard from the battlefield is logged by its id there), `ast/types.ts` (`ObjectFilter.putFromBattlefieldThisTurn`), `rules/select.ts` and `rules/filter-match.ts` (`FilterContext.graveyarded`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (a graveyard filter, never a static ability), `ast/features.ts` (`selector:putFromBattlefieldThisTurn`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'entersBattlefield', what: { kind: 'this' } }, targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'graveyard', types: ['creature'], controller: 'opponent', putFromBattlefieldThisTurn: true } } }], effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'exile', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }, { kind: 'createToken', token: ..., count: { kind: 'powerOf', of: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } } }] } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` ("Grave Skimmer": the Bears that died this turn, a card already in the graveyard, my own creature, the record empties with the turn), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: nothing new (the target filter has no words).
- What the parser must produce: the suffix after the owner of a graveyard phrase; "the exiled card's power" is "that card's power", the target of the instruction before it, read from last-known information once it has been exiled.

### "When ~ enters, draw a card, then you may put a land card from your hand onto the battlefield. If you put a Cave onto the battlefield this way, you gain 4 life." and "Lands you control enter untapped."

- CR: 614.1c, 614.12, 616.1, 608.2c, 400.7j
- Gap class: P + T
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Replacement` `groupEntersUntapped`), `rules/enters-group.ts` (`groupEntersUntapped`), `rules/zones.ts` (the permanent enters untapped, and a question about paying life to avoid entering tapped is not asked), `ast/validate-angels.ts` (`checkEnteringFilter`), `ast/features.ts` (`replacement:groupEntersUntapped`); the Cave sentence needs no engine change (`objectMatches` of `moved`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'groupEntersUntapped', what: { types: ['land'], controller: 'you' } } }`; `{ kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'objectMatches', of: { kind: 'moved' }, filter: { subtypes: ['Cave'] } }, effect: { kind: 'gainLife', ... } }` after the `moveToZone` of a chosen card
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` ("Daylight Hall": a land that enters tapped on its own, a land with the life payment, a land an effect puts onto the battlefield tapped, the opponent's lands, without the permanent), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts` (a Cave put onto the battlefield gains 4 life and enters untapped, another land gains nothing, none put gains nothing)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Lands you control enter untapped." (`game/static-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `static.group-enters-untapped` for a plural group of the controller's own, refused for a permanent that is one of the group; `condition.put-this-way` as the lead of a sentence, lowered to `objectMatches` of `moved`.
- Limits: the order against another replacement effect is not asked (CR 616.1: the controller's best choice, untapped); a token is not covered; the card's official rulings were not read (Scryfall is not reachable from the sandbox).

### "When ~ enters, until your next turn, creatures your opponents control attack each combat if able and attack a player other than you if able." and "Whenever an attacking creature dies, each opponent loses 1 life and you gain 1 life."

- CR: 508.1d, 611.2a, 611.2c, 500.1, 603.10a, 506.4
- Gap class: P (and a second gap behind the second ability: a creature that was attacking as it died)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `requireAttack`), `state/types.ts` (`ContinuousChange` `requireAttack`, `EffectEnd` `turnStart`), `rules/continuous.ts` (`runRequireAttack`, `expireTurnStartEffects`), `rules/flow.ts` (`startTurn` ends the effects waiting for the turn), `rules/combat-legality.ts` (`groupRequirements`, `requiredAttackers`, `narrowedTargets`), `rules/select.ts` (`attackingCreatures`, `attackersOfPlayer` keep a creature that left combat by dying), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`effect:requireAttack`, `duration:untilYourNextTurn`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'requireAttack', creatures: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'opponent' }, duration: 'untilYourNextTurn', awayFromYou: true }`; `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'dies', what: { types: ['creature'], attacking: true } }, effect: ... }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` ("Herding Horror", "Forced March", "Attack Bane": every creature that can attack must and a declaration without them is refused, a creature that entered later is required too, the effect ends as the controller's next turn begins, a third seat leaves only the other players as targets; an attacker killed by its blocker triggers it, a blocker and a creature outside combat do not), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Until your next turn, creatures your opponents control attack each combat if able and attack a player other than you if able" (`game/korvold-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `effect.require-attack` for "creatures" and a controller after "until your next turn,"; lowered to `requireAttack`; "this turn" stays `effect.restrict-all`.
- Limits: with two players "attack a player other than you" has no player to name and the creatures are only required to attack (CR 508.1d); the older `restrict` of "creatures ... attack this turn if able" keeps its set fixed at resolution.

### "Equipped creature gets +1/+1 for each color among permanents you control." and "As long as ~ is attached to a creature, your opponents can't cast spells during your turn."

- CR: 105.2, 611.3a, 101.2, 604.1, 601.2, 301.5
- Gap class: P
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticPtAmount` `colorsAmong`, `StaticEffect` `opponentsCantCastOnYourTurn`), `rules/derive-count.ts` (`colorsAmongForSource`), `rules/cast-restrictions.ts` (`castForbiddenByOpponentsPermanent`), `rules/legality.ts` (`canCast`, `canCastDuringResolution`), `rules/static-conditions.ts` and `ast/validate-static-condition.ts` (`objectMatches` of `attached` in a static ability's condition), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`amount:colorsAmong`, `static:opponentsCantCastOnYourTurn`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'attached', types: ['creature'] }, power: { kind: 'colorsAmong', filter: { controller: 'you' } }, toughness: { kind: 'colorsAmong', filter: { controller: 'you' } } } }`; `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'opponentsCantCastOnYourTurn' }, condition: { kind: 'objectMatches', of: { kind: 'attached' }, filter: { types: ['creature'] } } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-triggers.test.ts` ("Prism Blade": one color, two, the opponent's colors are not counted; "Tide Seal": unattached it stops nothing, attached the opponent cannot cast during my turn and I can, nor can the opponent be stopped during their own turn), `ast-korvold-triggers-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p37.test.ts`, `korvold-triggers-text.test.ts` (web)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 38, 2026-11-02
- Web: "for each color among permanents you control" (`game/pt-text.ts`); "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn as long as this permanent is attached to a creature." (`game/angels-text.ts`, `game/condition-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `amount.for-each-color` after "gets +1/+1" in `static.pt-count`; `static.opponents-cant-cast` alone or as the statement of `static.conditional-prefix` and `-suffix`; `condition.attached-to` for a creature host.
- Limits: a step other than one for the colors is read and refused; no "spells with ..." variant.

### Status of the seven cards at parser version 42

The Balrog of Moria, Kaervek the Merciless, Lavalanche, Lobelia Sackville-Baggins, Spelunking, Kardur, Doomscourge and Conqueror's Flail read completely and pass `validateCard` (run from the owner's request file with a placeholder power and toughness for the creatures, because the file has none). Whether the deck is fully supported is the owner's run of `pnpm corpus:decks` and `pnpm precon:snapshot` on `decks/korvold.txt`.

## The Korvold slice 3, casting and targets (D-181, D-182)

The three cards of slice 3 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29): Tear Asunder, Stir Up Trouble and Bolt Bend. Four engine pieces (D-181, `ENGINE_VERSION` 39) and the parser stage P38 (D-182, `PARSER_VERSION` 43). `convert-grammar-engine-p38.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each, `parser-p38.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs, `scenario-xx-casting-targets.test.ts` pins the engine pieces with SYNTHETIC fixtures (`synthetic-casting-targets.ts`), and `casual-casting-targets.test.ts` and `casting-targets-text.test.ts` cover the AI and the web wording. No new dialog: the cost choice is a purpose of the existing optional question and the new target a flag of the existing targets question, so no Playwright test was added.

### "Kicker {1}{B}", "Exile target artifact or enchantment. If this spell was kicked, exile target nonland permanent instead."

- CR: 702.33a, 702.33d, 702.33g, 601.2b, 601.2c, 601.2f
- Gap class: P (the analysis said P: the parser read it, the engine refused the target "chosen only if the spell was kicked")
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`TargetSpec.ifKicked`, `unlessKicked`), `rules/legality.ts` (`targetSpecsOf` with the kicked fact, `targetsPossible`, `hasKickedTargets`, `canCast` and `canCastDuringResolution` try both ways), `rules/cast.ts` (`toKicker`: the question is asked only when both ways can be cast, the spell is kicked without asking when only the kicked way can), `ast/validate-cast.ts` (`kickedTargetsUsedOutsideTheirCase`, the face rules: Kicker not Multikicker, no modes, a spell's own text), `ast/supported.ts`, `ast/features.ts` (`ability:ifKicked`)
- AST node: `spell.targets: [{ id: 't1', unlessKicked: true, what: artifact or enchantment }, { id: 't2', ifKicked: true, what: nonland permanent }]` and `effect: { kind: 'conditional', condition: { kind: 'kicked' }, effect: exile t2, else: exile t1 }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-casting-targets.test.ts` ("Rend Asunder": the unkicked cast asks the artifact slot only, the kicked cast the nonland slot only and pays the kicker, a creature only: kicked without asking, no mana for the kicker: unkicked without asking, nothing to exile: not castable, a copy of a kicked spell has the kicked slot; "Kicked Hex": a kicked slot with no unkicked one), `ast-kicker-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p38.test.ts`, `parser-p38.test.ts`, `golden.test.ts` (the hold-out card changes status)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 39, 2026-11-02
- Web: the kicker question is the old one; nothing new.
- What the parser must produce: a target in a spell's "If this spell was kicked, ..." sentence is `ifKicked`; the target of the sentence replaced by "... instead" that the replacement does not name again is `unlessKicked`.
- Limits: Kicker only (not Multikicker), not in a modal spell, a spell's own text only; the AI always pays the kicker when asked (D-110 heuristic), which for this card means a kicked cast whenever the mana is there.

### "As an additional cost to cast this spell, sacrifice an artifact or creature or pay {4}."

- CR: 118.8, 601.2b, 601.2f, 601.2h
- Gap class: P (a choice between two costs)
- Code: `cards/definition.ts` (`CardFace.additionalCostChoice`), `rules/cast.ts` (`toCostChoice` after the kicker, replicate and gift; `answerCostChoice`), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`CostRequest.costOption`, `canPayRequest`, `spellCost` reads `StackData.costOption`), `rules/legality.ts` (`canCast` and the kicker question ask `canPayRequest`), `state/types.ts` (`StackData.costOption`), `state/decisions.ts` (`ConfirmOptionalDecision` purpose `costChoice`, `otherCost`), `api.ts` and `legal-actions.ts` (the answer, cancel), `view.ts` (`ObjectView.additionalCostChoice`), `ast/validate-cost.ts` (`checkAdditionalCostChoice`), `ast/supported.ts`, `testing/driver.ts` (`additionalCost`)
- AST node: `additionalCostChoice: [[{ kind: 'sacrifice', what: { anyOf: [artifact, creature] } }], [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['4'] }]]` on the face, and the spell `destroy target creature`
- Decision and action: after the kicker, replicate and gift and before X and the targets, a `confirmOptional` with `purpose: 'costChoice'`, `cost` the first way and `otherCost` the second, answered by `acceptOptional` (the first) or `declineOptional` (the second); `cancel` rewinds the cast. Not asked when only one way can be paid (the cast goes that way), and the card is not castable when neither can.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-casting-targets.test.ts` ("Stir Trouble": both ways, each way's total cost and payment, only the sacrifice, only {4}, neither, an artifact alone, cancel, the view), `ast-casting-targets-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p38.test.ts`, `casual-casting-targets.test.ts`, `casting-targets-text.test.ts` (web)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 39, 2026-11-02
- Web: the question (the button of each way, the prompt "Stir Up Trouble: sacrifice an artifact or creature, or pay {4}, as an additional cost?", Cancel) and the card's preview line "Additional cost" (`ui/decisions/optional.ts`, `game/cost-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `static.additional-cost` reads "A or B" (one cost each) and lowers it to `additionalCostChoice`.
- Limits: two ways, one cost each in the grammar (the engine takes a list), no X, one choice per face; the AI prefers mana, then a card or life, then a permanent (`casual-permanents.ts`), and casts the spell only when one way is worth paying (`casual-costs.ts`).

### "This spell costs {3} less to cast if you control a creature with power 4 or greater."

- CR: 601.2f, 118.7a, 113.6d
- Gap class: P (a condition on the spell's own reduction)
- Code: `ast/validate-static-condition.ts` (the spell's own `costReduction` takes any condition an `amountIs` condition may, not only the ones `derive` reads), `rules/cast-cost.ts` (`reductionFor` evaluates it for the caster as the cost is determined), `ast/features.ts` (`ability:selfCostReducedIf`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'costReduction', spells: { kind: 'this' }, amount: 3 }, condition: { kind: 'controlsAtLeast', filter: { types: ['creature'], power: { cmp: 'atLeast', value: 4 } }, count: 1 } }`
- Tests: `scenario-xx-casting-targets.test.ts` ("Bent Bolt": the cost in the view with and without the creature, an opponent's creature does not count, the cast is offered with one land only with the creature, only the generic part is reduced, the cost stays locked in), `ast-casting-targets-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p38.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 39, 2026-11-02
- Web: the view's `castCost` is the reduced one (as for the other reductions); no wording of the static ability.
- What the parser must produce: `static.self-cost-reduction` reads " if " and a condition of the controller's own permanents or commander, and the lowering attaches it to the ability.
- Limits: a reduction of other spells (a permanent's) takes no condition.

### "Change the target of target spell or ability with a single target."

- CR: 115.7a, 115.7f, 115.9a, 608.2b, 603.2c
- Gap class: P (a new effect and the question of the new target)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Effect` `changeTarget`, `ObjectFilter.singleTarget`), `rules/change-source.ts` (the instruction and the single-target test, the light half), `rules/change-target.ts` (`runChangeTarget`: no other legal target, the only one, or the question), `rules/change-target-answer.ts` (the answer, then the resolution goes on), `rules/effects.ts` and `rules/stack.ts` (the interpreter makes the instruction, the stack runs it), `rules/filter-match.ts`, `state/events.ts` (`targetChanged`), `state/decisions.ts` (`TargetsDecision.forChange`, `subject`), `api.ts`, `legal-actions.ts` (no cancel), `ast/validate-parts.ts`, `ast/supported.ts` (the slot must say "single target"), `ast/features.ts` (`effect:changeTarget`, `selector:singleTarget`)
- AST node: `spell.targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'stack', singleTarget: true } } }]` and `effect: { kind: 'changeTarget', object: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' } }`
- Decision and action: a `targets` decision of the controller of the effect with `forChange: true`, `subject` the spell or ability, `current` its target and one slot whose `legal` lists the other legal targets; answered by `chooseTargets` with one target; there is no cancel. Nothing is asked when there is none or one.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-casting-targets.test.ts` (the only other target taken with its events, no other target: unchanged and silent, several: the question, the answers listed and accepted, never the current target, a spell with two targets or none is not a legal target, a spell is never its own target, a triggered ability changed for the player who controls it, an opponent's hexproof creature is not an alternative, a new target with Ward triggers it, the division stays, a countered spell), `ast-casting-targets-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p38.test.ts`, `casual-casting-targets.test.ts`, `casting-targets-text.test.ts` (web)
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 39, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Choose a new target for Lightning Bolt." in the targets dock (no cancel), and the log line "You change the target of Lightning Bolt to Giant Spider." (`game/describe.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `effect.change-target` reads "Change the target of target spell or ability with a single target" (also "target spell with ..." and "target activated or triggered ability with ..."); the noun is a target of the stack with `singleTarget`.
- Limits: one target only (CR 115.7b, 115.7c are other effects); the new target is chosen by the controller of the effect among the legal ones (the engine has no planeswalker permanents, `docs/ROADMAP.md`); the AI sends a harmful spell to the opponent's side and a helpful one to its own.

### Status of the three cards at parser version 43

Tear Asunder, Stir Up Trouble and Bolt Bend read completely and pass `validateCard` (run from the owner's request file). Tear Asunder is a hold-out card of the golden corpus and is supported there now. Whether the deck is fully supported is the owner's run of `pnpm corpus:decks` and `pnpm precon:snapshot` on `decks/korvold.txt`.

## The Korvold slice 5, mana spent to cast a spell (D-183, D-184)

The two cards of slice 5 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29): Prossh, Skyraider of Kher and Coin of Mastery. One engine piece with four small parts (D-183, `ENGINE_VERSION` 40) and the parser stage P39 (D-184, `PARSER_VERSION` 44). `convert-grammar-engine-p39.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each, `parser-p39.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs, `scenario-xx-mana-spent.test.ts` pins the engine with SYNTHETIC fixtures (`synthetic-mana-spent.ts`), `mana-payment.test.ts` pins the planner and `mana-spent-text.test.ts` the web wording. No new decision and no new dialog: no agent handler and no Playwright test. The card rulings could not be read (Scryfall is not reachable from the sandbox), so the points that rest on one are marked.

### "When you cast this spell, create X 0/1 red Kobold creature tokens named Kobolds of Kher Keep, where X is the amount of mana spent to cast it."

- CR: 601.2h, 601.2i, 603.2, 113.7a, 111.4, 106.1, 605.3a, 707.10
- Gap class: P + T as the analysis said (a primitive, and templates: the cast trigger of the spell itself, the token name and the amount); the engine side is one piece, not a subsystem (see D-183 for why the "S" fear was not borne out)
- Code: `mana/types.ts` (`ManaObject.sourceTypes`), `mana/payment.ts` (`PaymentPlan.spentTypes`, `actMask`: the mana that pays the activation cost of a costed mana ability is not spent on the spell), `mana/payment-shared.ts` (`PaymentSource.types`), `rules/mana-engine.ts` (the types written on the mana a hand tap makes; `applyPaymentPlan` returns `spent`), `rules/mana-sources.ts`, `rules/pay-manual.ts`, `rules/effects.ts` (`addMana`), `state/types.ts` (`ManaSpent`, `StackData.manaSpent`), `rules/cast.ts` (`withManaSpent`, written as the payment is made, by the automatic payment and by hand), `rules/mana-spent.ts` (`manaSpentOf`), `rules/amounts.ts` (the amount, read from the source or its last-known information), `ast/types.ts` (`Amount` `manaSpent`), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (only in the effect of a `castSpell` trigger with `self`, a `fromType` that is a card type), `ast/features.ts` (`amount:manaSpent`)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'castSpell', filter: { stackKind: 'spell' }, by: 'you', self: true }, effect: { kind: 'createToken', token: { kind: 'custom', definition: { name: 'Kobolds of Kher Keep', subtypes: ['Kobold'], colors: ['R'], power: 0, toughness: 1, ... } }, count: { kind: 'manaSpent' } } }`
- Decision and action: none. The trigger goes on the stack above the spell as it is cast (CR 601.2i, 603.3b) and makes the tokens when it resolves; the amount is what the payment spent: the total cost (the mana cost, X, commander tax, kicker, cost increases) less nothing the player did not pay with mana (a cost reduction lowers the total, Phyrexian life, Convoke, Improvise and Delve are not mana, a free cast is 0). Unused sources and unspent pool mana are not counted. If the spell is countered in response, the trigger still counts what was spent (last-known information).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mana-spent.test.ts` (six lands, a seventh source unused, paid by hand, a Treasure, commander tax (eight), a cost reduction (four), a counter in response), `mana-payment.test.ts` (the planner), `ast-mana-spent-validate.test.ts`, `convert-grammar-engine-p39.test.ts`, `parser-p39.test.ts`, `parser-p39-needs.test.ts`, `mana-spent-text.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 40, 2026-11-02
- Web: the trigger reads "When you cast this spell" (`game/trigger-text.ts`), the token "named Kobolds of Kher Keep" (`game/token-text.ts`) and the amount "the amount of mana spent to cast it" (`game/amount-text.ts`, `game/mana-spent-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `trigger.cast-self` (the event `castSelf`, lowered to `castSpell` with `self`), `token.custom` with `named`, and the value phrase of `amount.where-x`.
- Limits: the amount is read in the cast trigger of the spell itself and in the enters-with count only; a spell's own effect does not read it. A copy of the spell is not cast and has spent nothing.

### "Each creature you control enters with an additional +1/+1 counter on it for each mana from an artifact source spent to cast it."

- CR: 601.2h, 614.1c, 614.12, 122.6, 111.10a, 205.3g
- Gap class: P (the amount; the replacement `othersEnterWithCounters` is D-149's)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`othersEnterWithCounters.count` may be `ManaSpentAmount`), `rules/enters-group.ts` (the count reads the spell the permanent came from, as it enters), `ast/validate-angels.ts`, and the same recording as above
- AST node: `{ kind: 'replacement', replacement: { kind: 'othersEnterWithCounters', what: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' }, counter: '+1/+1', count: { kind: 'manaSpent', fromType: 'artifact' } } }`
- Decision and action: none. A creature spell paid with mana from Sol Ring, a Treasure or any other artifact source (also a Treasure sacrificed for its mana, tapped by hand earlier: the types are written on the mana when it is made) enters with one counter for each such mana; a creature put onto the battlefield without being cast, a token, one cast without paying its mana cost, and one paid with lands only have none. It does nothing for itself (Coin of Mastery is an artifact) and two such permanents add up.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-mana-spent.test.ts` (Sol Ring, lands only, a Treasure, a Treasure tapped by hand earlier, the Kobold tokens that were not cast, a cascaded creature, "for each mana spent", two together), `convert-grammar-engine-p39.test.ts`, `parser-p39.test.ts`
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 40, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Each creature you control enters with an additional +1/+1 counter on it for each mana from an artifact source spent to cast it" (`game/angels-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `amount.for-each-mana-spent` after the quantity in `static.others-enter-with-counters`, with "it" the creature that enters.
- Limits: a Treasure counts because it is an artifact (the rulings could not be read); "an artifact source" is the permanent's card types at the time the mana was made.

### Status of the two cards at parser version 44

Prossh, Skyraider of Kher and Coin of Mastery read completely and pass `validateCard` (run from the owner's request file). Neither is in the golden corpus (they are acceptance names for the owner's `pnpm corpus:extract`); a training card of it, Sowing Mycospawn, is supported now because both its "When you cast this spell" lines read (`convert-grammar-engine-p39.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with its wording). Whether the deck is fully supported is the owner's run of `pnpm corpus:decks` and `pnpm precon:snapshot` over `decks/korvold.txt`.

## The Korvold slice 6, keywords (D-185, D-186)

The three cards of slice 6 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29): Impulsive Pilferer (Encore), Dragon Broodmother (Devour 2 on a token) and Rhythm of the Wild (the group "can't be countered" and Riot given to a group). The engine part is D-185 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 41) and the parser stage P40 is D-186 (`PARSER_VERSION` 45). `convert-grammar-engine-p40.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each, `parser-p40.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs, `scenario-xx-encore.test.ts` and `scenario-xx-devour-riot.test.ts` pin the engine with SYNTHETIC fixtures (`synthetic-korvold-keywords.ts`), `casual-korvold-keywords.test.ts` the agent, `korvold-keywords-games.test.ts` the soak (the deck style `korvoldKeywords`) and `korvold-keywords-text.test.ts` the web wording. The official rulings could not be read (Scryfall is not reachable from the sandbox): nothing below rests on one.

### "Encore {3}{R}"

- CR: 702.141a (Encore), 602.2, 113.6b, 602.5d, 118.1, 113.7a, 608.2h, 707.2, 111.4, 508.1d, 702.10, 603.7, 701.21, 400.7, 903.9a
- Gap class: K as the analysis said, with two engine pieces it did not list: the cost "Exile this card from your graveyard" and a requirement to attack a given player
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `encore`, `Cost` `exileThis`, `Effect` `encore`), `rules/derive-keywords.ts` (`encoreAbility`: the expansion), `rules/activate-legality.ts` (`hasGraveyardAbility`), `rules/activate-costs.ts` (the cost), `rules/effects.ts` (the effect `encore`: one `enterToken` instruction for each opponent), `rules/enter-token.ts` (the token and `bindEncoreToken`), `rules/encore.ts` (the requirement, the haste and the delayed sacrifice), `rules/combat-legality.ts` (`mustAttackPlayer`), `state/types.ts` (`ContinuousChange` `mustAttackPlayer`, `Instruction` `enterToken.encoreFor`), `ast/validate*.ts`, `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `faces[n].abilities: [{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'encore', cost: [{ kind: 'mana', symbols: ['3', 'R'] }] } }]` on a permanent card. The engine expands it into `{ kind: 'activated', cost: [mana, { kind: 'exileThis' }], effect: { kind: 'encore' }, timing: 'sorcery', functionsIn: 'graveyard' }`. A card may also write an ability of its own with the cost `exileThis` and `functionsIn: 'graveyard'`.
- Decision and action: `activate { object, abilityIndex }` for the card in its owner's graveyard at sorcery speed, as for Unearth. The mana is paid and the card is exiled as costs (a cancel before the last cost rewinds both); the ability is on the stack with the card in exile. As it resolves a token that is a copy of the card as it last was in the graveyard (last-known information) is created under the controller's control for each opponent (one with two players); a question the token asks as it enters (riot, devour, "choose a color") is asked. Each token has haste until end of turn, must attack its opponent if able (the declaration of attackers that leaves it out is refused; a battle or a planeswalker does not satisfy it) and is sacrificed by a delayed triggered ability at the beginning of the next end step, which goes on the stack and can be responded to. The copy has the abilities of the card, so a "dies" trigger triggers when it is sacrificed.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-encore.test.ts` (the option from the graveyard only with the mana, sorcery speed in the opponent's turn, with something on the stack and in combat, the card in exile while the ability waits, a cancel, the card used once, the copy with haste and with the abilities of the card, the declaration of no attackers refused, the damage, the requirement and the haste ending with the turn, the sacrifice at the end step of either main phase, a token that left before then, a copy that asks riot, validation refusals), `casual-korvold-keywords.test.ts`, `korvold-keywords-games.test.ts`. Mutation-checked on a scratch copy: no delayed sacrifice fails 5 tests, no haste 5, the attack not required 2, the card not exiled as the cost 3, not sorcery speed 1; one mutation survives and is equivalent with two players and no battle or planeswalker in the game (the narrowing of the attack targets to the opponent: the only target is the opponent).
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 41, 2026-11-02
- Web: a card in the viewer's graveyard with an `activate` option gets the chip and badge "Name Encore {3}{R}" (`ui/decisions/ability-label.ts`, `isEncore`; the exile is part of the keyword, not of the price), the stack shows "For each opponent, create a token that's a copy of this card that attacks that opponent this turn if able. The tokens gain haste. Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step", a cost of the card reads "Exile this card from your graveyard" (`game/cost-text.ts`), and the keyword chip "Enc" carries the reminder text (`ui/board/keywords.ts`, `game/keywords-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `Encore {3}{R}` (a line of its own, reminder text dropped) lowers to the keyword above, declaring `keyword:encore` and `cost:mana`; a cost that is not plain mana, an {X} or a keyword list is not read.
- Notes: the AI (`casual-activations.ts`) activates Encore as it does Unearth: before combat, for a creature that would attack safely or for the win.

### "Devour 2" on a token: "create a 1/1 red and green Dragon creature token with flying and devour 2"

- CR: 702.82a (Devour), 614.12, 122.6, 701.21, 111.2, 603.7
- Gap class: K (a decision as a permanent enters, which the pipeline already asked for the choices of D-100 and D-151: not S)
- Code: `rules/enters-keywords.ts` (`devourOf`, `devourCandidates`, `devourDecision`, `keywordEntryCounters`), `rules/zones.ts` (`moveObject`: the wrapper that sacrifices (`sacrificeForDevour`) and the question, asked last), `rules/enter-token.ts` (`tokenAsks`, `runEnterToken`), `rules/tokens.ts` (`createTokens` takes what the keywords gave, `hypotheticalToken`), `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionDevour`), `api.ts` (the answer), `state/decisions.ts` (`choosePermanent` purpose `devour`), `state/types.ts` (`ResolutionContext.entersDevour`, `enterToken.devoured`), `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `devour`), `ast/validate-keywords.ts`, `ast/validate-cast.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (one on a token; none on a token a replacement creates)
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'devour', count: 2 } }` on a permanent card that is not a land, or in the `abilities` of a token definition.
- Decision and action: as the permanent enters (a spell resolving, an effect putting it onto the battlefield, a token being created) its controller gets a `choosePermanent` of purpose `devour`: `legal` are the creatures they control, `min` 0, `max` the number of them, `subject` the permanent that enters (for a token, `source` is the resolving ability). The answer is a `choosePermanents` action; none is a legal answer. The creatures chosen are sacrificed first (the `sacrificed` event, their "dies" triggers go on the stack afterwards), then the permanent enters with N +1/+1 counters for each. Nothing is asked when there is no creature to sacrifice, and the permanent itself is never one of them (it is not on the battlefield yet).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-devour-riot.test.ts` (the question as the spell resolves, one and two creatures sacrificed with their "dies" triggers, none, nothing to sacrifice, the opponent's creatures and non-creatures refused, a refused answer, a card that returns from the graveyard, the token of the Broodmother in each upkeep with its counters, none, flying, the Broodmother sacrificing itself, the opponent's upkeep, riot and devour together, validation), `convert-grammar-engine-p40.test.ts`, `casual-korvold-keywords.test.ts`, `korvold-keywords-games.test.ts` (161 questions in 100 games). Mutation-checked: the counters ignoring N fails 6 tests, no sacrifice 3, the token not asking 6.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 41, 2026-11-02
- Web: the question uses the permanent picker (`ui/decisions/permanents.ts`: "Choose any number of creatures to sacrifice as X enters (devour). None is fine." and "Choose any number of creatures to sacrifice as the token Y creates enters (devour)."), the chip "Dev 2" has the reminder text, the token reads "with flying and devour 2" (`game/token-text.ts`). The log says the sacrifices as it says any (`sacrificed`).
- What the parser must produce: `Devour 2` (a keyword of `keyword.line`, the number in digits) and "with flying and devour 2" inside a token description (`token.custom`): one keyword and "devour N", or "devour N" alone. "Devour [quality]" is not read.
- Notes: the AI (`casual-keywords.ts`) sacrifices only tokens of power 1, never a card.

### "Nontoken creatures you control have riot" and Riot

- CR: 702.136a, 702.136b (Riot), 614.12, 122.6, 702.10, 305.1
- Gap class: K + P. The analysis proposed a replacement of the entering creature; riot is a keyword, which a static ability gives and which each instance asks separately
- Code: `rules/enters-keywords.ts` (`riotInstances`: the printed ones and those the battlefield's static abilities give, `riotDecision`, `keywordEntryCounters`, `riotGivesHaste`), `rules/zones.ts` and `rules/enter-token.ts` (the questions, riot first), `rules/stack.ts` (`answerResolutionRiot`), `api.ts` (`answerChooseOption`, and `playLand` for a land creature), `rules/derive-alt.ts` (`riotHaste` gives haste), `state/types.ts` (`GameObject.riotHaste`, `ResolutionContext.entersRiot`, `enterToken.riot`), `state/decisions.ts` (`chooseOption` purpose `riot`, `riotSoFar`), `ast/types.ts` (`Keyword` `riot`), `ast/features.ts` (`GRANTABLE_KEYWORDS`), `ast/validate-attach.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/validate-continuous.ts`, `ast/validate-dragons.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'keyword', keyword: { kind: 'riot' } }` (printed) and `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'grantKeyword', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you', token: false } }, keyword: { kind: 'riot' } } }` (given to a group; any other subject, an effect and "enters as a copy" are refused by `validateCard`).
- Decision and action: for each instance of riot a `chooseOption` of purpose `riot`, options `counter` and `haste`, before the permanent is on the battlefield (a spell resolving, an effect, a token, a land played from the hand, which waits in the hand and is played again with the answers). `counter` is an additional +1/+1 counter as it enters; `haste` sets `GameObject.riotHaste`, which `derive` turns into the haste keyword for as long as the permanent has its abilities. A permanent with two instances (its own and one the enchantment gives) is asked twice, each question carrying the earlier answers in `riotSoFar`; two counters, a counter and haste or haste twice are all legal. A token is not a nontoken creature, so the enchantment gives it nothing; a creature that is already on the battlefield when the enchantment arrives is not asked.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-devour-riot.test.ts` (the counter, haste and the attack the turn it arrives, a refused answer, two instances, one counter and haste, a token with riot of its own, the opponent's creatures, no enchantment, one that came first, a creature that returns from the graveyard, a land creature played from the hand with one instance and with two, validation), `convert-grammar-engine-p40.test.ts`, `casual-korvold-keywords.test.ts`, `korvold-keywords-games.test.ts` (477 questions in 100 games). Mutation-checked: the haste not remembered fails 6 tests, the granted instances ignored 7, one question for two instances 2, the land path forgetting the earlier answer 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 41, 2026-11-02
- Web: the question is the named-option dialog (no new dialog): "As X enters, choose a +1/+1 counter or haste (riot)." with the buttons "A +1/+1 counter" and "Haste" (`ui/decisions/option-names.ts`, `Overlays.tsx`), the log "You choose a +1/+1 counter for X (riot)." (`game/describe.ts`), the chip "Riot" with the reminder text, the static line "Nontoken creatures you control have riot".
- What the parser must produce: `Riot` (a keyword of `keyword.line`) and, through the shared grantable words, `Nontoken creatures you control have riot.` (`static.permanents`), lowered to the group grant above, declaring `static:grantKeyword` and `keyword:riot`.
- Notes: the AI (`casual-keywords.ts`) takes haste when the creature can attack at once (its own turn, before combat) and nothing can block it, else the counter.

### "Creature spells you control can't be countered."

- CR: 101.2, 701.6a, 604.1, 113.6g
- Gap class: P, as the analysis said (a group version of the spell's own `cantBeCountered`)
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticEffect` `spellsCantBeCountered`), `rules/restrictions.ts` (`canBeCountered`, `spellsCantBeCounteredByPermanent`), `ast/validate-artifacts.ts`, `ast/validate.ts`, `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'spellsCantBeCountered', spells: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } } }`; the filter describes the spell as it is on the stack (no zone or stack kind), `controller: 'you'` is the controller of the permanent.
- Decision and action: none. A counter effect that names such a spell does nothing to it (it stays on the stack and resolves); the same permanent does nothing for the opponent's spells or for a noncreature spell of its controller, and a permanent that lost its abilities gives nothing. The spell's own "can't be countered" is unchanged.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-devour-riot.test.ts` (a creature spell survives a counterspell and resolves, a noncreature spell is countered, the opponent's creature spell is countered, no enchantment, validation), `convert-grammar-engine-p40.test.ts`. Mutation-checked: the ability ignored fails 1 test, any controller instead of the permanent's 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 41, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Creature spells you control can't be countered." (`game/static-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `static.spells-cant-be-countered`: "Creature spells you control can't be countered." and its siblings ("Spells you control", "Noncreature spells ...", a subtype or a list of types), declaring `static:spellsCantBeCountered`.

### Status of the three cards at parser version 45

Impulsive Pilferer, Dragon Broodmother and Rhythm of the Wild read completely and pass `validateCard` (run from the owner's request file wording through `parser-p40.test.ts`). None is in the golden corpus (they are acceptance names for the owner's `pnpm corpus:extract`), and no card of the corpus changed status: training 162 of 192, hold-out 52 of 61, as at parser version 44.

## The Korvold slice 7a, expensive singles (D-187, D-188)

Three of the four cards of slice 7 of `docs/proposals/korvold-deck-analysis.md` (the owner's request file of 2026-10-29): Swashbuckler Extraordinaire, Decadent Dragon // Expensive Taste and Moraug, Fury of Akoum (Ancient Adamantoise waits for the owner's decision on the order of prevention, `docs/proposals/damage-redirection.md`). The engine part is D-187 (`ENGINE_VERSION` 42) and the parser stage P41 is D-188 (`PARSER_VERSION` 46). `convert-grammar-engine-p41.test.ts` plays a SYNTHETIC card with the real wording of each (the Adventure through a two-face stored card), `parser-p41.test.ts` holds the real texts as the nodes the engine runs, `scenario-xx-korvold-singles.test.ts` pins the engine with SYNTHETIC fixtures (`synthetic-korvold-singles.ts`), `casual-korvold-singles.test.ts` the agent, `korvold-singles-games.test.ts` the soak (the deck style `korvoldSingles`) and `korvold-singles-text.test.ts` the web wording. The official rulings could not be read (Scryfall is not reachable from the sandbox): nothing below rests on one.

### "Whenever you attack, you may sacrifice one or more Treasures. When you do, up to that many target creatures gain double strike until end of turn."

- CR: 508.1, 603.2, 603.2c, 603.12, 603.3d, 601.2c, 701.21, 608.2d, 111.10a, 702.4 (double strike), 611.2a
- Gap class: P (hard), as the analysis said: the sacrifice of a chosen number, a number read by a later instruction, "up to that many" targets
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`ChooseSelector.atLeastOne`, `TargetSpec.upToThatMany`), `rules/sacrifice-choice.ts` (`chooseSacrifice.atLeastOne`: `min` 1), `rules/effects.ts` (the question of a `may` of such a sacrifice with no candidate is not asked), `rules/may-effects.ts` (`runReflexive`: the slot gets `min` 0 and `max` the number sacrificed), `ast/validate-parts.ts` (`declareTargets`: `upToThatMany` only in a reflexive ability after a sacrifice, with no `min`, `max` or `divide`), `ast/validate-angels.ts` (`attackBatch` with `atLeast` 1), `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'attackBatch', who: 'you', atLeast: 1 }, effect: { kind: 'sequence', effects: [{ kind: 'may', effect: { kind: 'sacrifice', objects: { kind: 'choose', filter: { subtypes: ['Treasure'] }, anyNumber: true, atLeastOne: true } } }, { kind: 'reflexive', targets: [{ id: 't1', what: { kind: 'object', filter: { zone: 'battlefield', types: ['creature'] } }, upToThatMany: true }], effect: { kind: 'grantKeyword', objects: { kind: 'target', id: 't1' }, keyword: { kind: 'doubleStrike' }, duration: 'untilEndOfTurn' } }] } }`
- Decision and action: the `may` is a `confirmOptional` (accept or decline; not asked when no Treasure is there); accepting is a `choosePermanent` of purpose `sacrifice` with `min` 1 and `max` the Treasures the player controls, answered by `choosePermanents`; the reflexive ability is created at once, and as it goes on the stack its controller answers a `targets` decision of one slot with `min` 0 and `max` the number sacrificed (nothing is asked when no creature can be chosen); the targets gain double strike until end of turn. Declining sacrifices nothing and makes no ability.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-singles.test.ts` (sacrificing both Treasures and two targets, one Treasure and at most one target or none, none refused after yes, declining, one question for two attackers, an opponent's creature as a target, the keyword gone at the cleanup step, no attackers, no Treasure and no question, validation of every shape that is refused), `convert-grammar-engine-p41.test.ts` (double strike doubles the damage). Mutation-checked: `atLeastOne` ignored fails 1 test, a target more than the Treasures sacrificed 1, the question asked with no Treasure 1.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 42, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Whenever you attack" (a trigger with `atLeast` 1 says no count), the gist "You may sacrifice one or more Treasure permanents you control. When you do, up to that many target creatures gain double strike until end of turn" (`ui/decisions/ability-label.ts`), the sacrifice prompt "Sacrifice one or more Treasure permanents you control for X." (`ui/decisions/permanents.ts`, `isOneOrMore`).
- What the parser must produce: `trigger.you-attack` (lowered as `attackBatch` with `atLeast` 1), `effect.sacrifice` with `noun.one-or-more-permanents` (`selector:atLeastOne`), the reflexive sentence "When you do, up to that many target creatures gain ... until end of turn" (`effect.gain-keyword-targets`; `selector:upToThatMany`).
- Notes: the AI never says yes (the casual agent declines a costly "you may"); if it said yes, `choosePermanentToSacrifice` gives up the Treasures first.

### "Each creature you control gets +1/+0 for each time it has attacked this turn."

- CR: 508.1, 611.3a, 613.4c, 604.1
- Gap class: P, a small amount, as the analysis said
- Code: `ast/types.ts` (`StaticPtAmount` `timesAttacked`), `rules/derive-count.ts` (`staticAmountOf` with the recipient: the entries of `TurnLog.attacked` that are the permanent), `rules/derive-statics.ts`, `ast/validate-parts.ts` (plain +N/+N only), `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'static', static: { kind: 'modifyPt', subject: { kind: 'filter', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } }, power: { kind: 'timesAttacked' }, toughness: 0 } }`
- Decision and action: none. A creature that has not attacked this turn has no bonus; one that attacked in two combat phases has +2; the opponent's creatures are not in the group; the bonus ends with the turn (the turn log is emptied as a turn begins).
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-singles.test.ts` (no bonus before an attack, +1 after, each creature on its own, not for the opponent's creatures, kept to the postcombat main phase and gone next turn, +2 after two combats), `convert-grammar-engine-p41.test.ts`. Mutation-checked: every attacker counted instead of the creature's own fails 2 tests.
- Added in: `ENGINE_VERSION` 42, 2026-11-02
- Web: "Creatures you control get +1/+0 for each time they have attacked this turn." (`game/static-text.ts`, `game/pt-text.ts`).
- What the parser must produce: `static.pt-times-attacked`: "Each creature you control gets +1/+0 for each time it has attacked this turn." (a step of +1 or +0 for each stat, not both 0), declaring `static:modifyPt` and `amount:timesAttacked`.

### "Landfall — Whenever a land you control enters, if it's your main phase, there's an additional combat phase after this phase. At the beginning of that combat, untap all creatures you control."

- CR: 500.8, 500.9, 500.10, 500.11, 505.1, 506.1, 603.4, 603.7, 702.4
- Gap class: P (hard), as the analysis said, but not S: the "ordered list of added phases" is a short list in the turn state, because CR 500.8 adds a phase directly after the one that is going on, so the newest is always first
- Code: `state/types.ts` (`TurnState.addedPhases`, `addedAfter`, `AddedPhase`), `ast/types.ts` (`Condition` `yourMainPhase`, `Effect` `extraCombat.atBeginning`), `rules/conditions.ts` (`yourMainPhase`), `rules/effects.ts` (`extraCombat` puts the phase first in the list, after the phase that is going on or the one an added phase follows), `rules/flow.ts` (`endStep`: when the last step of a phase ends the first added phase begins, with its delayed untap created as it begins; when the list is empty after an added phase the turn goes on with the phase after the one the added phases followed), `ast/validate.ts` and `ast/validate-dragons.ts` (an `extraCombat` only in an ability of an attack or with the intervening "if" `yourMainPhase`; `atBeginning` is a group untap), `ast/features.ts`
- AST node: `{ kind: 'triggered', trigger: { kind: 'landfall' }, interveningIf: { kind: 'yourMainPhase' }, effect: { kind: 'extraCombat', atBeginning: { kind: 'untap', objects: { kind: 'each', filter: { types: ['creature'], controller: 'you' } } } } }`
- Decision and action: none. A land that enters in a main phase of its controller's turn adds a combat phase directly after that phase: after the precombat main phase it comes before the combat of the turn (so the turn has two combats, then the postcombat main phase), after the postcombat main phase it is followed by the end step (no main phase follows it). Two lands in one main phase add two (CR 500.8), each untapping at its own beginning; the combat of the turn untaps nothing. A land that enters in another phase, or in a main phase of an opponent's turn, adds nothing.
- Tests: `scenario-xx-korvold-singles.test.ts` (the steps of a turn with a land in each main phase, the creatures untapped as the added combat begins and attacking again with +2/+0, two lands in one main phase and the combat of the turn untapping nothing, a land that enters in the beginning of combat step, a land in the opponent's main phase, validation), `scenario-xx-extra-combat.test.ts` (the Dragons cards run as before on the list), `convert-grammar-engine-p41.test.ts`.
